## Automatic updates: "Update failed: already-installed" (2026-10-20)
Bug (owner's screenshot, a user report): Settings → Composer Plus updates stays on "Checking for updates…" / fails when the user presses the button. Reproduced in an isolated dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 web home with `dsh-composer-plus@1.7.1` from npm: **Update now** → "Update failed: already-installed". Cause: dsh 0.2.1's `installBundle` refuses a registry spec whose name the profile already has when HMR runs (`dsh-plugin-manager` index.js:1991); dsh's own **Update** button removes and installs again. 0.2.0-rc.2 has no such refusal.
Fix: first `name@version` (the user's registry, no URL dependency; 0.2.0 and non-HMR profiles keep working as before); only when dsh answers `already-installed` (it refuses before pnpm runs), install the exact npm tarball (`https://registry.npmjs.org/dsh-composer-plus/-/dsh-composer-plus-<v>.tgz`): a spec the profile does not have, so pnpm replaces the dependency in one step and dsh restores the files on a failure. The profile then keeps the tarball URL as its dependency. Accept `application: 'applied'` (live: status "current", notification `notify.applied`), `restart-required`, and a `failed` result that has `version === latest` (pnpm installed it; only the live start failed, for example when the bundle patch changed from 1.7.1 to 1.8.0: "sessionController … already registered"). After such an install the old Host routes can answer 404 "not found" until the restart: `check()` keeps "restart" then, and other non-JSON answers show `HTTP <status>` instead of a JSON parse error.
Checks: `test/update.test.mjs` (tarball spec, applied → current + notify.applied, failed-with-version → restart, 404 after install keeps restart, 404 alone = `HTTP 404`) red on the old client, green now. Real dsh (isolated homes, owner's Chrome through the Playwright extension): 0.2.1-rc.2 1.7.1 → 1.9.1 "installed, restart" and 1.9.1 loads after restart; 0.2.1-rc.2 1.9.0 (tarball) → 1.9.1 applied live, route 200 with 1.9.1; 0.2.0-rc.2 1.7.1 → 1.9.1 "restart", 1.9.1 loads after restart. Texts: `notify.applied` in en, zh, 11 locales. README (13): install spec and the restart limit.

## Feature ideas: Plan all (2026-10-20)
Request (user): with several ideas in the Later list, start them all together in one plan-mode chat; the AI prepares a suitable prompt.
Design (part of `featureIdeas`, no new switch: it starts a chat only on a click): a **Plan all (N)** button in the Later list head when the list has 2 or more ideas. The client sends `startAll: [ids it shows]`; the Host checks 2..50 distinct ids that all exist (else 409, nothing starts), guards them with the same `starting` set as a single start, calls `startIdeaChat` with the list (always plan mode, also when `plan: false` comes along), then removes exactly those ids (an idea that another window added meanwhile stays). `ideaPrompt([...])` numbers the ideas and asks for one plan that builds shared parts once, in an order that keeps the project working, with checks per feature. No extra model call: the plan-mode chat itself is the AI that plans. Started list shows "A + B + C".
Checks: `test/feature-ideas.test.mjs` (bad input 400, missing id 409 keeps the list, always plans, one /plan, a newly added idea stays, the prompt text, a list of one = the single prompt); `test/feature-ideas-client.test.mjs` (label with count, ids sent, disabled while starting, no button for one idea, blocked while a save runs); `test/browser-feature-ideas.mjs` step 7 PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (one new chat gets both ideas, plan chip on, list empty). Texts: en, zh, 11 locales; README + 12 translations + llms.txt.

## Project name: click shows the chat in the sidebar (2026-10-20)
Request (user): a click on the project name under the composer finds the chat in the workspace list, in list or folder view, opens a collapsed project, scrolls, blinks 5 s; say in Settings that it is clickable.
Design (part of `projectName`): the pill is a button; `locateChat` steps (120 ms apart, at most 40) until `[data-row-key="session:<id>"]` exists: open the left sidebar (dsh's toggle), a closed parent project (project tree view), the closed project, then its `overflow:<id>` Show more; then `scrollIntoView({block:'center'})` and `data-composer-plus-locate` for 5 s (CSS `cp-locate` blinks 5 × 1 s; reduced motion: a steady outline). A new click stops the old search; unmount stops it too. No open project is ever closed. Settings description and tooltip say what a click does (`projectName.locate`). ponytail: dsh has no reveal API for plugins (its own `revealSessionId` stays inside the sidebar), so this clicks dsh's rows.
Checks: `test/project-name.test.mjs` (button + tooltip, fake sidebar: closed sidebar → toggle → project → Show more, open project not clicked, project tree parent first, a new click stops the old one, blink ends at 5 s, unknown chat gives up); `test/browser-turn-rollback.mjs` step 0 PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (closed project opens, row gets `cp-locate`, marker gone after 5 s). Texts: en, zh, 11 locales; README + 12 translations + llms.txt.

Review (subagent, a464930): P2 locateChat clicked dsh's sidebar toggle at each step when the open sidebar showed no project rows (search results, empty filter) → toggle at most once, and any `[data-row-key]` row means open. P3: registry spec first, tarball only on `already-installed`; a `failed` start after the install is logged (`console.warn`); Plan all removes all ids in one write (`laterStore.remove(cwd, ids)`); Windows `\\` paths in the project-tree parent search; the `live` ref in ProjectNamePill removed. Each fix has a test that fails on a464930. Real dsh 0.2.1-rc.2, 1.7.1 → 1.9.1: `name@version` refused, tarball installed, warning logged, "restart" shown.

## Feature ideas: no repeats + History (2026-10-20)
Request (user): skipped, started and dropped ideas came again; do not suggest an idea twice, and keep the earlier ideas in a History.
Design (part of `featureIdeas`): the Host keeps `storages/dsh-composer-plus/idea-history.json` (the same owner-only, ordered, atomic `laterStore`, `HISTORY_MAX` 200 per project, oldest leave first). `suggestIdeas` gets `avoid(cwd)` = history + Later titles: they go to the model ("Do not suggest any of them again"), a repeated title (case and spaces do not count) is dropped, new ones are recorded; only repeats → 502 "try again", nothing recorded. A start (card ✓/▶, Later Plan/Build now/Plan all) marks the titles `started` (adds them when missing). Route `/api/composer-plus.idea-history`: GET list, POST `remove` (Forget) or `clear` (Forget all). Client: a **History** button in the head opens the list (title, summary, Started/Suggested, date, Forget, Forget all); keys do nothing there; the deck busy guard covers every history request. ponytail: a repeat is found by its title only (the prompt asks the model to skip renamed ones too).
Checks: `test/feature-ideas.test.mjs` (prompt has the titles, repeats dropped, only repeats = error and nothing recorded, store order/dedupe/started/cap, routes 400/GET/remove/clear, owner-only file, start and Plan all mark started) red on the previous commit; `test/feature-ideas-client.test.mjs` (History view, marks, Forget, one change at a time, keys off, back); `test/browser-feature-ideas.mjs` step 7-8 PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (second call lists the 4 earlier titles, the repeat is not a card, History shows 6 with the 5 started marked, 0600 file). `featureIdeas.webp` retaken (16 kB, shows History). Texts: en, zh, 11 locales; README + 12 translations + llms.txt.
Review (subagent): P1 Forget all called a missing `history.change` (500) → `history.clear`; P2 a broken later.json/idea-history.json blocked Suggest → `avoid` reads each store best-effort (the file stays as it is); P2 the card start route read the session again after the chat started (a failure there put the card back → duplicate chat) → `startIdeaChat` returns its `cwd`; P3 Forget on an idea still in the Later list did nothing → "In the Later list" instead of Forget; P3 a failed history read showed "Saving…" → only the error; P3 the unused `laterStore` max parameter removed. Each fix has a test; Forget all also in the real-app test (both dsh versions).

## Tether pairing: approval vanishes, no scan feedback, Camera app QR (2026-10-21)
Request (owner, on his iPhone 13): the Approve card on the Mac shows and then goes away; nothing on the phone says the QR was read; first-time users scan with the iPhone Camera app and nothing works; the pairing UI looks generic ("AI-made").
Root cause (verified): the phone still had a pairing for this Mac's old certificate (`activeMac` eb26…, the gateway now pins 7c97…, `devices.json` empty). Its reconnect loop calls `http(oldFp)` every few seconds; `http()` keeps one shared `URLSession` and calls `invalidateAndCancel()` when the fingerprint changes, so it cancelled the pending `POST /pair`. The gateway saw the socket close and dropped the pending request: the Mac's card vanished. The QR text was raw JSON, so the Camera app could only offer a web search.
Invariants: pinning, one-time code, Mac approval and the RPC allowlist stay as they are. A pairing link that another app opens never pairs without a tap in Tether. Old pasted JSON codes still work.
Design:
- Gateway: QR/paste payload = `tether://pair?v=1&host&port&fp&code&mac[&ts]` (Camera offers "Open in Tether"). `state().last` = the latest pairing result `{name, result: approved|denied|cancelled|expired, at}` for 60 s. `/approve` and `/deny` for a request that is gone → 409 with a plain sentence.
- Admin page: a pending request replaces the QR (name, IP, countdown, Deny/Approve); a result line (connected / stopped waiting / timed out / denied); a large QR with two short lines (Camera or Tether, Tether must be installed); "Copy pairing link" instead of raw JSON; no `alert()`.
- iOS: pairing has its own pinned `URLSession`, a cancel button, a background task and no screen lock while it waits. Phases: code read → waiting for Approve (countdown) → connected (haptic) or a specific failure with "Scan again". The scanner shows a frame and a hint, and keeps scanning on a QR that is not a Tether code. `tether://pair` links open a confirm card. A new pairing replaces an old entry with the same address and Mac name (old certificate). New start screen.
Checks: `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` (link payload, result record, 409); iOS unit tests (link + JSON invites, replacement rule) on the iOS 26.5 simulator; device build, signed install, agent-device on the iPhone; `test/browser-mobile-connect.mjs` (ports from env) + SHOT=1 on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; `npm test`.
Allowed paths: `tether/**`, `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs`, `test/browser-mobile-connect.mjs`, `test/tether-e2e.mjs`, `assets/features/mobileConnect.webp`, README*.md, llms.txt, this plan.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-21): `npm test` exit 0 (gateway 19/19: link payload with %20, outcomes approved/denied/cancelled/expired, 409 for a gone request); iOS unit tests 26/26 on the iOS 26.5 simulator, no new warnings. Simulator end to end against a throwaway gateway on ports 19490/19491 (`tether/build/demo-gateway.mjs`, not shipped): `simctl openurl` → iOS "Open in Tether" prompt → confirm card "Connect to Demo Mac?" → waiting card with countdown → Approve → "Connected to Demo Mac" → Sessions; with a stale pairing (old certificate) reconnecting in the background the request stayed pending for 30 s (the original bug), and after Approve the stale entry was removed (one "Demo Mac" in Settings); Cancel → Mac page `last.result = cancelled`; no Approve → phone "Nobody clicked Approve on the Mac in time". Mac page checked in a browser (idle, pending card, outcome). Signed build installed and launched on the owner's iPhone 13 (iOS 27.2). Self-review fixes: watchdog flag set on the TLS callback itself; `.done` counts as busy; `settle` on a destroyed socket records `cancelled` and makes no device. Open: `test/browser-mobile-connect.mjs` + SHOT=1 (needs ports 19390/19391, used by the live dsh); live gateway copy (needs the owner's approval; takes effect after a dsh restart).

## Tether: Composer Plus features on the phone, kept in step (2026-10-21)
Request (owner): every Composer Plus feature must also be in Tether; when a feature is added to the plugin, Tether must follow.
Research (subagent, read-only): 23 `FEATURES`. Already covered: contentSearch (`session/search`), mobileConnect, activeProjectsFirst (Tether follows dsh's order), projectName (folder name), notifications (socket-only; APNs out of scope). Not for a phone: resumeAfterRestart (Host-only), inputHistory (hardware keys; Edit/Resend cover it), keepFocus (desktop DOM focus), autoUpdate (desktop plugin install). Add: changesPanel, featureIdeas, turnRollback, deleteChat, projectStatus, recentProjects, collapseProjects, statusOrder, allowForChat, continueButton, planToggle, queueReorder, copyDeeplink, copyProjectPath.
Invariants: the phone never moves a Host switch (`{on}`), never calls the updater, the screenshots or the Mobile connect route; pinning, token and the RPC allowlist stay. An older plugin without a route answers 404 and the phone hides that feature.
Design:
- Gateway: `PLUS` allowlist (`/plus/<name>` → `/api/composer-plus.<name>`, method per route, query/body as is, a body with `on` → 403); `/me` lists `plus`. Allow for this chat: the gateway answers an Auto Review ask (`approval/request`, reason starts with `Auto review denied tool `) with `allowed-once` when `grants.json` has that tool for that chat, so it also works with no window open.

## Queue drag-and-drop: "does not drag" (2026-10-21)
Report (owner, desktop dsh 0.2.1-rc.2, live copy v1.10.0): a queued message did not move.
Evidence: the owner's own chat log (this session, `agent/inbox/spliced`) had, at 11:54–13:38, a queue with a message that carries an image (`[image, text]`) next to text rows, and no `edit` splice at all. The client moved rows only by rewriting texts through dsh's `updateQueue {kind: edit}`, which the Host rejects for non-text content (`queue edits accept text content only`), so the image row had a disabled grip and no text row could pass it. A real-app check (`test/browser-queue-drag.mjs`, isolated dsh, streaming fake model) reproduced it: with an image row in the range, up and down drags left the order unchanged; with text rows only, both drags worked on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (also with the owner's other plugins dsh-rewind-plugin and dsh-backup and Auto Review loaded).
Design: Host route `POST /api/composer-plus.queue-move {sessionId, itemId, to, before}`: checks that `before` (the ids the user saw) is still the queue, that every message in the range is the user's own (`source.kind === 'user'`; goal rounds and question replies have listeners on a discard), then one `inbox.splice('next-turn', lo, n, reordered)`. dsh logs it as canceled + inserted with the same ids; its chat view and turn bookkeeping do not count it as a claim, and pending submissions are not in the queue (they are not moved). Client: the route first; a 404 (older Host) falls back to the text rotation. Tether: the same route through the gateway (`PLUS` + `queue-move`).
Checks: `test/queue-drag.test.mjs` (route first, 404 fallback remembered, image row movable, goal row not, refusal edits nothing; red on the previous client.js), `test/queue-move.test.mjs` (one splice, image stays with its message, partial `before`, changed queue 409 and no splice, foreign rows 409, bad input 400, one-shot subagent 409, one move per chat), `test/browser-queue-drag.mjs` IMAGE=1 and without, PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.

## Project search in a new chat (2026-10-21)
Request (owner, with a picture of the new chat project menu): add a search box to that menu and show the projects in alphabetical order; with many projects in dsh's saved order he cannot find the one he wants.
Evidence: the menu is dsh's `WorkspacePickFlow` (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-workspace`), drawn with the `Menu` primitive and portaled to <body>; items come from `useWorkspaces().items` in dsh's saved order; the `conversation.hero.workspace` slot is `single` and dsh fills it, so a plugin cannot replace it. Same markup on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
Design: feature `projectPicker` (on by default, `shell.overlay` component). While the hero project button (`_heroWorkspaceRow` > `_workspace`, `aria-expanded="true"`) is open, the last `[role="menu"]` in <body> gets a host above its row list with dsh's `Input`. Rows stay dsh's React rows: CSS `order` puts them A–Z (`Intl.Collator` of the UI language, numeric), `display: none` hides non-matches (the settings search `matchesQuery`: words, case, accents, ı). A window capture key handler runs before dsh's menu keys: Enter in the box clicks the first shown row (never the footer), ↑/↓/Home/End follow the order on screen and include dsh's footer, Tab goes to the first shown row, a printable key on a row goes back to the box, Esc clears the box first. Switching it off while the menu is open removes the host and every style it set. Tether: the New session sheet lists projects A–Z with the same match rule and Return picks the first match.
Checks: `test/project-picker.test.mjs` (fails on the previous client.js), `test/browser-project-picker.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (SHOT=1 makes `assets/features/projectPicker.webp`), toggles/shots/locale/parity tests, iOS unit tests.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-21): unit test and real-app check PASS on both dsh versions (A–Z, box focused and fixed on top, filter, Enter/click/keys, no-match notice, Esc, footer kept, switch off restores dsh's menu, Turkish texts, no page errors); `npm test` without the README check exit 0; iOS 30/30.

## Next message prediction (2026-10-21)
Request (owner, link to a post that shows Codex "composer predictions"): when the AI finishes a turn in a chat, the empty composer shows a guess of the user's next message in that chat (grey text, "Press Tab to accept"). The model must be chosen in Settings from dsh's own model list (dynamic); the default is dsh's default model. Not feature ideas: only the next message of the same chat.
Evidence: dsh's `session.modelCatalog()` (Host `buildModelCatalog`, same on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2) returns `{default, groups:[{id,name,models:[{id,name}]}]}` from the live LLM registry; the client model picker uses it through `remote.session`. `ctx.get('llm').prepareCall` + `call.stream` already serve Feature ideas. The composer shows dsh's placeholder only while `draft === ''`; it is a `[data-composer-placeholder]` div next to `[data-composer-input]`.
Invariants: it calls a model without a click, so it is OFF by default and the Host keeps the switch (`GET/POST {on}`); nothing is sent or written to the chat; Tab inserts the text only into an empty composer; a stale prediction (newer turn, user typed) never shows; one prediction call per chat at a time; the model choice is validated against the live catalog on the Host (unknown → the default).
Design:
- Host route `/api/composer-plus.predict`: `GET` → `{on, model}`; `POST {on}` / `POST {model: {provider, model} | null}` → saved in `storages/dsh-composer-plus/predict.json`; `POST {sessionId, afterSeq}` → when on, reads the chat log (last user messages and assistant replies, bounded), calls the chosen model (or dsh's default) with a short system prompt, returns `{text, afterSeq}`; 409 when off, no model, or the chat moved past `afterSeq`. `GET ?catalog=1` → dsh's catalog (`{default, groups}`) for the picker.
- Client feature `nextPrediction` (`conversation.input.right` entry): after a `turn/end` with reason `completed` in a root chat, with an empty plain composer, asks the Host once per `afterSeq`; shows the text over dsh's placeholder (same position, caption colour) with a "Press Tab to accept" hint; Tab (no modifiers, no open menu) inserts it with `inputActions.setDraft`; Esc or typing hides it. Settings row: a model select (dsh default + the catalog groups) under the switch.
- Tether: the gateway forwards `predict` (POST only with `sessionId`; the switch and the model choice stay on the Mac). The phone shows the prediction above the empty composer; a tap puts it in the composer.
Checks: unit tests (Host prompt + route, client component + settings), real isolated dsh with a fake model on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (catalog shows both fake models, default follows dsh, prediction appears after a turn, Tab accepts, a second model choice is used), SHOT=1 screenshot, toggles/shots/locale/parity/readme, iOS tests.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-21): Host test `test/predict.test.mjs` (switch, model choice validated against the live catalog, catalog = dsh's providers/models + default, transcript without tools/hidden context/reasoning, only after a completed turn, subagent refused, one shared call per chat that a closing tab cannot cancel, failures) and client test `test/predict-client.test.mjs` (one ask per finished turn, shown over dsh's placeholder, Tab/Esc/typing/new turn, stale answers dropped, Settings Model menu with dsh default first, one model change at a time) pass and fail on deliberate breaks. Real isolated dsh with two fake providers: PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (off by default = no call; menu lists Demo model, Mini, Nano with "dsh default (Demo model)" selected; prediction after a finished turn; Esc; Tab; one call per turn; chosen model Nano is called; Turkish hint). Gateway 21/21 (phone may POST a prediction; `{on}`, `{model}` and the catalog GET are refused). iOS 30/30; simulator against the demo gateway: the guess shows above the empty composer and a tap puts it in the field. Open: README translations (subagent), review findings.

## Queue drag: lifted card and a clear drop place (2026-10-22)
Request (owner): the drag felt forced and it was not clear where a message lands; the held row must float up in 3D.
Design: on pointer down a copy of the row goes to `<body>` (`position: fixed`, so the dock's scroll box does not clip it) and lifts with a scale, a small tilt and a deep shadow; it follows the pointer. The row itself becomes a dashed empty slot (`data-queue-drag-slot`, its content hidden). The other rows slide apart with transforms only (no layout change, so the hit test uses the places measured at the start). A place the row cannot go: the card fades, the cursor is `not-allowed`, the rows stay. Drop: the card settles into the slot and the new order stays on screen until dsh redraws the list (MutationObserver on the list, 1.5 s limit); text-rotation fallback clears the drag first; a refused move or Esc sends the card back. dsh redraws the list during the drag (a message starts to send) → the drag stops and nothing moves. One drag at a time (module `queueDrag`); switching the feature off mid-drag removes the card and the styles.
Checks: `test/queue-drag.test.mjs` (lift, follow, parting, settle, back on Esc/refusal, fade on a blocked place, redraw stops the drag, off mid-drag); real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (IMAGE=1): PASS, card and slot seen during the drag, nothing left after the drop; `npm test` exit 0. Tether keeps its Move up / Move down menu (no change needed). Tether signed build installed on the iPhone (owner approval in chat).

## Complete while typing (2026-10-22)
Request (owner): the composer already shows a next-message guess; it should also complete while the user types, quickly. Both use the same model (the owner picks a fast one). A separate Settings switch, because it uses many more tokens.
Evidence: dsh's composer is a Lexical contenteditable (`[data-composer-input]`, one `<p>` per line). dsh already draws grey text right after the last line: `.._input p:last-child:after { content: var(--dsh-composer-hint) }` (a slash command's argument hint; set only while a command claim is active, `phase !== 'plain'`). Present in 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2. IME composing hides it (`[data-composer-composing] p:last-child:after { content: none }`). Input state: `{draft, attachmentIds, phase, occurrences}`; `inputActions.setDraft(text)` puts the caret at the end.
Invariants: off by default (a model call at each pause while typing; the owner's token concern); the Host keeps the switch (`GET/POST {on}`, `complete.json`); the model is the one chosen under Next message prediction (dsh's default unless chosen), checked against dsh's live catalog; nothing is sent or written to the chat; Tab only adds text at the end of the draft; a stale answer (the draft changed) never shows; a newer request for the same chat cancels the older model call.
Design:
- Host `/api/composer-plus.complete`: `GET` → `{on}`; `POST {on}`; `POST {sessionId, text}` (text 1..4000 chars) → `{text, completion, model}`. Context: the chat's user and agent text (last 3000 chars; a new chat has none); subagent chat → 409; off → 409. One running call per chat: a newer request aborts the older one (409 for the older). 15 s timeout, lowest reasoning effort. `cleanCompletion(typed, raw)`: one line, no repeat of the typed text or of the last half word, at most 150 chars.
- Client feature `typingCompletion` (`conversation.input.right`, id `typing-completion`): a plain draft of 3+ characters, not a slash command, no chips, the caret at the end of the composer; asks 350 ms after the last change (an older ask is aborted); shows the rest through `--dsh-composer-hint` on the input (removed on cleanup only when it is still ours); typing the next characters of the guess uses the same guess (no call); Tab (no modifiers, not composing, no open `/`/`@` menu) appends it; Esc hides it for that text. Screen readers: a polite status line. Settings: Host switch + the same Model menu as Next message prediction.
- Tether: gateway `PLUS` gets `complete` (POST; a `{on}` body is refused). The phone asks after a pause while typing in the composer and shows the rest in the suggestion row above the field; a tap appends it. The switch and the model stay on the Mac.
Checks: `test/complete.test.mjs` (Host), `test/complete-client.test.mjs` (client), toggles/shots/locale/readme/parity tests, real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-complete.mjs`, SHOT=1), gateway test, iOS unit tests + simulator.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/typingCompletion.webp, README*.md, llms.txt, test/*, package.json (test script), tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/Sources/*, tether/ios/Tests/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-22): Host `test/complete.test.mjs` (off by default and with a broken file, switch saved, bad bodies 400, dsh default model with lowest effort and a 2000 cap, chat text + typed text only, a new chat completes, the prediction's model is used and falls back to dsh's default, a newer request stops the older call (red when the abort is removed), an aborted request stops its call, subagent 409, failure 502, no model 409) and client `test/complete-client.test.mjs` (no entry until the Host says on; one ask after the pause; the rest through `--dsh-composer-hint`; typing along the guess, no new ask; Tab adds, Esc hides; Shift+Tab, IME, an open menu and other elements keep their keys; stale answer and 409 show nothing; slash command, chip, claim, caret not at the end and subagent chat ask nothing; an older ask is aborted (red when removed); dsh's own hint is left alone (red when removed); Settings row with the Host switch status and the shared Model store) pass. Real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-complete.mjs`): PASS (off by default = no call; switch on in Settings with the Model menu under both switches; grey rest after the cursor after a pause, drawn by dsh's `p:last-child::after`; one call per pause; no call along the guess; Tab adds; Esc hides; slash command skipped; Nano chosen in the prediction's menu is called; Enter sends only the typed text; no page errors). SHOT=1 wrote `assets/features/typingCompletion.webp` (6 kB). Gateway 21/21 (`complete` POST forwarded, `{on}` and GET refused). iOS 30/30 (`completable`, `completionRest`). Simulator against the demo gateway: typing "Add a logout" shows "…button to the header · Tap to add" above the field; a tap adds it and the row goes. Tether parity 26 features, 22 in Tether. README translations done by a subagent (12 files, `readme checks passed (13 files)`); `npm test` exit 0.

## Feature ideas focus topic, Later checklist copy, chat export as Markdown (2026-10-22)
Objective (owner request, one plan): (1) an optional focus field next to "Suggest features" with fixed preset chips (Performance, Accessibility, Tests, Security, Usability, Docs); the model suggests only features about that topic. (2) A button in the Later list copies the saved ideas as a Markdown checklist (`- [ ] Title — Summary`), with no network call. (3) "Export as Markdown…" in a chat's menu: the user's messages and the replies, code blocks kept, copied or saved as a `.md` file.
Invariants: an empty focus sends the same request as before (byte for byte); an old Host ignores `focus`, an old client never sends it; the export route is read-only and reads no project file; copy runs inside the user's click; one export at a time, a late answer for an older chat is dropped.
Design:
- Shared: `copyText(text)` wraps `primitives.writeClipboard` (copy checklist, copy export).
- (2) client only: `laterChecklist(ideas)`; head button `data-composer-plus-ideas-copy-later` in the Later view; "Copied" / "Could not copy" for 2 s.
- (1) Host `ideasFocus(focus)` (clip 80, one line, JSON-quoted) appended to the user message of `suggestIdeas(…, locale, focus)`; route passes `body.focus`. Client: `deck.focus` (memory per chat), `FOCUS_PRESETS`, a text field (Enter suggests; not while composing or busy) and a chip row (`aria-pressed`; a second click clears).
- (3) Host `chatTranscript(events)` (human `user/message` + `assistant/message` text blocks, rollback spans out, 4 MB cap → `truncated`) on `GET /api/composer-plus.export?sessionId=`. Client feature `exportMarkdown` (own switch, `SHOT_OF`, `SHOTS`, webp): menu item (order 470) + `shell.overlay` dialog with Copy and Save .md; `closeFences`, `chatMarkdown`, `mdFileName`.
- Tether: focus field + chips in the Ideas sheet; "Copy as checklist" in the Later list; the existing "Copy/Share conversation" uses the same Markdown shape; FEATURES.md row `exportMarkdown`. `PLUS` stays as it is: the phone makes the Markdown from its own transcript and never calls `/api/composer-plus.export` (AGENTS.md rule 8 adds a route only when the phone calls it).
Checks: `test/feature-ideas.test.mjs`, `test/feature-ideas-client.test.mjs`, new `test/export-chat.test.mjs`, toggles/shots/locale/readme/parity, real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-feature-ideas.mjs` steps 9-10, new `test/browser-export-markdown.mjs`, SHOT=1), iOS unit tests.
Allowed paths: client.js, index.js, locales/*.json, package.json (test script), test/*, assets/features/{featureIdeas,exportMarkdown}.webp, README*.md, llms.txt, tether/FEATURES.md, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/Sources/*, tether/ios/Tests/*, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the desktop save dialog for `<a download>` is an inference (the main window session has no `will-download` block; only login and sidebar-browser sessions do); fallback: the Host writes to ~/Downloads with O_EXCL. A narrow focus can more often give "only earlier ideas".
Last checkpoint (2026-10-22): all three done in client.js, index.js and Tether. ## Complete while typing: its own model (2026-10-22)
Objective (owner request, choice "her biri kendi modelini seçsin"): Next message prediction and Complete while typing each choose their own model in Settings. Complete while typing defaults to "Same as Next message prediction", so a saved setup does not change.
Invariants: `predict.json` keeps the prediction's model only; `complete.json` gets `model?` beside `on`; no model saved there = the prediction's model (then dsh's default), as before. A switch write keeps the saved model and a model write keeps the switch (one write at a time, read-merge-write). The phone can never set a model (`isSwitch` refuses `{model}` in the gateway).
Design: Host `completeStore` reads/writes `{on, model?}` through a `change(mutate)` like `predictStore`; `/api/composer-plus.complete` gets `GET ?catalog=1` and `POST {model}` (null = same as prediction; a model must be in dsh's list); the call uses `routeOf(complete.model ?? predict.model)`. Client: `createPredictionModel(route)` serves both routes; `PredictionModel` takes a `fallback` label ("Same as Next message prediction (…)") for the completion menu.
Checks: `test/complete.test.mjs` (own model used, null back to the prediction's, a switch write keeps the model, unknown model 409), `test/complete-client.test.mjs` (a second store on the complete route, its own menu), `test/predict-client.test.mjs`, real dsh `test/browser-complete.mjs` (two menus, a change in one leaves the other) on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, test/complete*.mjs, test/predict-client.test.mjs, test/browser-complete.mjs, README*.md, llms.txt, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-22): Host `completeStore` keeps `{on, model?}` (read-merge-write), shared `saveModel` for both routes, complete `GET ?catalog=1` adds `fallback` (the prediction's model); the call uses own model → prediction's → dsh default (each only while dsh lists it). Client: one `createPredictionModel(route, onSaved)` for both; the completion menu (`followPrediction`) shows "Same as Next message prediction (…)" first and reloads after a prediction change; its own aria-label. Checks: `test/complete.test.mjs` (red on HEAD: own model saved beside the switch, used, prediction unchanged, switch write keeps the model, unlisted model falls back to the prediction's, null back, old `{on}` file reads), `test/complete-client.test.mjs` (red on HEAD: own store and route, first entry names the prediction's model, choice and null go to the complete route, a prediction save reloads it), `test/predict-client.test.mjs`, `npm test` exit 0; real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2: `test/browser-complete.mjs` PASS (two menus, Same as … follows Nano, own Demo choice changes only completions, files on disk), `test/browser-predict.mjs` PASS. Texts: 3 new keys + description in en, zh, 11 locales; README (5 sentences) in 13 files; llms.txt; tether/FEATURES.md.

## Open review findings (2026-10-22, review-guard of the uncommitted change on 6be2738)
Status: RESOLVED locally (2026-10-22), not committed or released. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-22. Close each item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-22): Export as Markdown keeps messages that dsh-rewind-plugin withdrew. Signature: `chatTranscript` (index.js) hides only `rollbackSpans(events)`, which matches only `source.kind === 'composer-plus-rollback'`. When dsh-rewind-plugin is installed, Composer Plus shows no ↶ and the plugin writes its own marker (`source.kind: 'dsh-rewind'`, `surfaceOp: replace`, `sourceEventSeqs` = the target message and all later surface seqs; checked in 0.17.0 (live desktop profile) and 0.18.0 (npm latest)). Evidence: a node check with a `dsh-rewind` marker over seqs 3–4 gave all four messages, "withdrawn q" and "withdrawn a" included. Impact: the exported file has text that the user took back. This conflicts with README "messages that Turn rollback took out are left out" and llms.txt "rolled-back messages are left out". The phone export (Tether `withoutRolledBack`, `rollbackSource`) has the same gap. Resolved when: a `dsh-rewind` marker test in `test/export-chat.test.mjs` and an iOS `withoutRolledBack` test fail on the old code and pass, and `npm test` and the iOS unit tests pass.
- F-02 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-22): the completion route repeats the "listed in dsh's catalog" check. Signature: `const listed = (m) => …` in `installComplete` (index.js) copies the check in `routeOf`. Resolved when: `routeOf` takes the candidate models in order, the local copy is gone, and `test/complete.test.mjs` and `test/predict.test.mjs` pass without changes.
- F-01 fix: `index.js` exports `REWIND_SOURCE = 'dsh-rewind'`; `rollbackSpans(events, kinds = [ROLLBACK_SOURCE])` (the ↶ view and the rollback GET route keep their old answer); `chatTranscript` passes both kinds. Tether `rollbackSpan` accepts `withdrawKinds` (both kinds), so the phone view and Copy/Share conversation leave them out too. `/compact` (`compact-checkpoint`) is never a withdraw. Evidence: `node test/export-chat.test.mjs` red before the fix ("a dsh-rewind span is out; a /compact span stays"), green after; `node test/turn-rollback.test.mjs` red before (no `REWIND_SOURCE` export), green after; iOS unit tests red before (2 failures at Tests.swift:573–574), 30/30 after; real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 `test/browser-export-markdown.mjs` PASS with the new step 5 (↶ Chat only on message 2 → export has 2 messages, the withdrawn turn is out). Docs: README.md + 12 translations (limits bullet), llms.txt, tether/FEATURES.md, AGENTS.md rule 9.
- F-02 fix: `routeOf(ctx, get, ...saved)` returns the first saved model that dsh lists, else dsh's default; `installComplete` calls `routeOf(ctx, get, settings.model, predict.model)` and has no own copy. Evidence: `node test/complete.test.mjs` and `node test/predict.test.mjs` pass with no change in this step.
- Both: `npm test` exit 0 (2026-10-22). Not run for F-02: `test/browser-complete.mjs` (a refactor; the unit tests cover the model order).

## Issue #3: ▶ Continue no longer takes the queued message (2026-10-22)
Objective: after dsh's Stop, ▶ (One-click Continue) and Resume after restart continue only the stopped task; messages that wait in the queue stay there and run after it. Out of scope (owner decision): a backlog / Ctrl+Send queue while idle (a new chat or Cmd/Ctrl+Enter steering covers it).
Cause (dsh source, same in dsh-v0.2.0-rc.2 and dsh-v0.2.1-rc.2): Stop keeps the inbox (`keepInbox: true`), but a turn opens with `inbox.claim('next-turn')`, which takes every next-step message plus the queue head in one batch. The Continue marker went in as a steer (next-step), so the queue head went with it.
Fix: `wakeWithContinue(agent)` in index.js puts a second marker at the queue head (`inbox.prepend`) before the steer, so the claim takes two markers; the `agent/pre-step` hook keeps one continue note. A failed steer removes the head marker. A dsh without `inbox.prepend` keeps the old steer only. No new switch, text or screenshot (a bug fix in `continueButton`).
Last checkpoint (2026-10-22): `test/continue-host.test.mjs` and `test/resume-restart.test.mjs` red on 8befdda (queued message claimed), green now. New `test/browser-continue-queue.mjs` (fake model, one queued message, Stop, ▶): red on 8befdda with dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 (the continued request held the queued text); PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 now (continued request = note only, queue row stays with no empty row, then the queued message runs in its own request). README + 12 translations + llms.txt: one sentence. Not done: version bump, commit, push, release, issue reply (owner's call).

## Open review findings (2026-10-23, review-guard of the uncommitted Tether change on cf1b8c1)
Status: RESOLVED (2026-10-23), commit 0ccd2a3 on main, CI run 39926017650 green. Tether is not in the npm package, so no release. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-23. Close the item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-23): after a press and hold on the Mac name, the first tap does not open the Mac menu. Signature: `MacPicker` (tether/ios/Sources/UI.swift) sets `held = true` in the `LongPressGesture` and clears it only in the `Button` action; the release after a press and hold does not always fire that action, so `held` stays true and the next real tap is eaten. Evidence (iOS Simulator iPhone 17 Pro, agent-device, test gateway on this Mac only): press and hold → the rename box opens (2 nodes) → Cancel → first tap: 0 menu items; second tap: 1 menu item ("Add a Mac…"). Impact: one extra tap to switch Macs or add a Mac after each rename; no data loss. Resolved when: the simulator sequence (press and hold → Cancel → one tap) gives 0 menu items on the old code and 1 on the fix, the same is true after Save, a single tap still opens the menu and a press and hold still opens only the rename box, and the iOS unit tests pass. (No UI test target exists and xcodegen is not installed, so the regression check is this agent-device sequence.)
- F-01 fix: `MacPicker` has no `held` flag. The `Button` action is `guard c.renamingMac == nil else { return }; menu = true`: the rename box that the press and hold opens is the state, and its Save and Cancel both clear it. Evidence (iOS Simulator iPhone 17 Pro, agent-device, test gateway on this Mac only): press and hold → Cancel → one tap: 1 menu item (old code: 0); press and hold → "Ev" → Save → one tap: 1 menu item, title "✓ Ev"; the Cancel sequence twice more: 1 each time; two plain taps: menu each time, no rename box; press and hold: rename box (2 nodes), 0 menu items; pairing → "Name this Mac" → Cancel → one tap: 1 menu item, no "Rename" entry. iOS unit tests 30/30, `npm test` exit 0, `git diff --check` clean. Not checked: the physical iPhone (Developer Mode is off on this Mac, so agent-device cannot read its screen).

## Docs from one source, label and route checks, What's new, Settings help links (2026-10-23)
Objective (owner request, one plan): (1) a test compares the bold button and menu names in each `README.<id>.md` with the label texts in `locales/<id>.json`; (2) `docs/ROUTES.md` lists every Host route (method, body, feature, Tether PLUS) and a test fails on an undocumented route; (3) `npm run build:readme` makes the feature table of the 13 READMEs and the feature list of `llms.txt` from one source (`docs/features.json` + language packs), and `--check` runs in `npm test`; (4) a test checks that each `FEATURES` id has a row in README.md, an entry in llms.txt and a row in each translation; (5) after an automatic update, the first start shows the release notes of that version once (from the update check's own GitHub answer), closable; (6) a small `?` link on each Settings → Composer Plus feature row opens the feature's README row in the README of dsh's UI language.
Invariants: README pictures, code blocks and version notes stay the same in all 13 files (`test/readme.test.mjs`); no new network call (What's new reuses `checkUpdate`'s release answer; `?current=1` answers from the package); no new feature switch (What's new is part of `autoUpdate`); `PLUS` does not change; `docs/` is not in the npm package.
Design: shared `docs/features.json` (`header`, `features[]` with `id`, `name?`, `shot`, `llms`, `how` per language, `routes[]`); one generator `scripts/build-docs.mjs` (tables between `<!-- BEGIN/END GENERATED FEATURES -->` markers, llms list between the same markers, `docs/ROUTES.md` whole file; `--check` fails with the stale file names). Row anchor `<a id="feature-<id lower-case>"></a>` (GitHub keeps it as `user-content-feature-…`, lower-case). Row name = pack `feature.<id>` (language row = `language.title`); image alt = pack `shot.alt`.

Allowed paths: docs/features.json, docs/ROUTES.md, scripts/build-docs.mjs, client.js, index.js, locales/*.json, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json, test/*, AGENTS.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: github.com scrolls `#feature-x` to the `user-content-` anchor (inference; check after push); the desktop app opens `target=_blank` in the system browser (inference, same path as "Release notes"); two windows can both show What's new once.
Out of scope: version bump, commit, release, npm publish (owner's call); Tether (no new FEATURES id, no new phone route); label check for zh and English.

## Improve prompt (magic wand) (2026-10-23)
Request (owner): a magic wand in the chat composer that improves the typed prompt with the chat's context and the project's context. Research first (Roo Code Enhance Prompt, Augment Prompt Enhancer, Bolt, claude-code-prompt-improver, Anthropic prompt improver / prompting best practices), then plan. dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 have no such feature (package search).
Owner decisions: its own Model menu in Settings (default: the chat's model); the improved text replaces the draft in place, with undo (no preview dialog).
Objective: feature `promptImprove` (on by default: a model call only on a click). A wand button in `conversation.input.right` sends the draft to `POST /api/composer-plus.improve {sessionId, text}`; the Host reads the chat (rollback/rewind spans out), `projectBrief(cwd)`, `git status` paths and `git ls-files` paths that share words with the draft, calls one model and answers `{text, improved, model}`; the client writes it with `inputActions.insertText(improved, whole-draft span)` (one undo step, refused when the draft changed); the button then offers "Undo improvement".
Invariants: nothing is sent or written to the chat or the project; project files are read only through `projectBrief` (realpath inside the folder, regular files, O_NOFOLLOW|O_NONBLOCK); the context is marked as data, not instructions; the draft's language, intent and scope stay; no invented paths or names; one call per chat; a changed draft is never overwritten; the phone can never set the model (`isSwitch`).
Design: `settingsStore` (shared by predict/complete/improve: ordered atomic 0600 writes); `improve.json` `{model?}`; model order: saved (when dsh lists it) → the chat's newest `request/header` model → dsh's default; route `GET` (`?catalog=1` adds dsh's catalog and the chat-model fallback label), `POST {model}` (`saveModel`), `POST {sessionId, text}`. Client: `PromptImprove` component, disabled for an empty draft, a slash command, a file chip or > 8000 characters; Esc or a second click cancels; Settings Model menu (`PredictionModel` variant `improve`). Tether: `PLUS` + `improve` (POST), a wand next to the phone composer.
Phases / checks:
0. Real dsh probe (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2): whole-draft `insertText` + Cmd+Z, header `cwd` of a blank chat, button fits.
1. Host + `test/prompt-improve.test.mjs` (store, 400/409/502, model order, context filters incl. both withdraw kinds and /compact kept, symlink and FIFO in the brief, clean output, one call per chat, abort).
2. Client + `test/prompt-improve-client.test.mjs`, toggles (28 rows).
3. Texts en, zh, 11 locales; docs/features.json, README limits/FAQ in 13 languages, llms.txt, ROUTES.md.
4. `test/browser-prompt-improve.mjs` on both dsh versions + SHOT=1 `promptImprove.webp`.
5. Tether gateway test, iOS unit tests + simulator.
6. `npm test`, Semgrep, `npm pack --dry-run`, review-guard. No commit, release or live copy without a separate request.

## Tether: the green dot follows the Mac's read state (2026-10-25)
Request (owner): in Tether, some finished chats stay green ("not read") although they are read in dsh; they must update by themselves, and pull to refresh changes nothing.
- F-01 (P2, FIXED LOCALLY 2026-10-25, not committed): Tether's green dot never learns that a chat was read on the Mac. Signature: `Client.finished` (tether/ios/Sources/Core.swift) loses an id only on `open(sid)` on the phone, when the chat leaves the list, or when it runs again; `loadSessions()` only intersects. dsh's own dot is `UiSession.completionUnread` (`@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-session`), a Set in the browser client's memory, cleared when the chat is the main view; neither `session/list` nor `$events` carries it (desktop app bundle and rc.2 read). Impact: dots stay until the chat is opened on the phone; pull to refresh cannot clear them.
- F-02 (P3, FIXED LOCALLY 2026-10-25, not committed; the old rule stays only for an older Host): `Client.justFinished` adds a dot when `updatedAt` grew and the title stayed. dsh computes `updatedAt = max(createdAt, lastPromptAt)` (`dsh-api-session-controller` list.js), so a message the owner typed on the Mac also gives a green dot on the phone.
Decisions: the Host keeps the unread list (Mobile connect on only): a root chat's `agent/status` running → idle adds it, running removes it; `POST /api/composer-plus.unread {sessionIds}` marks read. The desktop client reports reads from dsh's own `sessionStatus` (dot cleared; finished while open; on load: Host ids that dsh shows without a dot). The Host pushes each change to the phones as `{type:'emit', event:'composer-plus/unread', args:[ids]}` (gateway `emit`). Tether shows Host ids ∩ idle root chats − the open chat; an older Host (no route) keeps the old local rule.
Invariants: nothing is collected while Mobile connect is off (switching it off clears the list); subagent chats never get a dot; the phone never sends a switch body; a reply after a Mac switch never reaches the new Mac (`plusCall` CancellationError).
Phases / checks:
0. This record.
1. Host `unreadStore`, status tracking, route + `test/unread.test.mjs` (red on HEAD).
2. Gateway `PLUS unread`, `emit` + `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs`.
3. Client `watchRead` in `feature('mobileConnect')` + `test/unread-client.test.mjs`.
4. Tether `shownUnread`, `loadSessions`/`open`/`onEvent` + iOS unit test.
5. docs/features.json route, `npm run build:readme`, tether/README.md, tether/FEATURES.md, 13 README limit sentences.
6. `npm test`, real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2, `xcodebuild test`, `git diff --check`. No commit, release or live copy without a separate request.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/Sources/Core.swift, tether/ios/Tests/Tests.swift, test/unread*.mjs, test/tether-gateway.test.mjs, test/browser-recent-finished.mjs, package.json (test script), docs/features.json, docs/ROUTES.md, README*.md, tether/README.md, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Note: the working tree also holds the uncommitted Improve prompt work (section above); this change does not touch it.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-25): phases 0-6 done, not committed.
- Red on HEAD, green now: `test/unread.test.mjs` (HEAD has no route), `test/unread-client.test.mjs` (HEAD: "on: one read watcher"), `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` (HEAD: 2 tests fail, routes + events).
- `npm test` exit 0 (31 Host route methods documented); iOS `xcodebuild test` on iPhone 17 Pro simulator: 30 tests, 0 failures.
- Real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-recent-finished.mjs`, reinstalled under ~/Documents/testdeepseekharness/work): PASS on both (background finish unread, opened here read, reload read, finished while open never unread, no page errors).
- Not checked: Tether on a real iPhone against the live dsh (needs the live copy + a dsh restart by the owner).

## Open review findings (2026-10-26, review-guard of the uncommitted Improve prompt + Tether unread change on a3ea597)
Status: RESOLVED locally (2026-10-26), not committed or released. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-26. Close each item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-26): the gateway cancels every Composer Plus route when the phone's request closes, not only Improve prompt. Signature: `plus()` in tether/gateway/server.mjs passes `gone.signal` (aborted on `res` close) to `upstream.rpc` for every `PLUS` name. Tether's first address has a 5 s time-out when the Mac has LAN and Tailscale addresses (`requestTimeout`), so a slower write route is closed on the Mac. For `rollback {files: true}` the Host first stops the turn and removes the queued steering (index.js `installRollback`), then `store.restore(…, request.signal)` cannot start git ("aborted before spawn", dsh-subprocess-local `validateSubprocessSpec`): no marker, no restore; the second address gets 409 "a rollback of this chat is already running". Evidence: a throwaway probe (mock dsh + real gateway, phone aborts a hanging `/plus/rollback`): working tree "CLOSED (dsh request aborted)", HEAD server.mjs "still open (Mac keeps working)"; dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 both refuse a spawn with an aborted signal. Resolved when: a gateway test that aborts a hanging `/plus/rollback` sees dsh's request stay open (red on the current code), the hanging `/plus/improve` case still closes, and `npm test` passes.
- F-02 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-26): Improve prompt accepts a model answer that stopped at the token cap. Signature: `improvePrompt` (index.js) throws only for finish `error`/`aborted`; `max-tokens` (dsh-llm `FinishReasonMap`) is returned as the improved prompt and replaces the draft. Evidence: a throwaway probe with a fake LLM (`text-delta` "Goal: fix the login form.\n1. Open src/log", finish `max-tokens`) → `improvePrompt` answered it as `improved`. Resolved when: a `test/prompt-improve.test.mjs` case with finish `max-tokens` gets 502 (red on the current code), the other cases stay green, and `npm test` passes.
- F-01 fix: `plus()` (tether/gateway/server.mjs) makes its AbortController and the `res` close listener only for `improve`; every other route gets no signal and runs to its end on the Mac. Rule: tether/AGENTS.md "Gateway". Evidence: `node test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` red before the fix (AssertionError "a write route is not stopped with the phone's request": a hanging `/plus/rollback` closed with the phone), green after (22/22: the rollback request stays open, its late answer does not break the gateway, the hanging `/plus/improve` request still closes).
- F-02 fix: `improvePrompt` (index.js) throws 502 "the answer was cut off at the token limit; try a shorter message or another model" for finish `max-tokens`. Evidence: `node test/prompt-improve.test.mjs` red before the fix (status 200, expected 502), green after ("prompt improve checks passed"; the stop, error, empty and abort cases unchanged).
- Both: `npm test` exit 0 (2026-10-26); `git diff --check` clean. Not run: the real dsh browser checks and the iOS tests (no client.js or Swift change).

## Changes panel: git branch tree (2026-10-26)

Request (owner, chat): show a real tree of the branches in the Changes panel; a click goes to the tree. Owner's choices: the branch chip at the top opens the tree (also **Tree** in the mode menu); a branch name in the tree scrolls to that branch's newest commit; a commit opens its files and diffs; Tether gets the same. Not chosen: checkout from the panel.

Invariants: the panel stays read-only (no checkout, commit, fetch); git runs through `runGit` (argv, scrubbed env, timeout, bounded output); only the chat's own cwd; a commit id from the browser must match `^[0-9a-f]{40}([0-9a-f]{24})?$` before it reaches git; no new dependency; no separate switch (Tree is a mode of `changesPanel`, so its switch removes it).

Design: Host `GET /api/composer-plus.graph?sessionId` = `git log --branches --remotes [HEAD] --topo-order --decorate=full`, newest 300 commits (`more` when cut); `changes`/`diff` get `mode=commit&commit=<sha>` = `git show --format= --root -m --first-parent <sha>`. Client `graphRows(commits)` assigns lanes (pure, tested); rows draw one inline SVG each. Tether: `PLUS` + `graph`, Swift `graphRows`, TreeView/CommitView.

Phases: 1 Host + test; 2 client + test; 3 texts (en, zh, 11 locales); 4 docs (features.json how/llms/routes, 13 README limit sentence); 5 Tether + tests; 6 real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-changes-tree.mjs`), changesPanel shot; 7 `npm test`, iOS tests, simulator, `npm pack --dry-run`, review.

Out of scope: commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy (separate approval).

Checkpoint (2026-10-26, phases 1–6 done, uncommitted):
- Host: `parseRefs`, `commitGraph`, `GRAPH_PATH`, commit mode (`SHA_RE` before git; a `commit` with another mode = 400). `test/changes.test.mjs`: order (children first, side branch before its fork point), parents, refs (current, tag, remote, no `origin/HEAD`, no stash), `more`, empty repo, not a repo, detached HEAD, commit files for root/merge/rename, untracked never in a commit, `--output=…`/`HEAD`/uppercase/short id → 400, unknown id → 500.
- Client: `graphRows` (lanes; a side line keeps its color until it joins), `CommitTree` (SVG per row, HEAD ring), branch chips (current, local, HEAD, remote), commit view (`FileList`, diff with the commit id), back marks the commit, the last tree of the same chat shows while the new one loads, polling skips a tick while a load runs (tree 15 s, commit never). `test/changes-tree.test.mjs` red when the lane color or the scroll is broken on purpose.
- Probe (/tmp, not shipped): octopus merge, duplicate parent and a parent already on a left lane give sane lanes. `git log` for the tree on a 53,345-commit repository: 0.06 s with commit-graph, 0.32 s without.
- Tether: `graph` in `PLUS`; Swift `graphRows`/`treeBranches`/`GraphCommit` (same cases in Tests.swift); TreeView (Branches menu scrolls and marks), CommitView, DiffView `commit`. iOS tests 34/34. ## Open review findings (2026-10-26, review-guard of the uncommitted branch tree change on f85b4aa)
Status: RESOLVED (2026-10-26, /implement-plan), not committed. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-26.
- F-01 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-26): the branch chips above the tree hide the keyboard focus ring. Signature: the chip `button` in `ChangesTab` (client.js) sets the inline style `outline: ref.sha === focusSha ? '2px solid rgba(59,130,246,.6)' : 'none'`. An inline `outline: none` wins over the browser's `:focus-visible` ring, and dsh 0.2.1-rc.2's stylesheet has no global button focus rule (all its `:focus-visible` rules are class-scoped), so nothing else draws one. Evidence: headless Chrome probe (`/tmp`, removed), Tab to a button with inline `outline:none` → `:focus-visible` true, computed `outline-style` `none`; the same button without it → `auto`. Impact: a keyboard user who tabs through the chips cannot see which chip has the focus (only the marked chip shows a ring). Resolved when: `test/changes-tree.test.mjs` asserts that the chip style sets no `outline` and that the marked chip has its mark (red on the current code), `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs` asserts that Tab from one chip to the next gives a computed `outline-style` other than `none` (red on the current code), and `npm test` passes and the browser test passes on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
- F-02 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-26): dead check in `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs`. Signature: `const root = git(project, 'rev-list', '--max-parents=0', 'HEAD').trim();` is used only by `assert.ok(root.length === 40);`, which tests no behavior; the comment above says "the oldest commit", but the step clicks origin/main. Resolved when: the two lines are gone, the comment names the real step, and the browser test passes on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
- F-02 fix (done first): the `root` line and its `assert.ok` are gone; the comment is now "Low page: origin/main → its commit → ← Tree brings it back into view."
- F-01 fix: the chip style has no `outline` key; the marked chip gets `boxShadow: '0 0 0 2px rgba(59,130,246,.6)'`, the others `undefined`, so the browser draws its own `:focus-visible` ring. Evidence: `node test/changes-tree.test.mjs` red before the fix (AssertionError "main: no inline outline"), green after ("changes tree checks passed"); the new step 2 of `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs` (focus `main`, Tab → `dark-mode`, `:focus-visible` true, computed `outline-style` not `none`) red before the fix on 0.2.1-rc.2 ("the focused branch chip has a focus ring", actual `none`), PASS after on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2.
- Both: `npm test` exit 0, `git diff --check` clean (2026-10-26). Not done: a new `SHOT=1` screenshot (optional; the mark changes from an outline to a box shadow of the same size and color).

## Security set: grant time limit, Tailscale only, access log, Tether app lock, paired iPhones (2026-10-26)
Request (owner, one plan): (1) Auto Review "Allow for this chat" grants end after a chosen time (for example 1 hour); then the agent asks again. (2) Mobile connect gets an option that refuses LAN connections and accepts only Tailscale addresses. (3) The Mac gateway logs each connection attempt (time, IP, device, allowed or refused); the newest entries show in a list. (4) Tether asks for Face ID or the device passcode when it opens or comes back from the background; a switch in Tether's Settings. (5) Paired iPhones are listed with their name and last connection; one click revokes a device's key, and it cannot connect until it pairs again.
Owner decisions (questions in chat): (2), (3), (5) live in the Mobile connect panel (`tether/gateway/admin.html`, English only, like today); the grant time is one setting under Settings → Composer Plus → Allow for this chat: 15 min / 1 h (default) / 4 h / 8 h / until revoked; the phone uses the Mac's setting.
Decision: no new `FEATURES` id and no new screenshot: (1) is part of `allowForChat`, (2)(3)(5) of `mobileConnect`, (4) is phone-only (like "In one list").
Invariants: the phone never changes a Mac setting (`isSwitch` also refuses `ttlMs`); pinned TLS, one-time code, Mac approval, hashed tokens and the RPC allowlist stay; no new dependency (iOS adds the system `LocalAuthentication`); new files 0600, atomic rename, a broken file reads as empty/default; the live dsh (ports 19390/19391 open) is not touched.
Shared parts: `accessLog` in server.mjs (rejects of (2), the log of (3), "last connection" of (5) come from the same hooks); `track()`'s new-socket moment writes the allowed entry and `lastSeenAt`; `state()` carries `tailnetOnly`, `log`, devices with `createdAt`/`lastSeenAt`; `settingsStore` in index.js holds the grants and the time setting.
Phases:
- A Gateway: `isSwitch` + `ttlMs`; `allowed(…, tailnetOnly)`; `dir/settings.json` + admin `POST /settings {tailnetOnly}` (drops open connections that the new rule refuses); `dir/access.json` (≤ 200, merged repeats within 60 s, 2 s debounced write, flushed on close); `lastSeenAt` (memory per new socket, file at most every 60 s). (2) Tether: `open()` read grants in a race with Allow/Revoke → `grantWrites` counter (a read answer after a newer write is dropped, a write applies only its own newest answer). (3) client: grant read and expiry timer ran with the feature off and a late Host answer could start a timer after unload → `startGrants()` inside `feature('allowForChat')`, `grantsOn` flag; chat-grant test (switch off clears the timer, a late answer starts none, on again loads) red without the flag. Also found by `npm test`: a Settings row child order that `test/resume-restart.test.mjs` reads (GrantTtl moved last), and the first grants read must go out in the same tick (`grantsBusy`: an empty queue sends at once; `test/complete-client.test.mjs`).

## Open review findings (2026-10-26, review-guard of the uncommitted security set on 87f1b1d)
Status: RESOLVED (2026-10-26, /implement-plan), not committed. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-26. Close the item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-26): with "Tailscale only" on, the gateway still announces itself on the local network over Bonjour. Signature: `start()` in tether/gateway/server.mjs runs `spawn('dns-sd', ['-R', mac, '_dshmobile._tcp', 'local', String(port), 'fp=' + fp])` whenever `opts.bonjour !== false`; it does not read `tailnetOnly`, and admin `POST /settings` changes only the TCP filter (`allowed(…, tailnetOnly)`) and never stops or starts `bonjour`. Impact: on a shared Wi-Fi (the use case of the option) each device on the network can read the Mac name (for example "Furkan MacBook Pro"), the port and the certificate fingerprint; the fingerprint stays the same for 825 days, so it identifies the Mac on each network. The TCP filter still refuses each LAN connection, so no access; this is information disclosure only. The phone needs no Bonjour on the tailnet (it connects with the `ts` address from pairing; multicast DNS does not cross Tailscale). Evidence (2026-10-26): a throwaway probe (/tmp, removed) with a `dns-sd` stub first in `PATH`: (a) `settings.json {"tailnetOnly": true}` before `start()` → the stub got `-R Test Mac _dshmobile._tcp local <port> fp=…`; (b) `POST /settings {tailnetOnly: true}` while running → the advertisement process stayed alive; it ended only at `close()`. The live dsh (PID 86867) runs `dns-sd -R <Mac name> _dshmobile._tcp local 19390 fp=…` now (`pgrep -lf dns-sd`). Not caught: every gateway test passes `bonjour: false`, so no test sees the spawn. Resolved when: a gateway test with an injected `spawn` sees no advertisement at start with `tailnetOnly` on, the advertisement process killed on `POST /settings {tailnetOnly: true}`, one new advertisement on `{tailnetOnly: false}` and after a network rebind, none after `stop()`; the test is red on the current code; `node test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` and `npm test` pass.
- F-01 fix: one `advertise()` in `createGateway` (tether/gateway/server.mjs) runs `dns-sd` only while `running && !tailnetOnly && opts.bonjour !== false`, and does nothing when the state is already right. `start()`, `stop()` and admin `POST /settings` (after the save and the socket cut; a failed save is a 500 and changes nothing) call it. `opts.spawn` (default `child_process.spawn`) lets the test see the process. Evidence: new gateway test "Tailscale only also stops the Bonjour advertisement…" (fake spawn: arguments, on → killed, same value → no new process, bad body 400, off → one new, network rebind → one for the new port, stop → none, restart with the option on → none) red before the fix ("switching it on stops the advertisement at once", actual 1); `node test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` 28/28 after. Mutation (a /tmp copy, removed): only `start()` ignores the setting → red ("no advertisement at start while Tailscale only is on"). Real `dns-sd` on this Mac (throwaway gateway "Bonjour Check Mac", port 0, /tmp dir removed; `dns-sd -B _dshmobile._tcp local` for 3 s): off → seen, on → not seen, off → seen, after close → not seen, no process left. `npm test` exit 0; `test/browser-mobile-connect.mjs` (19490/19491) PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2. tether/README.md (Tailscale only bullet): the advertisement stops too. The 13 READMEs need no change (they do not name Bonjour, and their Tailscale only text stays true). The live dsh still advertises until its plugin copy is updated and dsh restarts (out of scope).

## Six features: mark all read, message link, queue edit, redo, This chat, hide whitespace (2026-10-26)
Request (owner, one plan): (1) project "…" menu → Mark all as read clears every unread dot of that project; (2) each message gets Copy message link (session link + message seq); opening it scrolls the chat to that message and highlights it; (3) double-click a queued message edits it in place (Enter saves, Esc cancels, order kept); (4) after a turn rollback a Redo button brings the withdrawn messages and restored files back, with a warning for files changed meanwhile; (5) Changes → This chat: the files that tools wrote in all turns of the open chat and their total diff; (6) Changes → a switch that shows diffs with `git diff -w`.
Plan approved by the owner in chat (2026-10-26). Evidence read before planning (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 sources):
- No project-row menu slot (fixed Rename/Delete items, `Menu portal: true`). The dot is `UiSession.completionUnread` (private Set; cleared only by main view or a new run); `publishStatus()` republishes. Same code on both versions.
- No message action slot; `MessageIconActions` has Copy and Branch only. `dsh-session:` links do nothing on click; markdown links allow http/https/mailto only. 0.2.1 has `uiConversation.userText.register(rule)` + `conversation.user.text.node` slot (0.2.0 not). `binding.loadThrough(seq)` exists on both.
- Queue editors (0.2.0 `QueueEditor`, 0.2.1 `InlineEditor`): Enter saves, Esc cancels, Shift+Enter newline; `edit` keeps the id (order kept).
- Surface replace swaps a range for one node; withdrawn nodes cannot come back, only copies. Only user/system/developer messages may replace; `assistant/message` cannot carry `sourceEventSeqs`.
- `git diff --numstat -w` drops an indentation-only/CRLF-only file (probe, git 2.54).
Decisions: new FEATURES ids `markProjectRead`, `copyMessageLink`, `queueEdit` (all on by default; no port, no model call). Redo is part of `turnRollback`; This chat (`mode=chat`) and whitespace (`ws=1`) are part of `changesPanel`.
Shared parts: Host diff opts `{commit, ws, paths}`; `snapshotStore.diffSince` + shared restore writer; `withdrawnSpans(events, kinds)` (replaces `rollbackSpans` for views/exports); client `useChatRows`; `createRollback` redo state on the same busy flag (rule 7).
Phases: 0 record + probes; A ws; B chatWrites/diffSince/mode=chat; C Changes client; D queueEdit; E markProjectRead; F useChatRows + copyMessageLink; G withdrawnSpans + Redo; H Tether; I docs, final checks, review.
Allowed paths: client.js, index.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/{markProjectRead,copyMessageLink,queueEdit}.webp, test/*, package.json (test script), docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, AGENTS.md (rule 9), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md, tether/ios/{Sources,Tests}/*, tether/FEATURES.md, tether/README.md.
Risks: private-field access (1); copied-node redo (4) needs the phase 0 probe, else stop and ask; This chat base falls back to HEAD without a snapshot (5); link opening only on 0.2.1+ (2); DOM portals (1, 2, 3).
Out of scope: commit, version bump, release, npm, live desktop copy, real iPhone, Security set F-01.
Note: the working tree still holds the uncommitted Security set (section above); this work goes on top of it.
Checkpoint phase 0 (2026-10-26): throwaway probe plugin + browserless driver in /tmp/cp-redo-probe (fake model, temp DSH_HOME; not shipped). Auto Review refused a /tmp probe that launched Chrome (browser policy), so the browser-side probes (mark read, userText rule, project menu, queue double-click) run as the project's own headless tests in phases D, E, F.
- In-place redo with copied nodes: refused. dsh accepts the live appends and the next model request is identical, but the stored log is then corrupt for dsh itself: "SessionFormatError: assistant/message does not match an open turn and step" (cold read and restart, 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2). The copies must not be written.
- Redo as a fork: `ctx.sessionController.fork({ sessionId, atSeq: markerSeq - 1 })` on both versions: the new chat's next model request has the exact pre-rollback context (same prefix), its log reads cold, it opens and runs after a restart, the parent chat stays readable.
- Owner decision (question in chat, 2026-10-26): "Dal olarak yeniden uygula". Redo = a new chat from the state just before the rollback (dsh's own fork) + the files back; the rolled-back chat stays as it is.
Design change: no new marker kind, so `withdrawnSpans` is not needed (rule 9 text unchanged). Redo state: rollback with files saves the pre-rollback tree as `refs/r/<markerSeq>` and `rolled/<markerSeq>.json` (paths) in the chat's store; a redone marker is remembered in `storages/dsh-composer-plus/redone.json`. The offer shows while the newest user/message of the chat is our marker, the chat is idle and that marker was not redone.
Checkpoint phases A + B (2026-10-26):
- A: `changes`/`fileDiff` take `{ ws, paths }`; `wsArgs` adds `-w` (numstat and patch, all modes incl. commit); `params()` accepts only `ws=1`; `repoFacts`/`fileSummary` shared. `test/changes.test.mjs` red with `-w` removed ("with -w the indentation/CRLF-only file is gone"), green after; `ws=2|''|true` → 400.
- B: `chatWrites(events, cwd)` (write/edit file_path, str_replace_editor path except view; object or JSON-string args; relative paths from `working-directory/change`; failed results and unanswered calls out; newest last; ≤ MAX_FILES). `chatChanges`: against the chat's oldest `refs/s/*` snapshot through `snapshotStore.chatView` (a temp bare view repo with alternates store → repository, `* -text -filter -ident`, read-tree base + update-index of the chat's paths + diff-tree; not in the chat's queue, so no tool waits), else against HEAD (`base: 'head'`). Paths below a symlinked folder and FIFOs/devices are skipped (`skipped`), outside paths only counted (`outside`). `mode=chat` in MODES; routes take the paths from the chat's own log. New `test/chat-changes.test.mjs`: two chats one repo, committed work still shown, `-w`, single-file diff only for listed files, FIFO with a 5 s unblock timer, subfolder chat, routes; red without the snapshot base and without the symlink guard ("git update-index failed: … beyond a symbolic link"). `npm test` exit 0.
Checkpoint phases C–G (2026-10-26):
- C: Changes menu "This chat" (`mode=chat`, 15 s refresh, a note: snapshot or HEAD base, outside/skipped counts), whitespace switch (`data-composer-plus-ws`, `aria-pressed`, `localStorage dsh-composer-plus.changes.hideWhitespace`; part of the load key; passed to every diff). `test/changes-tree.test.mjs` extended (red with ws dropped from the URL).
- D: `queueEdit` (`conversation.input.dock` slot `queue-edit`): a double-click on a queued row clicks dsh's own Edit (`queueEditTarget`: never during a drag, on a control, in the editor, on an echo row or a disabled Edit). New `test/queue-edit.test.mjs` (red without the disabled guard).
- E: `markProjectRead` (`markReadApi` on `UiSession.completionUnread` + `publishStatus()`; `MarkProjectRead` portal into dsh's project "…" menu, Esc closes it). New `test/mark-read.test.mjs` (red without publishStatus, red with a cross-project id).
- F: `useChatRows` shared by TurnRollback and `MessageLinks`. `copyMessageLink`: 🔗 in user rows and turn tails; `dsh-session:<payload>#<seq>`; user-text rule (order 5, before dsh's 10) on 0.2.1+, which also matches the stored form `@<chat id>#<seq>` (dsh's session reference rewrites the link when the message is sent); `jumpToMessage` (open chat, `binding.session.loadThrough(seq)`, nearest visible row ≤ seq, 2 s blink, one jump at a time). New `test/message-link.test.mjs` (dsh decoder check; red with rule order 15 and without loadThrough).
- G: Redo as a fork. Host: `writeTree` shared by restore and redo (found: `return writeBack(...)` inside try/finally without await removed the scratch folder first; fixed with `return await`), `keepRolled` (`refs/r/<marker>`, `rolled/<marker>.json` with the blob each file was left with), `redoPlan` (`changed` = not as the rollback left it), `redoTarget`, `redoneStore` (`redone.json`, 0600). Route: GET adds `redo`; POST `{sessionId, redo, preview}` / `{sessionId, redo, files, overwrite}`: idle only, one action per chat, files written only after `sessionController.fork({ sessionId, atSeq: marker - 1 })` worked. Client: redo state + `rev` (an older GET answer never brings the offer back), `runRedo` on the same busy flag, `RedoBar` in `conversation.input.dock`. Red checks: changed-file guard, files before the fork, rev rule.
- Texts: 36 keys in en/zh; 11 locales by a subagent (placeholders checked; tr labels as the owner wrote them); locale test 11 × 3279.
- Real dsh: new `test/browser-six-features.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (all six; the link chip opens the message) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (link copy only: no user-text rules there). `test/browser-turn-rollback.mjs` failed on HEAD too (the last model request was Next message prediction's summary); the fake model now answers prediction requests apart; PASS on both versions. `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs` PASS on both. SHOT=1: markProjectRead.webp 11 kB, copyMessageLink.webp 5 kB, queueEdit.webp 9 kB, checked by eye (demo projects only).
Checkpoint phase H (Tether, 2026-10-26, subagent): Core.swift `messageLink`/`parseMessageLink`/`jumpTarget`/`changesQuery`/`unreadIn`/`redoTarget`/`redoShown`; Client redo offer (GET `redo`, rollback `markerSeq`, live marker), `redo` with its own `redoing` guard, `markAllRead` (POST unread), `openMessage` + jump; UI: Redo bar + sheet, Mark all as read in the project header menu, Copy message link + Open message button, Changes → This chat (15 s, base note incl. `partial`) and Hide whitespace (`ws=1` for every changes/diff call). Queue edit: no change (Edit keeps the item id). FEATURES.md rows for markProjectRead, queueEdit, copyMessageLink; changesPanel and turnRollback rows extended. No new gateway route (rollback, unread, changes, diff are in PLUS). `xcodebuild test`: 46 tests, 0 failures (run again by me after the `partial` note). Not done: a simulator check with agent-device.

Now caught and logged (`snapshotStore({ log })`); Redo then offers the chat only. `blobsOf` sends paths in ~64 kB parts (argv limit).
