## Open review findings (2026-10-27, review-guard of the uncommitted six-feature change on deda507)
Status: RESOLVED (2026-10-27, /implement-plan), not committed. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-27. Close an item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): Changes → This chat fails (HTTP 500) when the chat wrote a file below a symlinked folder that points inside the repository, or below a dangling symlink. Signature: `chatView` in `snapshotStore` (index.js) skips a path only when `relativeInside(realTop, realpath(dirname(target))) === undefined` (the real parent is outside the repository) or when the target is not a regular file, a symlink or missing (a FIFO, a device); a real parent inside the repository through a symlink, or a parent whose `realpath` fails (dangling link), is listed; then `git update-index --add --remove -z --stdin` gets `alias/a.js` and stops: "error: 'alias/a.js' is beyond a symbolic link / fatal: Unable to process path alias/a.js". Evidence (2026-10-27, /tmp probes, removed): repo with `lib/a.js` and `alias -> lib` (committed), a snapshot, tool path `<repo>/alias/a.js` → `chatChanges` throws "git update-index failed: … beyond a symbolic link"; with the HEAD base (no snapshot) the same path is only left out (`changes` filters it with a pathspec, no error). A dangling `alias -> missing` gives the same git error (direct `git update-index` probe with the same index setup). A path in a deleted plain folder (`gone/x.js`) works (git records the delete). Impact: one such write in a chat's log makes This chat (list and each diff) a 500 on every open until the chat is gone; no data change. Not caught: `test/chat-changes.test.mjs` tests only a symlink to a folder outside the repository (`link -> ../outside`). Resolved when: a test with `alias -> lib` and a dangling `ghost -> missing` (both written through by the chat's tool) is red on the current code, then This chat answers 200 with the other files, counts the two paths in `skipped`, and the single-file diff of a listed file still works; `node test/chat-changes.test.mjs` and `npm test` pass.
- F-02 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): in a forked chat (Redo's new chat, dsh's Branch), This chat says `partial` ("compared with the project before message #N: Turn rollback keeps only the 50 newest snapshots…") although nothing was pruned. Signature: `sessionFacts` in `apply` (index.js) takes `first` from `observation.events.find(user/message, kind user)`; dsh's fork seeds the child with the parent's events `0..boundary` and `inheritedEventCount = boundary + 1` (dsh-api-session-controller `fork`, dsh-session `buildForkSeed`, same on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2), so `first` is the parent's first message, and the fork's own oldest snapshot (`since`) is always later. Evidence (2026-10-27, /tmp probe, removed): route with `inheritedEventCount: 11`, an inherited user message at seq 1 and the fork's own at 12, snapshot at 12 → `base: 'partial', since: 12`. Impact: a wrong note in every forked chat (Redo makes forks); the file list is right. Not caught: the route tests in `test/chat-changes.test.mjs` have no `inheritedEventCount`. Resolved when: a route test with a fork observation (inherited first message, own snapshot at the fork's first message) answers `base: 'snapshot'` (red on the current code), a non-fork chat whose oldest snapshot is later than its first message still answers `partial`, and `npm test` passes.
- Rules added to AGENTS.md with this record: rule 6 (paths below an inside or dangling symlinked folder when the Host gives chat paths to git), rule 10 (a fork's inherited events; use `inheritedEventCount`).
- F-01 fix: `chatView` (index.js) checks each folder of a written path with `lstat` from the repository top (`viaLink`): a part that is not a folder (a symlink, also one that points inside the repository or nowhere) skips the path; a part that is gone ends the walk (the file counts as a delete). The old `realpath(dirname)` check (it caught only links that leave the repository) is gone. Evidence: new block in `test/chat-changes.test.mjs` (`alias -> src`, `ghost -> missing`, deleted `gone/`) red before the fix ("git update-index failed: error: 'alias/deep.js' is beyond a symbolic link"), green after: list `gone/x.js` (delete) + `shared.js`, `skipped` 2, the diff of `shared.js` works, no diff for `alias/deep.js`; the old outside-link, FIFO and subfolder checks stay green.
- F-02 fix: `sessionFacts` in `apply` (index.js) looks for the first user message after `observation.inheritedEventCount` (0 when it is not a positive safe integer). Evidence: new route block in `test/chat-changes.test.mjs` (fork with 12 inherited events, own first message and snapshot at 12) red before the fix (actual `['partial', 12, ['shared.js']]`), green after (`snapshot`); a chat of its own with an older first message still answers `partial`. Real dsh: `test/browser-six-features.mjs` now sends a message in the redone (forked) chat and checks the This chat note: with the old line restored it fails on 0.2.1-rc.2 ("This chat in the redone (forked) chat: its own first message"); with the fix PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2.
- Both: `npm test` exit 0; Semgrep p/javascript + p/secrets on index.js and the two tests: 0 findings; `git diff --check` clean; `build-docs --check` passed (2026-10-27). No README change: the README does not describe symlinked folders or forks in This chat, and its text stays true.

- Docs found stale or missing (each checked against the code): language pack count 3,143 → 3,280 (`test/locale.test.mjs` counts 3280; 13 READMEs + docs/features.json → llms.txt); the Settings sentence on Host-kept values had no grant Time limit; the keyboard table had no Improve prompt (`Cmd/Ctrl+Z`, `Esc`) and no queue edit (`Enter`/`Esc`) rows; the Development block listed no command for 8 browser tests (six-features, changes-tree, grant-ttl, predict, complete, project-picker, queue-drag, continue-queue); no README link to docs/ROUTES.md. tether/README.md: "only LAN" lines although the gateway and the app also accept the tailnet; history paging listed as missing although `Load earlier messages` exists (`session/page`); the admin page has no "Remote access" row (the text is "Away from home: Tailscale is on (…)"); iOS test count 6/6 → 47/47.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): Tether New session → Projects → a project fails with "session.create accepts workspaceId or cwd, not both" and makes no chat. Signature: `NewSessionSheet` calls `create(w.path, w.id)` (UI.swift) and `Client.create` put both `cwd` and `workspaceId` in the request; dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 (`dsh-api-session-controller/lib/index.js:687`) and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`:756`) refuse that. Found by the simulator check (subagent, agent-device, throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 on 19490/19491). Fix: `Client.createRequest(cwd:workspaceId:)` sends only `workspaceId` when there is one (dsh then takes the workspace's own path and adds the chat to it), else only `cwd`. Evidence: `testCreateRequestSendsWorkspaceOrFolder` red with the old request shape (XCTAssertEqual failed), green after; iOS tests 47/47 on the iPhone 17 simulator.
- Simulator check (subagent, agent-device, iPhone 17 Pro iOS 26.5, throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 + gateway on 19490/19491, fake model, made-up projects; all removed after; live dsh not touched): Copy message link PASS, Queue edit PASS, Redo core PASS (files back on disk, old chat kept), Changes (This chat, Hide whitespace, branch tree, commit diff) PASS, the F-01 fix PASS (New session → Projects opens a chat in that project; Quick chat still works). Evidence in tether/build/agent-device/v113/ (git-ignored).
- F-02 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): a Redo chat (and Tether's own Fork) is missing from Tether's Sessions and acts as a subagent ("Open parent session", no Delete, no rollback/redo/predict/improve/continue). Signature: `Sess.init` set `parent` from `parentSessionId`; dsh's session/fork writes `parentSession` and no `origin` (dsh-api-session-controller `fork`, 0.2.0-rc.2 :816, 0.2.1-rc.2 :889), and `listFields` sends it as `parentSessionId`. Fix: `parent` only when `origin == "subagent"` (or the subagent projection), like index.js `isRootChat`; `Client.snapshot` keeps `origin`. Evidence: `testForkIsARootChat` red with the old line (3 failures), green after; subagent fixtures now carry `origin: "subagent"` as dsh sends it.
- F-03 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): a rolled-back chat opened later shows an empty chat, "Working… 9m" and Stop while dsh says it is idle. Signature: `rebuild()` draws `withoutRolledBack(events)`; the span [min(sourceEventSeqs), marker] hides the turn/end events but not the first turn/start, so the replay leaves `running = true`. Fix: after the draw, the newest turn/start or turn/end of the whole log sets `running` and `turnStartedAt` (the same rule as `continueAfter`). Evidence: new block in `testComposerPlusRules` red without the fix (2 failures), green after; iOS tests 48/48 on iPhone 17 Pro.
- F-04 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-27): the project header menu (Copy path, Mark all as read) did not open on the simulator: touch and hold on a Sessions section header only folds the project (agent-device longpress 1500/2500 ms, coordinates, pan hold); the same press on a chat row opens its menu. Signature: section header = `Button(.plain) { fold }.contextMenu { … }` (UI.swift). Not checked with a real finger. Resolved when: the menu opens on a device or the simulator, or the actions move to a control that opens with a tap.
- Minor (open, not fixed): Changes mode bar: "This chat" (5th segment) is off screen and needs a slow swipe; the keyboard comes up over Tree/diff after the Branches menu closes; only the switch (not its row text) toggles Hide whitespace; two taps on the queue "Edit" item did nothing while a turn streamed (a third worked).
- F-04 fix: each project header in Sessions has a "…" button (`Menu`, accessibility label "Project actions") that opens with a tap; it shares `projectActions(_:)` with the header's context menu (UI.swift). Evidence (subagent, agent-device, iPhone 17 Pro simulator, throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 on 19490/19491, all removed after): "…" on both headers; tap → "Copy path" + "Mark all as read"; Mark all as read clears only that project's dot (phone badge 2 → 1, Mac unread list keeps only the other project's chat); after it the menu has "Copy path" only, and `simctl pbpaste` gives the project folder; a title tap still folds, a "…" tap does not. A touch and hold on the header still only folds it (simulator), so the docs name only the "…" button. iOS tests 48/48. Open (minor): the "…" hit target is 32×28 pt (below 44×44).
- iPhone (owner's iPhone 13, iOS 27.2, cable): Xcode has no Apple account, so `-allowProvisioningUpdates` fails ("No Accounts"). The owner approved in chat signing with `~/Library/Application Support/TetherSigning/`; its `kc.pw` opens neither `tether-sign.keychain-db` nor `dev.p12`, so the app was signed from `dev.key` + `dev.pem` through a temporary keychain under `tether/build/signing/` (deleted after; the user keychain list is unchanged). Built from the working tree with the "…" fix, installed with `xcrun devicectl device install app` (keeps app data) and launched. Not done: agent-device on the iPhone. This Mac's `DevToolsSecurity` is disabled (`devtools_security_developer_mode_disabled`), and enabling it needs `sudo` (the owner's Mac password). Steps written in tether/AGENTS.md ("Signing for the iPhone", physical iPhone step 2) and AGENTS.md (Release).
- iPhone check (2026-10-27, owner's iPhone 13, iOS 27.2, live gateway, agent-device): the owner ran `sudo DevToolsSecurity -enable`. agent-device's own runner build still failed (`signing_provisioning_profile_missing`, "No Accounts"), so the runner was built with `CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO`, signed with the TetherSigning files (temporary keychain, deleted) and passed with `--ios-xctestrun-file` (steps in tether/AGENTS.md, physical iPhone step 4; run again from the doc text: PASS). Results: "…" tap opens the menu (Copy path; no Mark all as read because no project had a green dot); Copy path → pasted into Search: `/Users/furkancakir/Documents/testdeepseekharness` (agent-device cannot read a physical iPhone's clipboard); a title tap folds and unfolds; a 1.5 s touch and hold on the header only folds it, as on the simulator.
- Fixed from that check (UI.swift): the "…" hit target was 32×28 pt → 44×44 pt (`.padding(.vertical, -8)` keeps the header at 48 pt; measured rects 44×44 and 48). A tap 4 pt inside the new edge (outside the old 32×28) opens the menu. The header's `.contextMenu` never opens (simulator and iPhone), so it was removed. The taller "…" made VoiceOver read "Project actions" before the project name; `.accessibilitySortPriority(1)` on the fold button restores the order (snapshot: name, Collapsed, Project actions). iOS tests 48/48. Installed on the iPhone with `devicectl` (app data kept).
- Still open (minor, not checked on the iPhone): the Changes mode bar notes above.
- Minor notes, checked on the owner's iPhone 13 (2026-10-27, agent-device with the signed runner; throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 on 3298, fake model on 19497, gateway "Demo Mac" on 19490/19491 that approved only the first request; the pairing link went over the cable with `devicectl --payload-url`; after the check the phone was switched back to "İş", "Demo Mac" was unpaired, the processes stopped and the temp folders removed; the live dsh was not touched):
  - Changes mode bar: reproduced. The segmented control showed only Uncommitted, Unstaged and Staged; Branch and This chat were off screen. Fix (UI.swift `ChangesSheet`): a **Mode** menu row (`Picker` with the default menu style) lists all five. iPhone: Mode → This chat in one tap ("Mode, This chat", config.js +1, style.css +3 −3).
  - Hide whitespace: reproduced. A tap on the row text did not change the switch. Fix: the label text fills the row and toggles too (`onTapGesture { ws.toggle() }`); the switch keeps its own tap. iPhone: text tap on, text tap off, switch on, switch off; with it on, This chat shows only config.js (+1 −0).
  - Keyboard over Tree/diff after Branches: not reproduced on the iPhone (5 tries: Branches → a branch, a commit, a diff, back, and with the chat's composer focused before Changes opened). Not changed.
  - Queue Edit while a turn streams: not reproduced on the iPhone (4 of 4 taps opened "Edit queued message" at once while "Working . . ." streamed). Not changed.
  - iOS tests 48/48. Installed on the iPhone with `devicectl` (app data kept).

## Tether: a waiting indicator for every wait on the Mac (2026-10-27)
Request (owner): while Tether waits for the Mac, show a loading indicator, never an error or a frozen screen. (1) Start: about 1 s of "Not connected", then the chats: show a spinner while the first connect runs; "Not connected" only when the connect really failed. (2) Send: away from home a sent message showed nothing for 3–5 s: show it at once as "sending". (3) List every action that waits for the Mac and give each one the same kind of indicator. Test on the owner's iPhone with a throwaway dsh ("Demo Mac"), delay made on the throwaway gateway (owner's choice); never touch the live dsh or real projects. Work in the worktree `dsh-composer-plus-loading` (branch `tether-loading`), because the main checkout has another session's uncommitted work.
Root causes (read in the code):
- Start: `Client.state` starts as `.off` and `connect()` runs only on `scenePhase == .active`, so the first frames draw OfflineView ("Not connected").
- Back from the background: `drop()` set `.reconnecting` for a link that had worked, and every view treats `.reconnecting` as offline.
- Send: no row until the Mac's `user/message` comes on the stream; the old "Sending…" row stayed below the old messages. Away on another Wi‑Fi (owner: "evde denedim işe bağlanmayı"), `call()` tried the Mac's LAN address first on every request and waited its 5 s time-out before Tailscale.
- Other actions (`c.run { … }`): no feedback at all until the answer came.
Waiting actions found (UI.swift): create, fork, pin/unpin, archive/unarchive, rename, delete, goal create/edit/pause/resume/complete/clear, queue steer/edit/remove/move, stop, continue, leave plan, compact, slash commands, model/permission/preset select, approvals and questions (answer, Allow for this chat, Decide on Mac), revoke grants, Mark all as read, subagent message and interrupt. Already with their own spinner: Load earlier messages, Improve prompt, Changes/Tree/Commit/Diff loads, Feature ideas (`busy`), Redo, message search, Agents pull-to-refresh, rollback/redo previews.
Decisions:
- One app-wide `WaitPill` ("Waiting for your Mac…", spinner, no taps taken) for every `Client.run` action that takes longer than 0.4 s; `run(waits: false)` for actions with their own progress (send, resend, Load earlier, the file picker, the model list load).
- Start: `state = .connecting` in `init` when a Mac is paired; the Sessions and chat placeholders say "Connecting to your Mac…". A dropped live link retries as `.connecting` (`afterDrop`); foreground `wake()` starts a fresh try. "Not connected" stays for a connect that failed.
- Send: `Outgoing` (request id = `session/prompt` `requestId`): the message shows at once, faded, with "Sending…"; the Mac's copy (a `user/message` or a queued inbox item with `source.rpcId`) replaces it; a failed send removes it and puts the draft back (as before); a 15 s fallback removes a bubble whose copy never came on the stream.
- `addressOrder`: the address that answered last goes first (same order as before when nothing answered yet).
- A chat that opens shows "Loading the chat…" until its snapshot (`loadingChat`), not the empty-chat screen. Roll back gets a spinner like Redo.
Checks: iOS tests (new `testWaitingIndicators`), `npm test`; owner's iPhone against a throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 + gateway "Demo Mac" that delays every request; afterwards the phone back on "İş", Demo Mac unpaired, temp files removed.
Checkpoint (code): iOS tests 49/49 on the iPhone 17 Pro simulator.
- Change after the first iPhone run: the WaitPill timer moved from the view into `Client` (`waitShown`, 0.4 s, generation counter); in the view a `.task(id:)` that started in the first frame never showed the pill. The unit test checks it (not shown at once, shown after 0.4 s, gone with the last wait).
- iPhone check (owner's iPhone 13, iOS 27.2, 2026-10-27; agent-device with the signed runner; throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 on 3298, fake model 19497, a delay proxy on 3299 that holds each request, socket start and socket frame for `delay.txt` ms, gateway "Demo Mac" on 19490/19491; the pairing link went over the cable; live dsh not touched):
  - Start, 4 s delay: 0.9–4.8 s "Connecting to your Mac…" + "Connecting…", 5.5 s "Connected" + "Loading sessions…", then the list. No "Not connected" sample.
  - Start, gateway stopped: "Connecting to your Mac…" until ~10 s, then the offline view (Can't reach your Mac, Open Tailscale, Retry). Gateway back: "Connected" at ~17 s, list at ~29 s (backoff), no app restart.
  - 40 s in the background, then open: "Connected" at once, list shown.
  - Send "Say hello", 4 s delay: at +1.4 s the faded bubble with "Sending…"; at +5.5 s the Mac's copy and the answer, one bubble (no double).
  - Unpin with a full swipe, 8 s delay: "Waiting for your Mac…" at +1.6 s (screenshot), gone after the answer.
  - Open a chat, 4 s delay on socket frames: "Loading the chat…" from +1.4 s to +4.4 s, then the messages; never the empty-chat screen.
  - Not checked on the iPhone: "Sending…" for a message that goes into the queue while a turn runs (agent-device could not tap Queue: two tries typed into the field and the turn ended first). Covered by the unit test (inbox splice with the same request id ends the wait).
  - After: phone switched back to "İş" (Connected), Demo Mac unpaired (only Ev and İş left), processes stopped (3298, 3299, 19490, 19491, 19497 free), temp folders removed.
- Final checks: iOS tests 49/49, `npm test` exit 0.

## Five features: Tether photo, GitHub issue, branch badge, file chats, find in chat (2026-10-27)
Request (owner, one plan, approved in chat 2026-10-27): (1) Tether: attach a photo from the library or the camera to a message; (2) Later list: a button opens a GitHub issue for the idea with the user's `gh` CLI, and the issue link shows next to the idea; (3) sidebar: each chat row shows the git branch of the chat's folder at its last turn; (4) Changes: a file's menu lists the chats that wrote or edited that file (write/edit tools), newest first, each row opens that chat at that request; (5) Ctrl+F opens a find box for the open chat: highlights, previous/next, match count.
Evidence (read before planning):
- (1) already exists: `tether/ios/Sources/UI.swift` composer "+" menu (Camera, Photos ≤ 4, Paste image), 1600 px JPEG, `session/prompt` `image` block (`Core.swift` `promptContent`); dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 accepts `{type:'image', mediaType:'image/jpeg', data}` (≤ 20 images per message). No code: verify and document only. The subagent reply box sends no images (out of scope).
- Sidebar chat rows have no always-visible slot (`row.action` shows on hover only, `row.leading` gives way to the status dots); rows are `[data-row-key="session:<id>"]` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
- The chat flow is not virtualized (`useVirtualizer` only in the turn navigation rail); closed step groups do not render their members.
- dsh's shortcut registry refuses a web `primary+F` default (`isWebBindingAllowed`); the desktop app has no built-in find. dsh's Fork uses Cmd+Alt+F / Cmd+Shift+F.
- `sessionQuery.listSessions()` on both versions; observations carry `inheritedEventCount`; events carry `time`.
- dsh's subprocess scrub drops env names with KEY|PASSWORD|SECRET|TOKEN; `gh` keeps its login in the keyring.
Decisions: (2) part of `featureIdeas`, (4) part of `changesPanel` (no new switch); new ids `findInChat` (browser switch) and `sessionBranch` (Host switch: the Host records the branch at turn edges), both on by default (no port, no model call). Highlights use the CSS Custom Highlight API (no change to React's DOM).
Shared parts: `runTool` (git and gh), `writeCalls` (This chat and file chats), `useRowHosts` (project status and branch badge), `openMessage`/`jumpToMessage` (file chat rows), `foldChar` (find).
Phases: 0 record + baseline; 1 Tether photo verify/doc; 2 shared refactors; 3 GitHub issue; 4 file chats; 5 find in chat; 6 branch badge; 7 real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 + 0.2.1-rc.2, iOS, measurements, npm test, Semgrep, pack, review.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/{sessionBranch,findInChat}.webp (featureIdeas/changesPanel if retaken), docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/{Sources,Tests}/*, tether/{FEATURES,README}.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: DOM portals (sidebar rows, chat flow); file chats reads many logs (measure; cache when > 5 s); a Finder-started desktop dsh may not have `gh` on PATH (clear error).
Out of scope: version bump, commit, release, live desktop copy, a real iPhone install.
Decision (2026-10-27, during phase 3): the 11 locale packs, the 12 README translations and the non-English `how` texts are done once for all five features by one subagent after phase 6 (less repeated work). Until then `test/locale.test.mjs` and the README checks are red on purpose; every other suite runs after each phase.
Checkpoint phases 0–3 (2026-10-27): baseline `npm test` exit 0. Phase 1: no code (Tether README section). Phase 2: `runTool`, `writeCalls`, `useRowHosts`; chat-changes, changes, changes-tree, project-status, turn-rollback, toggles, feature-ideas, prompt-improve pass unchanged. Phase 3: Host `openIssue` + Later `{issue}`; client `issueLater` (`issuing` counts as busy) + Issue #N link (https issue URLs only); Tether `issueLink` + button; en/zh texts; docs/features.json en/zh + llms + route. Checks: new `test/github-issue.test.mjs` (argv `--title=`, stdin body, cwd, token by name, URL, gh reasons, stored text only, link saved, second issue 409, overlap 409 — red when the guard or `--title=` is removed), `test/feature-ideas-client.test.mjs` issue block (red without `issuing` in deckBusy), iOS 48/48.
Checkpoint phase 4 (2026-10-27): Host `fileChats` + `GET /api/composer-plus.file-chats?sessionId&path` (path checked: no `/` start, no `.`/`..`/empty part, no NUL, ≤ 4096; only compared, never opened); `writeCalls` gives each call's request seq. Client `FileMenu` (dsh Menu, one item) on each file row when the page has an opener, `FileChats` view with ← Back (kept per chat; a mode change or the branch chip leaves it); rows open `openMessage` (Copy message link's opener, moved before changesPanel and shared). Tether: `PLUS` + `file-chats` GET, swipe/touch-and-hold on a changed file → FileChatsView, `fileChatRows`. Checks: new `test/file-chats.test.mjs` (two chats + subfolder chat + other repo + failed call + fork + both withdraw kinds + /compact + symlinked chat folder + unreadable log + partial + route 400s + FIFO path; red with the fork offset removed and with only our withdraw kind), new `test/file-chats-client.test.mjs` (menu, URL, rows, click, notes, per-chat view, back, mode change; red when the view is not keyed by chat and when a mode change keeps it), gateway 28/28 (file-chats GET passes, POST 403; later `{issue}` passes), iOS 48/48.
Checkpoint phase 5 (2026-10-27): feature `findInChat` (browser switch, on by default; `shell.overlay` `find-in-chat`): `foldChar` (one character in, at most one out), `findMatches` (per block, never across blocks, no overlap, ≤ 2000), `chatBlocks` (dsh's outer `[data-chat-flow]`; our box, buttons, `[hidden]`, `aria-hidden`, rolled-back rows and undrawn elements left out), `matchRange`, `ChatFind` (window capture key: Ctrl+F, Cmd+F on macOS, never with Shift/Alt; not over a dialog or without a chat; CSS highlights `cp-find`/`cp-find-current`; Enter/Shift+Enter/↓/↑ wrap; Esc closes, clears highlights and gives the focus back; one MutationObserver on the flow while open, one redraw per frame, the current match stays the same match). Tether: chat title menu → Find in chat (`findRows`, `findFold`: your messages and answers on screen, tinted, n / total, ↑ ↓, Done). Checks: new `test/find-in-chat.test.mjs` (red when close keeps the highlights, when the fold returns two characters, when Ctrl+Alt+F counts, when rolled-back rows are searched, when the current match is not kept after older history loads), `test/toggles.test.mjs` (32 rows), iOS 48/48 (findRows, findFold).
Checkpoint phase 6 (2026-10-27): feature `sessionBranch` (Host switch `branches.json`, on by default). Host `branchOf` (`git branch --show-current`, else `(short id)`, none outside a repo) and `installBranches` (turn/start and turn/end of top-level chats; one read per chat at a time, edges during a read read once more; `session-branches.json` owner-only, newest 2000; route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.branches`). Client `createBranches` (newest read wins; off = none), `SessionBranchBadges` (pill in each `session:` row through the shared `useRowHosts`; read again 500 ms after a chat stops running), `createMobile(BRANCH_ROUTE)` in `createToggles`, MobileStatus prefix `branch`. Tether: `PLUS` + `branches` GET (POST 403), `sessionBranches`, pill next to the chat title. Checks: new `test/session-branch.test.mjs` (Host red without the merge, the root-chat filter, the delete on no-repo, the off switch; client red without the newest-wins seq, with project rows, without the host cleanup), gateway 28/28, iOS 48/48. Real dsh 0.2.1-rc.2: `main` pill, then `feature/login` after a branch switch and a turn (screenshot checked).
Found during phase 6: `test/turn-rollback.test.mjs` failed with the new `session/event` listener: its fake `ctx.on` kept only the last listener of an event, so the branch listener replaced the rollback snapshot listener (snapshot: false). Cordis runs every listener; the fake now chains them. Not a product bug (the plugin's other `session/event` listeners already coexist in real dsh). `test/mark-read.test.mjs` matched the FEATURES order text; updated for the new id.
Checkpoint phase 7, part 1 (2026-10-27):
- New `test/browser-five-features.mjs` (headless Chrome, fake model that reads then writes app.js, fake `gh` first on PATH that records argv/cwd/stdin and prints an issue URL; no GitHub): PASS on dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2: branch pill `main` → `feature/login` after a switch and a turn; app.js menu → both chats newest first, "this chat" mark, request text, Back, a row opens chat 1 and its request blinks, the other chat's panel shows its own view; Ctrl+Alt+F does nothing, Ctrl+F → "1 / 3" with 3 highlights, Enter "2 / 3", Shift+Enter "1 / 3", Esc clears the highlights and gives the focus back to the composer; Later → Open GitHub issue → gh got `issue create --title=Pantry tracker --body-file -` in the project folder, body on stdin, `GH_PROMPT_DISABLED=1`, link "Issue #7"; Host branch switch off → no pills; findInChat off → no box; no page errors.
- Found by it (test only): dsh's write tool refuses a file that the chat did not read first (the fake model now reads before it writes); the Ideas page opens from dsh's Start guide or a "New tab".
- SHOT=1 (0.2.1-rc.2, English UI, demo project acme-web): `sessionBranch.webp` 4.7 kB (row with `feature/login`), `findInChat.webp` 23 kB (box "2 / 3", three highlighted matches); both checked by eye. `test/shots.test.mjs` 33 images.
- `npm pack --dry-run`: 69 files, the two new webp, only the 3 gateway files from tether/. `git diff --check` clean.
- Simulator check of Tether (agent-device, throwaway demo gateway on 19490/19491 with mocks for branches, file-chats, later issue): the app built and installed, but it opened locked (Face ID lock from an earlier session) and the simulated Face ID match was refused by Auto review. Not done: the simulator walk-through of the new Tether screens; iOS unit tests cover the new pure functions (48/48).
Measurement (2026-10-27, `/tmp/cp5-measure.mjs`, not shipped): a copy of the largest real project (275 chat logs, 152 MB) in a throwaway DSH_HOME, dsh 0.2.1-rc.2, `GET /api/composer-plus.file-chats`: without a cache 7.9–9.5 s per open (every log read each time, over the 5 s rule of the plan). Decision: `writeSummaries()` keeps each chat's write summary until its log file changes (size + mtime from dsh's own `sessionPersistence.locate`; no stamp = read each time; ≤ 2000 chats, least recently used out). Then: first open 4.1 s, next opens 82–112 ms (also for other paths: one summary serves every file). The copy was deleted. `test/file-chats.test.mjs` cache block red when the stamp is ignored.
Checkpoint phase 7, final (2026-10-27):
- Texts: 31 new keys + 2 changed descriptions in the 11 packs (subagent; `npm run build:locales`, locale test 11 × 3311); docs/features.json `how` of findInChat, sessionBranch, changesPanel, featureIdeas, mobileConnect (photo) in 13 languages; README.md hand-written parts (3 keyboard rows, 4 privacy, 2 troubleshooting, 4 limits, 3 FAQ) and the same in the 12 translations (subagent). README "Load earlier" names dsh's own `chat.loadOlder` label (not Tether's "Load earlier messages").
- Live chats are never served from the file-chats cache (`listSessions` `live: true`: dsh answers their newest events from memory); test red without it.
- ChatFind: unused `move` field removed from `live.current`.
- Final checks: `npm test` exit 0 (35 Host route methods documented, 33 features, 3171 bold labels); `test/browser-five-features.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; iOS 48/48; Semgrep p/javascript + p/secrets 0 findings (client.js also with `--timeout 120`); `npm pack --dry-run` 69 files; `git diff --check` clean.
- Review: two read-only reviewer subagents did not report in time (75 and 25+ minutes); both were stopped. Self-review instead (fileChats live-chat cache fix above). An independent review is still open.
- Not done: Tether simulator walk-through (the app opened locked by Face ID; the simulated match was refused by Auto review); no commit, version bump, release or live desktop copy.

Review round 2 (2026-10-27, three read-only reviewer subagents, one per half; no P0/P1). Fixed, each with a check:
- Tether P2: Open GitHub issue used the 5 s two-address fallback, so a slow gh got a second POST and a false 409. Now `wait: 75` (one address, above the Mac's 60 s gh limit).
- Tether P2: Find in chat rebuilt the chat rows once per row on each render (each streamed token). Now one `chatRows` + `findRows` per render and an id → index map for the tint.
- Tether P3: 44 pt tap targets (find ↑ ↓ Done, Open GitHub issue, Issue #N); the Later list shows the running action and disables the issue button; FileChatsView shows the `unreadable` note; `loadSessions` asks the branches with `async let` (no extra round trip). iOS 48/48, no new warnings.
- Host P3: a busy idea answers 409 "another action for this idea is still running" (not "no longer in the list"); a gh output with an issue link counts as made whatever the exit code; a gh time-out says that the issue may exist; a failed link save still answers `{issue}` (500). `branchOf` returns `null` only for "not a git repository" (exit 128): a git time-out or a missing git keeps the saved branch, and a chat outside a repository with no entry writes nothing. `writeSummary` keeps one entry per (file, request) and reads the request texts in one pass. Mutations: each of the 5 guards removed → its test red (`test/github-issue.test.mjs`, `test/session-branch.test.mjs`).
- Client (found while checking the review): the query folded per code point but the page per code unit, so "😀" matched every emoji with the same first half. Both fold per code unit now; `test/find-in-chat.test.mjs` red before (emoji, combining accent), green after.
- Browser test: 0.2.0-rc.2 once failed "the box takes the focus" (the focus moves in the next animation frame); the check now waits up to 3 s. PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 after it.
- Checked, not changed: dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 `listSessions` sets `live` and sorts newest first (`dsh-session-query`); the gateway forwards only GET for `branches` (POST 403, `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs`).
- Client review (3 P2 + 7 P3), each checked against the code or dsh first:
  - P2 IME: the find box read `isComposing` on React's event; dsh's bundled React has no such field (its keyboard interface lists `key, code, …, repeat, locale`). Now `event.nativeEvent.isComposing || keyCode 229`. Test: an IME Enter does not move, an IME Esc does not close (red without the guard).
  - P2 chat change: the observer kept the old `[data-chat-flow]`. Now it checks the flow every second and moves to a new one (red without it).
  - P2 Korean/kana: `foldChar` kept only the first part of a decomposition ("한" = "하", "が" = "か"). Now only base + Latin marks fold; anything else stays (lowercased). P3 NFD: page marks are skipped and a position map gives the real offsets ("Türkçe" finds "Tu\u0308rkc\u0327e"). Both red before.

Mounting reads nothing. Test red when the older GET wins.
  - P3 a11y: each file chats row is a `listitem` that holds the button. P3 rule 1: switching Changes panel off stops its jump; the blink CSS is at the Changes page root. P3 issue link: the host must be a plain name (`user@host` refused) in index.js, client.js and Tether `issueLink` (tests in github-issue and iOS).
  - Shortcut (owner's decision, 2026-10-27): Cmd+F on a Mac, Ctrl+F on Windows and Linux. On a Mac, Ctrl+F stays the macOS text field key (cursor one character forward). `isFindKey` changed; `test/find-in-chat.test.mjs` red before ("on macOS Ctrl+F stays the text field key"), green after; `test/browser-five-features.mjs` presses Ctrl+F on the Mac first (no box), then Cmd+F (`ControlOrMeta`): PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. Texts: `feature.findInChat.description` in en, zh and the 11 locales; README table row, keyboard table, limits (with one sentence on the macOS Ctrl+F use) and FAQ in the 13 READMEs; `how` in 13 languages and `llms` in docs/features.json; `npm run build:locales`, `npm run build:readme`. Screenshot unchanged (it shows no key). `npm test` exit 0.

## Fix: Improve prompt lost its run when the user left the chat (2026-10-27, branch fix/prompt-improve-chat-switch)
Report (owner): click the wand, open another chat before the answer, come back: no answer, and the next click says that an improvement is still running.
Root cause (real isolated dsh 0.2.1-rc.2, headless probe): dsh mounts a new `conversation.input.right` entry per chat, so `PromptImprove` unmounted on leaving. Its unmount and `[sessionId]` effects aborted the ask (`fetch` → `net::ERR_ABORTED`), and its running state and answer lived in that entry (`useRef`/`useState`), so the new entry showed idle and nothing came back. The Host kept the chat in `running` (a Set, 409 "still running") until the model call ended: the browser did close dsh's request, but the desktop app's `dsh-app://` forwarder (Electron `protocol.handle`, 44.7.0) gives the handler a `Request` with no signal, so the page's cancel never reaches the Host there (inference from the Electron source and dsh desktop `forwardWebRequest`; not measured in the desktop app).
Fix: client `createImproveAsks()` (one store per plugin, per chat `{running?, outcome?}`, merged changes, one ask per chat checked by click, Esc and `start`, a cancelled ask's answer dropped, `stop()` when the switch goes off). The outcome waits for its own chat's composer; it is applied when the draft is still the clicked text (compared by text: dsh gives a new composer with new revisions) and dsh's published state has the editor's revision (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 first publishes an empty state). Host: `running` is a Map of AbortControllers: a newer ask for the chat stops the older call (409 "a newer improvement replaced this one" for the old request), like Complete while typing.
Checks: `test/prompt-improve-client.test.mjs` leave-and-return block (red on the old client.js: "leaving the chat does not cancel the ask"); `test/prompt-improve.test.mjs` newer-ask, leave-and-return and failure cases (red on the old index.js: "not locked: the newer ask runs"); `test/browser-prompt-improve.mjs` leave-and-return steps PASS twice on dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (the first version failed on 0.2.0-rc.2 with a false "message changed", fixed by the revision wait); `npm test` exit 0.
Not changed: Tether (its wand state is per phone view and the gateway already aborts the Mac call on cancel); README text (the documented behavior is unchanged).

## Worktree chats: start a chat on its own branch, see the branch (2026-10-27, branch `feat/session-worktree`)
Request (owner, chat 2026-10-27): 5–10 parallel developments per project without confusion and without Worktrunk (`wt`). (1) The New chat screen shows the branch and offers "start on the current branch", "start on a new branch" and the open worktrees; (2) the AI names the new branch from the first message; (3) the open chat's header shows its branch, changes and push state; (4) Tether shows the same; (5) the branch folders show inside their project in the sidebar, as a tree ("BOM tree"), not as separate projects; (6) shared ignored files (`.env`, `node_modules`) come into each worktree; (7) after this works, Promes moves from `wt` to Composer Plus and `wt` is removed.
Work happens in the worktree `../dsh-composer-plus-session-worktree` (branch `feat/session-worktree`, from e1cd30c). The main checkout has another chat's uncommitted five-features work (same files); merge after both are committed (conflicts expected in `FEATURES`, the test script, locales, `docs/features.json`, generated README tables, `tether/FEATURES.md`; reuse its `branchOf` then).
Evidence (read before planning, dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 / 0.2.1-rc.2 packages):
- `conversation.hero.options` (list, session scope) renders next to the project chip only on 0.2.1+, only with a session. 0.2.0-rc.2 has no hero slot: there only the header part works.
- Sidebar membership is `header.cwd` with exact realpath equality (`dsh-workspace` `attachSession`/`indexHeader`); a chat never moves to another project. So a branch chat must be a new session created in the worktree's own workspace, not a moved chat (`workingDirectory.set` would leave it under the main project).
- dsh's own "Workspace tree" view (`groupBy: 'workspace-tree'`, `owningParentFolder`) nests a registered workspace under the nearest registered parent folder. A worktree at `<repo>/.worktrees/<name>` therefore shows inside its project. The view is a dsh setting (localStorage `dsh.workspace.view.v5`, read once at start); a plugin cannot switch it live.
- `WorkspaceRegistry.create(path, title)` (Host, `workspaceRegistry`) is idempotent per real path and prepends new workspaces; `sessionController.create({ workspaceId, agentPreset? })`, `prompt`, `selectModel` exist on both versions.
- The composer send goes through `SessionInputShell.deps.defaultSink(text, attachmentIds, mode, signal)` (both versions; `conversation.input.for(actx)` returns the shell); `{kind:'error', text}` restores the draft and shows the text. Slash commands use `claim.submit`, not the sink.
- `git ls-files --others --ignored --exclude-standard --directory` lists ignored entries (folders collapsed). `--exclude-from=<include>` with `--directory` also lists parent folders, so a plain intersection could copy a whole ignored folder for one included file: matching must test each ignored entry against the include patterns.
- Copy speed: Promes frontend `node_modules` (16 861 entries) with `fs.promises.cp(..., { mode: COPYFILE_FICLONE, verbatimSymlinks: true })` 4.7 s, `cp -c -R` 2.85 s (APFS clones, no extra disk).
Decisions:
- One feature `worktreeChats` (browser switch, on by default: no port, the only model call is the branch name after the user sends with "New branch" chosen). It covers the New chat chip, the send hook and the header status.
- Worktree path `<main repo>/.worktrees/<branch with / → ->` (the Promes layout). When `.worktrees/` is not ignored, add it to `.git/info/exclude` (local, not committed).
- Copy = `.worktreeinclude` ∩ ignored entries, from the chat's repository top folder; no include file → nothing (the `--require-include` behavior). An entry is copied only when the entry itself matches (a folder is never copied for a file inside it: under-copy, never over-copy). Symlinks are copied as links (their target is never read); a FIFO/device is skipped; a source or target parent outside its tree is skipped; nothing is overwritten.
- Branch name: one model call (dsh default model, lowest effort, 10 s) for `<type>/<topic>` (`feat|fix|refactor|docs|chore|test`, lowercase ASCII words with `-`), validated with a regex and `git check-ref-format --branch`; fallback: a slug of the message (Turkish letters folded) `feat/<words>`, else `feat/chat-<time>`; a taken name gets `-2`, `-3`.
- New chat: workspace title = branch name, `sessionController.create({ workspaceId, agentPreset })` with the source chat's preset and model, then `prompt` (text only; a message with attachments is refused with a notice: send text first).
- Header status: `⑂ <branch> · N changes · ↑a ↓b` (upstream) and "not pushed" without upstream; refreshed on open, when the chat stops running and on window focus; a click opens Changes (when that feature is on).
- Tree look: the chip menu shows a one-line hint to choose dsh's View options → Workspace tree while another grouping is active (no hidden write to dsh's store).
- Invariant change: Composer Plus git was read-only (plus Turn rollback's own store and file restore). This feature writes: `git worktree add -b`, `.git/info/exclude`, copied ignored files, a workspace registration. It never removes, pushes, merges or deletes a branch or worktree.
Phases:
0. This record; baseline `npm test` in the worktree.
1. Host (index.js): `includeMatcher`, `ignoredEntries`, `copyIncluded`, `branchName`, `worktreeStatus`, `listWorktrees`, `startOnBranch`, `openWorktree`; routes `GET /api/composer-plus.worktrees?sessionId[&list=1]`, `POST /api/composer-plus.worktrees {action:'start', sessionId, text} | {action:'open', sessionId, path}` (`path` must be one of `git worktree list`). One start per repository at a time. Unit test `test/worktree-chats.test.mjs` (temp repos): no include → nothing; `.env.production` never; folder pattern; negation; collapsed folder not over-copied; symlink to outside copied as a link; FIFO skipped without a hang; dangling and inside symlinked folders; taken branch → `-2`; `.worktrees/` excluded when not ignored; start from inside a worktree → path under the main root; model name accepted / bad name → fallback; route 400/403/409.
2. Client (client.js): `BranchChip` (`conversation.hero.options`), sink wrapper (restored on mode change, feature off and after one send; guard: one start per chat), `BranchStatus` (`conversation.session.header.utilities`). `test/worktree-chats-client.test.mjs` (labels, wrapper install/restore, guard). Real isolated dsh `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` on 0.2.1-rc.2 (full flow, tree nesting, header) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (header only); `SHOT=1` → `assets/features/worktreeChats.webp`.
3. Texts and docs: en/zh, 11 `locales`, `docs/features.json` (how ×13, llms, shot, routes), `npm run build:locales`, `npm run build:readme`, limits/FAQ ×13, `SHOT_OF`/`SHOTS`, test script.
4. Tether: branch status in the chat title, "New branch" in the new chat sheet, `PLUS` `worktrees` GET/POST, `tether/FEATURES.md`, iOS unit tests (no install unless asked).
5. Promes: rule `docs/agent-rules/core-workflow.md` → Composer Plus flow, delete `.config/wt.toml` (keep `.worktreeinclude`); then `brew uninstall worktrunk` and `~/.config/worktrunk/` — each deletion and the commit only after the owner approves in chat.
6. `npm test`, Semgrep, `npm pack --dry-run`, review-guard subagent, checkpoint.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, locale/*.json (generated), assets/features/worktreeChats.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/{Sources,Tests}/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md; Promes phase 5 files only.
Risks: `deps.defaultSink` is dsh-internal (feature-detected: no sink → no "New branch" option); first send waits for naming + copy (about 2–8 s; the composer shows it is sending); "Workspace tree" also nests other registered projects under registered parent folders (dsh behavior); a worktree whose chat start fails stays on disk (never auto-removed).
Out of scope: removing/merging worktrees from the UI, push buttons, version bump, release, live desktop copy, iPhone install.
Checkpoint phases 0–4 and 6 (2026-10-27, uncommitted on `feat/session-worktree`):
- Host (index.js): `repoOf`, `branchStatus`, `listWorktrees`, `includeMatcher` (+ `reaches`, `excluded`), `copyIncluded`, `fallbackBranch`, `branchName`, `uniqueBranch`, `startOnBranch`, `openWorktree`, `installWorktrees` (route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.worktrees`). dsh's `prompt(request, signal)` needs a signal (`signal.throwIfAborted()` on both versions): the start passes a 15 s timeout signal, not the request's.
- Client (client.js): feature `worktreeChats` (on by default), `BranchChip` (`conversation.hero.options`), a wrapper on the chat's `deps.defaultSink` while "New branch" is chosen (one start per chat; restored on success, mode change, chip unmount, switch-off), `BranchStatus` (`conversation.session.header.utilities`), Workspace tree hint with dsh's own labels.
- Tether: `BranchState`/`BranchBar` under the title, `+` menu → New branch, `startOnBranch` (240 s wait), `PLUS` `worktrees` GET/POST, `tether/FEATURES.md` row.
- Docs: en/zh + 11 locales, `docs/features.json` (how ×13, llms, shot, route), limits/FAQ ×13, `docs/ROUTES.md`, `llms.txt`; `assets/features/worktreeChats.webp` 36 kB (SHOT=1, demo project, checked by eye).
- Review (subagent, read-only) found and fixed: P1 a committed `.worktrees` symlink put the checkout and the copied `.env` outside the repository (now 409 before git, plus a real-path check after `worktree add`); P2 `config/` + `!config/.env.production` copied the production file (now a `!` rule for a path inside a copied folder keeps it out, stricter than git); P2 Tether lost the draft on a second send while a branch was made (now an error, Send disabled while `branching`); P3 a gone upstream showed as in sync (now `upstream: null`), detached HEAD text differs on Tether (fixed), a failed `ls-files` failed the whole start (copy now best-effort), phone wait shorter than the Mac's steps (240 s), `fallbackBranch` simplified. Not done (P3, recorded): a branch named exactly `feat` blocks every `feat/*` name (git D/F conflict; clean error before any worktree); the lowest-effort model-call setup repeats in 5 places.
- Checks: `node test/worktree-chats.test.mjs` (incl. the 3 review regressions, each red with its fix removed), `node test/worktree-chats-client.test.mjs`, `npm test` exit 0, iOS 50/50, gateway 28/28, Semgrep p/javascript + p/secrets 0 findings, `git diff --check` clean, `npm pack --dry-run` 68 files (only the 3 gateway files from tether/). Real isolated dsh (`test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs`): PASS 0.2.1-rc.2 (New branch → AI name `feat/login-remember-me`, `.env` + `node_modules` copied, `.env.production` not, symlink kept, `.worktrees/` in info/exclude, main checkout clean, branch folder nested under its project in Workspace tree, header pill with 1 change) and PASS 0.2.0-rc.2 (header pill only; no hero slot).
- Open: phase 5 (Promes moves from `wt` to Composer Plus, `wt` removal) waits for the owner's approval; commit, merge with the main checkout's uncommitted five-features work, release and live copy are not done.

## Default workspace for the global New session (2026-10-28, branch `feat/default-workspace`)
Request (owner, chat 2026-10-28): the global "New session" button always opens in a default workspace set in Settings (the owner picks "casual"); a project's own "+" keeps its project; the user can still pick another project in the new chat. Owner decisions: with no choice, the general folder is dsh's own first-use folder `~/Documents/deepseek-harness/default-workspace`; Tether's "Quick chat" uses the same default.
Work happens in the worktree `../dsh-composer-plus-default-ws` (branch `feat/default-workspace`, from main 9f26d53). The main checkout has another chat's uncommitted `feat/goal-check` work (same files): merge after both are committed (conflicts expected in `FEATURES`, the test script, locales, `docs/features.json`, generated README tables, `tether/FEATURES.md`).
Evidence (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 packages, live 0.2.1-rc.3 app.asar):
- Sidebar "New session", the logo button, the collapsed rail button and ⌘N call `uiWorkspace.startSession()` with no workspace; dsh picks `workspaceId ?? open chat's project ?? most recently updated project` (`dsh-client-ui-workspace` `startSession`, same on the three versions). The Automation tasks page ("New", "New automation task") and the agent preset creator also call it with no workspace.
- A project's "+" and the add-workspace flow call `startSession(<id>)`; the New chat project chip calls `openWorkspace(id)` (not `startSession`).
- No plugin hook decides the target. dsh's only default folder is the first-use `initializeDefault` (`<Documents>/deepseek-harness/default-workspace`, only while there is no project and no chat).
- The shortcut gets the raw `UiWorkspaceService` object; `ctx.get('uiWorkspace')` is a Cordis proxy whose `[Symbol.for('cordis.original')]` is that object (cordis createTraceable, both versions).
- Host `workspaceRegistry`: `get(id)`, `list()`, `create(path, title)` (idempotent per real path), both versions. Client `workspaces.create({path})` resolves an existing path and merges the row at once.
Decisions:
- Feature `defaultWorkspace`, on by default (no port, no model call). The Host keeps the switch and the choice: `storages/dsh-composer-plus/default-workspace.json` `{on, workspaceId?}`.
- Routes: `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.default-workspace` (`{on}` switch, `{workspaceId|null}` choice; Settings only, not for the phone) and `POST /api/composer-plus.new-session-workspace` (prepares the target: the chosen project, else the general folder, made and registered on first use; 409 when off).
- Client: wraps `startSession` on the raw service object only for a call without a workspace; newest click wins; any failure falls back to dsh's own choice; switch-off restores the method (a later wrapper of another plugin stays).
- General folder: a registered project whose path ends in `deepseek-harness/default-workspace`, else `<home>/Documents/deepseek-harness/default-workspace` (ponytail: a moved Documents folder on Windows/Linux without dsh's own folder gets the home path).
Phases:
0. This record.
1. Host (index.js): store, `generalWorkspacePath`, `defaultWorkspaceTarget`, `ensureDefaultWorkspace`, routes, `SHOTS`. Unit test `test/default-workspace.test.mjs`.
2. Client (client.js): `FEATURES`, `SHOT_OF`, `createMobile.update`, `installDefaultStart`, Settings picker, en/zh texts. Unit test `test/default-workspace-client.test.mjs`; `test` script.
3. Tether: `PLUS` `new-session-workspace` POST, Quick chat, `tether/FEATURES.md`, gateway test, iOS unit tests.
4. Texts and docs: 11 `locales`, `npm run build:locales`, `docs/features.json` (how ×13, llms, shot, 2 routes), `npm run build:readme`, limits/FAQ ×13.
5. Screenshot `assets/features/defaultWorkspace.webp` and real isolated dsh check `test/browser-default-workspace.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
6. `npm test`, `git diff --check`, `npm pack --dry-run`, checkpoint.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, locale/*.json (generated), assets/features/defaultWorkspace.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/{Sources,Tests}/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: `startSession` is dsh-internal (no method → the feature does nothing); the first general-folder use creates a folder and a project.
Out of scope: dsh's start-up restore of the last chat, version bump, release, live desktop copy, iPhone install.
Checkpoint phases 0–6 (2026-10-28, uncommitted on `feat/default-workspace`):
- Host (index.js): `generalWorkspace`, `defaultWorkspaceTarget`, `ensureDefaultWorkspace`, `installDefaultWorkspace` (routes `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.default-workspace`, `POST /api/composer-plus.new-session-workspace`); an unknown project choice is 409 (not 404: the client reads 404 as an older Host). `SHOTS` + `defaultWorkspace`.
- Client (client.js): feature `defaultWorkspace` (on by default, Host switch), `createMobile.post`, `createDefaultWorkspace` (one choice at a time), `installDefaultStart` (newest click wins; a project's "+" also cancels a waiting global click; failure → dsh's choice), `DefaultWorkspacePicker` (general folder first, projects A–Z, safe `Intl.Collator` fallback), en/zh texts.
- Tether: `PLUS` `new-session-workspace` POST only; `createQuick` + `defaultWorkspaceId` (Quick chat), `tether/FEATURES.md` row.
- Docs: 11 locales (+ `build:locales`), `docs/features.json` (how ×13, llms, shot, 2 routes) → READMEs, `llms.txt`, `docs/ROUTES.md`; limits + FAQ ×13; `assets/features/defaultWorkspace.webp` 25 kB (SHOT=1, demo projects, checked by eye).
- Checks: `npm test` exit 0; `node test/default-workspace.test.mjs`, `node test/default-workspace-client.test.mjs` (mutations: without the newest-click guard, the "+" pass-through or the "+" race guard, the test fails); `node test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` pass; iOS unit tests 50/50 (incl. `testQuickChatUsesTheMacDefaultWorkspace`); `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 71 files (only the 3 gateway files from tether/). Real isolated dsh `test/browser-default-workspace.mjs`: PASS 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (general folder made under HOME/Documents and opened instead of the most recent project, "+" keeps its project, project button changes it, Settings choice opens from another project's new chat with the button and the shortcut, switch off gives dsh's choice back, no page errors).
- Review: the review subagent stopped without a result (no answer after 50 min); a manual review against the same list found and fixed a race (a project's "+" pressed while a global click waited could be replaced by the late default) and a needless warning for an older Host.
- Open: commit, merge with the main checkout's `feat/goal-check` work, release, live desktop copy, iPhone install (none asked yet).

## Goal check: test and independent verifier before a goal completes (2026-10-28, branch feat/goal-check)
Request (owner, plan approved in chat 2026-10-28): loop engineering (https://towardsdev.com/the-creator-of-claude-code-doesnt-prompt-it-anymore-bf2ac2e404d3): the agent must not approve its own work. When the model calls `update_goal` with `complete` in an automatic goal round, run the project's test command (detected; skipped when the agent's newest bash call already ran the same test with success and no file write came after it) and then one fresh-context verifier model call that reads the goal, the chat output since the goal started, `git status` and the test result. Either fails → `deny` with the reason; the goal stays active and the next round goes on. Owner choices: both checks; failure keeps the goal open; switch on by default.
Evidence: `tools/pre-execute` (allow/deny/cancel, `exec.agent`, `exec.arguments`, `exec.signal`), `goals.get/block`, `sandbox.confine` + `sandboxPolicy.resolve({session})`, `tokenUsage` projection: all in dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2. A completed goal cannot be resumed, so the check gates the call. Bash results end with `[exit code: N]`/`[timed out …]`/`[killed by signal: …]`/`[stopped: …]`; `meta` holds only `cwd` (real log).
Invariants: human-turn and `/goal`/goal-bar completions are never checked; other pre-execute gates run first (`next()` must allow); the test runs under the chat's sandbox mode (`SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE` → deny); the verifier has no tools and treats its input as data; project files read only through `head()` (rule 6); rolled-back messages left out (rule 9); fork offset (rule 10); one check per chat at a time; the route is not in Tether `PLUS`.
Phases: 0 branch + baseline; 1 Host + unit tests; 2 client + tests; 3 real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2/0.2.1-rc.2 browser test + shot; 4 texts/docs/Tether row; 5 npm test, Semgrep, pack, review.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/goalCheck.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: nightly goal start (dsh refuses `create_goal` from a schedule message), CI/PR hooks, version bump, release, live copy.
