## Edit queued messages with images or files (2026-10-28)
Request (owner): a queued message with an image or a file cannot be edited in place. Fix it so that such messages edit like text-only ones. Owner's choice (question in chat): edit the text, remove attachments and add new attachments.
Root cause (read in dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2): (1) dsh's Host `session/updateQueue {kind:'edit'}` throws `QUEUE_EDIT_NON_TEXT` ("queue edits accept text content only") when the new content has a non-text block, and dsh's QueueDock sends only `[{type:'text'}]`, so a save would drop the attachments; (2) QueueDock disables Edit when `textOf(content) === null` (title "Contains non-text content; editing is not supported yet"); (3) our `queueEditTarget` skips a disabled Edit on purpose (README Limits: "a message with images or files cannot be edited"). Tether's Edit alert sends text only through `updateQueue`, so a save there removes the attachments of the message (found while reading; not run).
Design:
- Host route `POST /api/composer-plus.queue-edit {sessionId, itemId, content}`: `content` parts in order: `{type:'text', text}`; `{type:'image'|'file', attachmentId}` = keep one attachment that the message has now (the Host uses its own block, never the client's); `{type:'image', mediaType, data, name?}` = new image (dsh's `attachments.admitPromptContent`, same limits; the chat's model must take images); `{type:'file', receiptId}` = new file uploaded through dsh's file upload for this chat (`fileUploads.resolve`, then `bindPrompt` to the message's rpcId). Only your own next-turn message; then `inbox.replace(itemId, …)` keeps the id, so the place in the queue stays. One queue change per chat at a time on the Host (shared with queue-move).
- Client (`queueEdit`): our own in-row editor (text, attachment chips with ✕, + to add, paste images), opened by a double-click or dsh's Edit button (also on rows with attachments). One lock per chat: an open editor, a save, a drag and a move exclude each other in every entry point (grip pointer, grip ↑/↓, Edit button, double-click, Save button, Enter). Older Host (404): text-only results go through dsh's `updateQueue` edit; with attachments a notice asks for a dsh restart.
- Tether: gateway `PLUS` + `queue-edit`; the Edit alert becomes a sheet with text, attachments (remove/add) through the route; without the route, rows with attachments have no Edit.
Allowed paths: client.js, index.js, locales/*.json, test/*, package.json (test script), docs/features.json (+ generated files), README*.md, llms.txt, assets/features/queueEdit.webp, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/ios/{Sources,Tests}/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Checks: `test/queue-edit.test.mjs` (Host route + client: attachments kept/removed/added, order, guards), `test/queue-drag.test.mjs` (lock), gateway test, `npm test`, real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-queue-edit.mjs`: the model gets the edited content with the image and the file), iOS unit tests.
Checkpoint (2026-10-28, done; not committed):
- Host (index.js): `installQueueEdit` (route `POST /api/composer-plus.queue-edit {sessionId, itemId, content, seen?}`); `seenOf`; kept attachments = the message's own blocks (a keep of an id that the message does not have, or of the wrong type, is a 409); new image through `attachments.admitPromptContent` after the model check (`sessionController.agents.selectionFor` + `llm.resolveModelInfo`, as dsh's `prompt`), with dsh's per-message image count including kept images; new file = `fileUploads.resolve` receipt, bound to the message's rpcId (`bindPrompt` + commit), so dsh retires it when the message leaves; `seen` is checked before and again after the image admission; `inbox.replace` with a frozen clone keeps the id. One queue change per chat on the Host: the `busy` set is shared with queue-move.
- Client (client.js): shared chat lock `queueLocks` (`lockQueue`/`unlockQueue`); QueueReorder: grip pointer checks it and holds it during the drag, grip ↑/↓ takes it, the text-rotation fallback takes it again after `settle`, grips are off (title "A queued message is open for editing") while an editor holds it. QueueEdit: the plugin's Edit button replaces dsh's (dsh's stays hidden, `data-composer-plus-native-edit`), double-click, an editor portaled into the row (textarea, chips with ✕, paperclip + paste through dsh's `createDrafts`, Save waits for file uploads, `serializeDraftAttachments`), Enter/✓ save once, Esc/✕ cancel, the editor closes when the message leaves the queue, an older Host (404) uses dsh's text-only edit and asks for a restart for attachments. 13 new texts (en, zh, 11 locales).
- Tether (subagent): `QueueEditSheet` (text, thumbnails/file chips with remove, Add photo or file), `queueEditContent`/`queueEditSeen`, `editingQueued` guard in Edit, Move up/down, Send now, Remove, `moveQueued`, `saveQueued`; gateway `PLUS` + `queue-edit`; FEATURES.md and tether/README.md.
- Checks: `test/queue-edit.test.mjs` (Host + client; red with each of 6 guards removed: grip pointer, grip key, second save, own-block check, seen check, shared Host busy); `test/queue-drag.test.mjs`; `npm test` exit 0; `test/browser-queue-edit.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (Edit on an image+file row, grips off and a drag/key does nothing, remove image + add PNG + add file + new text, Enter keeps the place, double-click + Esc, the model request has the new text, one `data:image/png`, spec.txt and notes.md, not the old text); regression `test/browser-six-features.mjs` and `IMAGE=1 test/browser-queue-drag.mjs` PASS on both; iOS 52/52; Semgrep p/javascript + p/secrets 0 findings (client.js with `--timeout 600`); `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 72 files. queueEdit.webp 12.5 kB (demo, checked by eye).

## Resume after restart keeps the queue (2026-10-28)
Objective: after a quit, Resume after restart continues the chat and the user's queued messages stay: none lost, same order, same text, images and files, also after a drag (Queue drag-and-drop) and an in-place edit; the continued turn does not take the queue head (issue #3).
Cause (dsh source, same in dsh-v0.2.0-rc.2 and dsh-v0.2.1-rc.2; real log): at a quit dsh disposes each agent with `machine.cancel({ kind: "disposed" })` without `keepInbox` (dsh-agent-loop `prepare` teardown), so `inbox.clear()` logs one `agent/inbox/spliced` with `outcome: "canceled"` and no `inserted`, per list, inside the stopped turn. After the restart the queue was empty: 0 of 4 messages ran (red on HEAD 3d252a2, both versions, `test/browser-resume-queue.mjs`). The issue #3 part (continued turn takes the queue head) was already right.
Fix: `wakeSession` folds the inbox splices of the chat's log (`droppedInbox`) and puts the user's own messages that this clear dropped back with one `inbox.splice` per list (same ids and content, so order, edits, images and files stay), then wakes as before. Only a clear inside the stopped turn with no later splice, `step/start` or `user/message`; a message already in the chat never comes back. A goal chat with restored messages gets the continue step too (dsh's goal driver starts no round while user messages wait). No new switch, route, text or screenshot (a bug fix in `resumeAfterRestart`).
Limit (README + 12 translations, Limits and Troubleshooting): dsh logs a user's Remove of the last queued messages the same way; if dsh quits before the next step starts, they come back. Chats that do not continue (switch off, stopped by you, done) keep dsh's empty queue.
Checks (2026-10-28): `test/resume-restart.test.mjs` new block red on HEAD ("the queue is back…"), green now; `npm test` exit 0; real isolated dsh with a fake model: `test/browser-resume-queue.mjs` red on HEAD (0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2: queue empty after the start), PASS now on both (continued request = note only; the four rows back in the dragged order with the edit, image and file; then four requests in that order, the image and file with their message); `test/browser-resume-restart.mjs` and `test/browser-continue-queue.mjs` PASS on both.
Not done: version bump, commit, push, release (owner's call).

## Fix: question card fits the window (2026-10-28, branch `fix/question-dialog-window-size`)
- Owner report: the ask_user_question card does not follow the window; only the answers scroll; the question can go out of view. Owner's dsh window: 1280 × 820 (System Events, desktop app 0.2.1-rc.3; Electron `hiddenInset`, so the page is 1280 × 820 too), dragged chat width 642.5 px.
- Cause (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.3, same CSS): the card is `max-height: min(60vh, 520px)` with `overflow: hidden`; the header (question) is `flex-shrink: 0`; only `[data-question-scroll]` (answers) scrolls. At 1280 × 820 the cap is 492 px; a 1,400-character question (462 px) leaves the answers a 0 px box and Skip/Submit outside the card. Upstream already knows it (Discussions #8203, #3650; GitHub Issues are disabled in deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness).
- Change: feature `questionFit` (on by default, browser switch). One `<style data-composer-plus-question-fit>` in `<head>`: the card scrolls as one box (question + answers), `max-height: max(200px, calc(100vh - 200px))`, the footer is sticky at the bottom. Only dsh's markup (`[data-question-key] > section`, `[data-question-scroll]`, `footer`), no generated class names; the plan review card is not changed. Off removes the style. Tether: `no` (its question sheet already scrolls).
- Checks: `test/question-fit.test.mjs` (style on by default, off/on/unload, no double style, no page = nothing; red with the feature line removed and with a no-op disposer); `test/toggles.test.mjs` 37 rows / 33 on; `npm test` exit 0. Real isolated dsh `test/browser-question-fit.mjs` (fake model, 1280 × 820, owner's chat width): switch off = question end, 3 answers, Skip and Submit not reachable; switch on = all reachable, Skip/Submit in view, card 400/620/800 px at 600/820/1000 px high, inside the chat area; off gives dsh's 492 px cap back; the answer reaches the model. PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.3. `test/browser-settings-shots.mjs` PASS (37 rows). `questionFit.webp` 57 kB (SHOT=1, demo, checked by eye).
- Not done: live desktop copy and release (not asked).

## Branches in the workspace tree (2026-10-28, branch `feat/auto-sync-branch-workspace-tree`)
Request (owner, chat 2026-10-28): (1) when the agent of a chat on the project's main folder creates a git branch and changed nothing on main, the branch shows in the Workspaces tree and the chat moves into it; (2) a deleted branch leaves the tree, its chats go to Ungrouped (dsh's own project delete); (3) the tree adds existing branches and drops deleted ones by itself.
Owner decisions (questions in chat): a branch is its own worktree folder `<root>/.worktrees/<branch, / → ->` (a dsh project that Workspace tree nests inside the project), not a subgroup in one folder; "changed nothing" = `git status` clean (staged, unstaged, untracked; `.worktrees/` left out) when the branch appears; existing branches are added only when they have a worktree (nothing is written to disk for them).
Evidence (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 packages):
- A chat belongs to the project whose real path equals its header `cwd`; the header cannot change and `attachSession` refuses another path (dsh-workspace entity). So a moved chat is a copy: `agents.create({ seed: events + session/end-seed{inherited}, inheritedEventCount, meta: { cwd: <worktree>, parentSession, isSeeded, agentPreset } })` + `workspace.attachSession`, the same steps as dsh's own `session.fork` (dsh-api-session-controller), only with the new cwd. 0.2.1 replays `working-directory/change` from the copied log: `workingDirectory.set(child, <worktree cwd>)` moves it and queues dsh's own notice; 0.2.0 tools use the header cwd.
- `workspaceRegistry.delete(id)` keeps the folder and the logs; dsh then shows the project's chats under Ungrouped ("delete.desc"). `create(path, title)` is idempotent per real path. The client follows the registry through dsh's workspace stream (no client code for the tree).
- `agent/pre-step` `{kind:'reject'}` ends the turn as `blocked` with the step's tool results recorded (dsh's archived gate and goal driver use it); the claimed next-step messages are dropped, so the hook takes the user's ones along. `tools/post-execute` runs after each tool call with `exec.agent`.
Decisions:
- One new feature `branchTree` (not part of `worktreeChats` or `sessionBranch`): it acts without a click (git writes, a copied chat that continues its turn), so it is OFF by default and the Host keeps its switch (`GET/POST {on}`), like Resume after restart. It reuses `repoOf`, `worktreeEntries`, `excludeWorktrees`, `copyIncluded`, `wakeWithContinue`.
- Detect: at `turn/start` of a top-level chat in a repository's main working tree on a branch, remember the branch and the local branches; after each tool call of that chat, a branch that is new and checked out = created by this chat. Clean status → `git switch <old>` in the main tree, `git worktree add <path> <new>`, copy `.worktreeinclude` files; further tool calls of the step are denied; the next pre-step rejects (turn ends), the queue goes along; when idle: copy the chat into the worktree, archive the old chat, continue the copy with a hidden note. Not clean, a detached start, a linked worktree, a subagent, a taken folder: nothing moves.
- Sync (at start, every 30 s, after each top-level turn end, on window focus through the client, at switch-on): for each registered project that is a main working tree, add each linked worktree inside its folder that has a branch (title = branch); remove a registered project under `<root>/.worktrees/` (or one this feature added) that `git worktree list` no longer names. One sweep at a time; a failing project does not stop the others.
- Client: Settings row (Host switch, status line), focus → `POST {sync:true}`, and when the open chat is archived by a move, open its copy.
- Tether: `no` (the Mac does it; the moved chat and the branch projects show as dsh projects).
Phases: 0 this record; 1 Host + `test/branch-tree.test.mjs`; 2 client + texts + docs; 3 real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-branch-tree.mjs`, SHOT=1); 4 `npm test`, `git diff --check`, `npm pack --dry-run`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/branchTree.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the copy uses dsh-internal `sessionController.agents.composeAgent` (feature-detected: without it nothing moves); two chats in one main folder that create branches at the same moment (one per repository at a time; the chat whose tool call ends first moves); a goal of the old chat is not armed in the copy.
Out of scope: commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy.
Checkpoint (2026-10-28, phases 0–4 done, uncommitted):
- Host (index.js): `syncBranchTree` (one `git worktree list` per registered main folder; adds `.worktrees/<x>` worktrees with a branch, title = branch; removes a project in `.worktrees/` that git no longer lists or whose folder is gone), `branchToWorktree` (clean `git status` without `.worktrees`, main folder on the new branch → `git switch <from>`, `git worktree add`, `.worktreeinclude` copy; a failed add switches back), `copyChatTo` (dsh's fork steps with the new cwd; dsh owns the copy via `resolveAgent`; 0.2.1 `workingDirectory.set`), `installBranchTree` (Host switch `branch-tree.json` 0600, off by default; turn-start read; `tools/post-execute` detects the checked-out new branch; `tools/pre-execute` denies further calls; `agent/pre-step` reject keeps user steering; idle → copy, archive with stopActivity, hidden note + `wakeWithContinue`; sync at ready, every 30 s, each turn end, route `{sync:true}`; `?moved=` for the browser). Decision change: only a branch that the chat checked out moves (a plain `git branch` does not).
- Client: `branchTree` in FEATURES + OFF_BY_DEFAULT + SHOT_OF, Host switch in `createToggles`, `watchBranchTree` (focus → sync at most every 5 s; the open chat archived → asks `?moved=` and opens the copy; reads dsh's `aria-selected` row), Settings status line. Texts en/zh + 11 locales (subagent).
- Docs: docs/features.json (how ×13, llms, route), `npm run build:readme`, README limits + FAQ ×13, dev command ×13, tether/FEATURES.md `no`.
- Checks: `test/branch-tree.test.mjs` (sync incl. paths below a symlinked and a dangling folder, detached/outside worktrees; move, refusals; hooks incl. a chat in a symlinked main folder and one below a dangling symlink; route), `test/branch-tree-client.test.mjs`; mutations (clean guard, pre-execute deny, open-chat guard) each red. `test/continue-host.test.mjs` fake `ctx.on` now chains waterfall listeners like Cordis (it kept only the last `agent/pre-step`). `npm test` exit 0. Real isolated dsh `test/browser-branch-tree.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (regression). `branchTree.webp` 30.5 kB (demo project, checked by eye). `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 73 files.
- Found by the real run: dsh's `bash` tool needs `description` (the fake model now sends it); a test turn-start read must end before the tool call (in dsh the model call is between them).
- Not done: commit, release, live desktop copy; a review subagent.

## Backup, restore and rescue (replaces dsh-backup) (2026-10-28, branch `feat/backup-restore-recovery`)
Request (owner): do what `@xiaoyuyu6420/dsh-backup` 0.13.4 does inside Composer Plus, so that plugin can go. Owner decisions (chat, "önerilerine göre"): 1A rescue console = terminal menu (no port); 2A full restore only in the console while dsh is closed (Settings shows a preview and how to start the console); 3B credential files, `*.pem`/`*.key`/`*.p12` and secret values in the yml config files (`token`, `secret`, `password`, `apiKey`, … key names) are masked, the real copies stay in a local vault; 4A same folder `~/Desktop/dsh-backups` and the same archive format (`dsh-<stamp>.tar.gz` + `.sha256`, `.meta.json`, `.redacted.json`); 5 `cache/`, `dsh-runtimes/` and `node_modules` are not archived; no GitHub sync, typed backups, migrate-check, `/backup` command or model tool.
Evidence (2026-10-28, read-only): the live archive (1.1 GB) cannot be restored by dsh-backup: its rescue refuses files over 256 MB ("超过哈希上限") and its archive check refuses the 20 symlinks in `skills/` (they point to `~/.codex`); masking covered only `.credentials.yaml` (6 token lines in each profile patch file and the TLS keys stayed in the archive). Node's zstd decoder reads one frame only, so a session log is checked frame by frame (dsh's own `scanZstdFrames` rules: a torn last frame is not damage; the first frame is exactly one header line). `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 "/Applications/DeepSeek Harness.app/Contents/MacOS/DeepSeek Harness"` runs Node 24.21 with zstd (RunAsNode fuse on).
Decisions:
- One self-contained file `backup.mjs` (Node stdlib only): tar writer and reader (ustar + pax; no system `tar`, no `tar -tv` text parsing), gzip, sha256 in the same pass, masking, per-archive vault `vault/<archive>/`, session log check and repair, restore, and the terminal console (`main()` when run directly). The Host imports it; each backup copies it to the backup folder as `composer-plus-rescue.mjs` with a double-click launcher.
- Archive safety: only paths under one top folder, no `..`, absolute paths, NUL or devices/FIFOs; a symlink is restored as a link (its target is never followed: links are made after every file), and an entry below a symlink entry refuses the archive. Files are written with `O_EXCL | O_NOFOLLOW` into a fresh folder next to the dsh home, then the two folders are swapped (the old one stays as `<home>.pre-restore-<stamp>`; `cache/` and `dsh-runtimes/` move back from it).
- Reading the dsh home for a backup: `lstat` walk, files opened with `O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK` and `fstat` must say regular file; FIFOs, sockets and devices are skipped. A file that grows while it is read is cut at its size at open (a session log then has a torn tail, which dsh repairs).
- One operation at a time: an in-process busy state plus a lock file in the backup folder (pid, stale when the process is gone), checked by the Host routes and the console; a second action gets 409. The client store blocks a new action while one runs.
- Features: `backup` (browser switch, on: Settings panel; nothing runs without a click), `autoBackup` (Host switch `backup.json`, off by default: every copy is about 1 GB; interval, keep count, pre-upgrade snapshot when the dsh version changes and before Composer Plus installs an update), `sessionDoctor` (browser switch, on: check; repair only for chats that are not open in this dsh, from the newest archive that has a healthy copy), `rescueConsole` (Host switch `rescue-console.json`, on: writes the console files with each backup; off removes them).
Phases: 0 record + baseline; 1 `backup.mjs` + `test/backup.test.mjs`; 2 Host routes + `test/backup-host.test.mjs`; 3 client + `test/backup-client.test.mjs`; 4 texts, docs, Tether rows, screenshots, real dsh; 5 `npm test`, Semgrep, pack, review.
Allowed paths: backup.mjs, index.js, client.js, package.json (files, test script), locales/*.json, assets/features/{backup,autoBackup,sessionDoctor,rescueConsole}.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, test/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: commit, release, live desktop copy, removing dsh-backup from the desktop profile.
Checkpoint phase 0 (2026-10-28): baseline `npm test` exit 0 (36 features, 42 route methods).
Checkpoint phases 1–3 (2026-10-28):
- `backup.mjs` (one file, Node stdlib only; also the rescue console). Vault folder renamed to `composer-plus-vault/` (dsh-backup empties its own `vault/` at each of its backups); dsh-backup archives read their secrets from its `vault/` mirror. AppleDouble `._` entries of macOS tar archives are skipped (the live dsh-backup archive had them; without the skip it read as "more than one top folder"). A carried folder (`node_modules`) is moved only below real folders of the restored home.
- Measured on a private temp copy target (deleted after): the owner's real dsh home → 28,386 files, 1.6 GB read, 834 MB archive in 30 s, event loop stall ≤ 1 ms, 34 masked files (credentials, TLS/mobile keys, every cordis.patch.yml and its .bak copies), check 3 s. The live dsh-backup archive (1.1 GB) checks OK in 5 s with this reader (dsh-backup's own console refused it: 256 MB hash limit and the 20 `skills/` symlinks).
- Host `installBackup` (3 routes), client store + panels, en/zh texts; 11 locales by a subagent (`npm run build:locales`, locale test 11 × 3423).
- Tests: `test/backup.test.mjs` (masking, archive vs system tar, symlink/FIFO/hard link, check refusals, restore incl. dsh-backup archive, lock, session logs, console), `test/backup-host.test.mjs` (routes, one action at a time incl. the console lock, cancel, transfer, repair with an open chat, switches, automatic backup with a version snapshot and a failure), `test/backup-client.test.mjs` (rows and defaults, store guards, panels). Mutations, each red: below-symlink check, whole-file masking, lock refusal, `..` refusal, seq check, running-dsh refusal, FIFO guards (red in 3 s, no hang), store busy guard, newest-answer rule, carry below a symlink. Semgrep p/javascript + p/secrets: 0 findings on the 6 new/changed JS files.
- Open: README how/sections in 12 languages (subagents), `test/browser-backup.mjs` written but not run (Auto review refused a real-dsh browser run without the owner's chat approval), so the 4 screenshots are missing and `npm test` (shots, docs) is red until they exist.
Review (subagent, read-only, 2026-10-28): 1 P1, 2 P2, 6 P3; all fixed, each P1/P2 with a red → green check:
- P1 two dsh processes on one home shared `host.json`: the one that quit removed it, so the console allowed a restore under the other. Now one marker per process, `storages/dsh-composer-plus/running/host-<pid>.json`; the console refuses while any listed pid lives. `test/backup-host.test.mjs` red with a shared marker name.
- P2 a click on Back up now rotated (keep 7) and also deleted dsh-backup's archives. Now a click deletes nothing; an automatic backup keeps the newest N of Composer Plus's own archives (`meta.by`); dsh-backup archives are never deleted. Test: a foreign archive stays; a call without `keep` removes nothing.
- P2 the session check called a log damaged that dsh still opens. dsh (SessionLogScanner, recovery "recoverable") drops the rows from the first bad row and fails only when a later `turn/end` follows. checkLog now follows that rule; a repair can no longer replace such a log with an older copy. Red with the old strict rule.
- P3: session logs are read whole before their tar header (no zero-filled zstd tail); a rotation failure keeps the new archive; Show folder errors are 422 (409 means busy); the `kind` body option is gone; the launcher skips a Node older than 18 and `statfs` is optional; the masking limits in README name JSON files, lists and flow maps. Repair also replaces a log only below real folders (no symlink on the way; test with a symlinked chat folder, red without it).
- ko/vi: `feature.sessionDoctor` had the same text as `doctor.check`; the feature label now differs.
Checkpoint (2026-10-28, 19:50): done except the screenshots.
- Docs: `docs/features.json` (4 features, `how` in 13 languages by a subagent, `llms`, 3 routes), `npm run build:readme` (13 READMEs, llms.txt, ROUTES.md: 48 route methods); hand-written Privacy, Troubleshooting, Limits and FAQ in README.md and the 12 translations (subagents); `tether/FEATURES.md` 4 rows (`no`, the work runs on the Mac).
- Checks: every `npm test` step passes except `test/shots.test.mjs` (and `test/readme.test.mjs` behind it): the 4 images `assets/features/{backup,autoBackup,sessionDoctor,rescueConsole}.webp` do not exist yet. Semgrep 0 findings; `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 73 files incl. `backup.mjs` (60.5 kB).
- Blocked (needs the owner's chat approval): `test/browser-backup.mjs` starts a throwaway dsh (temp DSH_HOME, temp backup folder, made-up data) and headless Chrome like the other `test/browser-*.mjs` checks; Auto review refused to run it without approval. `SHOT=1` with it writes the 4 images; then `npm test` passes. Run: `PLAYWRIGHT=~/.dsh/local/playwright-mcp/node_modules/playwright SHOT=1 node test/browser-backup.mjs ~/Documents/testdeepseekharness/work/dsh-0.2.1-rc.2/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/dsh/lib/bin.js ~/Documents/testdeepseekharness/work/dsh-0.2.0-rc.2/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/dsh/lib/bin.js`.

## Changes panel: Tree as a branch map, opens on Tree (2026-10-28)
Request (owner, with a sketch "GitHub Branch Haritası"): make the Changes panel's Tree look like the sketch, open details on a click, and open the panel on Tree. Owner answers: a box opens the branch's commits (then a commit's files and diffs); Merged opens the merge commit's files, Waiting the files that the branch brings into its target; the count cards filter the map; remote branches only without a local branch of the same name; target rule: main = origin's default branch, else main/master, else the open branch; a merged branch points to the branch that took it first, a waiting one to the nearest branch that it started from; green = in main, orange = not yet, blue = main.
Design:
- Host: `branchMap` (pure) + `branchMapOf` (one `git log --branches --remotes HEAD --topo-order -z --format=%H%x1f%P`, at most 20,000 commits, 60 branches; `partial` when longer) on `GET /api/composer-plus.graph?sessionId`. New `pending` mode for the changes/diff routes (`ref` + `target`, both `isBranchRef`, resolved with `show-ref --verify --hash`, then `git diff <t>^{commit}...<r>^{commit}`).
- Client: `BranchMap` (cards, boxes in rows by level, SVG arrows from measured boxes, pills, legend), levels `merge` / `pending` in `ChangesTab`, mode starts at `tree`, Tree first in the menu. 14 new texts (`changes.map.*`), 3 removed (`changes.tree.local/remote/branches`).
- Docs: `how` (13 languages), `llms`, routes, a Limits bullet (13 READMEs). Tether: map screen (subagent).
Checks so far: `node test/changes.test.mjs` (map rules on a real repo as the sketch, fast-forward, same tip, origin default, cut list keeps main, old tip; pending files/diff/gone/bad refs; routes 400/404), `node test/changes-tree.test.mjs` (opens on the map, cards filter, rows, colors, pills, Merged → commit mode, Waiting → pending mode + diff URL, gone, partial note, back marks, per chat), `npm test` exit 0, `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs` PASS on dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (6 boxes, counts, 3 colors, rows, 5 arrows with dashes, arrow ends at the target box, filter, commits, diff, Merged and Waiting files, low page in view, deleted branch, menu, git state unchanged, switch off/on). `assets/features/changesPanel.webp` 41.6 kB (SHOT=1, 0.2.1-rc.2, demo project `shop`).
Review (subagent, read-only, 2026-10-28): 3 P2 + 6 P3, all fixed with a test each: `--` after the revisions of the map's `git log` (a file `HEAD` or a folder `head/` broke the map); waiting tie (two features from an unmoved develop pointed to each other) and integration branches (main, a branch that took one) never start from a branch that took none; rows wrap (min 120 px a box); the arrow redraws when a state changes; a merged branch keeps the branch that merged it when another branch started from that merge (a branch with a merge commit wins the tie); at the 20,000-commit edge an unread first parent ends the line (the merge stays a merge); screen readers hear the target and the state; a card with 0 branches is disabled. Checked clean: git boundary (`isBranchRef` + `show-ref --verify --hash`, only SHA_RE ids reach git), cost (12–56 ms for 20,000 commits × 60 branches), octopus, criss-cross, detached HEAD, one branch, no cycles.
Tether (subagent): Changes opens on the Branch map when the gateway has `graph` (Back shows the modes); cards, colored boxes in tree order indented by level, arrow + ✅ Merged / ⏳ Waiting pill, legend, partial note; box → commits, Merged → merge commit's files, Waiting → new PendingView → DiffView with `pending`. iOS unit tests 54/54 (iPhone 17 Pro simulator). `tether/FEATURES.md` row updated. Not installed on the iPhone.
Last checkpoint: `npm test` exit 0; `test/browser-changes-tree.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 after the fixes; `changesPanel.webp` 41.7 kB; `git diff --check` clean. Not committed. Squash and rebase merges show as Waiting (git keeps no link): written in the Limits bullet of the 13 READMEs.
Checkpoint (2026-10-28, merge of origin/main): the branch work is committed and pushed (0111e48), then origin/main (backup, branchTree, questionFit, worktree fixes) is merged. Conflicts: LANGUAGE_PACKS rebuilt (`npm run build:locales`), this plan keeps the sections of both sides; version 1.15.0 (main). `npm run build:readme` up to date; `npm test` exit 0 (42 features, 51 route methods); `git diff --check` clean.

## Open waiting chat when idle (2026-10-28, branch feat/idle-switch-pending-question)
Request (owner): when a chat waits (the orange dot) and the owner does nothing, dsh opens that chat by itself; it must never interrupt work. Owner's answers: 30 s idle, the longest-waiting chat first, on by default, also while dsh is in the background.
Evidence (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 source): the orange dot is `StateDot` `warning` (`--dsw-alias-state-warn-primary`) from `sessionStatuses()` in dsh-client-ui-workspace, set by `uiSession.sessionStatus` `pendingInteraction.kind` ∈ approval / plan-review / question (`visiblePendingKind`), top-level rows only. The open chat is `sessions.list` `byId[id].retainedBy.mainView > 0`; the list publishes when it changes. `uiWorkspace.openSession(id)` opens a chat.
Design (client.js `watchIdleSwitch`, feature `idleSwitch`, on by default): window capture listeners (keydown, pointerdown, pointermove, wheel, touchstart, input, focus) set `lastActive`; at the switch time `busyNow()` also blocks on text in the focused field, a text selection, `[aria-modal="true"]`, `dialog[open]` or `[role="menu"]`. No `scroll` listener (dsh scrolls a streaming reply itself). A request must wait ≥ ATTENTION_GRACE_MS (1.5 s), so auto-answered approvals never open. Nothing opens while a waiting chat is on screen; a chat left unanswered can open again after the next 30 s idle. Subagent requests and `answerable === false` are ignored. Switch off removes the listeners, both subscriptions and the timer.
Checks: `test/idle-switch.test.mjs` (fake clock; 30 s, activity restarts, draft/selection/menu block, longest first, on-screen stays, leave/answer → next, grace, subagent, off/on teardown); 5 mutations of the logic each fail it. `test/browser-idle-switch.mjs` PASS on dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (real `ask_user_question` ×2 + a third open chat: typing/mouse/wheel 40 s keep it, a draft blocks 32 s, first question opens 30.4 s idle, stays 35 s, after Submit the second opens 30.4 s later; switch off: nothing in 40 s; no page errors). `idleSwitch.webp` 17 kB (demo chats, checked visually). `npm test` exit 0 (37 features). Two runs of `npm test` failed once each in `test/unread.test.mjs` and `test/complete.test.mjs` while another worktree ran a real-dsh test (load average 8); both pass alone 3/3 and the third full run passed: timing flakes, not this change.
Review: the read-only reviewer subagent gave no result in 30 min and was stopped (as in earlier rounds). Self-review of the final diff: the 1st real-app run showed that a once-only rule (`done`) left a question unopened after the user left its chat; replaced by "nothing opens while a waiting chat is on screen". ## Project memory, like DS Bot's memory (2026-10-28, branch feat/persistent-memory-feature)
Request (owner): study the memory of DS Bot (github.com/Fei2411/DS-Bot, `ds-bot/src/host/memory.js`) and the DS Bot app installed on this Mac (read only), then add a similar memory to Composer Plus. Memory files never go into the project folder, and never into DS Bot's folder. Owner choices (asked 2026-10-28): location `$DSH_HOME/storages/dsh-composer-plus/memory/`; "shared folder" means only DS Bot's folder, so a memory per project plus one global memory that every project reads is allowed.
Evidence (DS Bot 0.2.3 repo = installed `/Applications/DeepSeek Bot.app` app.asar, `memory.js` and `memory-pending.js` byte-identical; data `~/.ds-bot/bot/memory/`, read only):
- Format: Agent Memory Repo Markdown. `MEMORY.md` (`# Memory`, one `- entry [source: …; by: …; added: YYYY-MM-DD]` per line, `## Index` with `[[topic]]` links) and topic files `<topic>.md`. Limits: entry 300, MEMORY.md 4,000, topic 16,000, 40 topics.
- Scopes: `bots/<id>/`, `groups/<id>/`, `team/`. `journal.jsonl` (every change with the line before/after), `pending.json` (crash recovery), `snapshots/<session>.md` (frozen prompt section), `summaries/`, `reviewed.json` (review cursor per chat), `archive/`.
- Write: tools `remember` (add / replace), `forget`, `recall` (list, search, read a topic); refuse keys, passwords, ID numbers; one write at a time.
- When: after each turn (agent idle + 2 s), a "memory review" model call reads the new lines since the cursor (user lines kept longest) with the numbered entries and answers `{"changes":[add|edit|remove]}` (at most 8); the cursor moves only after a good answer.
- Read back: system prompt section `bot-memory` (order 645, `interpolate: false`) with the rules and both MEMORY.md files, frozen per Session on first render (prefix cache); `recall` finds later entries.
Design (Composer Plus, Host half):
- Scopes: `projects/<sha256(real path of the chat's first folder)[:32]>/` (with `project.json` {path}) and `global/`. Session id → scope only through dsh's own session header (`cwd`), never a path from the browser or the model.
- Same file format, limits, secret check, tools (`remember`/`forget`/`recall`, scope `project` default or `global`), `journal.jsonl`, `reviewed.json`, frozen section per session in memory (no snapshot files). No pending.json: one change writes one file (topic link written in the same MEMORY.md write; a new topic file is written first, then MEMORY.md). ponytail: crash between a topic write and its MEMORY.md link leaves an unlinked topic, which recall still lists.
- Review after each finished root turn, with the chat's own model; reads `ownEvents` after `inheritedEventCount` (rule 10) and leaves out rollback/dsh-rewind withdrawn spans (rule 9; /compact kept). No project file is read (rule 6 n/a).
- OFF_BY_DEFAULT (`projectMemory`): it starts model calls without a click. Host switch `GET/POST {on}` `/api/composer-plus.memory` + `?sessionId` view + `{sessionId, forget}`; client `createMobile`, `createToggles`, Settings MobileStatus. Off: no tools, no prompt section, no review.
- Settings row shows the switch; a right-sidebar page is out of scope (the owner asked for the memory structure). Tether: `no` (memory works on the Mac for phone chats too).
Phases: 1 Host + unit test `test/project-memory.test.mjs`; 2 client + toggles test; 3 locales ×11, features.json, READMEs, tether/FEATURES.md, screenshot; 4 npm test + real isolated dsh check.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, locale/*.json, assets/features/projectMemory.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: memory panel UI with model summary, group/team roles, archive, version bump, release, live copy.
Checkpoint (2026-10-28): Host `installMemory` + `memoryStore`, `memorySection`, `memoryLines`, `memoryReviewRequest`, `parseMemoryReview`, `looksSecret`, `entryProblem` (index.js); route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.memory` (`{on}` only; switch changes run in order). Tools and the prompt section (`composer-plus:memory`, order 650, `interpolate: false`) go through `ctx.inject(['tools','systemPrompt'])`, so switching off disposes them. Memory files are opened with O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK (symlink and FIFO tested). Client: `projectMemory` in FEATURES, OFF_BY_DEFAULT, SHOT_OF, `createMobile(MEMORY_ROUTE)` in `createToggles`, Settings MobileStatus, en/zh + 11 locales. Docs: features.json (how ×13, llms, route), READMEs generated, tether/FEATURES.md `no`.
Checks: `test/project-memory.test.mjs` passes; 6 guards removed one by one → red (fork offset, rollback filter, nofollow/nonblock, secret refusal, frozen section, switch-off removes tools). Real isolated dsh `test/browser-project-memory.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (off by default: no section, tools or review; on: section + 3 tools, memory_remember and the review write `storages/dsh-composer-plus/memory/projects/<id>/MEMORY.md`, project folder unchanged; a new chat reads both entries; off again: nothing; no page errors). The test sets HOME and switches Default workspace off in its temp home, so dsh's general folder of the real HOME is not used. `assets/features/projectMemory.webp` 9 kB (SHOT=1, chat with the memory_remember step).
Final checks (2026-10-28): `npm test` exit 0 (37 features, 37 shots, 44 route methods, 13 READMEs). `content-search.test.mjs` reads a row's status by position, so the Project memory status sits last in its row. `test/browser-project-memory.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 again after the last client change. `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 73 files (projectMemory.webp in). Translated README parts (privacy, limits, FAQ ×12) written by hand after the translation subagent stopped without output; `readme-labels` passes. DS Bot's own data (`~/.ds-bot`) and the app bundle were only read. Not done: commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy (not requested).

## Send after Stop goes first (2026-10-28)
Request (owner): while a chat runs, five messages wait in the queue; Stop; a sixth message is sent at once. Seen: the order breaks and other queued messages go too. Wanted: the sixth goes alone at once; the five keep their order and do not go with it. Owner choice: fix it in Composer Plus and write a dsh issue text; after the sixth turn the queue runs by itself, in order.
Cause (dsh, not Composer Plus; same code in dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.3, 0.2.1-rc.4 and 0.2.2-alpha.2 `dsh-agent-loop`): Stop keeps the queue (`keepInbox: true`). A message sent while dsh is idle goes to the END of `next-turn` (`send` → `inbox.splice(target, Infinity, …)`), and the turn that it wakes claims the queue HEAD (`claim('next-turn')` takes `next-turn[0]`). So the turn runs queued message 1, not the new one; the new one waits behind all five. A send in the short window while the stopped turn still ends (`wakingAfterAbort`) also goes to the end of `next-turn`. dsh's chat shows the new message as sent (a "transcript" echo) next to queued message 1 while the Mac runs message 1, which looks like "two queued messages go with it".
Evidence: `test/browser-queue-stop-send.mjs` (isolated DSH_HOME, fake model): request 2 = "Queued one apple" instead of "Sixth message melon", the sixth runs last; the same with Composer Plus not loaded (CP=off) on dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.3 (the desktop app's version), and with Composer Plus on. The owner's log (session 3685ba7b…, seq 1662-1667): Stop with 2 queued, a new message with images went to index 2, turn 6 claimed index 0.
Design: feature `stopSendFirst` ("Send after Stop goes first"), on by default, Host switch (`GET/POST {on}` at `/api/composer-plus.send-first`, `storages/dsh-composer-plus/send-first.json`). Host: `agent/inbox/inserted` listener (sync, inside dsh's `send`, before `wakeDriver` opens the turn): when the switch is on, the chat is top-level or a continuable subagent, the agent is idle or its turn is already stopped (`agent.status !== 'running'` is not enough: the stopped turn's phase is still `running`; use the inserted index), the message is the user's own (`source.kind === 'user'`) at the end of `next-turn` and at least one message waits before it, move it to index 0 with one `inbox.splice` (same id, images and files with it). Only the user's own messages: a goal round, a question reply or a subagent result keep dsh's order. A message sent while the turn runs (not stopped) stays at the end (normal queue).
Detecting "stands still": `agent.status === 'idle'`, or dsh's private `agent.phase` is `running` with an aborted signal (the stopped turn still ends). Verified on the real loop (phase 4: Stop then send at once, DELAY=0).
Invariants: no change in dsh files; one splice per move; nothing moves while a queue move, edit or Continue of the same chat runs (they each check their own `before`/ids and get 409 or `queue-item-not-found`, see rule 7); the client store merges state (`createMobile`).
Phases: 1 Host + unit test `test/send-first.test.mjs` (red without the feature); 2 client switch, texts en/zh/11, screenshot; 3 docs (features.json how × 13, route, README limits × 13, llms.txt, ROUTES.md, tether/FEATURES.md); 4 real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.3 (`test/browser-queue-stop-send.mjs`, also with the switch off = dsh's own order); 5 `npm test`, `git diff --check`, dsh issue text.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/stopSendFirst.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md,dsh-issue-send-after-stop.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: version bump, commit, release, live desktop copy (not requested).
Last checkpoint (2026-10-28): phases 1-5 done, not committed.
- Host `installSendFirst` (index.js): `agent/inbox/inserted` listener; two splices (waiting rows out, back after the new one; the new message is never removed, so dsh's composer does not restore its text); a failed step puts lost rows back; route `GET/POST {on}` (`send-first.json`, on by default). Top-level chats, own messages only.
- `test/send-first.test.mjs` (real `apply()`, a fake inbox that copies dsh's `send`/`claim` order): red with the move disabled (`'q1'` instead of `'six'`), green now; covers stopped-and-ended, stopped-still-ending, running turn, goal round / question reply in the queue, a goal message, a queue edit, an empty queue, a subagent chat, a queue-move after it, a failed splice, switch off (dsh's order) and bad bodies.
- Client: `stopSendFirst` in FEATURES/SHOT_OF (Host switch via `createMobile`, `createToggles`, `setFeature`, `MobileStatus` prefix `sendFirst`), texts en/zh + 11 packs (subagent), `assets/features/stopSendFirst.webp` (13 kB, SHOT=1). Tests updated for 37 features: toggles counts, queue-edit FEATURES order.
- Docs: features.json entry (how × 13) + route, `npm run build:readme` (13 tables, llms.txt, ROUTES.md), a limits bullet in 13 READMEs, the test command in 13 Development blocks, tether/FEATURES.md row, `docs/dsh-issue-send-after-stop.md` (issue text for dsh).
- Real isolated dsh (`test/browser-queue-stop-send.mjs`, fake model): PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.3 (requests: task, sixth, q1…q5; the dock keeps the five in order; Settings row + screenshot, switch off/on reaches the Host); DELAY=0 (send while the stopped turn ends) PASS on 0.2.1-rc.3 and 0.2.0-rc.2; CP=off and CP=send-first-off show dsh's own order (q1…q5, then the sixth) as expected.
- `npm test` exit 0 (37 features, 37 shots, 44 route methods); `git diff --check` clean.

## Recent projects filter in "Workspace Tree" (2026-10-28)
Request (owner): with "Workspace Tree" selected, "Active in the last 2 hours" does not work; in the tree it must also filter the chats, not only the projects. Owner choice: chats are filtered only in "Workspace Tree"; "WorkSpace" stays as it is (projects only).
Cause (client.js `staleProjects`): the rule hides a project's whole section. dsh's tree draws a child project inside its parent's section (`childRows` in the parent `groupSection`), so a parent folder with no active chat of its own (for example `dsh-composer-plus` with only old chats, and its `.worktrees/feat-…` child active) hid the active child too. README called this a limit ("use it with WorkSpace").
Fix: `staleRows(…, tree)` returns `{projects, sessions}`. In the tree, a project stays when one of its chats is active or a project in its folder stays (`parentProjects` = dsh's `owningParentFolder` rule); the not-active chats of the projects that stay hide (row directly in the section, or inside dsh's HoverCard span). The tree is read from dsh's saved view store `localStorage['dsh.workspace.view.v5'].groupBy` (same key in 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.3; not a public API: a missing or broken value = WorkSpace). A chat with dsh status (waiting, done dot, running) also counts as active (`projectStatus([id])`).
Checks: `test/recent-projects.test.mjs` new tree steps (parents two levels up stay, name-prefix folder is no parent, quiet projects hide, old chats hide in kept projects, row and hover-card selectors, back to WorkSpace, broken store). New `test/browser-recent-tree.mjs` (isolated dsh, fake model, page clock +3 h): red with HEAD client.js on 0.2.1-rc.3 (tree assertion), PASS now on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.3.
Docs: en/zh description + 11 packs (subagent), features.json how × 13 + llms, README limits sentence × 13 (old "use it with WorkSpace" removed), dev blocks, tether/FEATURES.md row (Tether has no tree view).
Last checkpoint (2026-10-28): done, not committed. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; on 0.2.1-rc.3 the existing `test/browser-sidebar-projects.mjs` and `test/browser-recent-finished.mjs` still PASS with the change, and `test/browser-recent-tree.mjs` PASS (SHOTS=<dir> keeps before/after sidebar pictures).

## Project status on parent folders in "Workspace Tree" (2026-10-28)
Request (owner): in "Workspace Tree", a branch folder row shows its status marker; the project row above it must show it too. There can be more than one branch, so the rule must be dynamic.
Fix (client.js): `treeSessionIds(items, tree)` gives each project its own chats and, in the tree, the chats of every project inside its folder at any depth (`parentProjects`, dsh's `owningParentFolder` rule). `ProjectStatusMarkers` passes these ids to the unchanged `projectStatus`, so the priority stays: waiting > unread done > running, and `running` counts the running chats of all branches. The view comes from the same saved view store as the recent filter (`groupByOf()`); "WorkSpace" and an unknown view keep own chats only. Active projects first and the recent filter keep their own rules (own chats).
Checks: `test/project-status.test.mjs` tree steps (two branches, a branch inside a branch, name-prefix folder `/r/p2` is not a child, trailing slash, WorkSpace back to own chats, dsh's lists not changed). `test/browser-recent-tree.mjs` new end part: a held fake-model reply keeps a mono-app chat running; mono's row shows "1 chat(s) running", also collapsed; WorkSpace: mono shows none; release: no spinner.
Docs: en/zh description, features.json how × 13 + llms, README limits sentence × 13 (subagent for 11 + zh), tether/FEATURES.md row (Tether has no tree view).
Last checkpoint (2026-10-28): done, not committed. Unit test red with the tree rule removed, green now. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean. `test/browser-recent-tree.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.3; `browser-sidebar-projects.mjs` and `browser-recent-finished.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.3.

## Branch per chat in plan mode (2026-10-28)
Request (owner): with "New branch" chosen, a message sent in plan mode did not start a branch; in plan mode it must start on a new branch too.
Cause (verified in dsh 0.2.1-rc.2/rc.3 `SessionInputShell`): a draft that starts with `/plan` (Shift+Tab) is a command claim. Enter runs `beginSubmit(attempt, claim, args)` → `claim.submit` (`commands.execute('/plan …')`), not `deps.defaultSink`, so the "New branch" sink wrapper never saw it and `/plan` ran in the open New chat. A New chat put in plan mode first (bare `/plan`) already went through the sink, but the new branch chat started in the default mode.
Fix:
- client.js `installBranchSink(sessionId, shell, …)`: also wraps the shell's `beginSubmit` while "New branch" is chosen. `/plan <message>` (or attachments) → the same one-start-per-chat path with `plan: true`; `/plan off`, a bare `/plan` and other commands go to dsh. Restore gives dsh's own submit back (own property deleted). After a `/plan` start the new chat opens in the next task, after dsh settles the command (else dsh drops the outcome and the old draft stays in the New chat; seen in the real app).
- index.js `startOnBranch(…, {plan})`: plan when asked or when the source chat's plan view is on (`pending` flips `active`). Before git: refuse (409) when dsh has no `commands.execute`/`resolveAgent`. After create: `commands.execute(agent, '/plan')`; when it does not succeed, no message is sent (no default-mode turn that changes files) and the error names the kept worktree. Route: `plan` must be a boolean (400).
- Tether: `planMessage(draft)`; with New branch on, `/plan <message>` calls `startOnBranch(…, plan: true)`; the Mac also covers a chat already in plan mode.
Checks: `test/worktree-chats.test.mjs` plan block (order /plan then prompt, plan views, no commands → 409 and no worktree, failed /plan → no prompt, route 400/200); `test/worktree-chats-client.test.mjs` plan block (wraps/unwraps beginSubmit, `/plan off`/bare/other commands pass, busy guard on both entry points, open after settle, composer without beginSubmit). Both red on the code before this change. `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` two plan steps.
Docs: en/zh description + 11 packs, features.json how × 13 + llms + route POST text, README limits bullet × 13 (subagent for 11 + zh), tether/FEATURES.md row. Tether: `planMessage` + `startOnBranch(plan:)`; gateway forwards the body as is (no change).
Last checkpoint (2026-10-28): done, not committed. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; iOS unit tests 50/50 (simulator). `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.3 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (both plan steps) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (header only, no chip there). Fixed on the way: an unescaped `dsh's` in the en description broke client.js (page SyntaxError, seen by the browser test).

Checkpoint (2026-10-28, merge of origin/main into `feat/queue-consecutive-prompts`): origin/main brought idleSwitch, questionFit, branchTree, projectMemory, backup, autoBackup, sessionDoctor, rescueConsole, queue edit with attachments, the branch map, "New branch" as the default and the worktreeChats Host switch. Both sides kept: FEATURES, SHOT_OF, SHOTS, createToggles, FeatureSettings props, setFeature, the `test` script (send-first after queue-edit), the 11 packs (`stopSendFirst` texts + main's queue edit text; the plan-mode sentence added to main's worktreeChats text), docs/features.json (`stopSendFirst` entry and `send-first` route after queue-edit; the plan-mode sentence and `plan?` added to main's worktreeChats texts), tether/FEATURES.md, the README limit bullets and dev commands. `apply()` runs `installSendFirst(ctx, home)` after main's shared `queueBusy` move/edit installs. `test/queue-edit.test.mjs` is main's version with `stopSendFirst` in the FEATURES check; `test/toggles.test.mjs` counts 45 rows and 38 on. `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs`: main's branch-delete step runs first and deletes `feat/login-remember-me`, so the two plan steps now check for a new `feat/login-remember-me(-N)` branch each time instead of fixed `-2`/`-3` names. After the merge, real isolated dsh (headless, fake model): `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (all steps, branch delete and both plan steps) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (header pill and branch delete; no chip there); `test/browser-queue-stop-send.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. ## Deleted branch projects stay in the sidebar (2026-10-30, `fix/cleanup-stale-branch-worktrees`)
Request (owner): the Workspace tree still shows projects of deleted branches (13 `.worktrees/<name>` projects whose folders are gone); they must go by themselves, not only while "Active in the last 2 hours" hides them.
Cause (verified in `~/.dsh/storages/workspace.json` and index.js): Branch per chat removed a gone branch folder's workspace only at the next step of a chat in it (`system-prompt/assemble`). A branch deleted from another chat, a terminal or `/merge-main` has no step there, so its project stayed. Branches in the workspace tree (`branchTree`) has a sweep, but it is off by default and `branch-tree.json` does not exist on this machine (off).
Fix (index.js `installWorktrees`): the same `leave(cwd)` move also runs as a sweep while the Branch per chat switch is on: at dsh start (`appReady.onReady`), every `WORKTREES_SWEEP_MS` (30 s) and when the switch goes on. A sweep reads `workspaceRegistry.list()`, `stat`s each `<x>/.worktrees/<name>` project and calls git only for a gone one; `moveOut` now skips git when `<root>` is not a folder (a dangling link: no `spawn ENOENT`). The effect clears its timer and ready listener on stop. dsh's `delete` keeps the folder and the logs; the chats show under Ungrouped.
Checks: `test/worktree-chats.test.mjs` sweep block (real git; not-ready registry; gone folder at start; a later delete at the next sweep; a path through a symlinked folder to the repository goes, a dangling link and a folder outside a repository stay; off → nothing; on → at once; stop → no timer, no listener); red with no sweep, red with the switch ignored, red with no cleanup. `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` new step "Branch deleted outside dsh": PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2, red on 0.2.1-rc.2 with the sweep off. README Limits sentence × 13. `npm test` exit 0.
Last checkpoint (2026-10-30): done, not committed. The live desktop copy is not changed (release step).

## Project memory: view and change the notes with AI (2026-10-30, branch `feat/project-memory-ai-editor`)
Request (owner): a UI for the saved Project memory: (1) see every saved note, (2) grouped or filtered by project, (3) a text field where the user writes what should change and a model updates the memory, (4) a clean UI. Owner choices (asked 2026-10-30): a button in the Project memory row of Settings → Composer Plus opens a wide dialog; dsh's default model writes the changes; the model's changes show as a preview first and change nothing before **Apply**; Tether gets it too.
Evidence (read 2026-10-30): memory files under `$DSH_HOME/storages/dsh-composer-plus/memory/` (`projects/<sha256(real path)[:32]>/{MEMORY.md,<topic>.md,project.json}`, `global/`, `journal.jsonl`); `memoryStore` queues each change; the only UI is the Settings switch (`GET/POST {on}` `/api/composer-plus.memory`). dsh's `Modal` primitive (0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2) takes `className`, `headless`, `closeLabel`; its Esc closes only the top modal.
Design:
- Host route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.memory-edit` (projectMemory; 409 while the switch is off). GET: every project memory (name, path, files, entries with `added`) and the global memory. POST `{preview: {project: <id>|null, text}}`: one call to dsh's default model (no tools) with the numbered notes of that project and the global notes (global only for `null`) and the user's text; answer `{changes:[add|edit|remove], note?}` as in the review; cleaned (secret, length, topic name, one change per note) into concrete changes; kept on the Host under a preview id with a version (sha256 of the files). POST `{apply: <id>}`: inside one store batch, the version must still match (else 409, preview again), then removes/edits, then adds; journal actor `editor`.
- One preview or apply at a time on the Host (409 busy), for the desktop and the phone. The client store refuses a second preview/apply while one runs; the button and ⌘/Ctrl+Enter both go through the store.
- Client: `createMemoryEditor()`, `MemoryEditor` (button + `primitives.Modal` headless, its own style tag inside the dialog, so closing or switching off removes it). Left: All projects, Global memory, projects A–Z with counts; right: notes by file, a filter field; bottom: the text field, Preview changes, the preview list, Apply / Discard.
- Tether: `memory-edit` in `PLUS`; chat title menu → Project memory sheet (the chat's project first), same preview/apply; `MemoryEditor` class with one action at a time.

Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/projectMemory.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/{FEATURES.md,gateway/server.mjs,ios/Sources/*,ios/Tests/*}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the model removes the wrong note (preview + Apply, journal keeps a copy); a review writes between preview and apply (version check); many topic notes (only the ones that fit are sent; the rest are counted and shown).

Route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.memory-edit` in `installMemory` (409 while off, 409 busy, 404 unknown project, 410 gone preview, 409 changed memory). The review's JSON reader is shared (`answerObject`, `okChange`).

Real isolated dsh `test/browser-project-memory.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (SHOT=1) and 0.2.0-rc.2: two projects grouped A–Z, dialog > 900 px wide, ⌘/Ctrl+Enter previews once (a second key while it runs sends nothing), preview writes nothing, Apply writes 3 changes (journal actor `editor`), Esc closes the dialog and its style (Settings stays), switch off removes the button. Found in the real run: dsh's Modal is 380 px wide by default, so the dialog sets its own class. `projectMemory.webp` 21 kB (demo paths, checked by eye). Not done: commit, version bump, release, live copy, iPhone install (not requested).
Review findings (2026-10-30; all resolved 2026-10-30, not committed):
- F-01 (P1, resolved): **Apply** saves a part of a preview. `applyEdit` (index.js) catches the error of each change and continues, so a "move" (remove from the project, add to global) removes the note and then fails the add (global MEMORY.md over 4,000 characters, the 40-topic limit, or a write error). The note is then in neither memory (only `journal.jsonl` keeps it), the route answers 200 with `failed: [...]`, and the dialog and Tether show only "Saved 1 change(s)." (client.js `createMemoryEditor().apply`, Core.swift `MemoryEditor.apply` ignore `failed`). Reproduced in a temp copy: size limit (4,053 of 4,000) and a read-only global folder (EACCES); both give 200, `applied: 1`, note in neither file, one editor journal row (`remove`). Not caught: the tests apply only changes that succeed. Fix: all or none (AGENTS.md rule 11).
- F-02 (P2, resolved): the reason of a refused **Apply** disappears. After a 409/410 the client reloads the list, and a good `load()` sets `error: null` (client.js `createMemoryEditor().load`); Core.swift `MemoryEditor.load` sets `failed = nil` the same way. Reproduced (client): the error is set right after `apply()` and is null after the reload; the preview is gone too, so the user sees no reason.
- F-03 (P3, resolved): "Saved N change(s)" counts an add that changed nothing. `applyEdit` counts `ok`, not `changed`; `addNow` answers `changed: false` for a note that is already saved. Reproduced: a preview that adds an existing note gives `applied: 1` and the note count stays 1.
Resolve each finding only with: its regression test red before the fix and green after, `npm test` exit 0, the iOS tests green, and `test/browser-project-memory.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2.
Fix (2026-10-30): `memoryStore.batch(scopes, job)` keeps every file of the scopes before the job and holds its journal rows; when the job throws it writes each kept file back (topics first, MEMORY.md last), removes each file that the job made, and writes no journal row (when the undo itself fails: 500, and the rows are written so that no note is lost). `applyEdit` stops at the first failed change with "Nothing was saved: <op> "<text>" in <name>: <reason>" (the change's own status, else 500) and answers only `{applied}`, the count of changes that changed a note. The client and Tether keep the error when the list is read again (`load` no longer clears it); a 500 keeps the preview, a 409/410 drops it. Docs: AGENTS.md rule 11 (all or none), the `memory-edit` route and `llms` text in docs/features.json (`npm run build:readme`), one limits sentence in the 13 READMEs.
Evidence (2026-10-30): before the fix the new checks in `test/memory-editor.test.mjs` were red (`failed` in the answer; 200 instead of 500 on a read-only global folder); after it green. Each guard removed alone makes it red again: file restore, new-file removal, held journal, stop at the first failure, count `changed`, `load` keeps the error, 500 keeps the preview; in Swift, `failed = nil` in `load` makes `MemoryEditorTests.testStaleApply` red. The temp repro of the review: a move to a full global MEMORY.md now gives 409, the note stays in the project, no editor journal row; a read-only global folder gives 500 with the same result; an add of a saved note gives `applied: 0`. `npm test` exit 0. iOS tests 61/61 (iPhone 17 Pro simulator). Real isolated dsh `test/browser-project-memory.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. `git diff --check` clean.

## Open waiting chat when idle: green chats too (2026-10-30, branch `feat/idle-switch-green-chats`)
Request (owner): the idle switch also opens a chat with dsh's green dot, with the same idle rule and the same focus change. Owner's answers: inside the existing `idleSwitch` switch (no new switch); orange chats first, then green ones.
Evidence (dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 source, dsh-client-ui-session `observeRunning`/`reconcileStatus`, dsh-client-ui-workspace `sessionStatuses`): the green dot is `StateDot` `done` (`--dsw-alias-state-success-primary`), from `completionUnread`. dsh adds it when a chat goes running → idle while it is not the main view (also chats that were running when the list loaded), and removes it when the chat becomes the main view or runs again. So green = "turn finished and not opened since" (both). A pending interaction wins over it (orange dot).
Design (`watchIdleSwitch`): a wait key is the request key (orange) or `done:<id>` (green); orange before green, the longest-waiting first in each group; same 30 s idle, grace, `busyNow()`, on-screen and subagent rules. Opening a green chat clears its dot, so after a green open no other green chat opens until a key/pointer/wheel/touch/input event (not `focus`: dsh can move the focus itself); an orange request still opens after 30 s idle.
Checks: `test/idle-switch.test.mjs` green cases (30 s, held until input, focus is not input, draft blocks, orange before older green, orange opens while green holds, subagent quiet); 6 mutations of the new logic each fail it. `test/browser-idle-switch.mjs` with a green step (two `slow:` chats finish while a new chat is open: the first opens 30.1–30.4 s idle, the second stays 40 s with its dot, then opens 30.1–30.5 s after the next mouse move). Docs: `docs/features.json` (13 `how` + `llms`), `build:readme`, Limits and FAQ of 13 READMEs, 11 packs + en/zh, `build:locales`, `tether/FEATURES.md` row (stays `no`). Screenshot unchanged (still correct: orange case).

## Chat header in a narrow window (2026-10-30)

Request (owner, screenshot): in a narrow window the options in the open chat's header slide over each other (title, "alt ajan", the branch pill, "Arka planda 2 iş çalışıyor").
Cause (measured in isolated dsh 0.2.1-rc.2, `test/browser-header-fit.mjs`): dsh's header row (`_titleRow`: `_titleCluster` = title `_crumbs` + options `_headerActions`; then `_headerUtilities`) has no wrap and keeps the options and the utilities at full width (`flex: none`); only the title shrinks. Composer Plus's ⑂ branch pill (up to 320 px, Branch per chat) and the grants chip (Allow for this chat) are wide utility items: from 1280 px the title goes to 12 px and from about 1100 px dsh's "background jobs" button slides over Open in and the pill. With both CP items off, dsh's own header overlaps only below about 480 px (with a subagent and 2 jobs).
Fix (client.js): `headerFitCss(marker)` / `installHeaderFit`, one `<style data-composer-plus-header-fit="<id>">` in each feature's `feature(id, setup)` (worktreeChats: marker `data-composer-plus-branch-status`; allowForChat: new marker `data-composer-plus-grants` on the chip). Every rule needs `:has(> _headerUtilities [marker])`, so without our item dsh's header is not changed. The row wraps (right-aligned), the title keeps 120 px, options then utilities go to the next line, the pill shortens, the chip stays on one line. `installQuestionFit` now uses the shared `installStyle`.
Checks: `test/header-fit.test.mjs` (one style per feature, rules scoped to the marker, nothing hidden; each switch removes only its own style; unload removes both); red with HEAD client.js and with a disposer that does not remove. `test/question-fit.test.mjs` "second off" now counts only its own change. `test/browser-header-fit.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2: 1920/1600 px identical item boxes with and without the style; 1280…440 px and 1440 px + right sidebar: no overlap, each item and the Chat tab get a click, nothing outside, title ≥ 110 px, one-line items; both switches off → no style, on → back. Red: HEAD client.js fails it; the same page with the style disabled overlaps at 1100…440 px. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
Not done: dsh's own overlap below about 480 px without CP items (dsh's markup, not changed). Commit, release and live copy not requested.

Follow-up (owner, 2026-10-30): also fix dsh's own overlap below 480 px. Measured in isolated dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 with only dsh's items (Standard mode, 1 subagent, 2 background jobs, Open in, More actions, right sidebar button): overlap at 480, 440, 400, 360, 320 px and at 1280/1100/960 px with the right sidebar open (chat column 352 px). No existing feature covers dsh's own header, so it is a new switch `headerFit` ("Fit chat header to window", on by default, browser switch): `feature('headerFit', () => [installHeaderFit(document, 'headerFit')])`, the same rules as the item styles but for every chat header row (`_titleRow:has(> _titleCluster)`).
Done: FEATURES/SHOT_OF (after questionFit), SHOTS in index.js, en/zh + 11 `locales` texts (`build:locales`), `docs/features.json` entry in 13 languages (bold labels = pack labels; "Open in" plain, dsh shows "Open in <app>"), `build:readme`, limits bullet × 13, `tether/FEATURES.md` row (no), `test/toggles.test.mjs` counts 46/39, `assets/features/headerFit.webp` 9.5 kB (SHOT=1: dsh's own 400 px header without and with the style, demo project, English UI).
Checks: `test/header-fit.test.mjs` (three styles; headerFit rules for dsh's row, no marker needed, same rules as the item styles; headerFit off stores `{headerFit:false}` and removes only its style); red with the headerFit feature line removed and with the wrong row selector. `test/browser-header-fit.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2: with Branch per chat + Allow for this chat off, Fit chat header alone keeps dsh's own header free of overlap at 760…320 px and 1280 px + right sidebar (without the style it overlaps at 480…320 px and 1280 px + sidebar); all three off → no style, overlap again; all three on → three styles. `test/browser-settings-shots.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (46 rows). `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` has headerFit.webp.

## Harness ideas from the X article (2026-10-30, branch `docs/x-post-feature-ideas`)
Source: @beamnxw, "Make Opus 5.5 Finish Long Tasks: A Reusable Harness Engineering Setup" (X article, 2026-10-06). Owner chose all five proposals; Protected files start with `.env*`, `*.pem`, `*.key`; the handoff note opens as a preview.
Objective:
1. Improve prompt also rewrites a `/goal <objective>` (and `/goal edit <objective>`) draft into an evidence-based completion condition (outputs, checks from the context, evidence shown in the chat, unresolved parts marked, a stop-and-report clause). The `/goal` prefix stays; other slash commands stay refused. Desktop (command chip kept) and Tether.
2. Goal check: the verifier answers an evidence table `items: [{item, verdict: verified|incorrect|unresolved, evidence, fix}]`; pass only when every item is verified; the deny text lists each open item with its evidence and fix; the row shows "N of M verified" and the table in its tooltip. An old `{pass, missing}` answer still parses. The token budget also counts the chat's subagent chats (any depth, their calls since the budget window started).
3. New feature `protectedFiles` (Host switch, on by default): Settings list of glob patterns (default `.env*`, `*.pem`, `*.key`, each "read and write" or "write only"); a `tools/pre-execute` gate (after `next()`) denies `read`, `read_image`, `write`, `edit`, `str_replace_editor` and `grep`/`glob` with a matching `path` argument; real paths are checked too (symlink). Limits: bash, MCP and folder searches are not covered. Not in Tether.
4. New feature `handoffChat` (browser switch, on by default; a model call only on a click): chat menu → **Continue in a new chat…**: one call to the chat's model writes a note (Task, Outputs, Completed, Decisions, Open issues, Next action; rolled-back steps left out); the note opens in an editable preview; **Start new chat** creates a chat in the same project with the note and a `dsh-session:` link to the old chat as its first message (all or none: a failed send archives the new chat). Tether: chat title menu → same sheet.
5. New feature `contextBreakdown` (browser switch, on by default; no model call): right sidebar → `+` → **Context**: what fills the open chat's context, from the chat log only (the surviving surface after compaction and rollback): system prompt, project instructions, skills, your messages, agent replies, tool calls and results by tool (MCP by server), tool definitions, with estimated tokens (dsh's own 4-characters-per-token rule) and the share of the context window. Tether: chat title menu → Context.
Invariants: every AGENTS.md rule (own switch, screenshot, 13 languages, docs, unit test, Tether row); no new dependency; no change to dsh files; no commit, release or live copy (not requested).
Phases and checks:
| Phase | Work | Check |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Improve prompt `/goal` | `test/prompt-improve.test.mjs`, `test/prompt-improve-client.test.mjs`, iOS `improvable` test |
| 2 | Goal check table + subagent budget | `test/goal-check.test.mjs`, `test/goal-check-client.test.mjs` |
| 3 | protectedFiles | `test/protected-files.test.mjs` (symlink, relative path, defaults, off) |
| 4 | handoffChat | `test/handoff.test.mjs` (withdrawn both kinds, fork, all-or-none start, one action at a time) |
| 5 | contextBreakdown | `test/context-breakdown.test.mjs` (compaction replace, rollback both kinds, fork, MCP grouping) |
| 6 | texts ×13, docs, README ×13, screenshots, Tether, real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 + 0.2.1-rc.2 | `npm test`, iOS tests, browser checks, `git diff --check` |
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/{protectedFiles,handoffChat,contextBreakdown,goalCheck,promptImprove}.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/{FEATURES.md,gateway/server.mjs,ios/Sources/*,ios/Tests/*}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: a too strict `/goal` costs rounds (dsh round cap, blocked state); the verifier JSON grows (bad JSON → the existing error path); Protected files gives a false sense of safety (limits in 13 READMEs); the handoff note can miss a decision (preview, link to the old chat); context numbers are estimates.
