## Open review findings (2026-10-30, review-guard of the uncommitted harness-ideas change on 242425c)
Status: RESOLVED locally (2026-10-30, /implement-plan), not committed or released. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-10-30. Close an item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-10-30): Protected files checks a path that is not the path dsh's file tools use. Signature: `protectedHit` (index.js) checks (1) the lexical path `resolve(cwd, raw)` and (2) `realpath(resolve(cwd, raw))` only when that file exists (`catch { /* a new file: only its own path */ }`). dsh-fs-local `resolveLocalTarget` (same code in 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2) uses `realpath` of `<cwd>/<raw>` without lexical `..` and, for a missing file, the `realpath` of the nearest existing folder plus the rest; ripgrep (`grep`) gets the path with the chat folder as cwd, so the kernel resolves it the same way. Symptoms (all with patterns that contain `/`; a pattern without `/` matches the file name, so the defaults `.env*`, `*.pem`, `*.key` are not affected): a new file below a symlinked folder (`alias -> published`, `write alias/new.md`); an absolute real path while the chat folder is a symlinked spelling (`/var/…` → `/private/var/…`, also `/tmp`); `..` after a symlinked folder, for a new file (`sub/link/../new.yml`) and for an existing file (`read sub/link/../app.yml` with `sub/link -> config/deep` reads `config/app.yml`). Evidence (2026-10-30, temporary /tmp scripts on the current index.js, Node 26.7.0, removed): each of these gives `undefined` (no hit) with `published/**` (write only) or `config/*.yml`; the kernel read of `sub/link/../app.yml` returns the protected file's text; `published/new.md` itself gives a hit. A prototype outside the repository that resolves like dsh gives the expected answer in all 11 cases (5 bypasses closed, 6 controls unchanged: `published/new.md`, `published/old.md` read, `src/new.md`, a dangling `ghost/x.md`, `sub/link/../../.env`, `nodir/../x.md`). Impact: the agent's own `write`/`edit`/`str_replace_editor` can create or change a file in a protected folder, and `read`/`read_image`/`grep` can read a file of a "read and write" folder pattern, although README and llms.txt say these tools are covered. Not caught: `test/protected-files.test.mjs` tests a symlink to a protected file and a new file by its name only. Resolved when: a test block with a symlinked folder alias, `..` after a symlinked folder (new and existing file) and an absolute real path through a symlinked chat folder spelling is red on the current code and green after the fix; the old cases (symlink to `.env`, outside link by its name, `new.key`, folder grep and bash not covered) stay green; `npm test` exit 0.
- F-02 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-10-30): Goal check counts "N of M verified" after the table was cut to 20 rows. Signature: `parseVerdict` (index.js) keeps 20 rows (open first) in `items`; `denyReason` counts `r.items`, and the client row (`GoalCheck` in client.js, `verifiedCount`, `items.length`) does the same. Also the deny text lists at most 20 open parts with no note of the rest (the `N more open part(s)` line is only in `missing`, which the items branch does not use). Evidence (2026-10-30, temporary /tmp scripts, removed): 24 verified + 1 incorrect → `pass: false` (right), deny text "(19 of 20 verified)" (expected 24 of 25); 25 open parts → deny "(0 of 20 verified)", 20 listed, no "5 more open part(s)". Impact: the model and the user see wrong numbers when the verifier answers more than 20 parts; the pass/fail decision is right. Not caught: the tests check the 20-row cut and `missing`, not the counts after the cut. Resolved when: `parseVerdict` answers `verified` and `total` of the whole table, `checkGoal` passes them on, the deny text and the row use them, the deny text names the open parts that it does not list, the new checks in `test/goal-check.test.mjs` and `test/goal-check-client.test.mjs` are red on the current code and green after; `npm test` exit 0.
- Rule added to AGENTS.md with this record: rule 12 (a Host gate checks the path that dsh's file tools resolve).
- F-01 fix: new `toolTarget(cwd, raw)` (index.js) resolves like dsh-fs-local `resolveLocalTarget`: `realpath` of `<cwd>/<raw>` with no lexical `..` (POSIX; `resolve` on Windows), else the `realpath` of the nearest existing folder plus the missing names (undefined for `..` over a missing folder, a dangling link or another error). `protectedHit` checks the path as given and this real path; `protectedBy`, `protectGlob` and the gate are unchanged. Evidence: the new block in `test/protected-files.test.mjs` (`alias -> published` new file and new folders, `src/link/../new.yml`, `read` and `grep` of `src/link/../app.yml`, the real path while the chat folder is `acme-link -> acme`, and a gate `write alias/post.md`) was red before the fix ("a new file below a symlinked folder"), green after; the unchanged answers (`published/new.md`, a read of `published/old.md` with write only, `src/new.md`, `ghost/x.md` below a dangling link, `src/link/../../.env`) and the old cases stay green. Mutations in a temp copy: the old `realpath(full)` → red ("a new file below a symlinked folder"); `resolve(cwd, raw)` in `toolTarget` → red (".. after a symlinked folder: a new file"). No README change: README and llms.txt already say that these tools are covered and that the real path counts.
- F-02 fix: `parseVerdict` answers `verified` and `total` of the whole table; `checkGoal` passes them on with `items` (so the saved `last` has them); `denyReason` uses them (an older result without them counts its rows) and adds "- N more open part(s), not listed here" for the open parts after the 20th row; the client row (`GoalCheck` in client.js) shows them when they are valid, else it counts the rows. Evidence: the new checks in `test/goal-check.test.mjs` (25 open → `[0, 25]` and the "5 more" line; 24 of 25; `checkGoal` `[1, 2]`) and `test/goal-check-client.test.mjs` (20 rows shown, "24 of 25") were red before the fix, green after; the old tally check (rows only) stays green. Mutations: count after the cut, `checkGoal` without the counts, no "more" line, client count and client total from the rows: each red. No Tether change (the row is not in Tether).
- Both: `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `test/browser-harness-ideas.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (2026-10-30). Not done (not requested): commit, release, live desktop copy.

## Task board, like Kanzo (2026-11-02, branch `feat/kanban-board`)
Request (owner): add a kanban board like the one in the X post of @DanKornas (2026-11-01, "Kanzo is a local kanban board and MCP server in one program"), with the same behavior and look. Source: github.com/emanuele-em/kanzo (e8740b7, Rust + one web page).
License: Kanzo is AGPL-3.0; this project is MIT. AGPL code copied into an MIT package would make the package AGPL. So no Kanzo code, CSS or text is copied: a behavior spec (our own words, `/tmp/kanzo-study/SPEC.md`, not committed) feeds a clean implementation. UI labels that are plain words ("New task", "Todo") are not code.
Owner choices (asked 2026-11-02): board + agent tools (no git sync, no network share, no images, no CLI); on by default, the agent instruction only in a project with open tasks; Tether too.
Design:
- Feature `taskBoard` ("Task board"), Host switch (it registers tools and a prompt section): `GET/POST {on}` and `{backlog}` (Settings only) at `/api/composer-plus.board`, file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/board-switch.json`. Data route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.board-tasks` (in Tether's PLUS). Off: no tools, no section, no page; data stays.
- Data: one owner-only JSON file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/board.json` (projects by real folder path with a stable slug, tasks with a global id, comments). One change at a time, atomic write; a broken file is kept as `board.json.broken-<time>` and the board starts empty. Limits checked at the route and the tools.
- Projects = dsh projects (workspaceRegistry), a `.worktrees/<name>` folder counts as its repository's project (Branch per chat). A chat's project: its folder, or the nearest dsh project above it.
- Columns: backlog (optional, Settings), todo, doing, review, done. Order per column over all projects (float position; new and status change → bottom; drop before a card → midpoint; renumber when the gap < 1e-9). Done shows the newest 50 per project, "Show N older".
- Tools (dsh registry, with the switch): board_list_projects, board_list_tasks, board_get_task, board_create_task, board_update_task, board_add_comment, board_next_task (top non-human todo → bottom of doing), board_delete_task. Agent comments keep the chat id (a link to the chat on the card).
- Prompt section `composer-plus:board` (interpolate false): the "keep the board current" rule, only for a chat whose project has open tasks; once shown it stays for that chat (prefix cache).
- Client: a main panel (dsh `main` keyed slot) + a `sidebar.panellist` row "Task board"; projects list, header (N open tasks, Human filter, New task), columns, cards, right detail panel (status, tags, Human, times, Markdown description, comments), `/` search, `n`, `h`, Esc, drag and drop, export Markdown/JSON. Poll 2 s while shown and visible. New task = a local draft that is created on its first saved field (no "New task" row that an agent could take).
- Store (rule 7): one move/delete/comment of a task at a time (drag, Status select, key and drop all check it); text saves send the value they started from (`was`) and the Host answers 409 when an agent changed it meanwhile; the newest view (rev) wins.
- Tether: chat title menu → Task board (the chat's project): columns, task sheet (status, Human, comments, add comment), new task.
Phases: 1 Host + `test/task-board.test.mjs`; 2 client + `test/task-board-client.test.mjs`; 3 texts en/zh + 11 packs, docs (features.json ×13, routes, README limits/FAQ/privacy ×13, tether/FEATURES.md); 4 Tether (gateway PLUS, Swift, iOS tests); 5 real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-task-board.mjs`, SHOT=1 → `assets/features/taskBoard.webp`), `npm test`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/taskBoard.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/*, tether/{FEATURES.md,gateway/server.mjs,ios/Sources/*,ios/Tests/*}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: git sync, network share, images, CLI, project colors picker/rename/delete (dsh owns projects), version bump, release, live copy.
Risks: tool definitions cost about 1,000 tokens per request in every chat (on by default; documented); an agent can still move a human or backlog task (instruction only, as in Kanzo); dsh's `main` and `sidebar.panellist` slots are public but young.
Checkpoint (2026-11-02, not committed): phases 1–5 done.
- Host (index.js, end): `boardStore` (one change at a time on a copy; the copy becomes the board only after the atomic write; a broken file is kept as `board.json.broken-<time>`), `cleanBoard`, `boardProjects`, `boardView`, `boardTask`, `boardSearch`, `boardExport`, `boardOps` (create, update with `was`, move, comment, uncomment, remove, next), `boardKey`, `installBoard` (8 tools, section `composer-plus:board` order 655, routes `board` and `board-tasks`). On by default (owner's choice); off: tools, section and page go, the data stays.
- Client: `createBoard` store, `BoardPage` (dsh `main` keyed slot + `sidebar.panellist` row, order 20), `BoardTaskPanel`, `BoardSearch`, `BoardExport`, `BoardSettings` (Backlog switch), texts en/zh + 11 packs (subagent).
- Tether: `board-tasks` in PLUS; `TaskBoard` (Core.swift), `BoardSheet`/`BoardTaskSheet`/`BoardNewTaskSheet` (UI.swift); tether/FEATURES.md `yes`. iOS tests 65/65 (4 new, simulator). Layout not looked at on a simulator.
- Docs: features.json (how ×13, llms, 2 routes), `build:readme` (tables, llms.txt, ROUTES.md), README privacy/limits/FAQ ×13, dev block line ×13.
Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` and `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red with each of 20 guards removed (was check, next skips human, renumber, write-then-swap, copy, broken file kept, section only with open tasks, section stays, off removes tools, off 409, worktree project, backlog off only empty; change lock, drag lock, create lock, was sent, edit order, draft kept on failure, poll stop, draft removed on off). `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (page, 3 tasks, agent tools + rule with the fake model: next task skips the human task, comment + review on the page, Status, comment, Esc, search into another project, drag above a card kept after reload, switch off/on). `assets/features/taskBoard.webp` 11 kB (SHOT=1, demo projects, English UI). `test/mobile-connect.test.mjs` fake `inject` now ignores the board's `['tools','systemPrompt']`. Review subagent stopped without output after 40 min; own pass fixed: Esc in a field no longer closes the panel, Esc drops a new task (no create), the toast timer clears on stop.
Not done (not requested): commit, version bump, release, live copy, iPhone install.
Review (2026-11-02, `/review` of the whole branch): one finding, fixed. `cleanBoard` computed `next`/`nextComment` with `Math.max(...list)`; a board inside the limits with about 125k comments threw RangeError, so a good file was renamed to `board.json.broken-<time>` and the board started empty. Now a `reduce` loop. New check in `test/task-board.test.mjs` (260 tasks × 500 comments load, nothing kept aside): red with the old spread, green now. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.

## Planned steps, from the HarnessMM X post (2026-11-02, branch `feat/port-x-post-feature`)
Request (owner): port the feature of the X post by @NFT_Chen (2026-11-02) about HarnessMM (arXiv 2610.32813, "guarded decision reuse"): the model plans the next commands with exact arguments and a guard; when a tool result meets the guard, the step runs without a model call. Owner choices (2026-11-02): off by default; a real DeepSeek call in phase 0 is approved.
Scope: only the guarded-step mechanism (EDG + GDR + CCS in the paper). Not RSI, not the typed selector model, not the paper's context or finalization configuration.
Design:
- Feature `plannedSteps` ("Planned steps"), Host switch (OFF_BY_DEFAULT): `GET/POST {on}` at `/api/composer-plus.planned-steps`, file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/planned-steps.json`. On: the tool `plan_next_steps`, the prompt section `composer-plus:planned-steps` (order 660) and an `llm/stream` listener, all in one `ctx.inject(['tools','systemPrompt'])` fiber; off disposes all three.
- Tool: `plan_next_steps({steps: [{id, after, when, tool, arguments}]})`, 1–8 steps; id `^[a-z0-9-]{1,32}$` unique; after `root` or an earlier id; when `ok|always`; tool `bash|read|grep|glob`; arguments a plain object, JSON ≤ 8 KB. A bad plan is a tool error (no plan).
- Listener (stateless, reads only `options.messages`): from the end, skip neutral messages (tool results, runtime-context, developer, system); count Composer Plus's own synthetic steps (tool-call id `cpgs-<8 hex>-<n>`); before them the newest real assistant message must hold a `plan_next_steps` call with a non-error result. Any user message between (user, steer, Continue marker, compact summary) ends the plan. Exactly one eligible step (predecessor done; `ok` = result not isError and, for bash, no dsh fail mark in the last 8 lines, the BASH_FAIL rule of Goal check) → a synthetic chunk stream; none or more than one → `next()` (the real model; that answer ends the plan). Only agent-loop requests of a chat that offers the `plan_next_steps` tool.
- Synthetic message: a reasoning block (DeepSeek thinking mode needs it, see phase 0), a text line, one tool-call block, finish `tool-calls`. The tool call goes through dsh's normal tool path (tools/pre-execute: approval, sandbox, Protected files stay).
Phase 0 (2026-11-02, done): probe plugin and runners in /tmp/hmm-phase0 (not committed).
- Fake model, `dsh --profile headless`, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2: the plugin's `ctx.on('llm/stream')` runs for each agent request with `sessionId`, frozen `messages` (with `source.kind`) and `tools`; a returned async generator of chunks (block-start/delta/block-end, finish `{kind:'tool-calls'}`) becomes a durable assistant message; its bash call passes tools/pre-execute (`allow`) and runs; the next request reaches the fake model with the synthetic message and the tool result; 2 chat calls instead of 3. Other `llm/stream` listeners: session-title (global, prepend, only observes) and session-checkpoint-policy (flush before dispatch; skipped when the plugin does not call `next()`, the turn still completed).
- Real DeepSeek (owner's account, deepseek-flash, thinking on), headless with `authorization`, `deepseek-account`, `credentials` enabled, the account grant copied to a temp DSH_HOME and deleted: without a reasoning block the next request fails: 400 "The `content[].thinking` in the thinking mode must be passed back to the API". With a reasoning block (labeled text, no signature): the turn completes on 0.2.1-rc.2 and on 0.2.0-rc.2; the model reads both results and answers.
- Decision: go. The synthetic message starts with a short reasoning block that says Composer Plus ran a planned step.
Phases: 1 Host + `test/planned-steps.test.mjs`; 2 client switch, texts en/zh, `test/toggles.test.mjs`; 3 11 packs, features.json (how ×13, llms, route), `build:locales`, `build:readme`, README limits/FAQ ×13, tether/FEATURES.md `no`; 4 `test/browser-planned-steps.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (SHOT=1 → `assets/features/plannedSteps.webp`), `npm test`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, package.json (test script), locales/*.json, locale/*.json (built), docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, llms.txt, README*.md, tether/FEATURES.md, assets/features/plannedSteps.webp, test/{planned-steps.test.mjs,browser-planned-steps.mjs,toggles.test.mjs}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the tool schema and the section cost tokens in every request while on (savings only when the model plans); a guard proves its condition, not that the work is right; the synthetic message is stored with `source.kind: 'model'` (dsh has no plugin author mark), so its text and reasoning say who ran it; `llm/stream` chunk shapes and the `[exit code: N]` mark are not a public contract.
Checkpoint (2026-11-02, not committed): phases 1–4 done.
- Host (index.js, end): `parsePlan`, `nextPlannedStep`, `plannedStepChunks`, `plannedStream`, `installPlannedSteps` (route `planned-steps`, switch file, one fiber: tool + section order 660 + `llm/stream`); `PLANNED_PATH`, `SHOTS` entry, `installPlannedSteps` in `apply`. The reasoning block goes only into a step whose planning message had reasoning (a model without thinking gets no foreign block).
- Client: `plannedSteps` in FEATURES (after contextBreakdown) and OFF_BY_DEFAULT, SHOT_OF, `PLANNED_ROUTE` store, createToggles, setFeature, Settings status (`planned.down`/`planned.restart`); texts en/zh + 11 packs; `build:locales`.
- Docs: features.json (how ×13, llms, route), `build:readme` (13 tables, llms.txt, ROUTES.md), limits + FAQ ×13 by hand (token cost measured: tool 1,114 + section 858 characters ≈ 500 tokens with dsh's 4-characters rule), tether/FEATURES.md `no`.
Checks: `test/planned-steps.test.mjs` red with each of 14 guards removed (user message ends the plan, ok guard, two ready, bash fail mark, plan tag, reused step, purpose, tool offered, plan error result, every root result, reasoning only when planned with it, off disposes, off by default, no background bash). `test/browser-planned-steps.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2: off by default; chat A 2 model calls instead of 3, the step line in the turn; chat B build exit 1 → no step; chat C a planned read of `.env` refused by Protected files; chat D a test-only ask gate → dsh's approval card, the step waits and runs after Allow once; off → no tool in the request; no page errors. `assets/features/plannedSteps.webp` 12.8 kB (SHOT=1, demo-app, English UI, checked by eye). `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` ships the image.
Not done (not requested): commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy.

## Blocked commands, from dsh-superagent's `blockedCommands` (2026-11-02, branch `docs/superagent-feature-plan`)
Source: github.com/deepseek-ai/dsh-superagent-prototype (6337fc8, research prototype, MIT). Its Executor blocks named commands with shims in `worker-bin` (docs/003). dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and the desktop app have no command rule (searched `blockedCommands`, `deniedCommands`, `commandRules`, `prefixRules`, `execPolicy`: none). Owner approved the plan of 2026-11-02 (only this feature; the other superagent parts exist already or do not fit).
Objective: new feature `blockedCommands` ("Blocked commands"): the agent's `bash`/`pwsh` call that contains a command matching a user rule is denied ("block") or asks the user first with dsh's approval card ("ask first"). Empty list or switch off: no change.
Invariants: Host switch (`GET/POST {on}` / `{rules}` at `/api/composer-plus.blocked-commands`, owner-only `storages/dsh-composer-plus/blocked-commands.json`), on by default with an empty list (no port, no server, no model call); the gate runs after `next()` and never turns a deny or cancel into something else; the ask reason never starts with `Auto review denied tool ` (Allow for this chat and Tether's `isGrantedAsk` must not answer it); the scanner never throws and is linear in the command length (no regex on the command); no new dependency; no change to dsh files; no commit, release or live copy (not requested).
Rule syntax: words split by spaces; the first word is the command name (basename, `.exe/.cmd/.bat/.com` dropped, both sides), it may be any word of a simple command (`sudo`, `env`, `timeout 5`, `xargs`); the other words must follow in the same order, other words may be between; `*` any characters (also `/`), `?` one; case ignored.
Scanner: bash: `; & | ( )` newline and `{ }` at a word start end a command; `'…'`, `"…"`, `\`; `$(…)` and backticks (also in "…") are commands of their own; `#` at a word start is a comment; a here-document body is skipped, unless its delimiter is unquoted (then only its `$(…)` and backticks count). pwsh: same separators, backtick escape, `$(…)`. `sh|bash|zsh|dash|ksh|fish|pwsh|powershell -c…|-Command <text>` and `eval <text>` are read again, at most 3 levels.
Acceptance: `test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` (parse, scanner, match table, gate decisions, route, 0600, 1 MB command time, Tether `isGrantedAsk` false), `test/blocked-commands-client.test.mjs` (one list save at a time from Add, Enter, mode, ✕, Use the defaults; refused save keeps the list; a refresh or the switch during a save does not overwrite it; Protected files uses the same list), `test/toggles.test.mjs` counts, texts en/zh + 11 packs, docs (features.json ×13, route, README limits/FAQ/privacy ×13), `tether/FEATURES.md` `no`, `assets/features/blockedCommands.webp` (SHOT=1, demo project), real dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 headless, `npm test`, `git diff --check`.
Phases: 1 Host + unit test; 2 client (shared `RuleList`/`createRules`, also for Protected files) + client test; 3 texts and docs (en/zh by hand, other languages by subagents); 4 screenshot + real dsh check; 5 `npm test`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, assets/features/blockedCommands.webp, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, package.json (test script), test/{blocked-commands.test.mjs,blocked-commands-client.test.mjs,toggles.test.mjs,browser-harness-ideas.mjs}, tether/FEATURES.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: false positives (any word can start a command: `echo npm publish`, `git stash push` for `git push`); bypass by a variable, alias, function, script file, another interpreter, `-EncodedCommand`; under an `approval: never` preset an ask is a deny.

## Open review findings (2026-11-02, review-guard of the uncommitted Blocked commands change on 0c4facc)
Status: RESOLVED locally (2026-11-02, /implement-plan), not committed or released. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-11-02. Close an item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-11-02): a `*` in the command text does not match a `*` in a rule. Signature: `wildMatch` (index.js) tests `p[i] === '?' || p[i] === s[j]` before `p[i] === '*'`, so a rule `*` consumes a literal `*` of the command as a plain character and sets no backtrack point. Evidence (2026-11-02, Node on the current index.js): `rm *` vs `rm *.log`, `rm -rf *` vs `rm -rf *.bak`, `*log` vs `*.log`, `*` vs `*x`, `a*` vs `a*x`: no match; 300,000 random rules/texts over `a b * ? .` against a regex reference: 3,492 wrong answers now, 0 with the star branch first (prototype in /tmp, outside the repo). Impact: a "block" rule with `*` does not stop a command whose word holds `*` (shell globs such as `rm -rf *.bak`). Not caught: no test has `*` in the command text. Resolved when: `wildMatch` checks `*` first; a new random-reference check and the cases above in `test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` are red on the current code and green after; `npm test` exit 0.
- F-02 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-11-02): the script of `sh -c` / `pwsh -Command` is missed when other options come between. Signature: `rereads` (index.js) takes the word right after the first `-…c…` word as the sh script and, for pwsh, the word after any word that starts with `-c`. Evidence (2026-11-02): bash runs `bash -c -x 'echo …'`, `bash -c -- 'echo …'` and `bash -c -o pipefail 'echo …'` (tried with echo); `blockedHit` gives no match for `bash -c -x "npm publish"`, `bash -c -- "npm publish"`, `pwsh -CustomPipeName p -Command "npm publish"` and `pwsh -ConfigurationFile c.json -c "npm publish"`. PowerShell's CommandLineParameterParser (`MatchSwitch(switchKey, "command", "c")`, `GetSwitchKey` drops one `-`, `--` or `/`) takes `-c`, `-co`, …, `-command` as Command (the rest of the words, joined) and `-cus…` as CustomPipeName; about_Pwsh: "All parameters are case-insensitive". Impact: a block rule is skipped through a common option order. Not caught: tests only have `bash -c 'x'`, `sh -lc`, `pwsh -Command 'x'`. Resolved when: sh finds the script as the first word that is not an option after a `-…c…` group (`--`/`-` end options, `-o`/`+o`/`-O`/`+O` take a value, case-sensitive); pwsh finds `-`/`--`/`/` + a prefix of `command` (rest of the words) and `-cwa`/`-commandwithargs` (next word); new cases in `test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` red now and green after, with the controls `bash script.sh`, `bash -C x.sh`, `pwsh -File s.ps1` unchanged; `npm test` exit 0.
- Rule added to AGENTS.md with this record: rule 13 (a Host gate that reads command text is tested as the shell reads it, and its matcher against a reference).
- F-01 fix: `wildMatch` (index.js) checks a rule's `*` before the `?`/literal branch. Evidence: the new checks in `test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` (the cases above, `a*b` vs `a**b`, 50,000 random rules/texts over `a b * ? .` against a regex reference, and `blockedHit` with `rm -rf *` vs `rm -rf *.bak`) were red before the fix ("rm * vs rm *.log"), green after.
- F-02 fix: `rereads` (index.js) reads the options of a shell the way the shell does. sh (case-sensitive): `-…`/`+…` letter groups are options (a `c` sets the script mode, each `o`/`O` takes the next word), `--` and `-` end the options, other `--long` words are skipped, a here-string is the script; after a `c` group, the first word that is not an option is the script. pwsh (any case): `-`, `--` or `/` and a prefix of `command` takes the rest of the words; `-cwa`/`-commandwithargs` takes the next word. Real bash 5.3 (this Mac) runs the script for each sh case in the test (`-c -x`, `-c --`, `-c -o pipefail`, `-co pipefail`, `-oc pipefail`, `-coO pipefail extglob`, `-c +O extglob`, `+c`, `-c -`, `--rcfile /dev/null -c`); sh and zsh run `-c -x` and `-c -o pipefail` too; `bash -c -- -x 'cmd'` runs `-x`. pwsh is not installed: its cases follow its source only. Evidence: the new cases in `test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` (22 texts, each read as bash and as pwsh, and 4 rows in the match table) were red before the fix (`bash -c -x "npm publish"` gave `["bash -c -x npm publish","-x"]`), green after; the controls `bash script.sh`, `bash -C x.sh`, `pwsh -File s.ps1`, `pwsh -c`, `bash -c` give no script. Known limit (ponytail comment): a word that is not an option before `-c` (`bash x.sh -c 'y'`) does not end the search, so `y` is read too; this can only make a rule match more often.
- Docs: `blockedCommands.llms` in docs/features.json (the `*` note and the option orders), then `npm run build:readme` (llms.txt). No README change: the README text stays true.
- Both: `node test/blocked-commands.test.mjs` and `node test/blocked-commands-client.test.mjs` pass; `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; real isolated dsh `test/browser-harness-ideas.mjs` (no SHOT, so no screenshot changed) PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.

## Fix: typing lag in Open memory (2026-11-02, branch fix/plan-mode-typing-lag)
Owner report (chat): keys came late and the UI stuttered while typing. Asked which field: the owner chose Settings → Project memory → Open memory (the text field), not the composer.
- Reproduced in a throwaway dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 (`test/browser-memory-typing.mjs`: 22 made-up projects with 10 chats each, "Workspace tree" with every project open, about 150 notes, CPU ×4). Every feature on: style recalc 35.2 ms per key press (Chrome Performance metrics), 2 recalcs per key.
- What runs per key press in client.js: MemoryEditor renders (React state `text`), and the body-wide MutationObservers of Project status / Branch badge (`useRowHosts`), Collapse all projects and By status run their scan. Measured JS per key: about 4 ms (observers about 1.4 ms). Not the cause.
- Features switched off one at a time: every client feature off → 0.24 ms; only Collapse all projects on → 32.9 ms; each other group on → 0.2-0.3 ms. The Host features named in the request (Complete while typing, Next message prediction, Planned steps) add no listener in Settings; Input history and Shift+Tab /plan listen only on the composer seat.
- Cause: `COLLAPSE_CSS` had `body:has([data-row-key^="workspace:"][aria-expanded="true"]) [host] { display: inline-flex; }`. A `:has()` on <body> makes the browser compute the style of the whole page again at each DOM change (each key in any field). More open projects and chat rows: more cost.
- Fix (client.js `CollapseProjectsButton`): the component reads "a project is open" in its existing scan (the observer now also watches `aria-expanded`) and portals the button only then; the CSS hides the empty host with `:empty`. Same behavior: shown only while a project is open, same place, same header width rule (its `:has(> host)` stays on the small header element).
- Evidence: after the fix, every feature on: 0.29-0.32 ms style per key (0.2.1-rc.2), 0.22 ms (0.2.0-rc.2). `test/browser-memory-typing.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2, and fails on the old client.js (35.23 ms, limit 5 ms). `test/browser-sidebar-projects.mjs` PASS on both (Collapse all: hidden when none open, 3 open → 0, keyboard focus). Other sidebar options checked: "Active in the last 2 hours" 0.69 ms, "By status" 0.28 ms.
- Unit tests: new `test/has-selectors.test.mjs` (in `npm test`) fails on any `:has()` rule on html/body/:root in client.js (red on the old client.js); `test/project-tools.test.mjs` checks the new observer options, no button while all projects are closed, the button after a project opens and after all close. `npm test` exit 0.
- The old open item "project-status MutationObserver watches all of document.body" (Issues found in 1.0.0): measured here, about 1 ms per key press at CPU ×4 for all body observers together. Not the lag; left as is.
- Not done: release and live desktop copy (not asked).

## Release notes in the UI language (2026-11-02, branch `feat/localized-release-notes`)
Request (owner): What's new shows the release notes in the language of Application language (Türkçe → Turkish notes); every release has notes for en, zh and the 11 `locales/` languages; change release.yml for it. Owner choice (2026-11-02): a UI language without notes shows the English notes (option A).
Verified flow (before): `checkUpdate` (index.js) reads `body` of GitHub `releases/latest` → `releaseNotes()` → `info.notes`; the old copy's `install()` (client.js) saves `{version, notes, url}` in `dsh-composer-plus.update.whatsNew`; the next start asks `?current=1` and shows the saved English text. The UI language is `ctx.locale.getLocale().active` (`en`, `zh`, our 11 ids; dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 `LOCALE_IDS = ["zh","en"]`).
Design: the notes ship in the package (`release-notes.json`: `{version, notes: {<13 ids>: markdown}}`), because the copy that saves the record is the old one. Host `shippedNotes()`; `?current=1` answers `{current, notes?}` (still no network call). Client: `install()` saves the record also without notes; `load()` takes the Host's map, else `{en: saved notes}`; the dialog follows the active locale (`matchLanguage`: exact id, then base id; else `en`), `lang` on the text.
Invariants: no new network call, no new feature switch or screenshot (part of `autoUpdate`), Tether and `PLUS` unchanged, GitHub release body = `notes.en` (older copies save it), no new UI text (option A).
Acceptance: `scripts/release-notes.mjs` (`checkReleaseNotes`: version, 13 ids, no unknown id, no empty or cut text, no copy of `en`, tag, GitHub body) runs in release.yml before `npm test` and in `test/release-notes.test.mjs` (in `npm test`); `test/update.test.mjs` updated; `test/browser-whats-new.mjs` shows the Turkish notes in a `tr-TR` start on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; docs (features.json how ×13, llms, route; README Limits ×13; AGENTS.md Release); `npm test` exit 0; `npm pack --dry-run` lists `release-notes.json`.
Phases: 1 Host, release-notes.json (1.18.0 notes, 12 translations by subagents), script, release.yml, unit test; 2 client + update.test; 3 docs; 4 checks.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, release-notes.json, scripts/release-notes.mjs, package.json (files, test), .github/workflows/release.yml, test/{release-notes.test.mjs,update.test.mjs,browser-whats-new.mjs}, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, llms.txt, README*.md, AGENTS.md, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: a regional id gets its base language (`zh-TW` → simplified Chinese); the check finds missing, empty and untranslated notes, not bad translations; about 40 kB more per package; a live desktop copy without `release-notes.json` falls back to the saved English text.
Checkpoint (2026-11-03, not committed): phases 1–4 done. The README Limits line ×13 was added after the owner's chat approval (2026-11-03); `npm test` exit 0 again.
- Host: `shippedNotes` (index.js), `?current=1` → `{current, notes?}`. Client: record without notes, `notesByLanguage`, `matchLanguage` (also `readmeFile`), `notesIn`, dialog follows `locale.getSnapshot().active`, `lang` on the text.
- `release-notes.json` 1.18.0: `en` = the v1.18.0 GitHub body (checked equal), 12 translations by 3 subagents; each has the English shape, code spans and only language-pack labels in bold (script check). 43.8 kB in the package.
- `scripts/release-notes.mjs` + release.yml step before `npm test` (skipped without a tag or for tags without the script). AGENTS.md Release: write the file, `--notes` from `notes.en`, live copy gets the file.
- Docs: features.json (how ×13 with the **Application language** label of each pack, llms, route), `build:readme`.
- Checks: `npm test` exit 0; `test/update.test.mjs` red on 6 client/Host mutations, `test/release-notes.test.mjs` red on 6 check/Host mutations; `test/browser-whats-new.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (English UI: shipped English notes, tr-TR: Turkish title and notes), red with a client that ignores the UI language; `npm pack --dry-run` 90 files with `release-notes.json`; `git diff --check` clean.
- README Limits ×13 (after the update limit, by hand in each language): base-language match (`en-GB`, `zh-TW`), a language without notes (another pack's language, `pt-PT`) shows English, and a copy without a readable `release-notes.json` shows GitHub's English notes. `readme-labels` 5468 labels, `readme` and `build-docs --check` pass.
- Not requested: commit, version bump, release, live copy.

## Branch pill only in "In one list"; SDK came back over promes (2026-11-02, branch `fix/plan-branch-label-workspace-nesting`)
Request (owner): (1) "/plan" fix not complete; (2) no `main` or branch name on the chat rows of the Workspaces sidebar (Git branch on chat rows): the chat already sits under its folder; (3) promes shows inside the SDK folder; SDK was deleted and dsh restarted, SDK came back: find what adds it again and fix the cause.
- (1) Asked the owner what is still broken (the earlier request, 2026-10-28 "Branch per chat in plan mode", 5453e45, looked done). Answer: "satırdaki main yazısı gelmesin" = the same row pill as (2). The plan-mode branch start still passes (`test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` plan steps below).
- (2) Cause: `SessionBranchBadges` hid the pill only under a project row with the branch's own name (e9e8953/b6b3cee rule). Fix (client.js): no pill under any project row (dsh's Workspace and Workspace tree views, Ungrouped too, `projectRowOf`); the pill shows only in "In one list". The unused `useWorkspaces` read went. Tether: `rowBranch(_, _, oneList:)` = the same rule (By project: none).
- (3) Cause (verified): not Composer Plus and not dsh. `~/.dsh/local/codex-import/index.js` (a local plugin, in `~/.dsh/profiles/{desktop,web}/cordis.patch.yml`) calls `workspaceRegistry.create` at each start for every path in its `projects.json`, which has `…/Projeler/SDK` and `…/SDK/promes`. Evidence: `report-desktop.json` at 14:06:51.856Z lists `created: SDK`; `workspace.json` has the SDK record `createdAt 14:06:51.876Z`; dsh started 17:06:49 local. dsh's own `create` is only for a new path (`initialized: true` makes no bootstrap). dsh's tree nests a project in the nearest registered parent folder (`owningParentFolder`), so promes showed inside SDK. Fix (outside this repo, backup `index.js.bak-20261102-sdk`): codex-import imports each path once; `imported.json` next to `projects.json` keeps the imported paths (seeded with the 16 already imported paths, so nothing comes back); a missing folder is not marked; a broken state file creates nothing. Self-check `~/.dsh/local/codex-import/check.mjs` (red on the old file: no `runImport`). `projects.json` not changed.
Checks: `test/session-branch.test.mjs` client block (no pill under a project row, Ungrouped, a nested project row; "In one list" keeps it) red on HEAD client.js, green now. `test/browser-five-features.mjs` step 1 now: "In one list" shows main → feature/login, Workspace view has no pill. Real isolated dsh: `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (both plan steps, no pill under the branch folder, "In one list" keeps it); `test/browser-five-features.mjs` both branch steps PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2, its later step 2 (Changes → app.js) times out on both, also with HEAD's own client and test (not from this change). iOS unit tests 65/65.
Docs (subagent): en/zh description + 11 packs (each uses its own `groupBy.flat` label; zh now uses dsh's “单列表”, not “列表视图”), features.json `how` × 13 + `llms`, README FAQ + Limits line × 13, tether/FEATURES.md row; `build:locales`, `build:readme`. `assets/features/sessionBranch.webp` retaken (SHOT=1, 0.2.1-rc.2, "In one list" view, demo project, 3.4 kB).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-03): done, committed c1c54d4; main merged into the branch twice (29024dd Open memory typing lag; c3822fa release notes in the UI language), every plan section kept. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean. ## Task board shows dsh's live chats (2026-11-03, branch `feat/task-board-live-chats`)
Request (owner): the board must not be a notebook. A card is a dsh chat with its live state (orange: approval, question or plan review waits; green: finished). The user types only a prompt; a new dsh chat starts. AI writes the title and the tags. A click opens the chat. An orange card can be answered on the board. The board fits its area, long titles do not overflow, and "Hide finished" hides Done.
Owner choices (asked 2026-11-03): model = the new chat's own model (dsh's default; dsh's own session-title service writes the title, one extra call writes the tags); every dsh chat shows (not archived, blank or subagent); an orange card goes to Review; the old notebook tasks and the agent board tools are removed (board.json stays on disk, unread).
Columns (client, from dsh's own stores: `uiSession.sessionStatus`, `sessions.list`, `workspaces.list`): pending approval/question/plan-review → review (orange); running (or a subagent) → doing (blue); started from the board, never ran, < 60 s → todo; else → done (green dot while `completionUnread`). Projects = dsh workspaces; a `.worktrees/<name>` workspace counts as its repository. Newest first; Done shows 50 (Show older); no drag.
Host: `/api/composer-plus.board` `{on}` only; new `/api/composer-plus.board-chats` GET `?rev` → `{rev, chats:{id:{prompt,tags,at}}}`, POST `{project, prompt}` → `startBoardChat` (create in the workspace, `/command` through `commands.execute` else `prompt` queue with a timeout signal; failure archives the new chat; record in `board-chats.json`, owner-only, one change at a time, merged, 2,000 newest); tags: one background call (lowest effort, ≤ 200 tokens, 60 s, prompt ≤ 4,000 chars) to the chat's model (`selectionFor(agent).current`, else dsh default).
Client: quick answer panel (approval Reject/Allow once, plan Approve/Request changes with text, questions with options and own text), one answer at a time in every entry point, refused when the request changed; New task = project + prompt, one start at a time, the text stays on failure; Hide finished per device.
Tether: `board-chats` in PLUS (replaces `board-tasks`); the sheet computes the columns from the phone's sessions/workspaces/prompts/finished; orange card opens PromptSheet; + starts a chat; Hide finished.
Checks: test/task-board.test.mjs, test/task-board-client.test.mjs, test/tether-gateway.test.mjs, test/toggles.test.mjs, iOS tests, test/browser-task-board.mjs on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (SHOT=1), `npm test`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, locales/*.json, locale/*.json, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, llms.txt, README*.md, assets/features/taskBoard.webp, package.json (test), test/*, tether/{FEATURES.md,gateway/server.mjs,ios/Sources/*,ios/Tests/*}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: a never-opened chat may not get its approval card in this window (checked in the real run); `main` slot and pending `answer`/`dismiss` are not documented contracts; Done gets crowded; one more model call per board start.
Checkpoint (2026-11-03, not committed): phases 1–5 done.
- Host (index.js): old notebook board removed (store, ops, 8 agent tools, prompt section, Backlog); `boardTags`, `boardChatsStore`, `boardProject` (workspace id, not a Branch per chat folder), `boardTagsFor`, `startBoardChat`, `installBoard` (routes `board` `{on}` and `board-chats`). Evidence that a never-opened chat gets its approval in the client: dsh's gateway client resolves a waterfall's agent with `sessions.retainAgentScope` (0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2).
- Client: `boardColumns`, `createBoard` (one start, one answer), `BoardPage`, `BoardNewTask`, `BoardAnswer` (dsh's `approval`/`question`/`workspace` labels); the Backlog setting row removed; 32 en/zh keys, 42 old keys removed from the 11 packs.
- Tether: `board-chats` in PLUS; `boardColumns`/`boardProjectOf`/`TaskBoard` (Core.swift), `BoardSheet`/`BoardCardRow`/`BoardNewTaskSheet` (UI.swift); an orange card opens PromptSheet; FEATURES.md row.
- Docs: features.json (how ×13 by 3 translation agents, llms, routes), README intro/privacy/limits/FAQ ×13, `build:locales`, `build:readme`.
- Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` and `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red with each of 21 guards removed (one checked guard, `creating` in `create()`, is not reachable: no panel opens while a start runs, tested); iOS 64/64 (3 new); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (composer chat on the board, prompt → chat "Chess game" + tags from the chat's model, Doing → Done green, approval/question/plan review of never-opened chats orange and answered on the board, 1180 px fit, long title wraps, Hide finished after reload, click opens chat, switch off/on, board.json untouched); `taskBoard.webp` 15.7 kB (SHOT=1); `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
- Not done (not requested): commit, version bump, release, live copy, iPhone install.

## Task board: card titles in a narrow window; project search and "Active in the last 2 hours" (2026-11-03, branch `fix/board-titles-project-search`)
Request (owner, screenshot of the board at about 1200 px): (1) a text breaks badly in a narrow window; (2) a search box and a "last 2 hours" filter where the projects are listed. Asked (2026-11-03): (1) = the Task board card titles ("prs firması / na", "araştırm / ası"); (2) = the Task board's left project list and Tether's Task board (the sidebar already has dsh's search and Recent projects filter; the new chat project menu already has Project search).
- Cause of (1) (verified in the code and in a real dsh): the time ("14 dk. önce") sat next to the title in `.cpb-cardtop` (`flex:none`), and `.cpb-cardtitle` had `word-break:break-word`; in a 140 px column the title got about 60 px, so `overflow-wrap:anywhere` broke words. Fix: the time moves to the end of the meta row (`margin-left:auto`, always shown), the title has the card's full width and no `word-break` (an unbroken name longer than the card still wraps).
- (2) Client: `boardColumns` marks each project `recent` (a chat waiting, running, finished unseen, starting, or updated in the last 2 hours; archived, blank and subagent chats do not count; a Branch per chat chat counts for its repository) with an injectable `now`; the store has `query` (this page only), `recent` (localStorage `dsh-composer-plus.board.recent`), `setQuery`, `toggleRecent`, `shownProjects` (every word in the name, `matchesQuery`: case, accents, dotless ı). The side list has a search box (Esc empties it and stops the page's Esc) and an "Active in the last 2 hours" toggle; no match shows "No matching project". Labels reuse `projectPicker.search`, `projectPicker.empty`, `recentProjects.option` (all 13 packs already have them). The chosen project stays on the board when the list hides it. Same feature switch (taskBoard); no Host change.
- Tether: `BoardProject.recent` (`recentChat` rule), `boardProjectList`; the title opens `BoardProjectSheet` (searchable, Active in the last 2 hours kept in `boardRecentProjects`, Hide finished, All projects / projects); FEATURES.md row.
- Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (recent rule, search, filter, Esc, time row, no `word-break`) red on 8 mutations; iOS 65/65 (new `testProjectSearchAndRecent`); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` new step 4 part (140 px columns: no title word on two lines, a Turkish title "Pending durumunda kalma sorunu", time not beside the title; red on the old client: "Pending", "durumunda" broke) and step 6 (search DOCS → docs-site, Esc, filter hides docs-site, kept after a reload); step 3 checks the opened chat by its text (its sidebar row can be under "Show N more sessions"). PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. `taskBoard.webp` retaken (SHOT=1).
- Docs: en/zh description, features.json `how` (en by hand, 12 languages by a subagent with each pack's labels) and `llms`, README Limits line ×13, tether/FEATURES.md; `build:locales`, `build:readme`. `npm test` exit 0, `git diff --check` clean, Semgrep 0 findings (also `client.js` with `--max-target-bytes 0`). `taskBoard.webp` 18.1 kB.

## Fix: Task board search box too short (2026-11-03, branch `fix/board-search-height`)
Owner report: "projelerde ara kısmı çok küçük olmuş yükseklik olarak".
- Cause (verified in a real dsh, measured): `.cpb-side` is a flex column with `overflow:auto`; the box had no `flex:none`, so with a project list longer than the side (the owner has about 20 projects) the flex layout squeezed the input to 18 px (the project rows are buttons and keep 35 px). Its own height was also only 30 px with 13 px text.
- Fix (client.js CSS only): `.cpb-search{flex:none; height:36px; font-size:14px; padding:0 12px; radius 10px; margin 2px 0 6px}`, the size of a project row.
- Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` asserts `flex:none` and 36 px (red without `flex:none`); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 6 measures the box in a side that overflows: 36 px ≥ 35 px row; the old client fails (18 px). PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `taskBoard.webp` retaken (SHOT=1). `npm test` exit 0.
- Also (owner, screenshot of New task): "şuna da proje arama ekle dropdowna". The native `<select>` (no search) became a button that opens the projects with a search box (`draft.find`: the query, null while closed; `projectPicker.search`/`.empty` labels): typing filters by name (`matchesQuery`), Enter picks the first match (none: nothing), a click picks one, Esc closes only the list (stopPropagation, the panel stays), a focus in the prompt closes it; the prompt text stays. Tether: New task → Project opens `BoardProjectPick` (searchable list, `boardProjectList`). Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 5 mutations (filter, Enter, no-match guard, Esc, focus); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 1c (DOCS → docs-site with Enter, Esc keeps the panel, a click picks acme-web, prompt kept) PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; iOS 65/65; features.json `how` ×13 (12 by a subagent), `llms`, FEATURES.md.
- Then (owner, screenshot of New task): "dosya ekleme yok yeni görevde … yeni bir pencere açmasın yeni sohbete yönlendirsin ilgili projeyi seçip ilk mesajı gönderince de otomatik kanbana geri dönsün". Asked (2026-11-03): Tether too (yes); All projects → dsh's own New session pick; Todo = the new task before its first message.
  - Client: the panel (BoardNewTask: project picker, prompt, Start) is gone. New task (button, `n`) calls dsh's `uiWorkspace.startSession(project)` (All projects: undefined, dsh picks; Default workspace for New session applies) and finds the new chat: the blank chat in `mainView` that was not there before (dsh can retain both a moment). The store records it (`task`), POSTs `{sessionId}`, one New task at a time; a failure shows its reason. `watchBoardTask` (dsh's sessions list) opens the board again (`layout.selectPanel`) when that chat is not blank any more (once, only that chat). Todo shows that blank chat as "New task" / "Write the first message" (a click opens it); other blank chats never show. Removed: `starting`, `create`, `editDraft`, 5 keys (field.project, promptPlaceholder, start, startHint, starting), the select/pick/error CSS; new key `board.waitingFirst` (en, zh, 11 packs).
  - Host: POST board-chats is `{sessionId}` only (404 for a chat that dsh does not have); the record entry starts with `prompt: ''`. `boardPromptOf(events, from)` = the first user message of its own (after `inheritedEventCount`, not withdrawn by a Composer Plus or dsh-rewind marker, text only, ≤ 20,000). A `session/event` handler and the POST (the chat's log, for a message that came first) call `name`: the record change sets the prompt only while it is empty, then one tag call. Removed: `startBoardChat`, `boardProject`.
  - Tether: `Sess.blank`; `boardColumns(task:)` shows only that blank chat in Todo ("New task", state draft) and no blank chat makes a project recent; `Client.boardNewTask(project:)` (create in the workspace, All projects → `new-session-workspace` as Quick chat), `boardSent()` after each session list → `boardBack` opens the board sheet again from the chat view. BoardNewTaskSheet, BoardProjectPick and `TaskBoard.start` removed.
  - Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` (boardPromptOf: fork offset, both markers, user source; route; once; fast first message from the log; two names at once) red on 10 mutations; `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (open one at a time, failure, sent only for the task, watcher, openBoardTask main-view/before rules, Todo draft, page button, off) red on 11 mutations; iOS 66/66 (testNewTaskDraft, testLoadRev); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 1c (New task → dsh's new chat in acme-web, back to the board: Todo "New task", click opens it, first message from dsh's composer brings the board back) and every other task now started the same way: PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `taskBoard.webp` retaken (SHOT=1, 18.0 kB). Note: the chat that is open behind the board gets no green dot (dsh's own `isMain` rule); the green dot is checked on a chat that is not open.
- Then (owner): "projelere renk verme canbanda renkler sadece durumu belirten yeşil turuncu filan olsun kafa karıştırıyor". Removed the color per project (`BOARD_COLORS`, `boardColor`): the folder icon of a project row uses the row's text color, and a card's project chip (All projects) is plain text without a colored dot. The only colors on the board are the states (orange: waits for you, blue: running, green: finished and not read). Tether already showed the project as gray text: no change. Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 2 mutations (a colored folder, a dot in the chip); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 6 (folder stroke = row text color, no child in a card's chip) PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `taskBoard.webp` retaken (SHOT=1, 18.0 kB).

## Task board UX pass (2026-11-03, branch `fix/board-search-height`)
Owner request: "Görev panosunun kullanım deneyimini iyileştir" — first a list, then only the approved items. Inspected in a real isolated dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 (20 made-up projects, 1440/1180/960/900/760/600 px, light and dark, search, filter, answer panel) and the Tether code. Approved (all 8; same branch):
1. Todo shows only while a New task waits (desktop and Tether): an empty Todo took a quarter of the width and pushed Done off screen at 760 px.
2. A project with a chat that waits for you has dsh's orange dot in the project list (and All projects), with a label for screen readers; Tether's project list too.
3. Only the project list scrolls: the title, All projects, Search projects and the filter stay in place (20 projects scrolled them away).
4. The New task card's "Write the first message" wraps instead of being cut (900 px).
5. The header stays on one line in a narrow board: below a container width the Hide finished button shows only its icon (name in `title` and `aria-label`), and in a very narrow board the counts line hides (the column heads show the counts).
6. "Active in the last 2 hours" looks like a button: a bordered chip, a check mark while it is on.
7. Tether: the board opens on Review when a chat waits for you (else Doing); a column that is not shown any more falls back to Doing.
8. The tooltip of a project row shows the full name, then the path; Tether's project list shows a long name on two lines.
Invariants: the colors stay the states only (orange, blue, green); everything stays in the page's own `<style>` and markup (the feature switch removes it with the page); no new listener; the search-height fix (acbd2da) stays.
Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (each item, red on a mutation of each), iOS tests, `test/browser-task-board.mjs` on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (Todo hidden then shown, orange project dot while a chat waits, the list scrolls and the search box stays, header one line at 760 px, draft hint not cut at 900 px), `taskBoard.webp` retaken, features.json `how` ×13 + `llms`, `tether/FEATURES.md`, `npm test`, Semgrep. Not in scope: merge to main, release.
Done (2026-11-03): all 8 items. No new language keys (the dot's screen-reader text is `board.counts`, the narrow toggle's label `board.hideDone`; Tether's "Waiting for you" label is English source). Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 12 mutations (Todo always, no waiting count, dot always, no scroll box, tooltip path only, toggle text kept, no toggle label, draft hint cut, check mark always, no screen-reader text, …); iOS 66/66, TaskBoardTests red on 3 mutations (Todo always, start column always Doing, no waiting flag); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (no Todo before New task and after the first message; orange dot on acme-web while 3 chats wait, "3 waiting for you" for a screen reader, gone after the answers; only the project list scrolls, the search box does not move; tooltip name + path; filter border and check mark; at 900 and 760 px the header is one line, the toggle icon only at 760 px, the New task hint not cut; step 4 at 640 px for 3 columns); `taskBoard.webp` retaken (19.4 kB).

## Task board: progress on hover (2026-11-03, branch `fix/board-search-height`)
Owner request: "devam eden göreve üstüne gelince hover çıksın o anki ilerleme durumunu göstersin kısa özet yazı olarak ai tool call yapabilir ayarlardan seçilsin varsayılan hızlı seçili olsun". Asked: both modes, chosen in Settings, default without AI ("ikisi de olsun ayarlardan seçilsin default aisız olsun"); Tether: one line under a running card.
- Host (index.js, installBoard): `boardProgressOf(events, from)` reads a running chat's own log (after a fork's inherited events, rule 10; withdrawn steps left out, rule 9): the open turn's start time, the newest todo list (done, total, current item), the turn's newest tool call (name, a short detail, still running or not) and the agent's newest text line. Route `GET board-progress?sessionId=` → `{mode, since, todos, tool, said}`; `GET` → `{mode}`; `POST {mode: 'fast'|'ai'}` (Settings only; `board-progress.json`, owner-only). 404 for a chat that does not run, 409 while the board is off. Mode `ai`: one background call to the chat's model (lowest effort, 200 tokens, 30 s, the chat's own newest 6,000 characters from `goalTranscript`, earlier turns too: see F-03 below) → `summary`; at most one call per chat at a time, a new one only when the chat moved on and at least 20 s after the last; unload stops it.
- Client: a Doing card shows a box after 400 ms of hover (at once on keyboard focus): the summary (AI), "Running for …", "Step n of m: …", "Now/Last step: tool · detail", the agent's line. It reads the route every 2 s while it shows. `role=tooltip`, `aria-describedby`, Esc and leaving the card hide it. Settings → Task board → "Progress on hover": Fast (no AI) / AI summary (a short model call); one change at a time.
- Tether: the board reads board-progress for the Doing cards (at most 8) with the tags (every 5 s) and shows one line under each. Gateway PLUS: `board-progress` GET.
Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` (boardProgressOf: todos, running vs finished tool, open turn only, fork offset, both withdraw markers; route: modes, validation, 404/409, AI once per step, 20 s gap, one call at a time), `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (box content, polling stops, mode store), iOS tests, `test/browser-task-board.mjs` on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (hover box fast, Settings menu → AI summary → summary text, back to fast), shot with the box, docs ×13, route doc, FEATURES.md, `npm test`, Semgrep.
Done (2026-11-03, not committed yet): Host `boardProgressOf` + `board-progress` route (fast/ai, `board-progress.json` owner-only; the tag call and the summary call share `boardCall`); client box (400 ms hover, focus at once, Esc / leave / click / column scroll close it; fixed to the window, kept inside it; no native title on a running card), Settings → Task board → Progress on hover (Fast (no AI) default, AI summary; one change at a time, an older load is dropped); 11 keys in en, zh and the 11 packs; Tether one progress line under a running card (at most 8 reads per 5 s); gateway PLUS `board-progress` GET; docs `how` ×13, `llms`, ROUTES.md, FEATURES.md. Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` red on 12 mutations; `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 15 mutations; iOS 67/67, 2 mutations red; gateway 28/28; `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 8 PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `taskBoard.webp` with the box (23.1 kB). `npm test` exit 0, Semgrep 0 findings. Open: commit + push (Auto Review asks for the owner's approval in chat). The README note on AI summary: done with F-02 below.

## Open review findings (2026-11-03, review-guard of the uncommitted "progress on hover" change on 7b79738)
Status: RESOLVED locally (2026-11-03, /implement-plan), not committed or released. Fix plan: the owner's /prepare-fix-plan answer of 2026-11-03; owner choices: F-03 A (text only), F-02 README edit approved. Close an item only with the evidence in its "Resolved when" line.
- F-01 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-11-03): the progress box comes back by itself. Signature: `BoardPage` (client.js) keeps `hover` (`{id, place}`) when the hovered card leaves Doing (the turn ends, or it waits for an answer that is given elsewhere): the card is a new element in another column, so no `onMouseLeave`/`onBlur` runs; the render only hides the box (`hover !== null && view.columns.some(... 'doing' ...)`). When the chat runs again (next message, an automatic `/goal` round), the box mounts again at the old place, with no pointer on the card, and `pollBoardProgress` reads `board-progress` every 2 s until Esc, a click, a column scroll, another card's leave or the board closes. In AI summary mode each read can start a model call (one per new step and 20 s). The same happens when the 400 ms timer fires after the card left Doing. Evidence (2026-11-03, a /tmp script with a stateful hook shim on the current client.js, removed): hover + timer → box true; `running: false` → box false, `hover` kept; `running: true` again with no new hover → box true. Not caught: the client test's React shim keeps no state between renders; the browser test always moves the pointer away first. Resolved when: `BoardPage` clears `hover` (and the timer) on each render where the hovered card is not in Doing; a new stateful check in `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (Doing → Done → Doing gives no box, also for a timer that fires late) is red on the current code and green after; `npm test` exit 0; `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2.
- F-02 (P2, RESOLVED 2026-11-03): the README privacy and limits text does not name the AI summary call. Signature: README.md line 15 ("**Task board** calls the new chat's model once for the tags …") and line 170 ("sends it (its first 4,000 characters, nothing else) to that chat's model in one more request"), and the same two places in the 12 translations (line 17 and line 172). With AI summary, `summaryOf` (index.js, installBoard) sends `goalTranscript(events, from, 6000)` (user messages, agent replies, tool calls cut at 1,000 characters and the last 2,000 characters of each tool result) to the chat's model, at most once per new step and 20 s, while a box shows (every 2 s) or while Tether's board sheet is open (up to 8 running cards every 5 s). Both places name only the tag call, so the text reads as if the board sends nothing else. Impact: a user who reads the privacy text does not know that AI summary sends chat text and tool output to the model. Not caught: no test compares README privacy text with the routes. Resolved when: the two places in all 13 READMEs say what AI summary sends, when and how often; bold names are the language pack labels; `node test/readme.test.mjs`, `node test/readme-labels.test.mjs` and `npm test` exit 0.
- F-03 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-11-03): `docs/features.json` taskBoard `llms` (and so `llms.txt` line 44) says "the turn's newest 6,000 characters". `goalTranscript(events, from, …)` starts at the fork offset `from`, not at the open turn: it sends the chat's newest 6,000 characters, earlier turns too. Evidence (2026-11-03, Node on the current index.js): a log with two turns → the transcript has the first turn's request (`true`); `boardProgressOf` of the same log has the open turn only. Resolved when (owner choice): A) the `llms` text says "the chat's own newest 6,000 characters (earlier turns too)", `npm run build:readme`, `npm test` exit 0; or B) `summaryOf` starts at the open turn's first event, a new check in `test/task-board.test.mjs` (an earlier turn's text is not in the model input) is red now and green after, and the text says "the open turn's".
- F-04 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-11-03): Tether calls `board-progress` without the `plus` gate. Signature: `TaskBoardSheet` (tether/ios/Sources/UI.swift, `.task`) binds `b.bindProgress { plusCall("board-progress", …) }` with no `c.plus.contains("board-progress")`. Evidence (2026-11-03): of the 25 routes that the Swift code calls, only `board-progress` has no `plus.contains` gate (`diff` and `idea-history` run inside the `changes` and `ideas` sheets); the released gateway (main, 1.19.1) has no `board-progress` in `PLUS`, so it answers 403 `endpoint not allowed` (server.mjs `plus()`), up to 8 calls each 5 s while the sheet is open; `try?` hides it. Impact: wasted round trips that delay the next board read; no error shows. Rule added to AGENTS.md with this record: rule 8 (the phone calls a Host route only behind `c.plus.contains`). Resolved when: the sheet binds the progress call only when `c.plus.contains("board-progress")`; a new check in `test/tether-parity.test.mjs` (each `plusCall("<route>"` in the Swift sources has a `plus.contains` gate for that route or its sheet's route) is red now and green after; iOS tests pass; `npm test` exit 0.
- F-05 (P3, RESOLVED 2026-11-03): a test-only prop. Signature: `BoardPage({ …, readProgress })` and `...(readProgress === undefined ? {} : { readProgress })` (client.js): dsh's `inject()` never gives `readProgress`; `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` passes one and fills `reads`, which no assertion reads. `BoardProgressBox`'s `readProgress = readBoardProgress` parameter has no caller that sets it either. Resolved when: the prop, the spread and the box parameter are gone (the box calls `readBoardProgress`), the test has no `readProgress`/`reads`, `npm test` exit 0.
- F-01 fix: `BoardPage` (client.js) computes `boxShown` once; an effect on `[hover, boxShown]` forgets `hover` when its card is not in Doing (only that card's: `setHover((v) => (v?.id === hover.id ? null : v))`). A late 400 ms wait for that card sets `hover` and the same effect forgets it. Deviation from "Resolved when": the timer is not cleared there, so a pointer's wait on another card in Doing goes on (a third check). Evidence: the new stateful block in `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` was red before the fix ("the chat runs again, nobody pointed at it: no box"), green after. Mutations (temp copy, restored with `cmp`): no effect → red (same check); deps `[boxShown]` → red ("a late wait does not bring the box back either"); `hideProgress()` in the effect → red ("the wait on another card goes on").
- F-02 fix: README.md lines 15 and 170 and lines 17 and 172 of the 12 translations: what **AI summary** sends (the chat's newest part, at most about 6,000 characters, tool calls and the end of their results included), when (while a progress box shows, or while Tether's board is open, up to 8 cards), how often (after a new step, at most once each 20 s per chat), that **Fast (no AI)** sends nothing, and the settings file. Bold names from each language pack. Checks: `node test/readme.test.mjs` (13 files), `node test/readme-labels.test.mjs` (5,721 labels), `node test/feature-docs.test.mjs` pass.
- F-03 fix (A): taskBoard `llms` in docs/features.json says "the chat's own newest 6,000 characters (earlier turns too: …)"; `npm run build:readme` changed only llms.txt; `node scripts/build-docs.mjs --check` passes. No code change.
- F-04 fix: `TaskBoardSheet` (UI.swift) binds the progress call only when `c.plus.contains("board-progress")`. Evidence: the new check in `test/tether-parity.test.mjs` (each `plusCall` route is in PLUS and has a `plus.contains` gate, `diff`/`idea-history` through their sheets) was red before the fix (`the phone calls "board-progress" only behind plus.contains("board-progress")`), green after. iOS tests 67/67.
- F-05 fix: `readProgress` is gone from `BoardPage`, its spread and `BoardProgressBox` (the box calls `readBoardProgress`); the test has no `readProgress`/`reads`.
- All: `npm test` exit 0; Semgrep (`--config auto`, repo and `--max-target-bytes 0` on client.js/index.js) 0 findings; `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (no SHOT, the picture is unchanged); iOS 67/67; gateway 28/28; `git diff --check` clean. Not done (not requested): commit, push, release, live desktop copy, Tether install.
