## agentbox ideas: Next waiting chat and Option picks (2026-11-02, branch `docs/agentbox-feature-comparison`)
Source: a read-only comparison with [savannahfeder/agentbox](https://github.com/savannahfeder/agentbox) at 3241b2b (2026-10-30). The owner approved the ranked list and chose items 1 (`nextWaiting`) and 2 (`optionPicks`) for this plan. Items 3–8 (`laterChat`, `branchCleanup`, `agentProposals`, `leftoverPrograms`, `chatPriority`, `runQueue`) are not in this plan.

### Step 0: the three open questions of the comparison (checked 2026-11-02)
Checked in the installed dsh packages (`~/Documents/testdeepseekharness/work/dsh-0.2.0-rc.2` and `dsh-0.2.1-rc.2`) and, for item 2, on the running dsh (0.2.1-rc.5).
1. **Can the plugin register a keyboard shortcut? Yes.**
   - `@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-shortcuts` (both versions, same `types.d.ts`) gives `ctx.shortcuts.register({ id, label, aliases, defaults, regions, modals, resolve })`. The return value is a disposer. The command shows in dsh's own Keyboard shortcuts page, and the user can change its key.
   - dsh's own plugins do this through `ctx.inject(['shortcuts', …])` (`dsh-client-ui-theme` line 1955, `dsh-client-ui-workspace` `installWorkspaceShortcuts`). Our package does not list `@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-shortcuts` in `dsh.client.inject` today: add it, and use `injectScope(['shortcuts'], …)`, so an older dsh without the service only loses the key.
   - Limits (`isWebBindingAllowed`, `bindingIssue` in `dsh-client-shortcuts/lib/client.js`):
     - `register` throws on a duplicate id or on a default that overlaps another command's default for the same profile.
     - On the web on macOS and Windows, a two-modifier key must be the primary key plus Alt or Shift.
     - A key without a modifier is refused, except on desktop macOS and Windows.
   - Taken defaults (macOS): Cmd+N/K/O/B/P/T/,/` and Cmd+Alt+G/F/B/O on desktop; Cmd+Alt+N/K/O/B/P/T/, and Cmd+Shift+F/B/O on the web.
   - Chosen for `nextWaiting`: **Cmd+Shift+E** (Ctrl+Shift+E on Windows and Linux).
     - No dsh package (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2) and no Composer Plus handler uses `KeyE`.
     - [Chrome's shortcut list](https://support.google.com/chrome/answer/157179?hl=en&co=GENIE.Platform%3DDesktop) (read 2026-11-02) has no Cmd+Shift+E or Ctrl+Shift+E.
     - Cmd+Shift+J is not usable: Chrome uses it for Downloads on a Mac and for Developer Tools on Windows and Linux.
     - dsh's own `bindingIssue` accepts Cmd+Shift+E for desktop macOS, Windows and Linux and for web macOS and Windows. I ran the functions from `dsh-client-shortcuts/lib/client.js` on Node.
     - It answers `unsupported-browser` for web Linux. dsh's own commands (`installWorkspaceShortcuts`) also set no `web:linux` default. Do the same: on web Linux the command has no key until the user sets one.
   - Fixed keys: `registerFixed` + `observeFixedInput` (used by `dsh-client-ui-theme` for Cmd+=) show a key in the shortcut list without making it editable. Not used here.
2. **Do background jobs and terminal tabs carry `DSH_SESSION_ID`? Yes.**
   - Code:
     - `dsh-shell-env` `collect()` puts `DSH_SESSION_ID` (the agent's chat id) into every bash tool call.
     - `dsh-tool-bash` passes it as `dshEnv` to the background path (`run_in_background`) and to the promoted path too.
     - `dsh-bash-local` spreads `spec.dshEnv` into the child `env`.
     - The persistent bash tool (`dsh-terminal-bash` `childEnvironment`) sets `DSH_SESSION_ID` and `DSH_PTY_SESSION_ID`.
     - A terminal tab (`dsh-api-terminal-controller`, line 992) sets `env: { DSH_SESSION_ID: agent.id }`.
   - Runtime: a background job of this chat (`node -e 'setTimeout(…)' probe`) showed this chat's `DSH_SESSION_ID` in `ps -E -ww` (macOS 27.0.1, same user).
   - Caveat: a process that rewrites its title (Node `process.title=…`) overwrites the area that `ps -E` reads: `ps -E` then shows no variables. A probe with `process.title` showed 0 variables. A leftover-program finder needs a second source, such as `ps -o ppid` up to the shell of the chat, or it misses such processes. This matters for item 6 (`leftoverPrograms`), not for this plan.
3. **Is there a hook that can hold the start of a turn? Yes, `agent/pre-step`.**
   - `dsh-agent-loop` awaits `dispatch.waterfall("agent/pre-step", { agent, messages, turn, step, signal }, next)` before every step (line 909 on 0.2.1-rc.2, 911 on 0.2.0-rc.2). The first step of a turn comes right after `turn/start` is logged.
   - A handler may await before `next()`. `dsh-compaction-basic` does this: it compacts and then calls `next()`. A handler may also answer `{ kind: 'reject' }`, which ends the turn as blocked (`dsh-api-session-controller` does this for archived chats).
   - So a run limit (item 8, `runQueue`) can hold a turn at its first step. The `turn/start` event is already in the log then, so the chat shows "running" while it waits. This matters for item 8, not for this plan.

### Objective
1. `nextWaiting`, client only, on by default:
   - One action opens the next chat that waits for you: first the orange dot (a question, an approval or a plan review that you can answer), then the green dot (a finished turn that you did not open), the longest-waiting first in each group. The chat on screen is skipped.
   - Entry points:
     - a "Next waiting chat (N)" button in the sidebar header, shown only while N > 0, placed like Collapse all projects;
     - a dsh shortcut `composerPlus.nextWaiting`, default Cmd+Shift+E / Ctrl+Shift+E (none on web Linux), which the user can change on dsh's Keyboard shortcuts page.
   - Tether: a "Next waiting chat (N)" button in the Sessions toolbar and in the chat title menu, from data that Tether already has (`waitingChats`, `finished`).
2. `optionPicks`, client only, on by default, no model call of its own:
   - dsh's question card (`ask_user_question`): keys `1`–`9` pick option N of the question on screen, the same as a click (dsh's `choose`). Enter still submits, as in dsh.
   - The latest finished answer of the open chat: when its last section is an option list, each option gets a button. A click sends `Option N: <text>` as your message (`queue` mode, like a typed message). The option marked "(recommended)" is highlighted.
   - Tether: the same buttons under the latest assistant message, which send through the phone's existing `prompt(...)`.

### Option list rule (`parseOptions`, one pure function, also in Swift)
- The text is the closing answer: the `text` blocks of `turn-tail` `closing.blocks`, joined with no separator, as dsh's own `assistantText` joins them (copy button; both versions). Reasoning and tool blocks are left out. A running or a stopped (`interrupted`) answer gives nothing.
- The options are a numbered list (`1.` or `1)`, 1 to 9 items, numbers in order from 1) that is the last block of the answer. Only blank lines may come after it.
- It counts only under a Markdown heading (`#`–`####`) or a bold line whose text is one of these words, case- and accent-insensitive, with or without a colon:
  - "Options", "Seçenekler", "Choices", "Next steps";
  - the `optionPicks.heading` word of each of the 13 language packs (for example zh "选项").
- An item with "(recommended)", "(önerilen)" or the language pack's `optionPicks.recommended` word is the recommended one. The marker is removed from the button text.
- Each button text is the item's first line, at most 200 characters. The whole item (also its later lines) goes into the sent message.
- No match: no buttons. A list in the middle of the answer, a list in a code block, a list under another heading, and more than 9 items: no buttons.
- No system-prompt rule that asks agents to end with an Options section (owner's decision, see Decisions).

### Invariants
- Every AGENTS.md rule:
  - own `FEATURES` key installed with `feature(id, setup)`; switching off removes every listener, observer, DOM host, style and shortcut that the feature added;
  - screenshot (`SHOT_OF`, `SHOTS`, `assets/features/<shot>.webp` ≤ 60 kB, demo dsh with made-up projects only);
  - texts `en` + `zh` + 11 `locales/*.json`, then `npm run build:locales`;
  - `docs/features.json` (`how` ×13, `llms`, `shot`), `npm run build:readme`, Limits and FAQ ×13 by hand;
  - unit tests in `package.json` `test`; real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2;
  - `tether/FEATURES.md` rows.
- Neither feature opens a port, runs a server or starts a model turn without a click. `optionPicks` starts a turn only on the user's click or key, through dsh's own send path. So neither feature goes into `OFF_BY_DEFAULT`.
- No new Host route, so no `PLUS` entry and no Host switch. The Turn rollback route `GET ?sessionId` answers two more fields (phase 3, see Decisions): `withdrawn` and `inherited`; its `routes` text in `docs/features.json` says so.
- Rule 9: no option buttons for an answer that Turn rollback or dsh-rewind-plugin withdrew. The browser cannot read the log, so the rollback route gives `withdrawn` (the spans of both marker kinds) and the client rollback store keeps it (merged like `spans`). Test both marker kinds in Tether with `withdrawKinds` / `withoutRolledBack`.
- Rule 7: one pick at a time per chat. A second click, key or tap while a send runs does nothing. After a pick, all buttons of that answer stay off until a new turn ends. The guard is in the store, so it covers button, key and Tether alike.
- Rule 10: the latest answer of a forked chat may be an inherited one. Show buttons only when the closing answer's seq is at or after the fork's `inheritedEventCount` (a fork starts with no buttons until its own first answer). The browser has no such value (checked: dsh's client session, chat view and session list do not carry it; only the Host `Session.inheritedEventCount`, both versions), so the rollback route gives it as `inherited`.
- No new dependency; no change to dsh files.

### Acceptance criteria
- `nextWaiting`:
  1. With chats A (question, since t0), B (approval, since t1 > t0), C (green) and D (green, older than C), the order is A, B, D, C. The chat on screen is skipped. A subagent row and a request with `answerable: false` do not count.
  2. Button: hidden when N = 0; N updates live; a click opens the chat (`uiWorkspace.openSession`).
  3. Shortcut: listed on dsh's Keyboard shortcuts page; `resolve` answers `blocked` with a translated reason when N = 0; it works while the composer has the focus (`regions: ['page', 'editable']`).
  4. Switched off: the button, its host and style, the shortcut row and every subscription are gone (a test counts them).
  5. dsh without the shortcuts service: the button still works, no error.
- `optionPicks`:
  1. `parseOptions` table test passes: the match cases, the no-match cases above, CRLF, a code fence, nested lists, 10 items, the recommended markers in 3 languages, an empty item.
  2. Question card: `1`–`9` call dsh's own option button (a `click()` on the N-th `[role="radio"]` or `[role="checkbox"]` in `[data-question-key]`). Keys do nothing:
     - when the focus is in a text field (the custom answer field);
     - with a modifier key held;
     - during IME composition;
     - when N is greater than the number of options;
     - when no question card is on screen.
  3. Answer buttons show only under the newest `turn-tail` of the open chat, when the chat is idle, the composer is empty, and the answer is not withdrawn (both kinds) and not inherited (fork).
  4. A click sends `Option N: <item>` once (`queue`). A double click, a second key or a Tether tap during the send sends nothing more. A failed send shows dsh's notice and enables the buttons again.
  5. While buttons show, Next message prediction hides its guess (one owner of the space above the composer); it comes back when the buttons go.
  6. Switched off: no key listener, no buttons, no style.
- Both:
  - `npm test` exit 0 (new tests in `package.json`; `test/toggles.test.mjs` 51 rows, 44 on after phase 4 (50 and 43 after phase 2); `tether-parity`, `shots`, `feature-docs`, `readme-labels`, `readme`, `locale`).
  - iOS unit tests green.
  - New `test/browser-agentbox-ideas.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
  - `git diff --check` clean.

### Phases and checks
| Phase | Work | Check |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `nextWaiting` core: pure `waitingOrder(statusSnapshot, byId, waits, now)` → `{ order, onScreen }` (shared with Open waiting chat when idle; `watchIdleSwitch` uses it, same behavior) | `test/next-waiting.test.mjs` (order, skip, subagent, answerable); `test/idle-switch.test.mjs` unchanged and green |
| 2 | `nextWaiting` UI: sidebar header button (host like `CollapseProjectsButton`), shortcut through `injectScope(['shortcuts'])`, `@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-shortcuts` in `package.json` `dsh.client.inject` | same test: fake `shortcuts` service (register, disposer, blocked reason), off removes all; no shortcuts service → button only |
| 3 | `optionPicks` parser and store: `parseOptions`, `createOptionPicks` (per chat: picked seq, busy; one send at a time) | `test/option-picks.test.mjs` (parser table; guard from button, key and a second caller; failure enables again; rollback span; fork offset) |
| 4 | `optionPicks` UI: question-card keys (window `keydown` in capture, only when `[data-question-key]` is in the open chat); answer buttons portaled under the newest `[data-chat-flow-kind="turn-tail"]` row (like `MessageLinks`); send through `ctx.conversation.send(text)` in the chat's scope (both versions: `IConversation.send(text)`); prediction hides while buttons show | same test with fake DOM; `test/predict-client.test.mjs` still green |
| 5 | Tether: `waitingOrder` and `parseOptions` in `Core.swift`; Sessions toolbar button and title-menu item; option buttons under the latest assistant `LineView`; one tap at a time | iOS unit tests (order, parser table same as JS, withdrawn both kinds, fork) |
| 6 | Texts ×13, `npm run build:locales`; `docs/features.json` `how` ×13 + `llms`; `npm run build:readme`; Limits and FAQ ×13; keyboard table rows in 13 READMEs; screenshots (`SHOT=1`, demo data); `tether/FEATURES.md` | `npm test`; `test/browser-agentbox-ideas.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; simulator check of Tether with `tether/build/demo-gateway.mjs` |

### Allowed paths
`client.js`, `package.json` (`test` script, `dsh.client.inject`), `index.js` (only `SHOTS`), `locales/*.json`, `locale/*.json` (generated), `assets/features/{nextWaiting,optionPicks}.webp`, `docs/features.json`, `README*.md`, `llms.txt`, `test/next-waiting.test.mjs`, `test/option-picks.test.mjs`, `test/browser-agentbox-ideas.mjs`, `test/toggles.test.mjs` (counts), `tether/FEATURES.md`, `tether/ios/Sources/{Core,UI}.swift`, `tether/ios/Tests/Tests.swift`, `tether/ios/Resources/*` (strings), `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`.

### Decisions
- One shared ordering function for Open waiting chat when idle and Next waiting chat, so the two features never disagree about "next".
- The shortcut is an editable dsh command, not a fixed key, so a user with a key conflict changes it in dsh's own page.
- Question card keys: digits, not Cmd+digit, as dsh shows the numbers on the card. They are local keys, not a dsh shortcut: dsh refuses a default without a modifier on the web.
- Answer buttons send a normal visible message (`Option N: …`), not a hidden note, so the chat log and Tether show what you chose.
- Phase 3: the two values that the browser cannot read (rule 9 `withdrawn`, rule 10 `inherited`) come from the existing Turn rollback route (`GET ?sessionId`) and its client store, which every chat already loads. No new route. The route answers this GET also while the Turn rollback switch is off (the switch only refuses POST), so phase 4 calls `rollback.load(sessionId)` itself and shows no buttons until that answer came (an older Host without the fields: `withdrawn` falls back to `spans`, `inherited` to 0).
- **No system-prompt rule (owner, 2026-11-02):** do not add a rule that asks agents to end with an Options section. The owner will discuss it separately later. `optionPicks` works only on answers that already end with such a list.
- Phase 4: the answer buttons use dsh's own list slot `conversation.chat.turnTail` (both versions; dsh-client-ui-plan and dsh-client-ui-deliverables use it), not a DOM portal. The slot gives `seq` (the answer's closing seq) and `turn` as dsh's turn object, not a number: the cell compares only `seq`. The chat, session and input hooks come from dsh's session scope (`useChat`, `useSession`, `useInput`, both versions).
- Phase 4: Next message prediction steps back through a synchronous gate (`createOptionsGate`): it reads the same chat view and asks "does this chat offer options now (not picked, not withdrawn as far as this window knows)". A gate that waits for the buttons to render was too late: the prediction call started first (seen in the real dsh). The gate is open only while the `optionPicks` switch is on.
- Phase 4: the buttons wait for a fresh Turn rollback GET per state of the idle chat (`createRollbackCheck`, key = node count and newest seq). So a rollback marker, from either plugin, is known before the buttons show. One GET per state, shared by every answer of the chat.
- Phase 4: the 800 ms key guard counts from the moment a question shows, per question of a card (`data-question-key` and the question's `aria-labelledby`). With two cards on screen, only the card that has the focus takes the key.
- Phase 4: the plan said "the composer is empty" for the buttons. While you type, the buttons hide, but the chat still offers options, so the prediction stays back too (it would only show for an empty composer anyway).

### Risks
- dsh markup changes (`[data-question-key]`, `role="radio"`, `data-chat-flow-kind="turn-tail"`, the sidebar header classes) make the keys or buttons disappear. They fail closed: no wrong send. Mark each with a `ponytail:` comment and the upgrade path (a dsh slot).
- A digit key pressed just after a question card appears can pick an option the user did not read. agentbox found the same problem and ignores a pick in the first 800 ms after the view changes. Use the same 800 ms guard.
- `parseOptions` can miss lists that agents write in other shapes. This is safe (no buttons). Keep the rule strict; widen it only with a test case.
- Cmd+Shift+E can be taken by a browser extension or another app; the user can change the key on dsh's Keyboard shortcuts page.

Label: first line without `**`, `__`, backticks and link targets, at most 200 characters; text: the whole item, its common indent removed.
  - `chatOffer(chat, spans, from)` → `{ seq, turn, latest, options }`: the closing answer of the newest turn that `spans` did not withdraw, at or after `from`; not `running` or `interrupted`; `latest` is the newest turn, withdrawn ones too.
  - `createOptionPicks(send)`: per chat `{ busy, picked: { seq, latest }, error }` (frozen); `open(id, offer)`; `pick(id, offer, n)` sends `Option n: <item>` once. Refused while one runs (any answer of that chat) and for a picked answer until a newer turn shows; a failed send keeps the offer open with the reason.
- Host and rollback store (rules 9 and 10, see Decisions): Turn rollback `GET ?sessionId` also answers `withdrawn` (both marker kinds) and `inherited` (`Session.inheritedEventCount`, else 0); `createRollback` keeps both (`withdrawn` merged like `spans`, also on a finished rollback). `docs/features.json` route text and `docs/ROUTES.md` updated.
- Tests:
  - New `test/option-picks.test.mjs` (in `package.json` after `next-waiting`): the parser's match cases and 26 no-match cases (CRLF, fences, nested and indented lines, 9 and 10 items, marks in 6 languages, empty items, headings that only contain the word); `chatOffer` (newest turn, reasoning left out, dsh's join, running, stopped, no answer, both marker kinds as spans, fork offset); the store guard (second click during a send, another answer during a send, picked twice, other chat independent, newer turn and rollback open again, failure keeps the offer, bad input, frozen states).
  - `test/turn-rollback.test.mjs`: `inherited` (a fork, not a count), `withdrawn` with a dsh-rewind marker. `test/turn-rollback-client.test.mjs`: `inherited`, `withdrawn` (both kinds, older Host, merge with a rollback that ends during a load); this also covers the old `spans` merge, which no test checked before.

## Task board: no Todo column, a simpler Done column (2026-11-04, branch `feat/simplify-task-board-done-column`)
Owner request: remove the Todo column; add **Clear all** to Done (it takes every card that is in Done now off the board; the chats stay in dsh); remove **Hide finished**; an × in each Done card takes only that card off the board (only Done cards have it). No action deletes a chat. Owner answers (2026-11-04): the New task that waits for its first message shows at the top of **Doing** (no blue dot); removed cards are kept on the Mac in a file (they stay removed after a dsh restart, Tether sees the same list); when a removed chat runs again, its card comes back (Doing while it runs, then Done again).
- Host (index.js, installBoard): `board-chats.json` gets `hidden: {<sessionId>: at}` next to `chats` (the same store, one change at a time, merged into the newest data, owner-only, at most BOARD_LIMITS.hidden = 5,000, the oldest go). `GET board-chats` adds `hidden: [ids]`; `POST board-chats {hide: [ids]}` (1..5,000 ids) adds them and answers `{rev, hidden}`; a chat that runs at that moment is not added. `agent/status` running → that chat leaves `hidden` (a change that finds nothing writes nothing).
- Client: BOARD_STATUSES doing/review/done; the draft card first in Doing; Done leaves out `hidden` + the ids of the hide in progress (only Done: a removed chat that runs shows in Doing); × (Done cards only) and **Clear all** (Done column head; every Done id of the shown project, also the older ones behind Show N older) call `board.hide(ids)`: one hide at a time in every entry point, the cards go at once, the Host's answer is the newest list, a read that started before it is dropped, a failure brings them back with its reason. No × and no Clear all when the Host sends no `hidden` (an older Host half before a restart). Hide finished, its key, its CSS and its localStorage use are gone.
- Texts: new `board.clearAll`, `board.clearAllHint`, `board.hideCard`; removed `board.status.todo`, `board.hideDone`; `feature.taskBoard.description` updated (en, zh, 11 packs).
- Tether: no Todo (draft first in Doing), no Hide finished toggle; × in Done rows and a Clear all row, only when the Mac's answer has `hidden` (an older Mac: none); one hide at a time, an older read dropped. FEATURES.md row.
- Docs: features.json `how` ×13, `llms`, route texts (ROUTES.md); README privacy + limits ×13 and the dev comment ×13; `build:locales`, `build:readme`.
Invariants: no chat is deleted or changed; the colors stay the states only; × and Clear all never overwrite each other's state (Host merges, client one at a time + seq); the feature switch removes everything as before.
Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` (hide merge, limits, running skip, running unhides, persistence, 400s), `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (no Todo, draft in Doing, hidden only in Done, Clear all ids, one hide at a time, stale read dropped, failure, no × outside Done or with an older Host), iOS tests, `test/browser-task-board.mjs` on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (draft in Doing, × and Clear all, still removed after a reload and a dsh restart, chats still in dsh, a removed chat that runs again comes back), `taskBoard.webp` retaken, `npm test`.
Done (2026-11-04, not committed): all items. Host `boardChatsStore` keeps `hidden` in the same file (`change(fn(chats, hidden))`, false = no write), POST `{hide}`, `agent/status` running unhides; client `hide()` (one at a time, seq drops an older read, failure toast), × on the meta row of Done cards (not next to the title: a 140 px column broke "durumunda"), Clear all in the Done head; Tether `TaskBoard.hide`, × and Clear all rows, no Hide finished toggle, draft first in Doing. Gateway: no change (`board-chats` POST is in PLUS; `{hide}` is not a switch body). New keys `board.clearAll`, `board.clearAllHint`, `board.hideCard`; removed `board.status.todo`, `board.hideDone`. Checks: `test/task-board.test.mjs` red on 9 Host mutations; `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 13 client mutations; iOS 68/68 (new `testHide`), red on 2 Swift mutations; `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (SHOT=1, `taskBoard.webp` 14.9 kB) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (new step 3: ×, Clear all, chats still in dsh's sidebar, a removed chat that runs again comes back; step 9: dsh restart keeps them off); `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; Semgrep: only the 11 test `eval` findings that HEAD has too. ## Project memory: 7 more features (2026-11-04, branch feat/project-memory-features)
Request (owner): plan and build 7 features for Project memory ("Hafızayı aç" dialog): (1) export to AGENTS.md with a diff preview, (2) do not use the memory in this chat, (3) copy notes to another project (all or none), (4) the source chat link of a note, (5) memory suggestions from a chat (one model call on a click), (6) find stale notes (paths, npm scripts, branches), (7) version history with diff and restore. Owner choices (asked 2026-11-03): each feature gets its own `FEATURES` id; the per-chat setting of (2) is kept in the Host in `storages/dsh-composer-plus/memory-chats.json`.
Objective: the 7 features, shared parts built once, `npm test` green after each step; real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 for (2) and (5); iOS tests green.
Invariants: every memory save goes through `memoryStore.batch` (all or none, a copy first when History is on); memory actions use one Host lock (`editing`) and one client `busy`; project files only through realpath inside the folder + regular file + `O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK`; the browser never sends a path (a project id or a workspaceId); withdrawn messages out, `/compact` kept, forks start at `inheritedEventCount`; no new dependency.
Ids / routes (all inside `installMemory`, 409 while Project memory is off):
- `memoryHistory` (Host switch, on by default) `memory-history`; `memoryExport` `memory-export`; `memorySource` (no route: `source` in the `memory-edit` view); `memoryCopy` `memory-copy`; `memoryStale` `memory-stale`; `memoryPerChat` (Host switch, on by default) `memory-chat`; `memorySuggest` `memory-suggest`. Tether `PLUS` gets the 6 routes (switch bodies stay 403 through `isSwitch`).
Phases (each: code, en/zh + 11 locales, shot, features.json + build:readme, README limits/FAQ ×13, tether/FEATURES.md + PLUS + Swift, test in `package.json`, toggles counts, `npm test`):
0. [done] This section.

Risks: `agent.ctx.tools.restrict` throws on unknown names (call it only while the memory tools are registered); a subagent may not inherit the restriction (fallback: the memory tools refuse in a chat with the memory off); switching the memory off mid-chat changes the system prompt once (cache miss); AGENTS.md export can repeat notes that the memory section also has.
Out of scope: version bump, release, live desktop copy, iPhone install, commit.

## Anonymous daily active count (2026-11-04, branch chore/plugin-user-count)
Request (owner): count how many copies run, per version, fully anonymous; no off switch (owner decision); commit and push the branch only (no release).
Objective: each copy asks `github.com/<repo>/releases/download/v<version>/active.txt` at most once per UTC day; GitHub raises the asset's `download_count`; `node scripts/active-count.mjs` shows the counts per version and the change since its last run.
Invariants: no id, cookie, query or chat data in the request (only `user-agent: dsh-composer-plus`); `redirect: 'manual'`, body not read; no request from a git checkout (`.git` next to index.js), another package name (`@local/...`) or a non-x.y.z version; the day is kept in `storages/dsh-composer-plus/active.json` (0600, atomic) and in memory; network error, 403, 429, 5xx: no day saved, retry in an hour; 404 (asset missing): day saved. No route, switch, `FEATURES` id or UI.
Checks: `test/active-count.test.mjs` (in `npm test`), `test/release-notes.test.mjs` (asset check), `npm pack --dry-run` (no active.txt, no scripts/), real headless dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 from a packed copy (active.json appears) and from the symlinked repo (no file).
Allowed paths: index.js, assets/active.txt, scripts/{active-count,release-notes}.mjs, test/{active-count,release-notes}.test.mjs, package.json (test script), README*.md (Privacy), AGENTS.md (Release), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: users who share one NAT IP and ask in the same ~10 min count once (GitHub dedup, per parttimenerd/download-count-experiment); the release event payload must list `assets` (else the release check stops: upload the file, run the workflow again with `-f tag`).
Out of scope: version bump, release, live desktop copy, merge.
Last checkpoint (2026-11-04): done. `npm test` green; `npm pack --dry-run` has no active.txt or scripts/; real headless dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2: packed copy writes active.json with today's UTC day, symlinked checkout writes nothing; `node scripts/active-count.mjs` runs against the live API (no release has active.txt yet). Next: the next release attaches assets/active.txt.

## Remote computers: a computer picker for dsh (2026-11-04, branch `feat/dsh-multi-computer-picker`)
Request (owner): like Tether's Mac picker over Tailscale, the dsh desktop and web UI (with Composer Plus) can open the dsh of other computers. A picker at the top; pick a computer and continue its chats there. Owner choices (asked 2026-11-04): (1) open the remote dsh's own UI, which its Composer Plus exposes on Tailscale only (off by default, pairing with Approve); (2) the picker sits in `conversation.header.leading` (left of the chat title); (3) desktop opens the computer in dsh's right-sidebar Browser tab, full screen when possible; (4) every computer is a Mac with desktop dsh and Composer Plus.
Objective: feature `remoteComputers` (OFF_BY_DEFAULT, Host switch): add (paste a `dsh-computer://pair` link, Approve on the other Mac), list, rename, remove, pick, open; the other Mac's "Let other computers open this Mac" front door; `npm test` green; real isolated dsh check on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
Facts (checked): the dsh UI talks to one Host fixed at page load; desktop Host binds `127.0.0.1:0`; dsh accepts only loopback/bind-IP/trustedHosts `Host`, same-authority `Origin`, no `sec-fetch-site: cross-site`, and an authority-bound cookie (`GET /?token=` → 303 + cookie; a cookie alone serves the index); the `/api` bridge passes the raw request headers to Host routes; desktop window navigation goes to the system browser; the Browser tab (iframe on web, `<webview>` with a per-workspace partition on desktop) opens loopback URLs; `ISidebarRight.openTab(kind, {params})` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; `conversation.header.leading` is a root single slot with no shipped occupant (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, desktop 0.2.1-rc.5).
Design:
- B (the Mac that is opened): `createGateway({mode: 'computer'})` in `tether/gateway/server.mjs`, own dir `storages/dsh-composer-plus/computers/`, port 19392 (`DSH_COMPUTERS_PORT`), bound to its Tailscale IPv4 only, no Bonjour, no `$events`, no admin page; `/pair` needs `kind: 'computer'`; after the token check it streams every request and upgrade to B's dsh: drops host/origin/cookie/authorization/sec-fetch-site/x-composer-plus-via, sets loopback host, same-authority origin (only when sent), dsh cookie, `x-composer-plus-via: computer`; drops `set-cookie`, makes `location` relative. Rate limit only before auth.
- A (the Mac that opens): `createRelay` per computer on `127.0.0.1:0`: pinned TLS (fingerprint checked at `secureConnect`, before any byte), one-time 60 s ticket `/?cp-ticket=` → HttpOnly SameSite=Strict cookie + 303 `./`; checks Host, Origin, sec-fetch-site; adds `Authorization: Bearer`.
- Host route `/api/composer-plus.computers` (GET state; POST `{on}`, `{accept}`, `{action: code|approve|deny|revoke|add|cancel-add|open|rename|remove}`), store `storages/dsh-composer-plus/computers.json` (0600, atomic). Not in Tether `PLUS`.
- Client: `ComputerPicker` (header leading, no-drag, menu: This Mac, computers, Add a computer…), `AddComputerDialog` (shell.overlay), Settings panel (computers list; accept switch with warning, pairing link, requests, devices). Hidden when the page is served through a front door (`via`).
Invariants: the front door never starts without the owner's accept switch; every pairing needs Approve on B; a pin mismatch sends nothing; a phone token never opens the dsh UI; switching the feature off closes every relay and the front door; one add/open/accept action at a time in every entry point.
Phases: 0 discovery (no product code) · 1 gateway computer mode + relay + `test/computers.test.mjs` · 2 Host route + store · 3 client + `test/computers-client.test.mjs` · 4 texts, docs, screenshot, FEATURES.md · 5 `test/browser-computers.mjs` on both dsh versions, `npm test`, `npm pack --dry-run`.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/FEATURES.md, locales/*.json, locale/*.json, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, assets/features/remoteComputers.webp, test/computers*.test.mjs, test/browser-computers.mjs, package.json (test script), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the desktop webview may refuse the relay or its cookie (fallback: system browser); no public full-screen API for the right sidebar (fallback: normal width); the right sidebar is per chat.
Out of scope: version bump, release, live desktop copy, iPhone changes, commit.
Phase 0 (code reading, 2026-11-04): the bridge keeps request headers (`Object.entries(req.headers)` → `new Request`) on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2, so `x-composer-plus-via` reaches the Host route; `authorizeIndex` serves the index for an authenticated cookie without a token. Still to see in a real dsh: the Browser tab shows the relay page.
Phases 1-3 done (2026-11-04): `createRelay`, `createGateway({mode: 'computer'})`, `computerLink`/`parseComputerLink`, `hopHeaders`, `relativeLocation` (server.mjs); `computersService`, `pairWithComputer`, route `computers` (index.js); `createComputers`, `openComputer`, `ComputerPicker`, `createAddComputer`, `AddComputerDialog`, `ComputersPanel` (client.js). Found in the real app and fixed: the waiting text had no Mac name (the link's `mac`/`host` now); dsh's Browser tab is a same-site navigation (the relay allows only GET `/` navigations from it, with `frame-ancestors 'self' <dsh page>`); a removed Mac showed the front door's JSON (the relay now answers its own text and marks the computer); the picker label kept the old name (`show` makes a new snapshot); the menu refreshes at each open. Checks: `test/computers.test.mjs` (11) + `test/computers-host.test.mjs` (7) red on 20/20 mutations; `test/computers-client.test.mjs` red on 13/13; `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` 28/28 and `test/mobile-connect.test.mjs` still pass.
Phase 4 (2026-11-04): en/zh texts; 11 locales (subagent, `locale.test` pass); `remoteComputers.webp` 7.5 kB (SHOT run, checked by eye); SHOTS, SHOT_OF, FEATURES.md row (`no`), features.json en/zh + route; README.md privacy/limits/FAQ. 11 languages of `how` and the 12 translated READMEs: subagent.
Phase 5 (2026-11-04): `test/browser-computers.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 (also with SHOT=1) and 0.2.0-rc.2 (two throwaway dsh, front door on loopback through `DSH_COMPUTERS_TEST_LOOPBACK=1`): off by default, B accepts + link, A adds with Approve on B, B's dsh opens in A's Browser tab with B's chat and no nested picker, B removes A → "pair again", switch off closes the relay and the front door. `npm pack --dry-run`: the screenshot ships; `tether/` only `gateway/*`.
Not checked: the live desktop app (Electron `<webview>` with its own partition) with a real second Mac over Tailscale.
Phase 4 done (2026-11-04): features.json `how` ×13 (11 by a subagent), README privacy/limits/FAQ ×13 (12 by the subagent), `build:readme`, `docs/ROUTES.md`. Review fixes: the stored list uses the pasted-link address rules (a hand-edited internet address or port 0 is dropped); one answer per pinned socket; the Settings rows of this feature come last in a row (older tests read rows by position); two `rejectUnauthorized: false` lines carry `nosemgrep` with the pin reason (Semgrep p/javascript + p/security-audit: 0 findings).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-04): `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; real dsh PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; not committed. Not done: release, live desktop copy, a check with a real second Mac over Tailscale and in the desktop app's `<webview>`.

## Task board: the project list becomes a dropdown at the top (2026-11-04, branch `feat/task-board-project-dropdown`)

Owner request: the board's own project list (the left column of the Task board page, not the project pick of New task: that panel is gone since 165433c, New task opens dsh's own chat) takes too much room. Remove it, put the projects in an area at the top of the board that opens as a dropdown on a click, and keep every behavior of the list: **Search projects**, Enter picks the first match, the project pick and the other functions.
- Client (same switch `taskBoard`, no Host change): the `.cpb-side` column and its brand row are gone; the board (`.cpb-main`) has the full width. The header's `h1` is a button (`data-cpb-project-button`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`; folder icon, or the board icon for All projects; the project name; dsh's orange dot while any chat waits; dsh's chevron). A click opens `nav[data-cpb-projects-menu]` (`.cpb-drop`, absolute under the button, over the columns, `max-height:min(480px, 100vh - 140px)`): Search projects, Active in the last 2 hours, All projects with its counts, the projects with their counts and orange dots (only `.cpb-projects` scrolls), No matching project.
- Store: `projectsOpen` (a closed list forgets its `query`); `toggleProjects(open?)`; `pickFirst(projects)` (Enter: the first project that `shownProjects` gives; an empty box or no match: nothing); `setProject` closes the list. `stop()` and `reset()` (feature off) close it and empty the query.
- Close: a pick (the focus goes back to the button), Enter on a match, Esc in the list (a full search box empties first; stopPropagation so the page's Esc does not close a panel), Esc on the page while open, Tab out of the list (`onBlur` with a `relatedTarget` outside), a pointer press outside (a capture `pointerdown` listener on `document` that lives only while the list is open; its cleanup also runs when the page unmounts, so feature off leaves no listener). Enter that ends an IME composition picks nothing.
- CSS: `.cpb-pwrap`, `.cpb-proj`, `.cpb-drop` (dark theme: layer-2 background), `.cpb-nav{flex:none}`; the counts line has `flex-shrink:1000` so that it gives way before the project name (without it the name was cut to "acme-…" in a 960 px board).
- No new language key (labels: `board.projects`, `board.allProjects`, `projectPicker.search`, `projectPicker.empty`, `recentProjects.option`). `feature.taskBoard.description` (en, zh, 11 packs) says that the project name opens the list and Enter picks the first match; `docs/features.json` `how` ×13 and `llms`; `npm run build:locales`, `npm run build:readme`. Tether: no change (its board already opens the project list from a tap on the title, `tether/FEATURES.md`).
- Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (closed: no list, no side column; button in the header; open/close; Enter: empty box, no match, IME, first match with and without the filter; click picks; Esc stops the page; Tab out; leaving the page; feature off) red on 11 mutations (empty-box guard, pick closes, Enter picks, IME guard, stop resets, reset closes, blur null guard, Tab out, Esc stopPropagation, dot on the button, leaving the board). `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 6 rewritten (board full width, button in the header, focus in the search box, list over the columns and in the window, only the list scrolls in a 260 px list, DOCS + Esc, Enter in an empty box does nothing, DOCS + Enter picks docs-site and the focus goes back, Esc and a click on a column close it, filter kept after a reload, the whole project name shows); step 4's narrow width is 540 px now (no side column). `taskBoard.webp` retaken (SHOT=1).
- Then (owner): "görev panosunu kolayca açmak için kısayol ekle klavye kısayolu". A dsh shortcut command `composerPlus.taskBoard` (label `board.title`; regions page and editable, so it works from the composer; no modals) with the default `Cmd+Shift+K` / `Ctrl+Shift+K` (K for kanban; no web Linux default, as in dsh). Checked free: dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 defaults (`KeyK` only with `primary`, dsh's session search; `primary+shift` only on B, O, Comma, Period, A) and Chrome's own list (no Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+K). Its run is `layout.selectPanel('composer-plus-board')`, the panel of the sidebar row. Installed in the `taskBoard` feature (`injectScope(['shortcuts', 'layout'])`), so the switch removes it. `registerCommand` (shared with Next waiting chat) registers with the default and, when dsh refuses it (a taken default), again without a key.
  - Texts: no new key. `feature.taskBoard.description` (en, zh, 11 packs) names the key; `how` ×13 and `llms`; the shortcut table and the Task board limit (Linux browser: no default; an extension can take it) in 13 READMEs; `tether/FEATURES.md` says that the phone has no key (the menu is one tap away).
  - Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (command shape, the key, opens the board panel, off removes it, on again gives one) red on 4 mutations (key, composer region, open, disposer); `test/next-waiting.test.mjs` still covers the refusal path. `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 0 (the key from the composer opens the board; the Keyboard shortcuts page lists "Task board ⇧⌘K"; back to the chat and the key again) and step 5 (off: the key opens nothing, no row on the Keyboard shortcuts page; on: the key opens it): PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (the first 0.2.0-rc.2 run timed out on a click in step 3, which the key does not touch; the next run passed with no change). `npm test` passes. Not committed.
  - 0.2.0-rc.2 time-outs (owner asked for more evidence, 2026-11-04): two runs today (also at 08:59 and 09:09 on the branch before this change) showed dsh's chat instead of the board after a first message. A disposable copy logged each `layout.selectPanel` call (with its caller) and each panel change from the first message to the end of step 1b; the test ran 20 times on 0.2.0-rc.2: 20 PASS, 0 FAIL. Each run had the same order: the card click opens the chat (dsh's `replaceMain` → `selectPanel(null)`), then `watchBoardTask` brings the board back after 296–341 ms, and no other panel change follows. Not reproduced, cause not known; no code change.

## Task board: a read card leaves Done by itself; "Recently finished" shows the read ones (2026-11-04, branch `feat/task-board-project-dropdown`)
Owner request: "bitti kısmında okunduktan sonra otomatik olarak kaybolsun ama son bitenleri göster gibi bir şey de olsun, ux odaklı ilerle". Decisions (agent, UX first; the owner said to go on without questions):
- Done shows the cards that you did not read yet (green dot). A card that you read (you opened its chat) leaves Done by itself; you are on the chat then, so no card moves under your eyes.
- Under them, a quiet row **Recently finished (N)** opens the read finished cards in the same column (the 10 newest, then **Show N older**); open or closed is kept on this device (`dsh-composer-plus.board.recentDone`, closed by default). The Done count in the column head counts the unread cards.
- Nothing is removed: × works on every Done card; **Clear all** shows in the Done head only while Recently finished is open and takes those read cards off the board (unread cards stay, as before); Undo, Removed (N) and Put back stay.
- "Read" is dsh's own green dot (`completionUnread`, lives only in this window, like the sidebar). dsh gives no dot to a chat that finishes while it is the open chat, also when a panel covers it: a New task finishes behind the board and would leave Doing without ever showing in Done. So the board keeps its own set `fresh`: a chat that finishes while it is the open chat and a panel (the board, Plugins, …) covers it counts as unread until it is the open chat with no panel over it (a card click, dsh's sidebar) or runs again. In memory only, like dsh's dots.
- Tether (fits a phone): the Done segment shows the unread cards (the phone's `finished` set) and a **Recently finished (N)** row that opens the read ones (10, then Show N older), kept on the phone; Clear all in ⋯ takes the read ones.
Invariants: no chat changes in dsh; the switch removes the watcher (its listeners) and the new key stays only in localStorage (as the project filter); one change of the Host list at a time as before.
Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (unread/recent split, 10 + Show N older, toggle kept, Clear all only while open and only read ids, `fresh` add/drop rules, switch off), iOS tests, `test/browser-task-board.mjs` on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (a read card leaves Done, Recently finished shows it, a New task that finishes behind the board is green in Done), `taskBoard.webp` retaken, texts en/zh + 11 + `build:locales`, docs `how` ×13 + `llms` + README limits ×13 + FEATURES.md + `build:readme`, `npm test`.
Done (2026-11-04, not committed):
- Client: `boardColumns` takes `fresh` and `recentAll`; Done keeps the unread cards (`unreadDone` = their count, shown in the head), `recent` (BOARD_RECENT_SHOWN = 10) and `recentCount` are the read ones; `clearIds` = every read finished id. Store: `recentOpen` (localStorage `dsh-composer-plus.board.recentDone`), `recentAll` (a project pick resets it), `fresh` + `setFresh(add, drop)` (a no-op sets nothing), `reset()` empties `fresh`. `watchFresh(board, status, list, panelInfo)`: a running → idle edge while the chat is dsh's main chat (`retainedBy.mainView`) and a panel covers it (`layout.panelInfo.activePanelId`) adds it; it drops when the chat runs, goes, or is main with no panel; no `panelInfo`: nothing. Installed in the `taskBoard` feature (`injectScope(['layout', 'uiSession', 'sessions'])`; optional chaining for test fakes without these services). UI: `.cpb-rdone` row (dsh's chevron, turns 90°, `aria-expanded`), the read cards under it, Show N older; Clear all only while open; × on every finished card.
- Texts: new `board.recentDone`; changed `board.clearAllHint` and `feature.taskBoard.description` (en, zh, 11 packs); `build:locales`.
- Tether: `boardDoneSplit` (Core.swift); Done shows the unread cards, a Recently finished (N) row (`@AppStorage("boardRecentDone")`), 10 + Show N older; the segment counts the unread cards; `cardRow` shared.
- Docs: features.json `how` ×13 (bold labels = the packs' labels; `readme-labels` passes) and `llms`; README limits ×13 (the read state lives in this window, as dsh's dots); FEATURES.md row; `build:readme`.
- Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 15/15 mutations (read leaves Done, fresh unread, 10 limit, Clear all only open, kept on device, project resets, covered, main, drop on run/seen/gone, unsubscribe, reset, no-op set, row only with read cards); iOS 79/79; `npm test` exit 0 (first run: `test/notify.test.mjs` crashed on `scope.sessions.list` with a fake that has no sessions service; fixed with optional chaining); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (new 3b: FAQ page read → Recently finished (1), closed by default; Chess game finished behind the board → green in Done; Changelog tidy (behind the board) and Onboarding flow (another chat open) green; opened → they leave Done; kept open after a reload). A rerun with the new chevron check failed once on 0.2.1-rc.2 at the Host count after the second Clear all (7 !== 8: the card moves at once, the Host write ends later); the test now waits for the Host list. `taskBoard.webp` 13 kB.
- Then (owner): "yanıtla çıkınca tıklayınca sohbete gitmesin yanıtla kısmı açılsın". A click (or Enter) on a card that waits for you (orange, it has Answer) opens the answer panel, not the chat; the card has `aria-haspopup="dialog"`; the panel keeps Open chat. Other cards open their chat as before. Tether already did this (`tap()` opens the waiting prompt first). Texts: `feature.taskBoard.description` (en, zh, 11 packs), `how` ×13 and `llms`; `build:locales`, `build:readme`. Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` (click and Enter on s1 open Answer, no chat; other cards no `aria-haspopup`) red on 2 mutations (click opens the chat again; no `aria-haspopup`); `test/browser-task-board.mjs` step 2 (a click on the orange card's title opens the panel and the board stays; Esc closes it) PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `npm test` exit 0; docs check and `git diff --check` clean. Not committed.
- Then (owner): "hover üstüne gelince ilerleme okunamadı filan diyor düzelt". Cause (verified 2026-11-04): the owner's dsh started on 2026-11-03 12:48 with Composer Plus 1.19.1; the live copy got 1.20.0 at 18:00 and dsh did not restart. The desktop profile has no client HMR (`client-hmr` is in `dsh-web-app` only, and `hmr` has `root: []`), so the page and the Host half stay 1.19.1 until a restart. Evidence: process 70196 start time; 1.19.1 `index.js` has no `composer-plus.board-progress` route and its `client.js` has no progress box. A request to a route that no plugin registered gets dsh's own empty 404 (`dsh-host-webserver` fallback), and the box showed "Could not read the progress: HTTP 404". Fix: `readBoardProgress` and `createBoardProgressMode.setMode` turn an empty 404 (no `error` in the body) into `restart`, as the board's other routes do; the box and Settings show the new `board.progress.restart` ("Restart dsh to see the progress.", en, zh, 11 packs; `build:locales`). The route's own 404 ("dsh has no open chat with this id") and 409 keep their text. Owner action: restart dsh once to load 1.20.0. Checks: `test/task-board-client.test.mjs` red on 4/4 mutations (read 404, mode 404, box text, Settings text). Not committed.


## t3code ideas: feature gap plan (2026-11-04, branch `docs/t3code-feature-gap-plan`)
Request (owner): find the t3code features that dsh and Composer Plus do not have and that help in daily use; rank them; write a plan; write no code now.
Status: plan approved by the owner (2026-11-04). No code changed. Open: the owner's choice of candidates and the three decisions below. Phase 0 starts only after that choice.

### Sources
- t3code: github.com/pingdotgg/t3code `main` at 8b44f42 (2026-11-04), MIT. Read: README, `docs/user/*` (composer, thread-sidebar, source-control, project-settings, keybindings, usage, permission-modes, html-renders, snap-shot, prompt-links, guest-links, outside-agents, devices, activity-log, faq), the file list of `apps/web/src/components/chat/`, `SecretRequestCard.tsx`, `AssistantSelectionToolbar.tsx`, `ThreadServersPanel.tsx`, `promptStashStore.ts`, `apps/server/src/secrets/SecretRequests.ts`. No t3code code is copied: behavior only, in our own words.
- dsh: the running app 0.2.1-rc.5 (`app.asar`, 308 packages, read only) and the test installs 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2. Read: locale dictionaries in `lib/client.js`, `installWorkspaceShortcuts` (dsh-client-ui-workspace), the approval card (dsh-client-ui-approval), the composer paste handler (dsh-client-ui-conversation), package READMEs.
- Composer Plus: `FEATURES` (56 ids), `docs/features.json`, `tether/FEATURES.md`, earlier comparisons in this file (agentbox, HarnessMM, superagent, X article).

### dsh facts checked for this plan (0.2.1-rc.5)
- Approval card: English dictionary has only `waiting`, `detail.aria`, `escalation`, `reject`, `allowOnce`. `dsh-user-approval` outcomes are `allowed-once | rejected | cancelled | unavailable`: no reason field.
- No `request_secret` tool, no secret prompt. `dsh-client-ui-secret-mask` README: "Masking changes only what is drawn; the message text, the copy action, and the model input keep the original characters."
- No Quote/Cite label in any client dictionary.
- Plan mode README: "every tool remains available, so use sandbox mode and approval prompts for enforced limits". So plan mode cannot make a side chat read-only.
- dsh shortcuts: `session.new/search/rename/fork/archive`, `workspace.add`, sidebar, terminal and approval commands. `navigation.back/forward` exist only inside the sidebar browser.
- Paste: the composer handler calls `keyboard.paste(text)` for any size.
- Fork: `sessionController.fork({ sessionId, atSeq? })`; no `atSeq` → the latest completed-turn prefix.
- Usage: per turn (`message.turnUsage.*`) and per chat (**Session statistics**). No report over all chats found. Settings → Account was not opened (only its class names were seen).
- Background jobs: `dsh-tool-jobs` `completionDelivery: wakeup` opens a turn on an idle owner when a job ends. dsh's jobs menu lists and stops jobs.
- Sidebar browser: `tab.actions.openTab("browser", { params: { url } })` inside dsh-client-ui-sidebar-browser. Plugin access to it is not verified.

### Candidates (ranked by daily use)
Wave 1:
| # | id | What | Side | Tether |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `rejectNote` | Approval card → **Reject with note…**: steer the note into the running turn, then reject | client (Host route only if phase 0 needs it) | yes |
| 2 | `secretInput` | Tool `request_secret`: a private card; the value goes to an owner-only file, the agent gets only the path | Host + client | yes |
| 3 | `quoteReply` | Select text in an answer → **Quote** → `> …` at the composer cursor | client | yes (whole message) |
| 4 | `sideChat` | Ask a side question in a fork of the chat while it works; **Bring back** puts the answer and a `dsh-session:` link in the composer | Host + client | yes |
| 5 | `diffComments` | Line notes on Composer Plus Changes diffs; **Send to composer** | client | yes (one line) |
Wave 2:
| # | id | What | Side | Tether |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6 | `chatHistory` | Back/forward through opened chats (two dsh shortcuts) | client | no (iOS back gesture) |
| 7 | `devServers` | Listening ports of the chat's processes: **Open**, **Stop** | Host + client | yes (list + Stop) |
| 8 | `largePaste` | A paste of 32 KiB or more becomes a `.txt` attachment | client | no |
| 9 | `usageReport` | Tokens, cache hit and estimated cost over all chats by day, model and project | Host + client | yes (read only) |
None opens a port, runs a server or starts a model turn without a click: none goes into `OFF_BY_DEFAULT`.

### Design notes per candidate
- `rejectNote`: steer first, then dsh's own Reject. A failed steer does not reject (the card stays). A failed reject leaves the note queued and shows the reason (rule 11). One action per request at a time from the button, Enter and a second click (rule 7). Tether: approval sheet button, existing steer and reject calls.
- `secretInput`:
  - Host switch `GET/POST {on}` at `/api/composer-plus.secrets`; answer route `POST /api/composer-plus.secret-answer {sessionId, requestId, value | decline}` (`PLUS`, `routes`).
  - Value file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/secrets/<random>` (folder 0700, file 0600), deleted when the asking turn ends and swept at Host start.
  - `tools/post-execute` replaces the exact value with `[secret]` in every tool result of that chat. Limit: encoded forms (base64) are not replaced.
  - `read`, `read_image`, `grep` cannot open the secrets folder (Protected files' `toolTarget`, rule 12 cases).
  - The gateway access log never holds the value.
  - Card: dsh `tool.call.toolview` slot first, else `conversation.input.dock`.
  - Default on (proposal); token cost of the tool schema measured in phase 0 (guess ~150 tokens).
- `quoteReply`: only a selection inside one assistant text block; at most 2,000 characters (cut with `…`); insert through `inputActions.captureInsertion()` / `insertText()` (one undo step).
- `sideChat`:
  - `POST /api/composer-plus.side-chat {sessionId, text}` and `GET ?sessionId` (list), both in `PLUS`.
  - Fork with no `atSeq`, then `prompt` (queue) with a "read only, change no files" lead.
  - Read-only is only an instruction: plan mode does not block tools (fact above). Option to check in phase 0: start the fork with dsh's read-only sandbox mode.
  - Own messages start at `inheritedEventCount` (rule 10); withdrawn messages are left out (rule 9); one start or Bring back at a time (rule 7).
  - Close = dsh's archive (undo stays).
- `diffComments`: notes per chat in localStorage; Markdown `path:12-14`, a ```diff``` block and the note; the tray empties after the send. Not on dsh's own Last Turn view.
- `chatHistory`: a stack of opened chats; archived or deleted chats are skipped; `composerPlus.chatBack` / `composerPlus.chatForward`; defaults chosen with dsh's `bindingIssue` and Chrome's list; no web Linux default.
- `devServers`: macOS `lsof -nP -iTCP -sTCP:LISTEN`, Linux `/proc`; match by `DSH_SESSION_ID`, else by the parent chain (a process that sets `process.title` hides its environment); poll every 5 s only while the chat shows; Windows: "not supported" (`ponytail:` note). Overlaps dsh's jobs menu: the new parts are the port, **Open** and processes outside jobs. Merges with agentbox item `leftoverPrograms`.
- `largePaste`: capture-phase `paste` listener on the composer root; attachment through `conversation.fileUploads`; one shortcut pastes as text. When the root is not found, the normal paste runs.
- `usageReport`: per-chat cache of the last read `seq`; inherited fork events are not counted (rule 10); withdrawn turns are counted (they were paid) and the docs say so; editable price table, first values checked on DeepSeek's price page on the day of the work. Drop it when the owner does not pay per token.

### Rejected (exists or low value)
Queue/Steer and steer queued (dsh); prompt recall, rewind with files, queue edit (`inputHistory`, `turnRollback`, `queueEdit`); `/goal`, context meter, `/compact` (dsh, `goalCheck`, `contextBreakdown`); message search, thread links, `@` threads (dsh search, `contentSearch`, `copyDeeplink`); pin, archive undo, fork, worktree per chat, notifications (dsh, `worktreeChats`, `notifications`); mobile app and remote (`mobileConnect`, Tether); HTML replies (dsh `present` + HTML preview); PR/CI watch (dsh job wakeup + `gh run watch`); git buttons and worktree cleanup (owner's `/gt`, `/merge-main`); prompt stash (dsh keeps a draft per chat); project model defaults (one provider); resume at quota reset (no quota windows; `dsh-llm-retry`, `continueButton`); guest links, webhooks, MCP for outside agents, push notifications (need an internet port or relay; Tether is LAN/Tailscale only); Devices, SnapShots (agent-device; system screenshot + paste); Mermaid/LaTeX (new 1–2 MB dependency); snooze, settle, multi-model send, themes, icons, viewed marks (rare or cosmetic); find over the whole chat (an extension of `findInChat`, separate request).

### Not verified (do not assume)
1. Does a subagent's approval request show in the parent chat? Check with one subagent in the real dsh. If not, it is one more candidate.
2. Does Settings → Account show usage history? (`usageReport`)
3. Can a plugin open dsh's sidebar browser tab? (`devServers` Open)
4. Does a steer sent while an approval waits reach the first model request after the reject? (`rejectNote`)

### Decisions (owner, 2026-11-04)
- Build 1–3 now: `rejectNote`, `secretInput`, `quoteReply`. 4–5 (`sideChat`, `diffComments`) later, not in this work. Wave 2 (6–8) is not chosen.
- `secretInput` is on by default; its token cost goes into the docs.
- `usageReport` is dropped: the owner does not pay DeepSeek per token.
- Phase 0 probes only what 1–3 need: steer during an approval, a long-waiting tool, `post-execute` value replace, the `toolview` slot, the token cost of the tool, the selection and insert path for quotes. Fork, shortcut, paste, browser-tab and subagent-approval probes wait for their features.

### Common work for each chosen feature (AGENTS.md)
Own `FEATURES` id and `feature(id, setup)`, off removes everything (Host switch for `secretInput`); `SHOT_OF`, `SHOTS`, `assets/features/<id>.webp` ≤ 60 kB from a demo dsh (`SHOT=1`, English UI, made-up data); texts en/zh + 11 `locales/*.json`, `npm run build:locales`; `docs/features.json` (`how` ×13, `llms`, `shot`, routes), `npm run build:readme`, Limits and FAQ ×13 by hand; unit test in the `test` script, `test/toggles.test.mjs` counts; real isolated dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 in a new `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (headless, `chromiumSandbox: true`); `tether/FEATURES.md` row, `PLUS` for new phone routes, calls only when `c.plus.contains(...)`; rules 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 where they apply.

### Phases and checks
| Phase | Work | Check |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | Probes in /tmp, no product code: steer during an approval; a long-waiting tool + `post-execute` value replace; `toolview` slot; token cost of `request_secret`; quote selection + insert | evidence for each item written here; the plan changes where a probe fails |
| 1 | `rejectNote` | unit test, real dsh ×2, iOS tests |
| 2 | `secretInput` | unit test (value absent from the log, the model request, tool results and the gateway log; symlink, `..`, FIFO; deletion at turn end), real dsh ×2, iOS |
| 3 | `quoteReply` | unit test, real dsh ×2, iOS |
| End | `npm test`, `git diff --check`, `node scripts/build-docs.mjs --check`, `npm pack --dry-run` | all green |
Close one phase at a time: diff review, checks, checkpoint here. Commit, version, release and the live copy only when the owner asks.

### Allowed paths
`client.js`, `index.js`, `package.json` (`test`, `dsh.client.inject` if needed), `locales/*.json`, `locale/*.json` (built), `assets/features/<new id>.webp`, `docs/features.json`, `docs/ROUTES.md`, `README*.md`, `llms.txt`, `test/*`, `tether/FEATURES.md`, `tether/gateway/server.mjs` (`PLUS`), `tether/ios/{Sources,Tests,Resources}/*`, `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`. No new dependency; no change to dsh files.

### Risks
- dsh markup (approval card, selection area, paste root) can change: the feature goes quiet, no wrong send; mark each with `ponytail:` and the upgrade path (a dsh slot).
- `secretInput` does not stop an agent that sends the value elsewhere or prints an encoded form: limits in 13 READMEs.
- `sideChat` read-only rests on the lead text unless a read-only sandbox works for the fork.
- `devServers`: `ps -E` misses processes that set their title; no Windows.

### Acceptance criteria
- Each chosen feature passes every item of the common work; `npm test` exit 0.
- Switched off, a feature leaves nothing (a test counts it).
- `secretInput`: the value is not in the session log, a model request, a tool result or the gateway log (a test searches each).
- `rejectNote`: in the real dsh, the note is in the first model request after the reject.
- `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.

### Last checkpoint
2026-11-04: comparison done, plan written. No code changed. Next: the owner's choice of candidates and decisions, then phase 0.

- API facts found (0.2.1-rc.2 types): the client session face has `prompt(content, 'queue' | 'steer')` (`ctx.sessions.sessionOf(ctx.sessions.scope(id))`); dsh's `IConversation.send` only queues. Host tools may return `PostToolDecision { kind: 'accept', content }` from `tools/post-execute` (replaces the content); `finalizeContent` exists for a tool's own last-mile transform. Composer Plus already registers tools through `ctx.inject(['tools', …])` and reads the approval card through `[data-approval-key]` (`allowForChat`).
- Probe written, not run: `/tmp/cp-phase0/probe.mjs` + `probe-plugin.mjs` (fake model, throwaway DSH_HOME, headless Chrome with `chromiumSandbox: true`, no Composer Plus). Auto review refused its run: a headless Playwright launch outside the project's own test commands needs the owner's explicit approval. Waiting for that approval.

2026-11-04 11:45, phase 0 done (the owner approved the headless probe in the chat). Same results on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2, no page errors:
- Setup notes: a Host plugin needs `export const inject = [...]` for `ctx.connection` (without it: "cannot get property "connection" without inject", the entry does not activate). The default permission mode runs `rm -rf build-old` without a card, so the probe adds a test-only `tools/pre-execute` gate that answers `ask` (the same trick as `test/browser-planned-steps.mjs`). Host `sessionController.prompt(request, signal)` needs a signal (without one: "reading 'throwIfAborted'"). Session logs are `session.v4.jsonl.zstd`; node's `zstdDecompressSync` gave text without the events, `zstd -dc` gives the whole log: use `zstd -dc` (or dsh's `sessionQuery`) in tests.
- Q1 `rejectNote`, answered YES:
  - While dsh's approval card shows, dsh hides the composer (`[data-composer-input]` is in the DOM but not visible). So the note needs its own field on the card (as planned), not the composer.
  - A steer while the card waits is accepted (`session/prompt` mode `steer` → `{accepted: true}`); the card stays; no model call happens.
  - After a click on Reject, the first model request ends with: assistant (the bash call), tool `Error: the user rejected tool "bash"`, user `<the note>`. One model call. So the model reads the rejection and then the note, in that order.
  - Decision: client side only. The client sends the steer through the chat's client session face (`ctx.sessions.sessionOf(ctx.sessions.scope(id)).prompt([{type:'text', text}], 'steer')`, the same RPC as the probe) and then answers the card (`pendingInteraction.answer('rejected')` or a click on dsh's Reject). No Host route. Tether: the gateway already forwards `session/prompt`; the phone sends the steer, then its existing reject.
- Q2 `secretInput`, answered YES:
  - A tool that waits 25 s for an answer keeps the turn open; no tool-call timeout (the tool declares no `timeoutMs`).
  - With a `tools/post-execute` listener that returns `{ kind: 'accept', content }` with the value replaced: the model request has `[secret]`, the session log has `[secret]` and not the value.
  - Control run (listener off, `NO_REPLACE=1`, 0.2.1-rc.2): the value is in the model request and in the session log. So the check measures what it should.
  - Decision: the design stands (Host tool + post-execute replace in every tool result of the chat). The tool should not return the value at all (only the path); the replace is the second line of defense for a later `cat` by the agent.
- Q3 tool row hooks (both versions): the call row is `[data-chat-flow-kind="tool-call"]` with `data-chat-node-key="<seq>:tool-call<callId>"`, and inside it `[data-tool="<tool name>"][data-state="running"]`. So the client can find the waiting `request_secret` row by `data-tool="request_secret"` and `data-state="running"` (DOM fallback, `ponytail:` note). Still open for phase 2: whether the `tool.call.toolview` slot gives a cleaner hook for our own tool (try it first).
- Q4 `quoteReply` hooks (both versions): an answer's text is inside `[data-chat-flow-kind="assistant-step"][data-turn-process-answer="true"]` (`data-chat-group-part="response"`) → `[data-slot="conversation.chat.node"]` → the Markdown elements. A selection counts when its range is inside one such `conversation.chat.node` of an `assistant-step` row. Tool output rows are `tool-call` rows, so they are left out by this rule.
- Q5 token cost: the probe schema (name, a 1-line description, one parameter) is 276 characters ≈ 70 tokens (dsh's 4-characters-per-token rule). The real `request_secret` schema with a usage description will be larger; measure it in phase 2 for the docs (estimate: 150–250 tokens).
- Probe files stay in /tmp only (not in the repo); the temp DSH_HOMEs were removed.

2026-11-04 13:05: phase 1 committed (8d6dff7, the owner asked; not pushed). Phase 2 started.

### Phase 2 design: `secretInput` (Secret input)
- Host switch, on by default (owner's decision): `GET/POST {on}` at `/api/composer-plus.secrets`, file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/secrets.json` (`mobileSwitch(file, true)`). On: one `ctx.inject(['tools'])` fiber with the tool `request_secret` and a `tools/post-execute` listener; a `tools/pre-execute` gate (always installed, it only acts on the secrets folder). Off: the fiber goes (no tool, no replace), waiting asks end as "declined".
- Tool `request_secret({ label, purpose? })`: `label` 1–80 characters (what the value is), `purpose` ≤ 300. It waits (no time limit of its own; the turn's abort signal ends it) until the user answers in the chat or the phone. Answer "save": the value (1–8,192 characters, no NUL) goes to `storages/dsh-composer-plus/secrets/<32 hex>` (folder 0700, file 0600, `O_CREAT|O_EXCL`), and the tool returns the path and the rule "use it only as input (`"$(cat <path>)"`, `< <path>`); do not print, echo or log it". Answer "decline": the tool returns "the user did not give it" (not an error, so the model can go on). One open ask per chat: a second call while one waits is a tool error.
- Pending asks in memory: `Map(askId → { sessionId, callId, label, purpose, resolve })`. Answer route `POST /api/composer-plus.secret-answer { sessionId, askId, value } | { sessionId, askId, decline: true }` (in Tether's `PLUS`); `GET /api/composer-plus.secret-answer?sessionId=…` lists the chat's open asks (askId, callId, label, purpose), never values. A wrong session or an unknown ask: 404. An answer is used once.
- Value out of the model's and the log's view:
  - the tool result never has the value;
  - `tools/post-execute`: every tool result of the chat that has a saved value of that chat, as its exact text, gets `[secret]` in place (text blocks only); the values are kept in memory per chat until the files go;
  - `tools/pre-execute` gate (after `next()`): `read`, `read_image`, `grep`, `write`, `edit`, `str_replace_editor` on a path inside the secrets folder (the real path, `toolTarget` of Protected files, rule 12) are denied;
  - the gateway passes the body through and logs only device, ip and result (checked: `plus()` in server.mjs).
- Files go when the asking chat's turn ends (`agent/status` → not running) and at Host start (a sweep of the folder). The in-memory values go with them.
- Client: a card under the waiting `request_secret` row in dsh's `tool.call.toolview` slot (keyed `request_secret`, dsh's own tool row slot; phase 0 found `data-tool="request_secret"` rows): label, purpose, a password field (`type="password"`, `autocomplete="off"`), **Save** and **Decline**. One answer per ask at a time (rule 7). After the answer the row shows "Saved" or "Declined". Settings row with the Host switch status (`createMobile(SECRETS_ROUTE)`).
- Tether: the chat's open asks (`GET secret-answer`) show a sheet with a `SecureField`; Save / Decline → `POST secret-answer`; only when `c.plus.contains("secret-answer")`.
- Limits (README ×13): the agent can still read the file with a shell command and send or print it in another form (only the exact text is replaced); a value that is also a common word gets `[secret]` in other results too; the file lives until the turn ends.
- Tests: `test/secret-input.test.mjs` (Host: parse, tool waits and returns no value, answer route, one answer, wrong session 404, a second ask is an error, post-execute replace in results of the same chat only, the gate denies read/grep/write in the folder with a symlink, `..` after a symlinked folder and the real path; a FIFO named like a value file does not block the sweep; deletion at turn end and at start; off removes tool and listener and declines waiting asks; 0700/0600), `test/secret-input-client.test.mjs` (card, one answer at a time, password field, off), real dsh ×2 in `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (the value is not in the model request, the log or the page after Save; Decline; SHOT=1), iOS unit test.

### Phase 3 design: `quoteReply` (Quote in reply)
- Facts (dsh 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 source):
  - `InputActions.captureInsertion()` returns the composer's last selection (`projection.selection`) or, without one, a collapsed span at the end of the draft, with the draft revision. `insertText(text, span)` inserts in one undo step and returns false when the draft changed or dsh locks the editor (`adjudicating`, `submitting`). The composer keeps its last caret while the focus is in the chat.
  - The chat body is `[data-conversation-content][data-conversation-session="<id>"]`; the composer is `[data-composer-seat]`. Answer text: `[data-chat-flow-kind="assistant-step"][data-turn-process-answer="true"]` → `[data-slot="conversation.chat.node"]` (phase 0, Q4).
- Client only, on by default, no Host route, no model call. Registered in `conversation.input.right` (it gets `inputActions` and `useInput` of the chat, as Plan mode toggle and Improve prompt).
- A selection counts when both ends are inside one answer node of an `assistant-step` row of this chat's `data-conversation-session` body, and its text is not only white space. Reasoning, tool rows, user messages and another chat's body do not count.
- A small floating **Quote** button shows above the end of the selection (portaled to `document.body`, fixed position; placed again on scroll). It goes with the selection. No shortcut: `Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+.` is the default key of dsh's inspector command (dsh-experimental-inspector), and the button is enough.
- Text: the selection's plain text (`Selection.toString()`), trimmed, at most 2,000 characters (cut with `…`), each line prefixed with `> ` (an empty line as `>`), followed by an empty line. When the text before the caret does not end with a line break, a line break goes first. Inserted at the composer's caret through `insertText(…, captureInsertion())`; false → a notice ("The composer changed. Try again."). Then the focus goes to the composer (`input.for(scope).focus()` when there, else `[data-composer-input]`).
- Rule 7: the insert is synchronous; a second click on the same selection inserts again (the user asked for it). No shared async state.
- Tether: touch and hold an answer → **Quote**: the whole message as a quote in the composer (iOS `Text` selection gives no range to an app; `SelectableText` sheet already lets the user copy a part). Pure `quoteText(_:)` with the same rule as the Mac; iOS test.
- Risks: dsh markup (answer node, chat body) can change → the button does not show; `ponytail:` note.
- Tests: `test/quote-reply.test.mjs` (quote text rules; the selection filter with fake DOM nodes; insert through `insertText` with the captured span; a refused insert gives the notice; off removes the slot and the listeners), real dsh ×2 in `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (select a part of an answer → **Quote** → the composer has `> part` at the caret and the user text around it stays; a selection in a user message or a tool row shows no button; send → the model request has the quote; switch off), iOS unit test.

Try again." No shortcut (decision above). en/zh, 11 locale packs.
- Found in the real dsh: with "\n\n" after the quote at the end of the draft, the next typed line joined the quote (the sent message had "> …\nDo it"). Now the quote ends with three line breaks at the end of the draft (dsh trims the end when it sends) and with as many as the text after the caret needs elsewhere. Known limit (`ponytail:`): a file chip before the caret can make the empty lines around the quote one off.
