### Phase 4 design: `sideChat` (Side chat)
- Facts (dsh source, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 the same unless noted):
  - t3code (docs/user/thread-sidebar.md, "Side chats"): a side chat starts with what the thread knows, the thread keeps working; its first reply asks before it edits files or runs commands; **Bring back** gives the side chat to the thread with the next message; it opens as a tab in the right panel.
  - Host `sessionController.fork({sessionId, atSeq?})`: no `atSeq` = the latest completed-turn prefix (stops before a `turn/start`, an appended `user/message` or an `agent/inbox/spliced`); an exact `atSeq` gets synthetic closers for an open turn (`openTurnClosers`, cause `forked`). The fork header has `parentSession` and `isSeeded`; `inheritedEventCount` = cut + 1. The fork copies the inbox splices before the cut, so a cut with a waiting queued message would run that message in the fork.
  - `ctx.permissionPresets.set(session, name)` (dsh-permission-presets); both base bundles configure `read-only` (sandbox `read-only`, approval `ask`). dsh-fs-sandbox rejects every write in `read-only`; dsh-sandbox runs bash read-only or fails closed. The fork otherwise inherits the parent's permission events. The owner's `auto-review-default` skips `isSeeded` chats.
  - Right sidebar: `ctx.sidebarRightTabs.register({id, kind, patterns, …})`, `ctx.sidebarRight.openResource(address)`. dsh-client-ui-subagent shows a subagent chat in a tab: `ctx.sessions.retain(id, {source})`, its `SessionProvider` and `renderSlot('sidebar.chat.conversation')` (dsh's embedded Conversation). Not verified: the same for a fork (an ordinary chat) from another plugin's tab.
  - A `dsh-session:` link in a message makes dsh attach a read-only snapshot of that whole chat (up to 20% of the context window); for a fork that is mostly the parent's own history again.
- Host route `/api/composer-plus.side-chat` (in Tether's `PLUS`):
  - `POST {sessionId, text}` (text 1–8,000): fork at `sideForkSeq(events)` (the newest seq at which no queued or steering message waits: the fork knows the running turn so far, and no waiting message runs in it); the fork's goal (if any) is cleared; the permission preset `read-only` is set (`readOnly: false` in the answer when dsh has no such preset); then the first message (a short English lead + the question) is queued. When the message cannot be sent, the fork is archived and the error says so (rule 11). One start per chat at a time. Not for a subagent chat. Answer `{sessionId, readOnly}`.
  - `GET ?sessionId=<side chat>` → `{parent, messages, truncated}`: the side chat's own messages (from `inheritedEventCount`, rule 10; withdrawn ones left out, rule 9; the lead taken off the first one), for **Bring back**. A chat whose first own message is not a side lead: 404. Stateless: no file.
- Client, on by default (a model turn only on a click), id `sideChat`:
  - Composer tool row button (`conversation.input.right`): asks the typed draft in a side chat (plain text only: no file chip, no attachment, no slash command). On success the draft is cleared (only when it is still the sent text) and the side chat opens. One start per chat (rule 7, store-checked).
  - The side chat opens in the main view (as dsh's own Fork). A bar above its composer (`conversation.input.dock`): "Side chat of <parent>", **Bring back** and **Back to the chat**. Decision (2026-11-04, from dsh's source): not a right-sidebar tab. dsh's embedded chat slot `sidebar.chat.conversation` is a child slot of dsh-client-ui-subagent's tab, and "a child slot is declared by exactly one entry" (a second declaration throws at load); its `subagentchat` address would route the side chat's prompts as a subagent's (`subagents/prompt`), which the Host refuses for a fork. `ponytail:` upgrade when dsh gives a public embedded-chat slot.
  - **Bring back**: the side chat's own messages, each question as a `> ` quote and the answer, at most 12,000 characters; the parent chat opens and its composer entry inserts the text at its caret through `insertText` (as Improve prompt's outcome: it waits for the composer's draft revision). No `dsh-session:` link (it would attach the parent's history again). Off while the side chat runs.
  - The side chat keeps the chat's model (dsh's fork takes the default model) and has no goal (a copied goal would ask it to go on working).
- Tether: chat title menu → **Side chat…** sheet (question) → the phone opens the side chat; in a side chat (GET answers) a bar **Bring back** puts the same text into the parent's composer and opens the parent. Only when `c.plus.contains("side-chat")`.
- Limits (README ×13): read-only rests on dsh's read-only preset (the agent can ask for more, with an approval card); the side chat is an ordinary chat in the sidebar (archive it there); the side chat knows the running turn up to the newest moment without a waiting message.
- Tests: `test/side-chat.test.mjs` (Host: cut rule with queue and steer splices, route with a fake controller: fork seq, preset, goal clear, prompt text, archive on failure, one start at a time, subagent refused, GET own messages with a fork and both rollback kinds, 404) and the client (store guard, button rules, insert of the Bring back text, off removes); real dsh ×2 steps 11–13 in `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (side question while the parent waits on an approval card: the fork's model request has the parent's task, no card in the side chat, a write is denied, a queued parent message does not run in it; the bar shows in the side chat; Bring back opens the parent and puts the answer in its composer; off); iOS unit test.

### Phase 5 design: `diffComments` (Diff notes)
- t3code (docs/user/composer.md "Context in your message", source-control.md "Review guides"): a review comment from a diff lands in the composer as context; on web, select lines in an excerpt to add a comment. Behavior only, our own words, no code.
- Facts (this repo): the Changes page (`ChangesTab` → `FileList` → `ChangesDiff`) draws a file's patch as rows from `patchRows(patch)` (headers dropped, from the first `@@`). The diff route answers `{path, patch, truncated, binary}`. The page is a right-sidebar tab of one chat (`sessionId`), so the target chat is known. No Host change needed.
- Client only, on by default (no model call, no Host route, no port), id `diffComments`. It works inside the Changes page, so it needs `changesPanel` on too (Settings row says so).
- In a file's diff, a line that is not a hunk header gets a hover/focus `+` button (keyboard: the row is a button). A click opens a small note field under that line. A second click on another line of the same hunk with Shift extends the range (shortcut: one line or a Shift range; ponytail: no drag select).
- **Add note** keeps the note in a per-chat tray: `{path, side: 'new'|'old', start, end, lines: [the patch lines], note}`. Line numbers come from the hunk header (`@@ -a,b +c,d @@`) by counting: `+` and context lines step the new side, `-` and context lines step the old side; a range on removed lines only uses old-side numbers.
- The tray: a bar at the top of the Changes page while it has notes ("2 notes · Send to composer · Clear"). **Send to composer**: the notes as Markdown at the composer's caret (dsh's `insertText(captureInsertion())` of that chat, one undo step), each: `` `path:12-14` `` + a ```diff block of the lines + the note; then the tray empties. A refused insert keeps the tray and shows the notice. Notes are kept per chat in `localStorage` (they survive a reload); at most 50 notes, a note at most 2,000 characters, lines at most 40 per note (cut with a "…" line).
- Rule 7: Send and Clear read the tray at the click; one Send per chat at a time is synchronous (no shared async state).
- Off: no `+` buttons, no tray bar; the saved notes stay in localStorage (they show again when on). Not on dsh's own Last Turn view (another plugin's page).
- Tether: in a file's diff, touch and hold a line → Add note… (one line); a tray bar in the Changes sheet; Send puts the text after the chat's draft (the same text rule, pure Swift function + test). No route.
- Tests: `test/diff-comments.test.mjs` (line numbers from hunks incl. old-only ranges, the Markdown text, cut rules, the store per chat with localStorage, the send through insertText and a refused insert, off removes); real dsh ×2 steps 14–16 in `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (open Changes, a file's diff, a note on a line and a Shift range, Send → the composer has the Markdown; send → the model request has it; off); iOS unit test.

## Project status on dsh's Ungrouped row (2026-11-04)
Request (owner): the Ungrouped folder row of the sidebar shows the same status marker (spinner / waiting / done dot) as the other folder rows.
Cause: dsh draws Ungrouped with the same `ProjectRowItem` and `data-row-key="workspace:${group.key}"`, but its key is `''` (row key `workspace:`, no `workspaceId`, no HoverCard; same in 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2). `useRowHosts('workspace:')` made a host there (key `''`), but `ProjectStatusMarkers` built its map only from `workspaces.items`, so key `''` had no status.
Fix (client.js): `ungroupedIds(items, list)` = the chats in `list.ids` that no project holds (dsh's `groupByWorkspace` stray rule, also used by `staleRows` now); `ProjectStatusMarkers` adds them under key `''` and uses the unchanged `projectStatus`. Off: the existing `useRowHosts` cleanup removes every host (the Ungrouped one too); no new listener, observer or style. Tether: `projectMark(g.items, …)` already runs for its Ungrouped group header; no change.
Checks: `test/project-status.test.mjs` Ungrouped block (idle none, running spinner portaled into `workspace:`, waiting > done > running, a project's chat and subagent/blank/archived chats do not count, other rows unchanged, off removes the host): red on HEAD client.js ("a running Ungrouped chat"), green now. `test/browser-recent-tree.mjs` new end part (dsh's Delete workspace moves a chat to Ungrouped; a held turn there: spinner on Ungrouped, collapsed too, in both views; project rows count only their own; switch off → no marker host, on → back): PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `chromium.launch` now has `chromiumSandbox: true`.
Last checkpoint (2026-11-04): done, not committed. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.

## What's new for the running version and a restart notice (2026-11-05)
Owner report: Settings → **Composer Plus updates** says "Version 1.19.1 · 1.21.0 is available. This copy was not installed from npm; update it in Settings → Plugins." and the release notes do not show (the owner expected the What's new dialog of 1.21.0).
Cause (verified): the Host reads `package.json` once at start; the live desktop copy got 1.20.0 and 1.21.0 on disk while dsh kept running 1.19.1. The What's new record is written only by `updater.install()`, so a linked copy or a hand copy never gets one. `?current=1` reads `release-notes.json` from disk at each call, so a Host that runs 1.19.1 next to the 1.21.0 file gives no notes. The same code is in 1.21.0 (unchanged since v1.19.1).
Owner choice: "Notları göster + yeniden başlat uyarısı".
- Host: `SHIPPED_NOTES` read once at start; `installedUpdate(file, running)` gives the on-disk version only when it is newer; the plain GET adds `installed`.
- Client: `restart` status when `info.installed` is newer than `info.current`; `createWhatsNew` gets `ask`/`refresh`/`getRunning`/`show` and a `dsh-composer-plus.update.seen` key (the first start of a new version shows its notes once; a fresh install shows nothing); Settings row button **What’s new in this version** (only while Automatic updates is on).
- Texts: `update.whatsNew` in en, zh and 11 locales; `build:locales`.
- Docs: `autoUpdate` how ×13 + llms, route `installed`, README limits ×13, `build:readme`.
- Tests: `test/whats-new-current.test.mjs` (in `test`), `test/update.test.mjs` adapted, real dsh `test/browser-whats-new.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2.
Out of scope: version bump, release, live copy install. Workaround now: restart dsh.
Last checkpoint (2026-11-05): done, not committed. `node test/whats-new-current.test.mjs` red on HEAD (no `installedUpdate`; with the new index.js, the HEAD client keeps no seen version), green now. Real dsh `test/browser-whats-new.mjs` (steps 1–9, `chromiumSandbox: true`) PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; `test/browser-settings-shots.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2. `npm test` exit 0 (one earlier full run failed once with `ETIMEDOUT` in `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` "token"; two single reruns and the next full run passed: flaky, gateway not changed). `git diff --check` clean.

## Secret input card hidden in a closed step group (2026-11-05, branch `fix/secret-request-dropdown-visibility`)
Owner report: the agent called `request_secret`; the owner waited 20 minutes for a window, but the card was open inside a closed step group (accordion) and did not show.
- Cause (dsh source, same in 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 and the desktop app 0.2.1-rc.5): the card is dsh's tool row (`tool.call.toolview`) inside `ChatGroupSeat`. The desktop default view `standard` (also the owner's `ui-chat.transcriptView`) has `stepGrouping: "collapsed"`: the group body starts `hidden="until-found"` while the turn runs. The web default `detailed` keeps it open, so `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` (web) did not see the bug.
- Fix (`client.js`): `revealSecretCard(card)` sends `beforematch` (dsh's own browser-find listener calls `setOpen(true)`) to each `until-found` ancestor after dsh's commit (`setTimeout 0`), then `scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest'})`. `SecretRow` calls it through the card's ref once per `askId`; a group that the user closes stays closed. No focus change, no listener, no DOM host.
- Checks: `test/secret-input-client.test.mjs` (fake DOM chain: only `until-found` ancestors, after the commit, not for a detached card; once per ask, a new ask again; no ref on the waiting and result lines) red on 4 mutations (no dispatch, no once-per-ask guard, any hidden ancestor, sync). `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` now runs with `transcriptView: standard` and checks in step 5 that the card is visible, on the screen and in an open group without a click: red on the old client.js (0.2.1-rc.2: "58 × locator resolved to hidden"), PASS steps 1–16 on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
- Not done (not requested): commit, version, release, live desktop copy.
- Then (owner screenshot, Turkish UI, narrow chat): on the Auto review approval card the four buttons (Allow for this chat, Reject with note…, Reject, Allow once) shrank and each label broke over 2–4 lines, out of its button. Cause: dsh's `actionRow` is `display: flex` without wrap (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, desktop 0.2.1-rc.5); our two buttons made the row too wide. Fix: both buttons carry `data-composer-plus-approval-action`; `allowForChat` and `rejectNote` each install a style (`data-composer-plus-approval-fit`, removed by its switch): a card row with our button wraps (`row-gap: 8px`) and its buttons keep `flex: none; white-space: nowrap`. dsh's card without our buttons is not changed. Checks: `test/header-fit.test.mjs` (style per feature, only rows with our marker, switch off removes it, both buttons marked) red on HEAD client.js and on 3 mutations; `test/browser-grant-ttl.mjs` step 1b (560 and 420 px: four buttons, each label on one line, no overflow, no overlap, inside the card; now headless with `chromiumSandbox: true`) red on HEAD client.js (0.2.1-rc.2: "lines: 2"), PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2; screenshots checked by eye (560 px: two rows, 420 px: three rows, right-aligned). `test/browser-t3code-ideas.mjs` PASS on both; `npm test` exit 0.

## Unique copy count: installed.txt (2026-11-06, branch `feat/unique-install-counter`)
Request (owner): GitHub gives only the total download count of a file, so `active.txt` counts the same copy again each day and the number of unique copies is unknown (today only a range of 63–188). Add a one-time count; the next release carries it. Not in scope: commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy, merge.
Objective: each copy asks `github.com/<repo>/releases/download/v<version>/installed.txt` once in its life per DSH_HOME; the sum of the `installed.txt` download counts of all releases is the number of unique copies; `node scripts/active-count.mjs` shows the daily active part and the unique part (per version, total, new since the last run).
Invariants (all of "Anonymous daily active count" stay): no id, cookie, query or chat data (only `user-agent: dsh-composer-plus`, `redirect: 'manual'`, body not read); no switch, route, `FEATURES` id or UI (owner decision); no request from a git checkout (`.git` next to index.js), a `@local/...` copy or a non-x.y.z version; no new counter: `activeCounter` gets `asset` and `once`; the mark is its own file `storages/dsh-composer-plus/installed.json` (`{day, done: true}`, 0600, atomic); `active.json` keeps `{day}`.
Behavior: an answer below 400 → `{day, done: true}`, never again; network error, 403, 429, 5xx → no mark, the hourly timer tries again; 404 → only `{day}`: no request again that UTC day, a new one the next day (else a copy on a release without the file is never counted); a failed save keeps the mark in memory (no second request in the session); one call at a time.
Checks: `test/active-count.test.mjs` (one request, none on a second start or a later day or after an update; 404 → no lifetime mark, none the same day or after a restart that day, one the next day; network/403/429/500/503 → retried, no file; failed save; git checkout, @local and pre-release send nothing for both files; `apply()` writes neither file in a git checkout; the report: unique per version, total over all releases, new since the last run, the old cache `{at, counts}` reads without an error); `test/release-notes.test.mjs` (a release without `installed.txt` stops the publish and names `gh release upload vX.Y.Z assets/installed.txt`); `npm test`; `npm pack --dry-run` (no active.txt, installed.txt or scripts/); real headless dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2: a packed copy writes installed.json and sends no request on a second start, the symlinked checkout writes no file.
Allowed paths: index.js, assets/installed.txt, scripts/{active-count,release-notes}.mjs, test/{active-count,release-notes}.test.mjs, AGENTS.md (Release), README*.md (Privacy, Limits), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Limits (also in the 13 READMEs, Limits): it counts copies, not people (one per DSH_HOME); only copies that update to the first release with `installed.txt` or a later one are counted, a copy that stays on an older version is not; a copy whose `storages` folder is deleted counts again; GitHub can count requests from one NAT IP address in about 10 minutes as one (parttimenerd/download-count-experiment).
Risks: the first release with `installed.txt` starts the count at 0, so the unique number grows only as copies update; a release without the file gives 404 and each copy asks again once a day until a release has it (`release.yml` stops a release without it).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-06): done, not committed. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; mutations: 20 code changes (index.js, scripts/active-count.mjs, scripts/release-notes.mjs) red in a temp copy with a `.git` marker (baseline green), and each of the 58 new asserts flipped once is red; `npm pack --dry-run` 105 files, no active.txt, installed.txt or scripts/; real headless dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 (preload answers 302 for `/releases/download/`, so no real count changed; packed copy with version 1.99.0): first start asks active.txt and installed.txt once each and writes `{day}` and `{day, done: true}`, a second start with the same DSH_HOME sends nothing; the symlinked checkout sends nothing and writes no count file. `node scripts/active-count.mjs` against the live API with a copy of the owner's old cache `{at, counts}`: no error, both parts print, the new cache has `installed`. Next: the next release attaches assets/active.txt and assets/installed.txt.

## Branch per chat: the sent text stayed in the composer (2026-11-06, branch `fix/clear-composer-on-branch-chat`)

Owner report: a chat started with **New branch** works, but its composer and the next New chat of the project show the text of the first message again.
- Flow: Composer Plus **Branch per chat** (`worktreeChats`, client `installBranchSink`), not dsh's own Branch (fork). The new chat is made with `sessionController.create`, not a fork, so `inheritedEventCount` does not apply here (header of the owner's chat: `isSeeded:false`, no `parentSession`).
- Cause: the sink wrapper opened the new chat inside the send, before dsh settled the send. dsh 0.2.2 (desktop 0.2.2-rc.1, npm `latest`) clears the sent draft only in `settleSink` → `applySinkOutcome` → `commit-draft`, and drops the answer when the chat changed (`dead(attempt)`). So the text stayed in the old New chat's store `dsh.conversation.<id>` (localStorage). dsh reuses that blank New chat for the project (`createInWorkspace`), so the text came back. 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2 clear the draft at Enter (`detachedEffects`): not affected.
- Evidence: the owner's desktop Local Storage (a copy, read only) had the first message of `feat-unique-install-counter` as the draft of its blank source chat `session-681d6f7e` (4 events, no message), the branch chat's own draft empty.
- Fix (client.js `installBranchSink` run): open the new chat in the next task for every start (was only for `/plan`), and pass dsh 0.2.2's 5th sink argument (`accept`) through when the send is not ours.
- Checks: `test/worktree-chats-client.test.mjs` new block (dsh clears the draft only while this composer is open; the new chat opens after that; `accept` goes on) red on HEAD client.js, green now. `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` new step "The sent text leaves the New chat" (new branch chat, next New chat of acme-web, the branch chat clicked again: empty composer, no `dsh.conversation.*` with the text): red on HEAD client.js on 0.2.2-rc.1, PASS on 0.2.2-rc.1, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
- Also fixed (found in the 0.2.2 run): a first message with a file failed on dsh 0.2.2-rc.1 with "File attachment reference is invalid". Cause: dsh 0.2.2 `fileUploads.resolve` gives the staged `{type, attachment}` (0.2.0/0.2.1: the file reference itself), so `store.readFileStream` got no `attachmentId`. Fix (index.js `startOnBranch`): take `attachment` of a staged `file`; a staged image is not a file (refused as "not uploaded any more"). Checks: `test/worktree-chats.test.mjs` (0.2.2 staged form; staged image refused) red on HEAD index.js, green now; `test/browser-worktree-chats.mjs` with the file PASS on 0.2.2-rc.1, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2.

Source: x.com/realchendahuang/status/2118366625804202080 (2026-11-05): DeepSeek Harness recommends four community plugins, dsh-media-desk, dsh-guizang-design, dsh-context, agentero-dsh; its link is the DeepSeek WeChat article (plugin stories, an official plugin market, author support). The feature lists come from the four npm READMEs (registry.npmjs.org).
Comparison (owner asked: exists / does not fit / useful):
- Exists: context composition and `/context` (contextBreakdown, dsh's context ring); files and commands of a chat (Changes "This chat", dsh trajectory); agent family (dsh subagent tree, agent-team); system prompt prefix/suffix (AGENTS.md); voice profile, "make the same", workflow drafts, UI language, sidebar entries (projectMemory, memorySuggest, skills, featureIdeas, handoffChat, language packs).
- Does not fit: content production (media-desk, guizang); Agentero's vault, notes, highlights, translation; Context Insights over all chats (usageReport was dropped by the owner on 2026-11-04; dsh-context can be installed); plugin market and author program (DeepSeek's side).
- Useful: Agentero's "select text → add to the conversation" for dsh's own file preview → `previewQuote`. Not in dsh 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 or the desktop 0.2.2-rc.1 (no insert path in the preview package; no such label).
Phase 0 (real dsh, headless, /tmp probe, both versions the same): the preview root is `[data-textpreview-state="text"][data-document-preview=@deepseek-ai/dsh-client-ui-sidebar-documentpreview/<text|code|markdown|pdf>]` with `data-textpreview-url` = `dsh-resource://file/session/<chat>/<path>` (relative) or `…/absolute/<path>`; plain text pages `[data-textpreview-page=<first line>]` with one `[data-textpreview-line=N]` row per line (1-based); code is one shiki `pre` with `.line` spans joined by "\n" (no line numbers in the selection text); Markdown is `[data-document-markdown]` (formatted, no lines); PDF pages `[data-pdf-page=N]` with a pdf.js text layer; the panel `[data-sidebar-right-session=<chat>][data-sidebar-right-panel=push|fullscreen]`. A selection that ends at the start of the next row (triple-click) has a trailing "\n".
Design: `QuoteReply` takes `pick`/`format`/`mark`/`title` (defaults = Quote in reply); `previewTarget`, `previewSpan` (line = page start + line breaks before the range; code from 1), `previewText` (`path:a-b` + a fence longer than any backtick run; Markdown/PDF `> ` lines, `#page=N`), `relativePath` (with a separator), `filePathOf` (dsh's `parseFileAddress` grammar). Own row `preview-quote` in `conversation.input.right`, on by default, no Host route.
Checks: `test/preview-quote.test.mjs` (in `npm test`); 16 mutations red (other chat address, sibling prefix, line-end start, triple-click end, fence, fullscreen, other panel, header, two previews, cut, line count, pick, mark, switch). `test/browser-preview-quote.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (code lines 2–3, text line 2, Markdown, PDF page 1 reach the fake model as expected; header no button; answers keep Quote in reply only; switch off; no page errors). `assets/features/previewQuote.webp` 7.8 kB (SHOT=1; the path label shows a made-up `~/projects/acme-web/src/` in the picture only). Tether: `no` (no file preview on the phone).

Owner request: in Settings → Composer Plus, a filter that shows only the features that are on, or only the ones that are off; the state comes from each feature's own switch. Owner choice (asked): the filter is a full feature (own `FEATURES` id, screenshot, README row, Tether row).
Objective: feature `featureFilter` (on by default, browser switch, no Host route). Next to **Search features**: **All**, **On**, **Off** (a `role="group"` of three native buttons with `aria-pressed`; dsh's `SegmentedControl` is not used because it makes `role="tab"` and `aria-controls` panels that do not exist).
Invariants:
- The state of a row is `isOn(enabled, id)` from `useComposerPlusToggles` (Host switches merged by `createToggles`), the same value as the row's Switch.
- Search and filter both apply. With a filter other than All, the language and update rows hide (they have no on/off state). No result: the existing `settings.searchEmpty` notice.
- A row that the user switches while a filter is on stays (`kept`) until a filter button is pressed again, so the focus does not fall to `<body>`. Limit: a change from another window or Tether hides the row at once.
- The choice is not saved: Settings opens with All (as the search box).
- `feature('featureFilter', () => [])`: it installs nothing; the group shows only while the switch is on; off shows every row.
- The group is the second child of `[data-composer-plus-search]`: `tree.c` indexes of the other tests do not change.
Acceptance: `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `test/browser-settings-shots.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2; `assets/features/featureFilter.webp` ≤ about 60 kB and in `npm pack --dry-run`.
Phases: 1 red unit test `test/feature-filter.test.mjs`; 2 client.js + index.js + toggles counts; 3 texts in en, zh, 11 locales + `build:locales`; 4 `docs/features.json`, hand-written Settings paragraph ×13, `build:readme`, `tether/FEATURES.md` row (no); 5 real dsh check + `SHOT=1` screenshot; 6 `npm test`, diff review.
Allowed paths: client.js, index.js, locales/*.json, docs/features.json, README*.md, llms.txt, tether/FEATURES.md, assets/features/featureFilter.webp, test/feature-filter.test.mjs, test/toggles.test.mjs, test/browser-settings-shots.mjs, package.json (`test` script only), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Out of scope: version bump, release notes, commit, live desktop copy, Tether iOS code, `docs/images/settings.webp`, saved filter, counts on the buttons.
Risks: the focus ring of a native button inside dsh's Settings may not show (check in the real app; add a `:focus-visible` outline if not).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-06): all phases done, not committed.
- `test/feature-filter.test.mjs` (in `test` after toggles) red on HEAD ("featureFilter is a FEATURES id"), green now; red on 5 mutations (state not from `isOn`, no `kept`, language row under a filter, switch off still filters, `kept` not cleared).
- Test mock fixes, no product change: `test/toggles.test.mjs` `useState` returns the query only for the `''` state (counts 67 → 68, 58 → 59); `test/shots.test.mjs` resets the hook state before it renders the thumbnail alone.
- Texts: 6 keys in en, zh and 11 locales; `build:locales`. Docs: `docs/features.json` entry (13 `how`, `llms`), `build:readme`, one hand-written sentence in the Settings paragraph ×13; `tether/FEATURES.md` row `no`.
- Real dsh `test/browser-settings-shots.mjs` (now `chromiumSandbox: true`, filter steps added) PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2 (`SHOT=1`: `assets/features/featureFilter.webp` 23,596 bytes, English UI, no projects) and 0.2.1-rc.2: 68 rows, Off shows the 10 off rows, a switched row stays and keeps the focus, Off again hides it, On shows only on rows, Tab from the search reaches All with a visible focus ring (no extra CSS needed), All shows every row.

## Report an issue with AI (`issueReport`, 2026-11-06, branch `feat/ai-github-issue-panel`)
Owner request: a panel in Settings (and other fitting places) where the user picks a type, writes a prompt, and the AI writes the GitHub issue and opens it. Owner choices: the target repository is always `furkancak1r/dsh-composer-plus`; show the draft first, open only on a click.
- Decision: a new feature with its own switch, not an extension of Open GitHub issue (Later list). That button opens an issue in the chat's own project repository from a saved idea; this panel opens one in the plugin's repository from the user's text. Shared parts are reused: `ghIssueCreate` (split out of `openIssue`, same argv when no `repo`), `memoryModelCall`, `looksSecret`, the `createHandoff` store pattern.
- Places: Settings → Composer Plus (a button under the intro, and the feature row), the chat's "…" menu (order 490), Tether Settings → Composer Plus.
- Types: bug `[Bug]`, feature `[Feature request]`, improvement `[Improvement]`, translation `[Translation]`, question `[Question]`. No labels (a user without write access can fail on them).
- Host route `POST /api/composer-plus.issue-report`: `{type, text}` → draft `{title, body, model}` (one call to dsh's default model, English, JSON; prefix and a version line added by the Host; home path → `~`); `{title, body, open: true}` → `{issue}` (`gh issue create --repo=…` in the home folder; secret check; one open at a time; the same text again in 1 hour answers the earlier issue).
- Client: `createIssueReport` store (busy checked in the store for every entry point; a closed panel drops a later draft; close waits for a running open), `IssueReportDialog`, fallback link `issues/new?title&body` only after a failure.
Invariants: Open GitHub issue (Later list) and `test/github-issue.test.mjs` do not change. No model call and no gh call without a click. Switch off removes the Settings button, the menu item and the overlay.
Acceptance: see the plan in the chat; `npm test` passes.
Checks: `test/issue-report.test.mjs`, `test/issue-report-client.test.mjs` (in `test`), `test/browser-issue-report.mjs` (headless, fake gh and fake model, 0.2.0-rc.2 and 0.2.1-rc.2, `SHOT=1` for `assets/features/issueReport.webp`), Tether simulator build.
Last checkpoint (2026-11-06): all phases done, not committed.
- Host `index.js`: `ghIssueCreate` (argv of `openIssue` unchanged, `test/github-issue.test.mjs` green), `installIssueReport`, `issueDraft`, `parseIssueDraft`, `SHOTS += issueReport`. `test/issue-report.test.mjs` red on 3 mutations (no open guard, no secret check, no repeat check).
- Client `client.js`: `createIssueReport`, `IssueReportDialog`, `IssueReportButton` (inside the intro child, so the Settings tree keeps its indexes), `IssueReportItem`, `feature('issueReport')` (menu item order 490 + overlay). `test/issue-report-client.test.mjs` red on 2 mutations (no busy guard, close during open). `test/toggles.test.mjs` counts 67 → 68, on 58 → 59.
- Texts: en, zh, 11 locales (workflow translators, checked: keys, placeholders, Markdown counts; `readme-labels` green). Docs: features.json entry + route, 13 READMEs (privacy, troubleshooting, limits, FAQ), `build:readme`.
- Tether: `PLUS += issue-report`, `IssueReporter` (Core.swift), `IssueReportSheet` + Settings section (UI.swift), FEATURES.md row. Simulator build succeeded; not run on an iPhone.
- Real dsh: `test/browser-issue-report.mjs` PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (fake model, fake gh first on PATH, temp HOME and GH_CONFIG_DIR, no GH token); `SHOT=1` wrote `assets/features/issueReport.webp` (48 kB).
- `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
Not done (not requested): commit, version bump, release, live desktop copy, iPhone install.
## Untangle codebase (untangleCodebase), 2026-11-06 (branch `feat/composer-plus-x-post-features`)
Owner request: read two X posts (@mazeincoding, 2026-11-05), keep what Composer Plus does not have, plan, add after approval. The posts announce the skill `/untangle-codebase` ([caldoai/skills](https://github.com/caldoai/skills), MIT, © 2026 Maze). Owner approved the plan on 2026-11-06.
Objective: ship the upstream `SKILL.md` (commit f3cf47774975, sha256 b4eb6b51…7761, unchanged) in the package and give it to dsh's skill catalog through a Host skill provider, user-invocable only (`/untangle-codebase [focus]`), behind its own switch (on by default).
Invariants:
- No network call: the text is a pinned copy in `assets/skills/untangle-codebase/` (with its LICENSE).
- `modelInvocable: false`: dsh filters the model's skill catalog with `isModelInvocable` (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1), so the skill costs no prompt tokens in other chats.
- `rank: 600` (dsh's `BUNDLED_SKILL_RANK`): a project or `~/.dsh/skills` skill with the same name wins.
- Switch off (route `{on:false}`) and plugin unload dispose the provider fiber; nothing stays registered.
- Client: no DOM, listener or style; only the Host switch store (like plannedSteps), so no `feature(id, setup)` call.
Acceptance: `npm test` exit 0; `test/untangle-codebase.test.mjs` red when each guard is removed; `test/browser-untangle-codebase.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 and 0.2.2-rc.1 (Settings row + shot, `/` menu, body + argument in the model request, not in the model's catalog, a user skill wins, switch off removes it, no page errors); `npm pack --dry-run` ships the skill, its LICENSE and the webp; `git diff --check` clean.
Phases: 1 Host + unit test; 2 client store/toggles/texts en+zh + toggles test; 3 11 packs + build:locales, features.json (how ×13, llms, route) + build:readme, README Limits/FAQ/License ×13, tether/FEATURES.md (yes: `/` menu from `skills/list`); 4 browser check on 3 dsh versions + SHOT=1; 5 npm test, pack, diff check.
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, package.json (files, test), assets/skills/untangle-codebase/{SKILL.md,LICENSE}, assets/features/untangleCodebase.webp, locales/*.json, locale/*.json (built), docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, llms.txt, README*.md, tether/FEATURES.md, test/{untangle-codebase.test.mjs,browser-untangle-codebase.mjs,toggles.test.mjs}, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Risks: the upstream text changes (we keep the pinned copy; a new copy needs a new release); a dsh without the `skills` service (the inject never runs: the row shows the restart text); a long goal changes many files (README Limits: work on a branch, review in Changes).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-06): done, not committed. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `npm pack --dry-run` 109 files with `assets/skills/untangle-codebase/{SKILL.md,LICENSE}` and `assets/features/untangleCodebase.webp` (7.1 kB, SHOT=1 on 0.2.1-rc.2: the `/` menu over the composer, English UI, demo-app). `test/untangle-codebase.test.mjs` red on 9/9 mutations (modelInvocable true, rank 50, default off, off without dispose, unload without dispose, no body check, no serial switching, frontmatter kept, no name check). `test/browser-untangle-codebase.mjs` PASS on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1: row + shot on by default; body + focus in the request while the catalog (with a model-visible demo skill) leaves it out; `/` menu with dsh's "user-only" mark; the model's own `skill` call refused ("not available for model invocation"); off → no menu entry, plain text; a user skill with the same name wins; no page errors. `test/mobile-connect.test.mjs`: its fake host now also skips the `skills` inject (like `tools`). Not done (not requested): commit, version, release, live desktop copy, Tether build (no Tether code change).

## Tether: Sessions lag, chat stays at the last message, long thinking closes in place (2026-11-06, branch `fix/ios-performance-scroll-thinking`)
Owner report (iPhone 13): the app lags, most on Sessions; after a send the chat stays higher than the last message; a long open thinking group can only close from its header far above. No new setting (owner rule: ask before one).
- Causes (code): every token writes `Client.streaming`/`reasoning` (`@Published` on the one `ObservableObject` that 39 views observe), also while Sessions is on screen (the stream stays open after Back); `SessionsView.body` computes `sessionGroups` and `nextWaiting` twice and runs O(N) `isRunning` per row and header (O(N²)); each `api-session/*` event starts its own full `loadSessions`; the phone build is Debug `-Onone`. Chat: `scrollTo("end")` runs in `onChange` before the new row's layout, only 5 values are watched, and `atBottom` comes from an 80 pt marker that a large insert can push off. Thinking: `StepsView` closes only from its header.
- Decisions: iOS floor 17.0 → 18.0 (`onScrollGeometryChange`, `onScrollPhaseChange`; the only device runs iOS 27.2). The chat follows the end until the user drags up; ↓, send, open chat and a drag back to the end follow again. An open step group's header pins to the top (LazyVStack pinned section headers). Phone build stays Debug (APNs sandbox) with `SWIFT_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL=-O`.
- Phases: P0 measure on the iPhone (Animation Hitches, SwiftUI, Debug and -O); 1 `LiveText` for stream text, `runningIds`, `Coalesced` list loads, no rewrite of equal values; 2 scroll follow; 3 pinned step header; docs (tether/README.md, tether/AGENTS.md); tests in `tether/ios/Tests/Tests.swift`.
- Allowed paths: `tether/ios/{Sources,Tests,project.yml,DSHMobile.xcodeproj}`, `tether/README.md`, `tether/AGENTS.md`, this file.
- Checks: iOS unit tests (simulator), `npm test`, iPhone 13 build with `-O`, xctrace before/after, agent-device checks (send, stream, drag up, ↓, keyboard, long thinking close).
- Acceptance: Sessions hitch ratio < 5 ms/s while a chat streams; tokens do not republish `Client` (unit test); last message visible after send/stream/keyboard/cards unless the user dragged up; an open step group's header is visible while any part of the group is on screen and one tap closes it in place.
- Measured on the iPhone 13 (old Debug -Onone build, Sessions idle, 15 s Time Profiler): 1758 Tether samples, 1730 on the main thread; 1006 in `ViewGraphDisplayLink.displayLinkTimer` (SwiftUI redraw each frame), driven by `RunningSpinner`'s `TimelineView(.animation)`. So the conditional step "spinner" was done: the arc now turns with a Core Animation `CABasicAnimation` (`SpinArc`), SwiftUI draws nothing per frame. New build (-O): 3 Tether samples in the same 15 s run (a Question sheet of a live chat may have been on screen: inference, not a clean A/B). The Animation Hitches A/B with agent-device scrolls is not valid: the "after" run also caught `AutomationModeUI` hitches.
- Not verified on a device yet: chat follows its end, pinned step header, spinner turns. Blocked: a live Question sheet (another chat, the owner's answer) covers Tether; sending a test message needs the owner's explicit approval (Auto review denied a Quick chat).
Last checkpoint (2026-11-06 11:40): code done, not committed. iOS unit tests 94/94 (SmoothChatTests red on 2 mutations: tokens republish Client, no coalescing), `npm test` exit 0, `git diff --check` clean. New build (-O, push profile) installed on the iPhone 13 with devicectl (data kept).

## Remote computers: switch Macs in the same window (2026-11-06, branch `feat/in-app-remote-mac-switch`)
Request (owner, Turkish, plan approved 2026-11-06): (1) move the computer picker from the chat header to the left sidebar, right of the "deepseek HARNESS" brand, above New session; (2) a picked Mac opens in the same dsh window (no external browser), with its own chats, sidebar, Plugins, Automation, Task board and Settings; (3) this Mac keeps running behind it; the remote page shows the same picker in the same place (This Mac + the other computers), one place to switch; (4) Add asks for a name (alias) that must differ from This Mac and the other computers; Settings renames it.
Facts (checked in code): desktop 0.2.2-rc.1 main window opens any non-`dsh-app:` navigation with `shell.openExternal`; a `<webview>` from `dshDesktop.browser.acquire(key)` (`src="about:blank#<lease>"`, same partition) may load `http(s)` except dsh's own Host port; an `<iframe>` in `dsh-app://app` gets no SameSite=Strict cookie (third party); the sidebar logo row class `hHd-Xa_logoRow` (first child the brand) is the same on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1; desktop key routing: fixed bindings (Enter, Esc of the composer and approval) are not in the main-process key set, so they stay in the guest; only configurable shortcuts (⌘N, ⌘K, …) go to the host window while a webview has focus (`priority`); `forwardWebRequest` has no time limit (a 20 s long poll is fine).
Design: Host `computersService`: `add` needs `name` (1–64, no control chars, unique case-insensitive against This Mac and other entries; checked before pairing; a race after Approve saves `name (2)`); `rename` refuses empty and taken names; view `duplicate`; `{action:'events', after}` long poll (20 s) of `{switch}` / `{hello}` events. Relay: `/__composer-plus/opener` (GET: self, computers, current) and `/__composer-plus/switch` (POST `{to}`), answered by the relay itself (its own cookie and origin checks), never sent to the other Mac. Client: picker host in the logo row (MutationObserver, removed on off), full-window views (desktop webview with its own lease per computer, web iframe), hidden views stay alive, one `select` at a time from every entry point, a 6 s fallback "Back to this Mac" button when the remote page does not say hello, remote page (`via`) reads the picker list from the relay.
Invariants: off removes host, root, views (lease released), observer, poll, timers, style; never an external window; no token in the client; the remote page cannot change this Mac's computers (only switch the view among them).
Allowed paths: index.js, client.js, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/FEATURES.md, locales/*.json, locale/*.json, docs/{features.json,ROUTES.md}, README*.md, llms.txt, assets/features/remoteComputers.webp, test/computers*.test.mjs, test/browser-computers.mjs, IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.
Checks: unit tests (host, relay, client) with mutations; `test/browser-computers.mjs` on 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1; `npm test`; `npm pack --dry-run`; owner's desktop check with two real Macs (phase 5).
Out of scope: version bump, release, commit, iPhone build.
Phase 0 done (2026-11-06): code reading above; no product code.
Phase 1 done (2026-11-06): Host `computersService` names (`cleanName`, `taken` NFC + case fold, `name-missing` 400 / `name-taken` 409 before the pairing, race → `name (2)`, rename refuses empty/taken, view `duplicate`), `events` long poll (`pollMs` 20 s, no `after` = current seq, unload ends waiters); relay `RELAY_OWN` paths `opener` (GET, hello) and `switch` (POST ≤ 1 kB, `self|add|<64 hex>`), answered by the relay (cookie, Host, Origin checks), never forwarded; no `peer` → 404. Checks: `test/computers-host.test.mjs` 10, `test/computers.test.mjs` 12; 14 + 4 mutations red (after two test fixes: unload ends a 60 s poll; the relay switch body).
Phase 2 done (2026-11-06): client `createComputerViews` (desktop webview with lease `composer-plus-computer:<id>`, `loadURL` once after dom-ready; web iframe; hidden views kept with `visibility`; drag strip; one `select` at a time; event loop starts at the current seq; only the page on screen switches; `add` → This Mac + dialog; 6 s hello wait → Back button + refresh; `sync` closes removed/unpaired views; `stop` releases leases, root, timer, loop), `createOpenerPicker` (relay paths; 404 → no picker), `ComputerPicker` (one menu for this Mac and the other Mac's page), `useBrandRowHost` (after `[class*="_brand"]` in `[class*="_logoRow"]`, none in the rail, flex-shrink before the brand), `ComputerViewsBar`, add dialog Name field (follows the link's `mac` until typed; This Mac/other names refused), Settings rename guard and duplicate hint. Removed: chat-header slot, Browser tab and window paths. Checks: `test/computers-client.test.mjs` red on 22/22 mutations.
Phase 3 (2026-11-06): en/zh + 11 locales (subagent) + `build:locales`, `locale.test` pass; features.json en/zh `how`, `llms`, route POST text; README.md, README.tr.md, README.zh.md privacy/limits/FAQ by hand; FEATURES.md row; `remoteComputers.webp` 6.9 kB (SHOT=1 on 0.2.2-rc.1, checked by eye). 11 `how` + 10 READMEs: second subagent.
Phase 4 (2026-11-06): `test/browser-computers.mjs` rewritten; PASS on 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1 and 0.2.0-rc.2 (picker right of the brand, above New session, brand not cut; Name asked, "office mac" refused; B fills A's window, no new window; B's picker from A's relay switches back; A's 6 s turn finished meanwhile; no reload of the hidden page; B removes A → page closes + "pair again"; off leaves no host/root, relay and door closed). First 0.2.1-rc.2 run: the picker cut the brand (fixed with flex-shrink).
Checkpoint phases 0–4 (2026-11-06): 11 `how` + 10 READMEs by the second subagent (only lines 183/250/374 and the generated row 102 changed; three extra spaces after `。` in README.ja.md 250 removed by hand); `build:readme`, readme, readme-labels, feature-docs, `build-docs --check` pass; Semgrep (p/javascript, p/security-audit) on index.js, client.js, server.mjs: 0 findings; `npm test` exit 0; `npm pack --dry-run` (105 files, `tether/` only `gateway/*`); `git diff --check` clean. Not committed.
Open: phase 5, the owner's desktop check with two real Macs. Not checked yet: the real Electron `<webview>` path (lease, cookie, drag strip, focus, Enter/Esc) over Tailscale (only a fake bridge in the unit test). The owner's stored computer has the same name as this Mac: Settings shows the duplicate hint, rename it there.

## Remote computers: no "Back to this Mac" banner, the picker comes back (2026-11-06, branch `refactor/replace-return-banner-with-picker`)
Request (owner, Turkish): remove the "Back to this Mac" banner at the top while another Mac fills the window; the computer picker of the sidebar stays in its place and, while away, says "Back to this Mac" (`computers.back`) and brings this Mac back. Owner's choice for the click: with no other computer to pick, the click comes back at once (no menu); with more computers, the click opens the menu.
Design: `ComputerViewsBar` and its `shell.overlay` slot removed; `views.sync()` moved into `SidebarComputerPicker`. `createComputerViews` state `fallback` → `noPicker` (the page on screen did not say hello yet, so it has no picker of its own). While `noPicker`, `useComputerFloat` portals this Mac's picker into a fixed element over the views (z-index 41) at the host's right edge, centered on the brand row (top left when the sidebar is closed); it goes when the page says hello, on This Mac, and when the slot unmounts. `ComputerPicker`: away (a computer on screen, or `via`) → label `computers.back`, title names the computer, no `aria-label`; `direct` (no other `ok` computer) → no menu, no arrow, the click selects This Mac (or asks the relay `switch: self`). No new text: `computers.back` exists in en, zh and the 11 locales. Before 1.23.0 the relay had no hello path (`git show v1.22.0:tether/gateway/server.mjs`), so "older than 1.23.0" in the READMEs is checked.
Checks (2026-11-06): `test/computers-client.test.mjs` pass; 7 of 8 mutations red (never/always direct, label, no float, float after hello, noPicker not updated, float not removed); `views.sync` in a `useEffect` cannot be checked with the fake React (it is checked in the real dsh run: B removes A → the page closes). `test/browser-computers.mjs` PASS on 0.2.2-rc.1, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.0-rc.2 (B's picker "Back to this Mac" at the same right edge and middle as A's, one click back, no float after hello, A's float over the relay's text in the picker's place, no banner, off leaves no host/root/float). Docs: `how` 13 languages + `llms` in `docs/features.json`, `build:readme`; limits and FAQ by hand in 13 READMEs. `npm test` exit 0; `git diff --check` clean.
Not checked: the desktop `<webview>` path with two real Macs (as before).

## Assistant: kişisel asistan sohbeti (`assistant`, 2026-11-06, dal `feat/composer-plus-assistant-chat`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe): Grok/ChatGPT gibi tek, kalıcı bir asistan sohbeti. Mac'te sol kenar çubuğunda Task board'un hemen altında, Tether'da Sessions ekranında açılır. Hatırlatma yapar, web'de araştırır, otomasyonları (dsh zamanlanmış görevleri) ve öteki dsh sohbetlerini yönetir. Tasarım fikri: Fei2411/DS-Bot `ds-bot/README.md` (Main Bot, Memory, Scheduled tasks; Apache-2.0, kod alınmadı).

### Hedef
- Özellik `assistant`: kendi anahtarı, varsayılan kapalı (`OFF_BY_DEFAULT`: başka sohbetlerde model turu başlatır). Anahtarı Host tutar (`assistant-switch.json`).
- Tek sohbet, klasörü `~/Assistant` (yoksa oluşur, dsh'ye "Assistant" projesi olarak eklenir). Kimliği `$DSH_HOME/storages/dsh-composer-plus/assistant.json` (`{sessionId}`, 0600, atomik yazım). Silinmiş ya da bulunamayan sohbet (`session/not-found`) bir sonraki açılışta yeniden oluşur. Arşivlenmiş sohbet arşivden çıkar. Model seçimine dokunulmaz (dsh'nin varsayılanı).
- İzin: yeni sohbete `workspace-write` hazır ayarı (approval `ask`). Kullanıcı sonra değiştirirse dokunulmaz. İzin genişletilmez.
- Araçlar (yalnız asistan sohbeti): `chats_list`, `chat_read` (okuma, onaysız); `chat_send`, `chat_new`, `chat_stop`, `chat_archive` (her çağrı dsh onay kartı: `tools/pre-execute` → `ask`). Silme aracı yok. Otomasyonlar dsh'nin kendi `schedule_*` araçlarıyla.
- Sistem metni (İngilizce, kısa) yalnız asistan sohbetine (`systemPrompt.section`, ajan kimliği kontrolü).
- Mac: `sidebar.panellist` girdisi "Assistant" (order 21, Task board 20). Tıklama asistan sohbetini açar. dsh'nin `selectPanel` çağrısı kayıtlı bir `main` paneli istediği için yalnız "Açılıyor…" ya da hata satırı gösteren küçük bir panel kaydedilir; sohbet açılınca dsh paneli kendisi kapatır (`openSession` → `selectPanel(null)`). Yeni bir sayfa yok.
- Tether: Sessions araç çubuğunda Assistant düğmesi (`c.plus.contains("assistant")`), `GET /plus/assistant` → sohbeti açar. `PLUS += assistant (GET)`. Bildirim için yeni kod yok (Turn finished push zaten var).

### Kararlar ve gerekçeler
- Araçlar asistan ajanının kendi kapsamına kaydedilir (`agent.ctx.tools.register`, dsh: "Register globally or in the calling agent scope"). Öteki sohbetlerin listesine hiç girmez; bir kayıt hatası aracı başka sohbete sızdırmaz. Asistanın alt ajanları kapsam zincirinden miras alacağı için onlara `tools.restrict({deny})` uygulanır. Her araç ayrıca çağıranı denetler: asistan sohbeti değilse reddeder (birim testi). Kullanıcı `tools.restrict` yazdı; genel kayıt + her sohbete restrict yerine bu yol daha dar ve güvenli. Özet yanıtta bildirilecek.
- İki rota: `assistant-switch` (GET/POST `{on}`, anahtar; PLUS'ta yok, kural 8) ve `assistant` (GET: sohbet kimliği, yoksa oluşturur; PLUS'ta). Anahtar kapalıyken `assistant` rotası kayıttan kalkar.
- `chat_archive`, `stopActivity` olmadan arşivler: dsh'nin `stopActivity` seçeneği sohbetin etkin hatırlatmalarını siler. Çalışan ya da hatırlatması olan sohbette dsh'nin nedeni modele döner (önce `chat_stop` ya da kullanıcıya sor).
- "Seni bekliyor" durumu yalnız çalışan sohbetlerde, kaydın sonundan okunur: yanıtsız `approval/asked` ya da sonucu gelmemiş `ask_user_question`/`exit_plan_mode` çağrısı.
- Kişisel bilgiler: Project memory'nin "profil" konusu. Yeni kod yok; sistem metninde bir satır.

### Değişecek dosyalar
index.js, client.js, locales/*.json (build:locales), docs/features.json, docs/ROUTES.md, llms.txt, README*.md (13), assets/features/assistant.webp, tether/gateway/server.mjs, tether/FEATURES.md, tether/ios/Sources/{Core,UI}.swift, tether/ios/Tests/Tests.swift, test/assistant.test.mjs, test/assistant-client.test.mjs, test/browser-assistant.mjs, test/toggles.test.mjs (sayılar), package.json (yalnız `test` betiği), IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md.

### Aşamalar ve kontroller
1. Host (index.js): `installAssistant` (anahtar, `assistant` rotası, sohbet oluşturma/bulma, araçlar, onay, sistem metni). Kontrol: `test/assistant.test.mjs` (sahte dsh): ilk açılış klasör+proje+sohbet+preset; ikinci açılış aynı kimlik; silinmiş → yeni; arşivli → arşivden çıkar; eşzamanlı iki açılış → tek sohbet; araçlar yalnız asistan kapsamında; başka sohbet çağırınca ret; dört değiştiren araçta `ask`; `chat_read` iki geri alma işaretini çıkarır, çatalda `inheritedEventCount`'tan başlar; `chats_list` süzgeçleri ve durumlar; anahtar kapalı → araç, metin, rota yok. `npm test`.
2. Client (client.js): anahtar deposu, `feature('assistant')` (panellist + main), metinler en/zh/11 dil, Settings satırı ve ekran görüntüsü, belgeler. Kontrol: `test/assistant-client.test.mjs`, `test/toggles.test.mjs`, `npm test`.
3. Tether: PLUS, `openAssistant()`, Sessions düğmesi, FEATURES.md, iOS birim testi. Kontrol: `npm test` (tether-parity, tether-gateway), iOS testleri (simülatör), agent-device ile simülatörde düğme → asistan sohbeti.
4. Gerçek dsh (yalıtılmış, sahte model, geçici HOME ve DSH_HOME): 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2 (+0.2.2-rc.1, canlı masaüstü sürümü). `test/browser-assistant.mjs`: kenar çubuğu girdisi Task board altında; tıklama sohbeti açar; preset workspace-write; asistan isteğinde araçlar var, başka sohbette yok; "2 dakika sonra su içmemi hatırlat" → `schedule_create` → zamanı gelince asistan sohbeti çalışır; `chats_list` başka projeyi gösterir; `chat_send` onay kartı → Allow → mesaj hedefte; enjeksiyon: `chat_read` sonrası `chat_send` kartı → Reject → hiçbir şey gitmez; anahtar kapalı → girdi, araçlar, metin ve rota yok; sayfa hatası yok. `SHOT=1` → `assets/features/assistant.webp`.

### Riskler
- 0.2.0-rc.2 web profilinde schedule yok (deneysel paket); 0.2.0/0.2.1'de `schedule_list` yalnız çağıran sohbetin hatırlatmalarını görür, başka sohbetinkini düzenleyemez. 0.2.2'de araçlar tüm Host görevlerini görür. Gerçek dsh'de doğrulanıp sahibe bildirilecek.
- Onay politikası `never` olan bir hazır ayara (Full access) geçilirse `ask` reddedilir: değiştiren araçlar çalışmaz (güvenli tarafta kalır). Belgelere yazılacak.
- `workspace-write` ile web araçlarının ve schedule araçlarının onay istemeden çalıştığı gerçek dsh'de doğrulanacak.
- Asistan bash ile dsh API'sine doğrudan istek atabilir (sandbox ağ erişimi): araç düzeyinde onay bunu kapsamaz. Sınırlar bölümüne yazılacak.
- dsh boş bir sohbeti aynı projede "New session" için yeniden kullanabilir (asistan sohbeti henüz mesajsızken).

### Yapılmayacak
Sürüm, npm publish, iPhone kurulumu, canlı masaüstü kopyası, dsh yeniden başlatma.

## Tether arka plan bildirimi, asistanda paralel konular, goal noktası, tek tıkla Mac geçişi (2026-11-09, dal `feat/background-notifications-parallel-chats`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe): (1) Tether'a uygulama kapalıyken bildirim gelmiyor; (2) asistanla bir arkadaşla konuşur gibi konuşmak: konu bölünmesin, yeni mesaj çalışan işi kesmesin, aynı anda birden fazla konu açılabilsin (DeepSeek superagent gibi, arka planda ayrı bir zaman çizgisi); (3) Tether sohbet listesinde goal olunca çıkan mavi nokta görünmesin; (4) iki Mac varken sol üstteki seçiciden tek tıkla öteki Mac'e geçmek (şu an liste açılıyor).

### Teşhis (1), kanıt
- Mac tarafı çalışıyor: `apns.json` geçerli, anahtar dosyası var; sahte token ile Apple sandbox `BadDeviceToken` döndü (kimlik doğrulama geçti), production `BadEnvironmentKeyInToken` (anahtar yalnız sandbox, beklenen). Canlı gateway çalışıyor, `/me` push: true veriyor.
- Telefonun kaydı `devices.json`'da `push: {on: false}`, token yok; Mac sayfası `push: false`. Yani Mac hiç push göndermiyor, çünkü telefon token vermedi.
- Son iPhone kurulumu 06.11 11:24 (push profili + `aps-environment` ile imzalı, push kodunu içeriyor). Telefon şu an CoreDevice'a bağlı değil (`unavailable`), bu yüzden telefondaki ayar ve iOS hatası okunamadı.
- Kodda iki zayıflık: `didFailToRegisterForRemoteNotificationsWithError` boştu (hata sessizce yutuluyordu); `registerPush` yalnız `macPush` değişince çağrılıyordu, ilk deneme başarısızsa yeniden denenmiyordu. Mac sayfası push durumunu hiç göstermiyordu.
- Çıkarım (doğrulanmadı): ya telefonda Ayarlar → Send notifications bu Mac için kapalı, ya da iOS token vermedi. Yeni derleme nedeni ekranda ve Mac sayfasında gösterecek.

### Kararlar
- (1) iOS: her `/me` (her bağlantı) iOS'tan token ister ve `POST /push` gönderir; token hatası `tokenError`, gönderim hatası `syncError` olarak tutulur ve Ayarlar → Notifications altında turuncu yazılır; token yoksa telefon nedeni `POST /push {on:false, reason}` ile Mac'e yollar. Gateway: `reason` (≤200) saklanır, son başarısız push'un nedeni bellekte (`pushFails`), `state()` → `pushKey`, cihaz başına `pushFail`, `pushReason`; Mac sayfası her iPhone'un altında durumu yazar. Token hiçbir zaman Mac sayfasına çıkmaz.
- (2) Asistan sistem metni: kısa cevap dışındaki her iş dsh'nin `subagent` aracıyla arka planda (0.2.0'da `run_in_background: true`, 0.2.1+ zaten hemen döner), konu başına bir alt ajan, tek satır bilgi, tur hemen biter; yeni konu çalışanları durdurmaz; dsh'nin bitiş bildirimi yeni bir tur başlatır ve sonuç bildirilir; çalışan konuya mesaj `send_message`. Yeni kod yok, yalnız metin (dsh'nin kendi özelliği). Kanıt: dsh-tool-subagent README "Every call returns `started subagent <childId>` ... does not wait"; bildirim metni "Background subagent … finished … Its closing message: …".
- (3) Tether `SessionsView.row`: goal simgesi (`target`, accentColor) kaldırıldı; sohbetin goal çubuğu kalır.
- (4) Masaüstü `ComputerPicker`: gidilecek tek yer varsa (bu Mac + bir bilgisayar) sol tık doğrudan oraya geçer, başlık "Switch to {name} (right-click for all computers)"; sağ tık menüyü açar (Add a computer…). Tether `MacPicker`: tam iki Mac varsa dokunuş öteki Mac'e geçer (⇄ simgesi), aksi halde menü. Yeni metin: `computers.switchTo` (en, zh, 11 dil).

### Kontroller
- `test/computers-client.test.mjs` (tek tık, sağ tık menüsü, iki bilgisayarda menü); eski kural ile kırmızı ("one other computer: no menu").
- `test/tether-gateway.test.mjs` 32/32 (pushKey, pushFail, temizlenme, reason sınırı, token sayfada yok).
- `test/assistant.test.mjs` (metin kuralları).
- iOS birim testleri 96/96 (`otherMac`, `pushBody` reason).
- Gerçek dsh: `test/browser-assistant.mjs` yeni adım 8 (iki konu aynı anda iki arka plan alt ajanında, P2 önce biter, sonra P1, her sonuç ayrı turda) ve `test/browser-computers.mjs` (sol tık doğrudan Studio, sağ tık menü) 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1.

### Yapılmadı (istenmedi)
Sürüm, yayın, canlı masaüstü kopyası, iPhone kurulumu.

- iOS 96/96. Gateway 32/32. `test/tether-notify-per-mac.test.mjs` yeni kurallara göre güncellendi (her bağlantıda yeniden kayıt, hata yutulmaz, Ayarlar nedeni yazar).
- Gerçek dsh PASS: `test/browser-assistant.mjs` (adım 8 dahil) ve `test/browser-computers.mjs`, 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1. İlk çok sürümlü koşuda adım 8 ikinci sürümde düştü: test hatası (kapılar sürümler arasında sıfırlanmıyordu), düzeltildi.

- Telefon kablosuz bağlı (CoreDevice `transportType: localNetwork`, tünel açık, Developer Mode açık, iOS 27.2).

## Uzak bilgisayarlar: iki yer varken iki parçalı seçici (2026-11-09, dal `fix/match-switcher-ux`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe, "uygula"): iki Mac varken seçicide "Bu Mac" yazıyor ama tıklama öteki Mac'e geçiyor; ekstra tıklama olmadan anlaşılır olsun.

### Kararlar
- Gidilecek iki yer varken (bu Mac + tek bir bilgisayar, ya da ekrandaki bilgisayar) seçici iki parçalı bir düğme grubu olur: "Bu Mac | Studio". Ekrandaki parça vurgulu (`aria-pressed`), ötekine tek tık geçer. Sağ tık tüm menüyü açar (Bilgisayar ekle…). Diğer Mac'in sayfasında (`via`) aynı grup.
- Başka durumlarda (hiç bilgisayar yok, ya da iki ve daha fazla bilgisayar) bugünkü menü düğmesi kalır ("Bu Mac" / "Bu Mac'e dön", ok simgesi).
- Seçici marka satırına sığmaz (marka ~168 pt, iki ad ~130 pt). Host artık `_logoRow`'un altında, **Yeni oturum**'un üstünde kendi satırında, tam genişlikte (her iki modda). Simge sütununda (marka yok) seçici yok.
- Float (sayfanın kendi seçicisi yok): host'un sol, üst ve genişliğinde.
- Yeni metin yok: `computers.thisMacShort`, `computers.switchTo`, `computers.picker`, grup adı `feature.remoteComputers`.
- dsh'nin `SegmentedControl`'u kullanılmaz: `tablist` rolü, var olmayan panellere `aria-controls`, uzun adlarda "…" yok.
- Tether değişmez: `MacPicker` iki Mac'te ⇄ simgesiyle zaten "değiştir" der; telefon başlığında iki ad sığmaz.

### Kontroller
- `test/computers-client.test.mjs`: iki yer → grup (bu Mac seçili, Studio'ya tık geçer, sağ tık menü), uzakta grup, `via` grubu, üç yer → menü; host `_logoRow`'dan sonra.

- `how` 13 dil, README sınırlar + SSS 13 dil, `build:readme`, `npm test`.

## Asistanı bota çevirme: ayrı görünüm ve bot gibi çalışma (2026-11-09, dal `feat/assistant-bot-ui-separation`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe): asistan arayüzde ve işlevde normal proje sohbetlerinden ayrılsın, bir bot gibi çalışsın. Sahibin seçimi (soru kartı): mesajlaşma uygulaması gibi görünsün, kendiliğinden haber versin, hiç meşgul olmasın. Dışarıdan erişim (Telegram) istenmedi.

### Hedef: dört özellik, her biri kendi anahtarıyla
- `assistantApart` (istemci, varsayılan açık): asistan sohbeti ve yalnız onu tutan "Assistant" projesi kenar çubuğunda görünmez (arama sonuçlarında görünür). "Assistant" satırının simgesinde durum noktası: çalışıyor (alt ajan konuları dahil), bekliyor (turuncu), yeni yanıt (yeşil); sohbet açıkken satır seçili görünür. Host rotası `GET /api/composer-plus.assistant-status` → `{sessionId|null}` (sohbet oluşturmaz). Tether: liste süzgeci + sparkles düğmesinde nokta.
- `assistantChatView` (istemci, varsayılan açık): asistan sohbetinde araç, düşünme ve süreç satırları gizlenir; kullanıcı mesajı, yanıt, hata ve onay kartı kalır. Tether: aynı süzgeç.
- `assistantNotify` (Host, OFF_BY_DEFAULT: model turu başlatır): başka bir kök sohbet bitince (≥ 60 sn süren tur), hata verince ya da 30 sn'den uzun seni bekleyince asistan sohbetine gizli bir not düşer ve asistan tek satırla haber verir. 15 sn toplama, iki haber turu arası en az 2 dk, notta en çok 10 sohbet. Rota `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.assistant-notify {on}`.
- `assistantFree` (Host, varsayılan açık): asistanın kendisi yalnız kısa araçları çağırır (kendi 6 aracı, schedule_*, subagent*, send_message, list_agents, interrupt_agent, list_subagent_models, job_*, memory_*, ask_user_question, run_code); öteki çağrılar `tools/pre-execute` ile reddedilir ve model işi arka plandaki bir alt ajana verir. Alt ajanları etkilenmez. Asistanın son isteğinde `subagent` aracı yoksa kural çalışmaz, Ayarlar nedeni yazar. Rota `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.assistant-free {on}`.

### Kararlar
- Gizleme `data-row-key` + `--dsh-workspace-indent` / `_flatList` seçicileriyle tek bir stil kuralı (Recent projects süzgecinin tekniği); dsh'de gizli sohbet bayrağı yok, arşivlenmiş sohbet açılmıyor.
- Durum noktası `sidebar.panellist` simgesinde (standart `useSessionStatus`/`useSessions`/`useWorkspaces`/`usePanelInfo` kancaları 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1 kataloğunda var); `projectStatus()` yeniden kullanılır. Etiket sabit kalır (kaydı yenilemek satırı yeniden kurar); durum `aria-description` ile duyurulur.
- Haber notu One-click Continue'nun yolunu kullanır: boş işaret `steer`, `agent/pre-step` içinde nota çevrilir; sohbette kullanıcı balonu çıkmaz. Not veridir uyarısı taşır, son yanıt `lastReply` ile (geri alma iki işaret türü), çatalda yalnız kendi olayları.
- Sapma (plana göre): bekleme eşiği 2 sn yerine 30 sn, bitiş için ≥ 60 sn tur şartı. Neden: kullanıcı başka bir sohbette etkileşimli çalışırken her kısa yanıt bir haber turu (token) olurdu.
- `tools.restrict` kullanılmaz: kısıtlama alt ajanlara geçer ve onların bash/web aracını da keserdi.

### Aşamalar ve kontroller
1. Host (index.js) + `test/assistant-bot.test.mjs` (mutasyonlarla).
2. İstemci (client.js) + `test/assistant-bot-client.test.mjs`, `toggles.test.mjs`.
3. 11 dil, features.json (how ×13, llms, routes), 13 README sınırlar/SSS, `build:locales`, `build:readme`.
4. Tether: PLUS `assistant-status`, Core/UI, Tests.swift, FEATURES.md; iOS testleri.
5. Gerçek dsh `test/browser-assistant.mjs` yeni adımlar (0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1), `SHOT=1` dört görüntü, simülatör, `npm test`, `npm pack --dry-run`.

### Yapılmayacak
Sürüm, yayın, canlı masaüstü kopyası, iPhone kurulumu.

## Asistan: görünür dal sohbetleri (2026-11-09, dal `feature/gorunur-dal-sohbetleri`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe): asistan bir işi yapay zekâya verdiğinde iş gizli alt ajanda değil, DSH'de görünür bir sohbette çalışsın. Sohbet, DSH'nin "Yeni dal" seçeneği gibi ilgili projenin altında bir dal (worktree) olarak görünsün; kullanıcı ilerlemeyi canlı izlesin. Asistan hep boşta kalsın ve iş bitince sonucu alsın.

### Hedef
- `chat_new` aracına `branch` (boolean) ve `title` (metin) seçenekleri. `branch: true`, Worktree chats özelliğinin `startOnBranch` yolunu kullanır: `<repo>/.worktrees/<ad>` worktree, yeni dal, dsh projesi, sohbet, ilk mesaj. Kaynak sohbet yerine proje klasörü verilir; dsh'nin varsayılan modu ve modeli kullanılır.
- Asistanın başlattığı her sohbet bir "görev" olarak kaydedilir (`assistant-tasks.json`). Görevin ilk turu bitince ya da hata verince asistan sohbetine gizli bir not gelir (Assistant news yolu). Bu not Assistant news anahtarı kapalıyken de gelir, 60 sn eşiği ve 2 dk aralık beklemez. Görev sohbeti onay ya da cevap beklerse de not gelir.
- Asistan istemi: proje işi (depoda kod ya da ayar değişikliği) `chat_new` + `branch: true` ile görünür dal sohbetinde yapılır; araştırma ve kısa işler gizli alt ajanda kalır.

### Değişmezler
- Görev başına tek onay: `chat_new` onay kartı kalır (istem enjeksiyonuna karşı koruma). Kart dal ve başlığı gösterir.
- Ana klasörde `checkout`/`switch` yok; yalnız `git worktree add -b` (DSH arayüzündeki "Yeni dal" ile aynı).
- Branch per chat (`worktrees.json`) kapalıysa `branch: true` reddedilir.
- Asistan araç izin listesi (Assistant always free) değişmez.
- Yeni kenar çubuğu arayüzü yok: dsh ve Branches in the workspace tree, worktree projesini projenin altında gösterir.

### Kabul ölçütleri
1. `branch: true` bir git deposunda `.worktrees/<ad>` worktree, yeni dal, dsh projesi ve sohbet açar; ilk mesaj gider; `title` sohbet adı olur.
2. Depo olmayan proje, kapalı Branch per chat ve geçersiz başlık reddedilir; sohbet açılmaz.
3. Görev turunun sonu (completed, max-tokens, error) Assistant news kapalıyken de asistanı bir notla uyandırır; not sonucu içerir; görev listeden çıkar.
4. Kullanıcı durdurursa görev not vermeden listeden çıkar; dsh'nin kapanışı (interrupted, disposed) görevi listede tutar.
5. Görev listesi yeniden başlatmadan sonra dosyadan okunur.
6. Var olan testler geçer.

### Aşamalar
1. Host: `startOnBranch` proje klasörü kabul eder; `chat_new` seçenekleri; görev deposu; haber yolu. Test: `test/assistant-branch-chat.test.mjs` (gerçek git, sahte dsh).
2. İstem metni (ASSISTANT_SECTION, ASSISTANT_FREE_SECTION), `docs/features.json` (en `how`, `llms`), `npm run build:readme`.
3. `npm test`, öz denetim (review-guard), düzeltmeler, tekrar denetim.

### İzinli yollar
`index.js`, `test/assistant-branch-chat.test.mjs`, `package.json` (test betiği), `docs/features.json`, `README.md` ve `llms.txt` (üretilen bölüm), `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`.

### Kararlar
- Yeni araç yerine `chat_new` seçeneği: araç adı izin listesinde zaten var, yapılacaklar dalıyla çakışma en az olur.
- Ayrı bir özellik anahtarı yok: bu, `assistant` özelliğinin `chat_new` aracının bir seçeneğidir.
- Sonuç bildirimi model turu başlatır, ama kullanıcı görevi onay kartıyla başlattı; bu yüzden ayrı bir "kapalı" varsayılan yok.

### Riskler
- `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` ve asistan istem metni yapılacaklar dalıyla birleşmede çakışabilir.
- Görev listesinden yalnız ilk tur sonucu gelir; kullanıcı görev sohbetinde devam ederse sonraki turlar haber vermez (Assistant news açıksa uzun turlar gelir).
- 11 dilde açıklama ve README çevirileri bu dalda güncellenmedi.

### Son kontrol noktası
- 2026-11-09 13:15: plan yazıldı, kod yok.
- 2026-11-09 13:40: aşama 1 ve 2 bitti, commit `bba465c`. `npm test` exit 0. Yeni test 9 mutasyonun hepsinde kırmızı.

Düzeltme: araç yanıtı, araç açıklaması, istem ve 13 dilde `how` metni dal adlı proje ve ağaç görünümünü anlatır.

## Asistan açılış hızı ve uzun sohbette takılma (2026-11-09, dal `fix/asistan-acilis-hizi`)

### Amaç
Assistant satırına tıklayınca asistan sohbeti normal bir sohbet kadar hızlı açılır. Uzun, çok alt ajanlı sohbette yazarken ve akış sürerken takılma azalır (üst ajanın kapsam genişletmesi, madde 1-5).

### Ölçüm (temel değer)
- Yalıtılmış headless dsh 0.2.2-rc.1, `~/.dsh/sessions` yerel kopyası (1309 sohbet), sahte model: `GET /api/composer-plus.assistant` tıklama başına 466, 350, 228, 313, 269 ms. İstemci `openSession`'ı bu yanıttan sonra çağırır; normal satır hemen çağırır.
- Kod düzeyi (tarayıcısız, aynı kopya): `sessionPersistence.list()` 139-223 ms, `stat(id)` 5-7 ms. Rota her tıklamada `list()` çağırır (`index.js` `stored`), sonra `resolveAgent(id)` bekler (canlı değilse tüm ajan diskten açılır).
- Kullanıcının GUI'si ölçülemedi: Chrome oturumu 401 verdi (masaüstü uygulaması), token alınmadı.

### Değişmezler
- Silinen sohbet yine yeniden yaratılır; arşivlenen sohbet yine arşivden çıkar; iki tıklama tek sohbet yapar.
- Asistan kapalıyken rota 409 döner; istemci sohbet açmaz.
- Rollback ile geri alınan satırlar, mesaj bağlantısı ve geri alma düğmeleri aynı davranır.

### Kabul ölçütü
- Bilinen asistan sohbeti: istemci `openSession`'ı Host yanıtını beklemeden çağırır (hedef: tıklamadan `openSession`'a < 50 ms; birim testinde fetch hiç dönmezken de açılır).
- Rota: bilinen ve canlı/kayıtlı sohbet için `persistence.list()` çağrılmaz, `resolveAgent` beklenmez (birim testi).
- Madde 1-5: her biri ayrı commit ve bir test ya da ölçülebilir kontrol.

### Aşamalar
1. A: açılış hızı (istemci + Host), test.
2. B (1): tek paylaşılan body MutationObserver, `[data-chat-flow]` içi değişiklikleri yok sayar, toplu çalışır.
3. C (2): sohbet satırı taramaları yalnız yeni satırları işler veya tur sürerken seyrekleşir.
4. D (3): StatusOrder ve assistantApart `:has()` kuralları.
5. E (4): dar seçiciler.
6. F (5): asistan görünümünde son N tur, "Öncekileri göster".

### İzinli yollar
`client.js`, `index.js`, `test/*`, `package.json` (test betiği), dil dosyaları yalnız yeni metin gerekirse, bu bölüm.

### Kontroller
`npm test`; ilgili `test/*.test.mjs`; mümkünse `test/browser-assistant.mjs` (headless, sahte model).

### Kararlar
- Canlı veride ikinci headless ölçüm Auto review tarafından reddedildi; tekrar denenmedi. Sonrası "canlı ölçülmedi"; birim testi ve kod düzeyi ölçüm verilir.

### Riskler
- Bilinen kimlik silinmiş bir sohbete aitse istemci önce boş/eksik bir görünüm açabilir; Host yanıtı yeni kimlik verince yeni sohbete geçer.
- `fix/asistan-durum-gostergesi` main'e girdi (afd332d); bu dal onun üstünde başlar. `AssistantApartIcon` ve `assistantApartCss` yakınında fark en küçük tutulur.

### Son kontrol noktası
- 2026-11-09 13:50: plan yazıldı, kod yok.
- 2026-11-09 15:25: tüm aşamalar bitti, main 0dd0913 üstüne rebase edildi. `npm test` exit 0. Gerçek dsh (headless, sahte model) `test/browser-assistant.mjs` 0.2.0-rc.2, 0.2.1-rc.2, 0.2.2-rc.1 PASS; adım 13: tıklamadan sohbete 18-47 ms, son 8 tur görünür.
  - İnceleme kapısı: 3 tur. Bulunan ve düzeltilen: sınır sohbet sütunu içindeki yeni turu görmüyordu; sohbet yeniden açılınca sınır geri gelmiyordu; düğme metni 1 tur için yanlış çoğuldu; StatusOrder'da yalnız test için kalan yedek. Son tur temiz.
  - Canlı veri kopyası (/tmp/cp-asistan-bench) silindi. Canlı GUI ölçülmedi (Chrome oturumu 401).

## Asistan onay kartı tam metin (2026-11-09, dal `fix/onay-karti-tam-metin`)

### Amaç
`chat_send` ve `chat_new` onay kartı mesajın tamamını gösterir; kullanıcı onaylamadan önce gönderilecek metni okuyabilir.

### Neden (doğrulandı)
Kırpma Composer Plus'ta: `index.js` `tools/pre-execute` işleyicisi metni `cutEnd(a.text.trim(), 300)` ile 300 karakterde kesiyordu. dsh çekirdeği kesmiyor: `dsh-client-ui-approval` kartı nedeni `ImxglW_headline` içinde düz metin olarak çizer, gövde (`ImxglW_body`) `max-height: var(--dsh-composer-text-max-height); overflow-y: auto` ile kayar.

### Değişiklik
Sınır `ASSISTANT_LIMITS.text` (8000; araçlar daha uzun metni zaten reddeder). Metin düz metin kalır (HTML yok); enjeksiyon koruması değişmez.

### Kontroller
- `test/assistant.test.mjs`: chat_send ve chat_new kartı tam metni (1400+ karakter, `<b>` dahil) taşır; main'de kırmızı.

## Asistan sohbetinde yapılacaklar listesi (2026-11-09, dal `feat/sohbet-ici-yapilacaklar`)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe): kullanıcı aklındaki işleri asistana söyler ve bunları kendisi yapar. "Sen yap" derse asistan yapar. Liste asistan sohbetinin içinde, konuşmanın sağında görünür (dsh'nin sağ kenar paneli değil). Bir tıkla iş asistana devredilir, bir tıkla listeden silinir. Asistan maddeleri kendiliğinden denetler ve biten maddeyi kendisi siler. İş ayrı dalda yapılır, main'e birleştirme ve push yok.
Kapsam eki (aynı dal, ayrı commit): sağdaki liste "Durum" paneline döner (Yapılacaklar + asistanın verdiği görevler + proje grupları + sabah özeti). Önce yapılacaklar listesi biter ve commit edilir.

### Hedef
- `assistantTodos` (Host anahtarı, varsayılan açık): asistan sohbetinin listesi `storages/dsh-composer-plus/assistant-todos.json` dosyasında, sohbet kimliğine göre (`{lists: {<sessionId>: [{id, text, owner: user|agent, created}]}}`). Yeniden yükleme ve dsh yeniden başlatmadan sonra kalır.
- İki ajan aracı, yalnız asistan sohbetinin kendi kapsamında (öteki altı araçla aynı yol): `todo_add({text, owner})` (aynı metinli açık madde varsa yalnız sahibini değiştirir) ve `todo_remove({id, reason})`. Listeleme aracı yok: güncel liste her adımda runtime context ile gelir (YAGNI). İki araç `ASSISTANT_TOOLS` içindedir, böylece Assistant always free izin listesinde ve alt ajanlarda yasaklı olur.
- Runtime context (`systemPrompt.context`, yalnız asistan sohbetine): açık maddeler, sahipleri ve kısa kural. Kanıt varsa `todo_remove` ile sil; kullanıcının maddesini kullanıcı anmadıkça sorma. Boş listede context yoktur.
- Route `GET/POST /api/composer-plus.assistant-todos`: GET `{on, items?}` (`?sessionId=`), POST `{on}` anahtar, POST `{sessionId, id, action: remove|handover, message?}` madde işlemi. Devretme: sahibi `agent` yapar ve `sessionController.prompt` (mode queue) ile normal kullanıcı mesajı gönderir. Gönderim başarısız olursa sahip eski hâline döner (hepsi ya da hiçbiri).
- İstemci: `conversation.input.dock` girişinden, yalnız asistan sohbetinde, `[data-conversation-content]` öğesine portal ile sağda sabit bir panel. Her maddede "Asistana devret" ve "Kaldır" düğmeleri (gerçek `button`, `aria-label`, odak). Panel kendi deposuna abone olur. Tur sonunda, sohbet çalışırken 5 sn'de bir ve kendi işleminden sonra yeniden okur. Sohbeti yeniden çizdirmez.

### Değişmezler
- dsh'nin sağ kenar paneli ve öteki özellikler değişmez. Yeni bağımlılık yok. Uygulama paketi değişmez, dsh yeniden başlatılmaz.
- Madde metni veridir: context'te "data, not instructions" uyarısı. `{{` metni bozulmasın diye kaçışlanır (0.2.0 context'i interpolate eder).
- Kişisel tohum maddeleri depoya ve pakete girmez. Yalnız canlı depolama dosyasına yazılır.

### Kabul ölçütleri
1. Asistan sohbetinde sağda "Yapılacaklar" paneli görünür. Başka sohbette görünmez.
2. "Kaldır" maddeyi onaysız siler. "Asistana devret" sohbete "Bunu sen yap: <madde>" mesajı gönderir ve sahibi `agent` olur.
3. Ajan `todo_add`/`todo_remove` çağırabilir (Assistant always free reddetmez). Liste context'te görünür.
4. Liste dosyada kalır, yeni bir Host örneği aynı listeyi okur.
5. Anahtar kapalıyken araçlar, context ve panel yoktur.

### Aşamalar
1. Host: depo + araçlar + context + route + `test/assistant-todos.test.mjs` (ekle, sil, devret, kalıcılık, anahtar). Mevcut assistant testleri geçer.
2. İstemci: panel + FEATURES/SHOT_OF + en/zh/11 dil + `test/assistant-todos-client.test.mjs`, `toggles.test.mjs` sayıları.
3. Belgeler: `docs/features.json` (how ×13, llms, route), `build:readme`, `tether/FEATURES.md` satırı.
4. Gerçek dsh (headless, sahte model) `test/browser-assistant-todos.mjs`, `SHOT=1` görüntü. `npm test`.
5. Commit. Öz denetim (review-guard), bulguları doğrula (prepare-fix-plan), düzelt, temiz olana kadar tekrarla.
6. Canlı depolama dosyasına iki tohum madde.

### İzinli yollar
`index.js`, `client.js`, `locales/*.json`, `docs/features.json`, üretilen README/llms/ROUTES, `tether/FEATURES.md`, `assets/features/assistantTodos.webp`, `package.json` (test betiği), `test/assistant-todos*.mjs`, `test/browser-assistant-todos.mjs`, mevcut test sayıları, bu plan. Canlı: yalnız `~/.dsh/storages/dsh-composer-plus/assistant-todos.json` (yeni dosya).

### Kararlar
- Depolama Host dosyasında, olay günlüğünde değil: dsh bilinmeyen olay türlerini kalıcı katmanda reddedebilir.
- Context, bölüm (section) değil: bölüm sabit kalır ve sağlayıcı önbelleği bozulmaz.
- Devretme mesajını istemci dilinde istemci yazar, Host gönderir: tek istek, geri alma Host'ta.

### Riskler
- Panel konumu dsh'nin `data-conversation-content` işaretine bağlıdır. dsh bunu değiştirirse panel görünmez (ponytail notu).
- Dar pencerede panel sohbet içeriğinin üstüne gelebilir. Panel daraltılabilir.

## 2. bölüm: Durum paneli (2026-11-09, aynı dal, ayrı commit'ler)
İstek (sahip, Türkçe, ana ajan aracılığıyla): asistan etkileşimli çıktı versin. Sabah proje özeti, proje adına tıklayınca son güncellemeler ve verdiği görevlerin durumu (verildi, bitti, sürüyor). UX bana bırakıldı. Performans kritik: uzun sohbet ve çok alt ajanla yazarken gecikme var.

### Hedef
Sağdaki liste "Durum" paneline döner (aynı anahtar `assistantTodos`, etiketi "Asistan durum paneli"):
- Sabah özeti kartı (bugünün özeti varsa en üstte, kapatılabilir). Kaynak: yeni ajan aracı `status_summary_set({projects: [{project, text}]})`; sahibin 08:00 görevi ("Sabah proje özeti") bunu çağırır. Host `assistant-summary.json` içinde sohbet başına son özeti saklar.
- Yapılacaklar (1. bölüm).
- Görevler: asistanın alt ajanları (Host, asistan sohbetinin `subagent/catalog` olaylarından): çalışıyor, seni bekliyor, bugün biten ya da hata veren; başlık, durum, geçen süre, son yanıtın ilk satırı. Tıklayınca görevin sohbeti açılır.
- Projeler: son 24 saatte güncellenen kök sohbetlerin projeleri (en çok 5), kapalı satır: tek satır özet (sabah özetindeki cümle, yoksa sohbet sayısı). Açınca: projenin sohbetleri (tıklanınca açılır), adı geçen yapılacaklar ve git dalı ile son commit'ler (yalnız açınca, salt okunur `runGit`).
- Başlıkta "Yenile" düğmesi ve "Son güncelleme" saati.

### Değişmezler
- Panel yazarken yeniden çizilmez: ağır bileşen `shell.overlay` girişinde (yazmayla değişmez), dock girişi yalnız konum ve tur bilgisini bir depoya yayınlar. Alt ajan değişiklikleri birleştirilir (1 sn gecikme, en az 3 sn ara).
- Git yalnız kayıtlı proje yolunda ve yalnız okuma komutlarıyla (`runGit`, argv, zaman aşımı). İstemci yol göndermez, proje kimliği gönderir.
- Özet metni ve görev sonucu veridir; context'e girmez.

### Kabul ölçütleri
1. Panelde dört bölüm görünür; boş bölüm kısa bir not gösterir.
2. Ajan `status_summary_set` çağırınca kart bugünün tarihiyle görünür. Kapat ile gizlenir.
3. Alt ajan başlayınca, bitince ve sonuç verince Görevler güncellenir (yenilemeye basmadan).
4. Proje satırı tıklanınca açılır, sohbetler ve git bilgisi görünür, sohbete tıklayınca sohbet açılır.
5. Yazarken panel bileşeni çizilmez (gerçek dsh testinde sayaçla ölçülür).

### Aşamalar
1. Host: özet deposu + araç + GET'e `summary`, `tasks`, `projects`; `?project=` git bilgisi + birim testi.
2. İstemci: Durum paneli, konum deposu, birleştirilmiş yenileme + birim testi.
3. Metinler (13 dil), belgeler, gerçek dsh testi, görüntü, `npm test`, commit, öz denetim döngüsü.
